### [1] [Probing Lepton Flavor Violation at FCC-ee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27057)
*L. Allwicher,R. Bartocci,M. Cepeda,C. Cornella,L. P. S. Leal,A. Lusiani,C. Martin Perez,P. Paradisi,E. Scantamburlo,O. Sumensari*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在SMEFT框架下评估FCC-ee通过τ衰变探测轻子味破坏（LFV）的潜力，发现τ→3ℓ与τ→ℓγ可将现有分支比上限改善30至800倍，且在多数LFV相互作用上优于Z→τℓ搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FCC-ee的Z极点运行预期产生约2×10^11对τ轻子，为轻子味破坏（lepton flavor violation, LFV）探测提供高统计量机遇；而以往对未来e+e-对撞机LFV研究的关注多集中于Z→τℓ与h→τℓ，τ衰变通道的探测潜力尚未被系统比较。

Method: 在标准模型有效场论（Standard Model Effective Field Theory, SMEFT）框架下，利用FCC-ee Z极点运行的τ轻子对产额估算τ→3ℓ与τ→ℓγ分支比的预期灵敏度，并与Z→τℓ、h→τℓ搜索的新物理（new physics）reach进行对比，覆盖不同SMEFT算符类别。

Result: τ→3ℓ与τ→ℓγ分支比的现有上限可依通道不同改善30至800倍；τ衰变对同一LFV相互作用的灵敏度超过Z→τℓ搜索的当前及预期水平，并可探测更广泛的SMEFT算符范围。对LFV汤川型（Yukawa-like）相互作用，h→τℓ直接搜索灵敏度最强；而对辐射诱导LFV希格斯衰变的半轻子（semileptonic）算符，该层级可反转，τ衰变成为主导探针。

Conclusion: τ衰变是FCC-ee上探测LFV的重要且常被低估的通道，与Z→τℓ、h→τℓ搜索形成互补；最优探针的选择依赖具体SMEFT算符类型，汤川型相互作用宜用h→τℓ，半轻子算符则宜用τ衰变。

Abstract: We investigate the prospects for probing Lepton Flavor Violation (LFV) at FCC-ee within the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT) framework. The approximately $2 \times 10^{11}$ $τ$-lepton pairs expected at the $Z$-pole run of FCC-ee could improve current limits on the branching fractions of $τ\to3\ell$ and $τ\to\ellγ$ ($\ell=e,μ$) by factors ranging from 30 to 800, depending on the specific channel. We compare the resulting new-physics reach with that obtained from searches for $Z \to τ\ell$ and $h \to τ\ell$, which have so far received greater attention in studies of LFV at future $e^+e^-$ colliders. We find that $τ$ decays can surpass both the current and the projected sensitivity of $Z\toτ\ell$ searches to the same LFV interactions, while probing a broader range of SMEFT operators. We further show that, for LFV Yukawa-like interactions, direct searches for $h\toτ\ell$ provide the strongest sensitivity. By contrast, for semileptonic operators that radiatively induce LFV Higgs decays, this hierarchy can be reversed, with $τ$ decays providing the leading probe.

</details>


### [2] [Two-loop helicity amplitudes for $gg\to W^+W^-$ using high-energy and forward expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27092)
*Mateusz Czaja,Ben Rott,Matthias Steinhauser,Daniel Stremmer*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文计算了双非壳（off-shell）W玻色子过程 gg→W⁺W⁻ 在包含虚顶夸克（top quark）圈图时的次领头阶（next-to-leading order, NLO）QCD修正，并通过组合相空间不同区域的展开式，获得覆盖全相空间的高精度螺旋度振幅（helicity amplitudes）结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确预言 gg→W⁺W⁻ 过程需要对含顶夸克圈的NLO QCD修正进行可靠计算；而双非壳W玻色子情形下，不同相空间区域（高能区、前向散射区、大顶夸克质量区）的展开适用性不同，需统一处理以获得全相空间的精确结果。

Method: 采用分区域展开相结合的策略：在 Mandelstam 变量远大于顶夸克质量时做高能展开（high-energy expansion），在前向散射极限（forward-scattering limit，即 t 较小）附近做展开，并给出大顶夸克质量展开（large top-quark mass expansion）作为独立交叉检验。

Result: 文中给出了各展开的技术细节，并证明在全相空间内对所有螺旋度振幅均能获得精确结果；结果显式依赖于全部输入参数。

Conclusion: 通过组合多区域展开，可在整个相空间内对双非壳 gg→W⁺W⁻ 过程的NLO QCD螺旋度振幅给出精确且依赖完整输入参数的计算结果，大质量展开提供了有效的验证手段。

Abstract: We calculate next-to-leading order QCD corrections for the process $gg \to W^+W^-$ with two off-shell $W$ bosons, accounting for virtual top quarks in the loop diagrams. Our approach relies on combining expansions valid in different regions of phase space. Specifically, we perform a high-energy expansion in which the Mandelstam variables are much larger than the top-quark mass. Furthermore, we expand around the forward-scattering limit, where the Mandelstam variable $t$ is small. We also provide an expansion for large top-quark mass, which serves as an important cross-check. We provide technical details regarding the various expansions and demonstrate that precise results are obtained for all helicity amplitudes across the entire phase space. Our results exhibit an explicit dependence on all input parameters.

</details>


### [3] [This is SParTA: A modular, efficient and scalable transformer architecture for jet-tagging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27106)
*Rajneil Baruah,Subhadeep Mondal,Sunando Kumar Patra,Satyajit Roy*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: SParTA 是一种模块化、可扩展且高效的 Transformer 架构，通过按横动量（transverse momentum）将喷注组分划分为硬核（hard core）与软组分两个拓扑区域，分别用二次自注意力与线性交叉注意力（cross-attention）处理，从而在高能对撞机喷注标记（jet tagging）任务上以远少于对比模型的参数量取得相当或更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全自注意力（full self-attention）对全部 l 个喷注组分计算带来 O(l²) 内存瓶颈，而喷注组分横动量谱（p_T spectrum）陡降，多数软组分信息冗余度较高。作者据此寻求一种兼顾效率与物理信息保留的可扩展 Transformer。

Method: 按 p_T 降序取前 N 个最硬组分构成硬核，在其上做 O(N²) 的差分交互注意力（differential interaction attention），由成对物理与可学习关系特征驱动；其余软组分通过 O(lN) 线性交叉注意力整合进硬核。成对交互特征在网络内由四动量（four-momenta）动态计算，并以喷注级子结构观测量（substructure observables）为条件。

Result: 在夸克-胶子标记（quark-gluon tagging）基准上，300K 参数的 SParTA 达到 92.02% AUC 与 85.06% 准确率，以约七分之一参数量匹敌 2.14M 参数的 Particle Transformer（ParT），并在 30% 信号效率下背景拒绝率比 IAFormer 高 21%。在 boosted top-tagging 基准上，511K 参数的 SParTA 优于 ParT 与 ParticleNet。

Conclusion: 按 p_T 分层的稀疏粒子交互分解可在保持软辐射（soft radiation）与色流（color-flow）信息的同时显著降低算力与参数开销；论文同时分析了该架构相对全稠密多层交互网络（fully dense multi-layer interaction networks）的架构权衡。

Abstract: We present the Sparse Particle-interaction Transformer Architecture (SParTA), a modular, scalable, and efficient transformer for jet tagging at high-energy colliders. Exploiting the steeply falling transverse-momentum ($p_T$) spectrum of jet constituents, SParTA factorizes constituent processing into two topological regimes. Ordered by descending $p_T$, only the $N$ hardest constituents (the hard core) participate in quadratic $O(N^2)$ differential interaction attention driven by pairwise physical and learned relational features. The remaining soft constituents are integrated via linear $O(lN)$ cross-attention to the hard core, preserving soft-radiation and color-flow information while avoiding the $O(l^2)$ memory bottleneck of full self-attention over all $l$ constituents. Pairwise interaction features are computed dynamically in-network from constituent four-momenta and conditioned on global jet-level substructure observables. On the quark-gluon tagging benchmark, a 300K-parameter SParTA model achieves an AUC of 92.02% and accuracy of 85.06%, matching the 2.14M-parameter Particle Transformer (ParT) at one-seventh the parameter count and outperforming IAFormer by 21% in background rejection at 30% signal efficiency. On the boosted top-tagging benchmark, SParTA outperforms ParT and ParticleNet with 511K parameters, while we analyze the architectural trade-offs relative to fully dense multi-layer interaction networks.

</details>


### [4] [Illuminating Light Dark Matter with Fixed-Target Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27131)
*Taylor R. Gray*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该博士论文围绕轻暗物质（light dark matter, MeV–GeV 质量区间）在固定靶实验（fixed-target experiments）中的探测，构建从模型构建、统计推断、全局拟合到信号模拟的理论与统计工具链，重点评估 LDMX 与 SHiP 的探测能力，并拓展至自旋-1 暗物质、暗光子电磁矩及核子多体建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非引力暗物质搜寻多针对 GeV–TeV 质量、以核反冲（nuclear recoil）为探测通道，迄今尚无发现；而 MeV–GeV 轻暗物质在理论上可自然再现观测到的遗迹丰度（relic abundance），且对核反冲搜寻不可见，需借助电子反冲（electron recoil）或固定靶实验探测，相关理论与统计工具尚不完善。

Method: 梳理轻暗物质固定靶搜寻的整体图景并以 LDMX 为核心；在最小暗物质模型（minimal DM models）内建立基于似然（likelihood-based）的统计框架，利用完整的二维反冲电子分布，覆盖排除、发现、参数估计与模型比较，并给出频率学派与贝叶斯（Bayesian）表述；对 SHiP 模拟暗物质信号以评估灵敏度；对标量与费米子轻暗物质开展结合宇宙学、天体物理与实验室约束的全局拟合（global fits）；将固定靶搜寻扩展至自旋-1 暗物质与具更高电磁矩的暗光子；并在暗光子产生的核建模中引入弹性核形状因子（nuclear form factors）与多体从头计算（ab initio）谱函数。

Result: 所建立的统计框架确立了 LDMX 的发现潜力，并量化了独立确认潜在信号所需的直接探测曝光量（direct detection exposure）；全局拟合给出标量与费米子轻暗物质的约束图景；自旋-1 暗物质与高电磁矩暗光子展现出与其他探针强互补的丰富信号；采用形状因子与从头计算谱函数的核模型改进了暗光子产生的标准核建模处理。

Conclusion: 固定靶实验是探测 MeV–GeV 轻暗物质的关键途径，需配套的统计推断、全局拟合与精确信号模拟工具；该论文提供的框架可支撑 LDMX 等实验的发现潜力评估，并通过扩展模型与改进核建模增强与其他探测手段的互补性。

Abstract: Astronomical and cosmological observations provide overwhelming evidence for a non-luminous matter component, dark matter (DM), whose particle nature remains unknown. Experimental searches for non-gravitational DM interactions have largely targeted DM with GeV-TeV masses via nuclear recoils, yielding no discovery thus far. DM in the MeV-GeV mass range, however, is theoretically motivated, reproducing the observed relic abundance while remaining invisible to nuclear recoil searches. Such light DM is better probed via electron recoils or at fixed-target experiments. This thesis advances the theoretical and statistical toolkit for light DM searches at fixed-target experiments, spanning model building, statistical inference, global fits, and signal simulation. We survey the landscape of fixed-target searches for light DM, focusing on the Light Dark Matter eXperiment (LDMX). We also simulate DM signatures at the next-generation experiment SHiP to assess its sensitivity. Within minimal DM models, we present a likelihood-based statistical framework for LDMX using the full two-dimensional recoil electron distribution, spanning exclusion, discovery, parameter estimation, and model comparison in frequentist and Bayesian formulations. This establishes the discovery potential of LDMX, and we quantify the direct detection exposure needed to independently confirm a potential signal. Beyond this paradigm, we perform global fits of scalar and fermionic light DM, combining cosmological, astrophysical, and laboratory constraints. We then extend fixed-target searches to spin-1 DM and higher electromagnetic moment dark photons, yielding rich signatures with strong complementarity to other probes. Finally, we go beyond standard nuclear modelling of dark photon production, incorporating elastic nuclear form factors and spectral functions from many-body ab initio methods in place of analytic treatments.

</details>


### [5] [Jets as differential probes of hot QCD matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27182)
*João M. Silva*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该博士论文基于BDMPS-Z框架发展微扰QCD工具，通过微分喷注子结构观测量（能量关联函数、分位数匹配定义的能损、能损函数及多点关联）提取热QCD物质的方向性各向异性与多点评射动力学信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超相对论重离子碰撞产生的解禁闭热QCD物质寿命极短，其早期远离平衡的动力学与后期流体夸克-胶子等离子体演化相连；高能喷注作为多尺度探针，可借其在介质中的修正反推介质的微观性质，但需要相应的微扰QCD理论工具。

Method: 以BDMPS-Z描述高能部分子在随机色场中传播为框架：将标量展宽参数替换为方向依赖的横向动量展宽以处理各向异性介质；引入能量关联函数（含重味EEC、方位角微分EEC与三叶草投影）作为敏感观测量；用分位数匹配定义重构能损，并发展能损函数及其矩在多点投影能量关联函数中的表达；进一步研究连续发射间的自旋诱导关联及含介质响应的更高点关联。

Result: 各向异性修正与介质诱导分裂中的自旋信息及末态能量流的方位角图样相关联；重构能损表现为真空与介质修正喷注谱之间的人群层面移动，能损函数的矩进入多点投影能量关联函数；自旋诱导的连续发射关联与更高点关联（含介质响应贡献）超出单一1→2介质诱导分裂的图像。

Conclusion: 微分喷注子结构观测量可探测QCD物质的方向性属性，以及喷注在介质中传播的多点动力学。

Abstract: Ultra-relativistic heavy-ion collisions create short-lived droplets of deconfined, hot QCD matter whose evolution connects far-from-equilibrium early dynamics to the hydrodynamic quark-gluon plasma. High-energy jets, produced in early hard scatterings and modified by this evolving medium, provide multi-scale probes of its microscopic properties. This thesis develops perturbative QCD tools to extract information about hot QCD matter from its imprint on differential jet substructure observables.
  Our framework is the BDMPS-Z description of high-energy partons propagating through stochastic colour fields. We first study anisotropic QCD matter by replacing the usual scalar broadening parameter with direction-dependent transverse momentum broadening. This relates anisotropic corrections to medium-induced splittings to spin information and azimuthal patterns in final-state energy flow. Energy correlators are then used as observables sensitive to these effects, including heavy-flavour EECs, azimuthally differential EECs and clover projections designed to isolate leading anisotropy-induced harmonics.
  We also examine how energy loss affects the interpretation of jet substructure modifications. Quantile matching defines reconstructed energy loss as a population-level shift between vacuum and medium-modified jet spectra. We further develop energy-loss functions, which encode the distribution of energy retained by jet-originated hadrons, and show how their moments enter multi-point projected energy correlators. Finally, we go beyond single $1\to2$ medium-induced splittings by studying spin-induced correlations between successive emissions and higher-point energy correlators including contributions from medium response. The results show that differential jet substructure can probe directional properties of QCD matter and multi-point dynamics of jets propagating through it.

</details>


### [6] [The $b\bar{b}c\bar{c}$ Fully Heavy Tetraquark at the LHC and the FCC: Multimodal Fragmentation and Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27396)
*João V. Bulhões,Francesco G. Celiberto,Victor P. Gonçalves*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本研究通过多模式碎裂（fragmentation）框架结合直接多夸克与标量双夸克机制，计算全重味混合味四夸克态（tetraquark）$b\bar b c\bar c$ 的产生，并在HL-LHC与FCC能标下评估其可观测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全重味混合味四夸克态 $b\bar b c\bar c$ 的微观形成机制尚不明确，需建立形成机制与其在对撞机上可观测性之间的定量联系。

Method: 采用直接多夸克（direct-multiquark）与标量双夸克（scalar-diquark）两种碎裂机制，应用标准DGLAP演化并通过HF-NRevo方案保证阈值一致性，将碎裂函数（FFs）嵌入高能与共线混合因子化（hybrid high-energy and collinear factorization）框架，在次领头阶（NLO）计算半单举四夸克加喷注产生。

Result: 在14 TeV HL-LHC与100 TeV FCC下比较快度间隔分布（rapidity-separation distributions），显示直接多夸克通道相对标量双夸克模式具有明显的唯象学偏好；积分截面与预期事例数为未来搜寻提供良好前景。

Conclusion: 结果在微观形成机制与 $b\bar b c\bar c$ 四夸克态在当前及未来强子对撞机上的可观测性之间建立了定量联系。

Abstract: We investigate the production of the all-heavy mixed-flavor $b\bar b c\bar c$ tetraquark through a multimodal fragmentation setup combining direct-multiquark and scalar-diquark mechanisms. Standard Dokshitzer-Gribov-Lipatov-Altarelli-Parisi (DGLAP) evolution is applied while preserving threshold consistency through the Heavy-Flavor NonRelativistic evolution (HF-NRevo) scheme. The resulting fragmentation functions (FFs) are then embedded into hybrid high-energy and collinear factorization at next-to-leading order for semi-inclusive tetraquark-plus-jet production. We compare the two mechanisms through rapidity-separation distributions at the 14 TeV High-Luminosity LHC (HL-LHC) and at the 100 TeV Future Circular Collider (FCC), finding a clear phenomenological preference for the direct-multiquark channel over the scalar-diquark mode. Integrated cross sections and expected event yields provide favorable production-level prospects for future searches at the HL-LHC, with substantially enhanced statistics at FCC energies. These results establish a quantitative connection between microscopic formation mechanisms and the prospective observability of the $b\bar b c\bar c$ tetraquark at present and future hadron colliders.

</details>


### [7] [Flavor decomposition of electromagnetic multipole moments in $[q_1q_2][\bar q_3 \bar c]$ tetraquarks: Light-quark analogs of $T_{cs1}(2900)^0$ with $J^P = 1^+$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27843)
*Ulaş Özdem*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在光锥QCD求和规则（light-cone QCD sum rules）框架下，采用双夸克-反双夸克（diquark-antidiquark）图像，系统研究 $J^P=1^+$ 的紧凑型 $[q_1 q_2][\bar q_3 \bar c]$ 四夸克态的磁偶极矩与电四极矩，并指出粲夸克贡献在算符层面代数消失这一结构性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在系统刻画单重味四夸克态的电磁多极矩，并通过电磁矩的结构特征区分不同的内部组织方式，进而为紧凑型（compact）与分子型（molecular）解释提供判别依据。

Method: 在双夸克-反双夸克图像与光锥QCD求和规则框架下，对所有容许的 $u/d$ 味配置做味分解（flavor decomposition），计算 $J^P=1^+$ 态的磁偶极矩与电四极矩，并引入无量纲多极比 $\mathcal{D}/\mu$ 作为结构可观测量。

Abstract: We study the negative Laplacian on the half-space $\mathbb{R}^n_+$, $n \ge 3$, subject to a constant impedance (Robin) boundary condition with $α> 0$. The radiation condition is imposed on the solution itself, with no decomposition into channels, and is shown to be equivalent to the vanishing of the incoming data.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在厘清量子信息论进路在黑洞信息悖论（black hole information paradox）研究中所依赖的假设，并说明其如何规避早期哲学层面的反对意见，从而呼吁物理学哲学界给予更多关注。

Method: 概念分析与综述性论证：考察 Page-time paradox 与 Hayden-Preskill 模型，将黑洞视为有限维量子系统（finite-dimensional quantum system），其动力学由量子电路（quantum circuit）表示，并明确该建模所隐含的前提假设。

Abstract: We review and analyse quantum information-theoretic approaches to black hole evaporation that purport to bring us closer to a resolution of the black hole information paradox. Our focus is on the Page-time paradox and on the contribution of the Hayden-Preskill model to understanding information release under the assumption of unitary evaporation. In this model, the black hole is taken to be a finite-dimensional quantum system whose dynamics can be represented by a quantum circuit, and its information-processing and communication abilities are studied. We identify the assumptions underlying this modelling and highlight two key features, fast scrambling dynamics and entanglement as a resource, that underpin the information release in the model. We argue that this reasoning exemplifies a broader change in how information is used in black hole physics, in which quantum information theory now provides a useful operational modelling framework. This application avoids earlier philosophical objections insofar as it is not based on epistemic ignorance and does not regard information as a substance. This line of reasoning has become central to the debate and, as such, warrants greater attention from the philosophy of physics community.

</details>

*Lin Sun,Qijin Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作提出利用粒子数失衡（population imbalance）与动量空间限制（momentum space confinement）的协同效应抑制配对涨落（pairing fluctuations），从而在中等强度吸引下实现超高的超流转变温度（superfluid transition temperature）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室温超导（room-temperature superconductivity）是物理学界与工业界的长期目标。按BCS平均场预言，配对强度增大时Tc初始上升，但配对涨落随强度进一步提高会压低Tc，使其难以升高，因此需寻找抑制配对涨落的机制。

Method: 在三维光晶格（three-dimensional optical lattice）中，利用配对费米子与多余多数费米子之间的强泡利排斥（Pauli exclusion），抬高配对激发能并抑制有限动量配对涨落；机制适用于任何具有有限布里渊区（Brillouin zone）的晶格。

Result: 最优失衡度较小（p≲0.1），可保持塞曼场（Zeeman field）较小并避免轨道复杂性；中等失衡下带隙的配对色散（gapped pair dispersion）指数式抑制热凝聚体损耗，给出可检验特征。带隙色散、凝聚体分数（condensate fraction）增强与赝能隙（pseudogap）减小均可在现有实验中观测。

Conclusion: 该机制为通向室温超导开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Achieving room-temperature superconductivity has been a long-sought goal of both the physics community and the industry. The superfluid transition temperature $T_\mathrm{c}$ of a Fermi system initially increases with increasing pairing strength between fermions following the mean-field prediction of the Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer (BCS) theory, and is then suppressed by pairing fluctuations as the strength increases further, which prevents $T_\mathrm{c}$ from becoming high. Here we propose a mechanism for suppressing pairing fluctuations using the synergy between population imbalance and momentum space confinement in lattice systems, enabling an ultrahigh $T_\mathrm{c}$ at moderately strong attraction. We show that in a three-dimensional optical lattice, strong Pauli exclusion between paired and excess majority fermions pushes up the pair excitation energy and suppresses finite-momentum pairing fluctuations. The mechanism is general to any lattice with a finite Brillouin zone; the optimal imbalance is modest ($p\lesssim 0.1$), keeping the Zeeman field small and avoiding orbital complications. The gapped pair dispersion at moderate imbalance exponentially suppresses thermal condensate depletion, providing a testable signature. The gapped dispersion, enhanced condensate fraction, and pseudogap reduction are all accessible in present-day experiments. This work opens new avenues toward room-temperature superconductivity.

</details>

Abstract: Cold atom experiments require optical shutters with rapid and repeatable actuation. Existing solutions often involve high cost, limited aperture diameter and slow transitioning. We present a 3D-printable shutter design featuring a large aperture of 4.5 mm diameter, low-cost assembly, and robust performance. The device achieves switching times of $399\pm8~μ$s with delays of $2.7\pm0.2$ ms for a fully illuminated aperture, with four different shutters demonstrating repeatable performance. We measure the shutter speed to be $7.0\pm0.1$ m/s. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Luther-Emery液体（LEL）虽长期被理论预言，即一个自由度无能隙激发而另一自由度有能隙，但迄今缺乏确切的实验观测，亟需可判别的谱学判据与可行实验平台。

Method: 采用Bethe ansatz精确求解吸引型Yang-Gaudin模型，完全确定自旋与电荷通道的低能激发谱；进一步用玻色化（bosonization）解析推导有能隙自旋激发的动态结构因子（dynamical structure factor, DSF），并分析囚禁气体中的自旋/电荷布拉格谱峰位。

Result: 对完全配对的费米子，谱呈现自旋有能隙而电荷无能隙的共存特征；囚禁气体中自旋布拉格谱峰频率显著高于电荷模峰，二者劈裂频率随相互作用强度近似线性变化；自旋DSF在自旋能隙处呈尖锐阈值，阈值以下谱权重完全缺失。

Conclusion: 自旋DSF的尖锐阈值构成LEL直接且明确的谱学指纹，确立准一维（quasi-1D）囚禁吸引型费米气体为长期寻求的LEL直接观测的纯净实验平台。

Abstract: One-dimensional (1D) interacting Fermi gases underpin a rich variety of quantum liquids, ranging from the Tomonaga-Luttinger liquid and the Fulde-Ferrell-Larkin-Ovchinnikov paired state to the Luther-Emery liquid (LEL). Notably, the LEL-characterized by gapless excitations in charge or spin degree of freedom and gapped excitations in the other-has long been theoretically predicted but has eluded definitive experimental observation to date. By exactly solving the attractive Yang-Gaudin model via the Bethe ansatz, we fully determine the low-energy excitation spectra for both spin and charge channels. For fully paired fermions, the spectrum features gapped spin excitation coexisting with gapless charge excitation. We further employ bosonization to derive analytical expressions for the dynamical structure factor (DSF) of the gapped spin excitation, which is a long-standing challenge in this study. In the trapped gas, we find that the spin Bragg spectroscopy peak occurs at a much higher frequency than the charge-mode peak, where the splitting frequency between these two peaks scales approximately linearly with the interaction strength. Crucially, the spin gap manifests as a sharp threshold in the spin DSF, below which spectral weight is completely absent, proving a direct and unambiguous spectroscopic fingerprint of the LEL. Our work establishes quasi-1D trapped attractive Fermi gases as a pristine experimental platform for the long-sought direct observation of the LEL.

</details>


### [28] [Computing excited states of dipolar Bose--Einstein condensates by constrained gentlest ascent dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27878)
*Fu Zheng,Wei Liu,Xu Qian*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文提出基于约束最缓上升动力学（constrained gentlest ascent dynamics, CGAD）的数值方法，用于系统计算偶极玻色—爱因斯坦凝聚体（dipolar BEC）中具有任意指定莫尔斯指数（Morse index）的约束鞍点（即激发态）。方法结合半隐式正弦伪谱离散、正交化修正步与非均匀快速傅里叶变换（NUFFT）处理非局域偶极—偶极相互作用（DDI），并在1—3维算例中揭示激发态可在地基态平均场失稳区域中持续存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偶极BEC的激发态对应集体激发模式，对动力学稳定性与量子相变至关重要，但相较充分研究的地基态，其数值求解尤其是偶极系统中的激发态研究仍十分缺乏。本文旨在填补这一数值方法空白。

Method: 在平均场框架下以含DDI项的Gross—Pitaevskii（GP）模型为对象，采用CGAD稳定地瞄准给定莫尔斯指数的约束鞍点；离散上使用半隐式正弦伪谱格式并加入正交化修正步，非局域DDI项通过NUFFT高效精确计算。

Result: 该格式被应用于一维、二维和三维偶极BEC，实现了对激发态的细致数值研究；模拟表明，即使凝聚体地基态在平均场意义下失稳，激发态仍可继续存在。

Conclusion: 工作为偶极量子气体中激发态的研究提供了计算框架，并为后续理论与实验探索提供参考。

Abstract: Dipolar Bose--Einstein condensates (BECs), i.e., BECs with long-range dipole-dipole interactions (DDI), exhibit extraordinarily rich physics and serve as a versatile experimental platform for exploring exotic quantum states of matter. Within the mean-field framework, a dipolar BEC can be described by the Gross--Pitaevskii (GP) model with a DDI term. Its stationary states consist of the ground state, which minimizes the GP energy functional under the normalization constraint, and excited states, which are higher-energy critical points that typically correspond to constrained saddle points. Excited states encode collective excitation modes and play a key role in the dynamical stability and quantum phase transitions of cold-atom systems. However, in contrast to the extensively studied ground state, they remain far less explored numerically, especially in dipolar systems. In this work, we develop a numerical method based on the constrained gentlest ascent dynamics (CGAD) that systematically computes excited states of dipolar BECs by stably targeting constrained saddle points with any prescribed Morse index. Specifically, a semi-implicit sine-pseudospectral discretization with an orthonormalization correction step is adopted, and the nonlocal DDI terms are evaluated efficiently and accurately via the nonuniform fast Fourier transform (NUFFT). The resulting scheme is applied to one-, two-, and three-dimensional dipolar BECs, enabling a detailed numerical investigation of their excited states. Remarkably, our simulations reveal that excited states can persist even in regimes where the ground state of the condensate becomes mean-field unstable. This work provides a computational framework for studying excited states in dipolar quantum gases and offers insights for future theoretical and experimental investigations.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激发态量子相变（excited-state quantum phase transition, ESQPT）与量子计量学（quantum metrology）中转动量子费舍尔信息的关联尚需刻画，尤其需要明确凹陷与总自旋涨落、经典密度及有限尺寸标度之间的关系。

Conclusion: 优化转动 QFI 的凹陷可作为 ESQPT 的探针，其定量行为由半经典轨道平均与总自旋涨落共同解释；κ 作为有效截断而非直接可测能隙量发挥作用，激发态动力学在谱边缘呈现显著的时间尺度放大与尺寸标度变化。

Abstract: We find a dip in the optimized rotation quantum Fisher information of a zero-magnetization ferromagnetic spin-1 Bose-Einstein condensate near the excited-state quantum phase transition. Its displacement from $ε_c=-q$ decreases on an inverse-size scale. At $q=0.6,0.8,1.0,1.2$, the dip value of $F_{\max}$ equals $2\langle S^2\rangle$ for $N$ up to $3840$, linking it to total-spin fluctuations. A semiclassical orbit average normalized by the classical density of states describes the dip over $N=120$--$3840$ with maximum relative residual below $0.028$; a small power law fits comparably. Inserting the measured energy offsets directly into the semiclassical formula leaves residuals of $17$--$30\%$, so $κ$ acts as an effective cutoff. Among retained states, spin-nematic squeezing occurs at the spectral edges. Separately, fixed-index excited-state sweeps have half-excitation times between $11.2$ and $13.1$ times their ground-state counterparts, with local size exponents increasing across the four sizes.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究两种隧穿机制的相对符号如何影响少体系统的布洛赫动力学，以及隧穿过程如何生成关联与纠缠（entanglement），从而理解多机制竞争在可控少体（few-body）环境中的作用。

Result: 两种隧穿过程的相对符号强烈影响布洛赫动力学，形成截然不同的增强与竞争区间；无在位相互作用时，早期纠缠增长随密度辅助隧穿振幅呈现相同标度（scaling）；有限在位相互作用打破两种区间间的对称性，并以不同方式增强关联；等隧穿振幅下，双占位（doublon）与单占位（singlon）扇区对任意在位相互作用、晶格倾斜与初始态完全解耦（decouple）。

Conclusion: 两种隧穿机制之间的相互作用可在可控少体环境中调控动力学与纠缠，且等隧穿振幅下的完全解耦为简化与设计此类系统提供了一条明确条件。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of two bosons in a tilted one-dimensional optical lattice with both standard and density-assisted tunnelling. The relative sign of the two tunnelling processes strongly affects the Bloch dynamics, leading to distinct reinforcing and competing regimes. We examine how the tunnelling processes generate correlations and find that, in the absence of onsite interactions, the early-time entanglement growth follows the same scaling with the density-assisted tunnelling amplitude. Finite onsite interaction changes this picture by breaking the symmetry between the reinforcing and competing regimes and enhancing the correlation differently in two regimes. This shows how the interplay between the two tunnelling mechanisms can change dyanmics and entanglement in a controlled few-body setting. Particularly, at equal tunnelling amplitudes, the doublon and singlon sectors decouple completely for arbitrary onsite interaction, lattice tilting, and initial state.

*Julian Kusch,Lauriane Chomaz,Wyatt Kirkby*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究受近期一项实验工作（arXiv:2608.20327）启发，旨在理论上厘清囚禁偶极量子气体中相变的性质，并为其实验研究提供指导。

Method: 采用包含热效应与量子效应的扩展Gross-Pitaevskii（extended Gross-Pitaevskii）模拟，通过调节接触相互作用强度（contact interaction strength）与偶极取向（dipole orientation）绘制相图，并借助虚时滞回分析（imaginary-time hysteresis analysis）判定各相之间的相变阶数。

Abstract: Motivated by a recent experimental work $[\href{https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20327}{\text{arXiv:}2608.20327}]$, we theoretically investigate the nature of phase transitions in a trapped dipolar quantum gas upon tuning the contact interaction strength and dipole orientation. Using extended Gross-Pitaevskii simulations including thermal and quantum effects, we map the phase diagram comprising unmodulated superfluids, triangular droplet arrays, and stripe states. We further characterize the order of the transitions separating them through imaginary-time hysteresis analysis. The unmodulated-to-modulated transition evolves from discontinuous to continuous with increasing dipole tilt before becoming discontinuous again at large tilts. The degree of continuity is further affected by particle number, temperature, and confinement. While continuous behavior generally correlates with the emergence of stripe states, this correspondence is not systematic, and finite-size effects also influence the continuity of the transition. The triangular-to-stripe transition is found to be discontinuous at small contact interaction strengths and becomes increasingly continuous upon approaching the unmodulated-to-modulated phase boundary. Our results reveal a tunable transition order in finite dipolar quantum gases and provide guidance for its experimental investigation.

### [32] [An Algebraic Formulation of Thermality in Nested Rindler Wedges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27007)
*Waheed A. Dar,Prince A. Ganai,Nirmalya Kajuri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文将 Padmanabhan–Kolekar 提出的“递归热性（recursive thermality）”现象在共形场论（conformal field theory）的代数框架下重新构造，证明在 Bisognano–Wichmann 性质下每一“代”楔形区域均呈现热性，并显式给出递归模流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作仅在二维自由场论（two dimensional free field theories）中演示了递归热性，即 Rindler 真空对相对 Rindler 观察者加速的观察者呈热性；作者希望把这一构造提升到更具一般性的代数层面，考察其在共形场论中的成立条件与结构。

Method: 在代数层面（algebraic level）构造：每一步将母楔形（parent wedge）共形映射到参考 Minkowski 时空（Minkowski spacetime），并在其中选取新的右 Rindler 楔形及其左侧对应区域；借助 Bisognano–Wichmann 性质与楔形及其因果对应区域的模结构（modular structure）进行分析。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 TCC 以确定性的经典历史给出暴胀 e 折数上限，但其在随机量子涨落背景下的含义并不明确。本文旨在用随机暴胀的概率语言重新表述 TCC，并厘清不同物理诠释（世界线与体积）是否导致不同约束。

Method: 在随机暴胀中把 TCC 设为置信度 1−δ 下的概率上界，分别按单世界线（worldline）、单一哈勃斑区生长区域（region）与再加热体积（reheated volume）三种解读推导闭式条件；对线性斜坡（linear roll）在领头阶随机暴胀下严格求解（区域情形采用分支模型，branching model），并保留 N_TCC 领头阶精度。

Result: 得到条件 1−N_cl/N_TCC ≥ {2P_ζ[w+ln(1/δ)/N_TCC]}^{1/2}，其中世界线 w=0、区域 w=3。无真空衰变的线性斜坡上，世界线与区域解读均不弱于经典 TCC，且在 CMB 归一化吸引子上与经典 TCC 高度一致；以平坦量子井（flat quantum well）结束的经典阶段可被允许，每条世界线有几 e 折余量。在永恒暴胀阈值附近各解读分化：区域要求在领头阶留出 √(6P_ζ) 的时限比例未被使用，从而排除所有使用有限比例时限的经典历史及一段非永恒斜坡带，并给出斜率界 |V'|>V^{3/2}/[√2π(1−N_cl/N_TCC)]；而按世界线则任何斜率均允许，仅有数个标准差的余量。假真空（false vacuum）需以约永恒阈值 Γ=9H^4/(4π) 或更快衰变（区域/体积），而世界线仅要求快于 3H^4 ln(1/δ)/(4πN_TCC)。

Conclusion: TCC 是否禁止永恒暴胀取决于所采用的解读：体积/区域解读给出显著更强的限制，世界线解读则宽松得多。经典 TCC 对暴胀能标的约束在随机表述下依然成立。

Abstract: The trans-Planckian censorship conjecture (TCC) limits inflation to a deadline of $N_{\rm TCC}=\ln(M_{\rm Pl}/H_{\rm end})$ e-folds. We formulate it in stochastic inflation as a bound at confidence $1-δ$ on the probability that a history outlasts the deadline, with three readings: one worldline, the region grown from one Hubble patch, and the reheated volume. Each reading becomes a closed-form condition, rigorous for a linear roll in leading-order stochastic inflation (for the region, in a branching model) and sharp at leading order in $N_{\rm TCC}$: a linear roll whose classical history lasts $N_{\rm cl}$ e-folds respects the TCC if $1-N_{\rm cl}/N_{\rm TCC}\geq\{2\mathcal{P}_ζ[w+\ln(1/δ)/N_{\rm TCC}]\}^{1/2}$, with $w=0$ for a worldline and $w=3$ for a region. On a linear slope without vacuum decay, the worldline and region readings are never weaker than the classical TCC, and on the CMB-normalized attractor all readings agree closely with it, so the bounds on the scale of inflation stand. A classical phase that ends in a flat quantum well, which the overdamped classical TCC excludes, is allowed with a margin of a few e-folds per worldline. Near the onset of eternal inflation, the readings differ. On a linear slope, a region must leave the fraction $\sqrt{6\mathcal{P}_ζ}$ of the deadline unused at leading order: eternal inflation is excluded for every classical history that uses a finite fraction of the deadline, as is a band of non-eternal slopes, and the slope bound becomes $|V'|>V^{3/2}/[\sqrt2\,π(1-N_{\rm cl}/N_{\rm TCC})]$. Per worldline, every slope is allowed, given a margin of a few standard deviations. A false vacuum must decay at about the eternal threshold $Γ=9H^4/(4π)$ or faster for a region or the volume, but only faster than $3H^4\ln(1/δ)/(4πN_{\rm TCC})$ per worldline. Whether the TCC forbids eternal inflation depends on the reading.

</details>

The normalizable coefficient $g_{(3)ij}$ does not contribute linearly to the finite Euler boundary term: its contribution from the cubic extrinsic- curvature sector vanishes by $\mathrm{Tr}\,g_{(3)}=0$, while curvature-- extrinsic-curvature terms containing $g_{(3)}$ vanish at the conformal boundary. A direct Codazzi analysis additionally shows that the Cotton tensor appears in the asymptotic mixed curvature but its contribution through the tangential-connection boundary variation vanishes for standard Dirichlet data. Abstract: The tree-level open superstring has an integer mass-squared spectrum on linear Regge trajectories. Abstract: We introduce a method that does for loop integrals in de Sitter and anti-de Sitter space what integration-by-parts identities do in flat space. Conclusion: 其他处理$O(\\alpha'^3)$修正的全息方案或可缓解截断带来的病理，但任何方案在$O(\\alpha'^3)$阶所得结果的有效范围都必须限于对N=4 SYM可观测量$O(\\alpha'^3)$效应的严格微扰处理（perturbative treatment）。

Abstract: In the AdS/CFT correspondence, correlation functions of the energy-momentum tensor in N=4 supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory at large but finite 't Hooft coupling can be studied using higher-derivative corrections to supergravity. The instability is a reflection of the unphysical causality violation brought about by treating perturbatively-derived equations as if they were non-perturbative, analogous to the instability of AdS black branes in five-dimensional Gauss-Bonnet gravity. Abstract: We propose a new framework for computing late-time cosmological correlators in de Sitter spacetime by analytic continuation from the Euclidean sphere. Using this representation, we reproduce the results obtained using the Schwinger-Keldysh formalism for a range of tree-level and loop diagrams, providing nontrivial checks of the equivalence between the Euclidean and the in-in formalisms. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DE 对对称表示与矩形表示已较好理解，但对非矩形表示 $R$，连与结无关的基 $Z_R^X$ 都未知，且原有假设 $\delta+1=\deg\Delta$ 可能过于简化，需要检验并改进缺陷的刻画。

Conclusion: DE 可借助演化族的三角变换获得，所得 $Z^R_X$ 对任意结基本普适，仅差可控约化；缺陷刻画需用序列 $\{k_j\}$ 取代单一 $\delta$。不同突变对（mutant pairs）的非矩形着色 HOMFLY 差异体现了这种普适性（与结无关），并与 DE 精神一致。

Abstract: We report the first results of phenomenological analysis, based on extended set of explicitly calculated colored HOMFLY-PT polynomials in \cite{knotebook}. They concern differential expansion (DE), which separates the dependencies on the knot ${\cal K}$ and the representation $R$. %of colored HOMFLY-PT polynomials. DE is well understood for symmetric and rectangular representations, while for non-rectangular $R$ even the knot-independent basis $Z_R^X$ of DE is not known. We concentrate on two issues: the possible derivation of DE and on improved characteristic of its defect. We discuss how evolution families -- twist knots, double braids and 3-pretzels -- can be used to find DE by a peculiar triangular transformation of evolution coefficients. Once $Z^R_X$ are found for particular family, they are expected to stay basically the same for arbitrary knots -- modulo controllable reductions, characterized by the defect of DE, which puts knots into different universality classes. Contrary to original expectation, these classes appear labeled not by a single number $δ^{\cal K}$, but by a whole non-decreasing sequence $\{k_j\}$, which is squeezed between two staircases, ${\rm entier}\left(\frac{j-1}{δ+1}\right)\leq k_j\leq{\rm entier}\left(\frac{j-1}{{\rm deg}\,Δ}\right)$, where the upper bound involves Alexander polynomial $Δ(q^2)$ and follows from the continuation of the Melvin--Morton--Rozansky-Kashaev limit to $A=q^{-m}$ with $m>0$, when the limit gives a {\it positive} power of Alexander. The old assumption $δ+1={\rm deg}\,Δ$ is oversimplified and fails for 19 non-alternating knots with 11 and 12 crossings. Last, we list the differences of non-rectangularly colored HOMFLY for various mutant pairs to demonstrate their universality (knot-independence) and nice consistency with the spirit of DE.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用爱因斯坦标架（Einstein frame）作用量而非Jordan标架，通过正则场映射（canonical field map）直接计算精确的势慢滚参数（potential slow-roll parameters）、领头标量跑动（scalar running）、标量振幅归一化、e折数关系与暴胀结束条件；随后以同一正则化标量场评估精细化的de Sitter猜想（FRDSSC）、标量弱引力猜想（SWGC）及其强形式（SSWGC），并结合解析极限与数值扫描。

Result: 全局拟合本可在n_σ水平探测的偏离，经后处理后仅以R·n_σ报告；零检验（null tests）给出的上限也按同一因子被报告得过紧。方法在含21个重叠啁啾正弦信号的玩具模型上通过完整马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（Markov-chain Monte Carlo）推断得到验证，其中一个信号携带代表遗漏物理的小非线性啁啾。

Conclusion: 该掩蔽比框架揭示了后处理策略中效应被吸收的系统性偏差并提供了可量化的校正与源筛选工具，可作为真实LISA模拟数据中广义相对论检验的基础，并可推广至不同引力波源之间系统误差影响的研究。

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna will observe up to $\sim 10^{7}$ overlapping signals, which have to be characterised simultaneously with a global fit. This is a challenge for model comparison since rerunning the global fit for every physical model of interest, including deviations from vacuum General Relativity, is computationally demanding. An alternative is a two-step post-processing strategy: a first global fit with vacuum models only, after which a selected group of target sources is reanalysed under a different model with all other sources subtracted. We show that in this strategy a beyond-vacuum effect in a target source can be partly absorbed by the overlapping nuisance sources in the first fit, subtracted with them, and never reach the reanalysis. In the linear-signal approximation, the loss is captured by a single number, the masking ratio $\mathcal{R}\in[0,1]$. A deviation the global fit would detect at $n_σ$ is reported by post-processing at $\mathcal{R}\,n_σ$, and bounds in null tests are also reported too tight by the same factor. We show how to compute $\mathcal{R}$ from the posterior samples of the vacuum global fit, and give an algorithm that ranks the nuisance sources by how much of the effect each can absorb, so that only those most correlated with it need to be refitted with the target in a down-scaled reanalysis with a few sources. We validate our method with full Markov-chain Monte Carlo inference on a toy model of 21 overlapping chirping sinusoids, where one carries a small non-linear chirp that represents the missed physics, while the other 20 act as nuisance sources. This method can serve as a basis for tests of General Relativity in realistic LISA mock datasets and can be adapted to study the impact of systematics between different gravitational-wave sources.

</details>

Abstract: Are the inverted Regge-Wheeler and Zerilli potentials arising in Mashhoon's bound-state approach to black-hole quasinormal modes isospectral? We also demonstrate that potential inversion destroys their first-order Darboux relation, used by Glampedakis et al. to provide a simpler proof of isospectrality. We also derive the leading asymptotic tensor polarizations $h_+$ and $h_\times$ directly from the metric perturbations. The resulting strain is reconstructed from the radiative gravitational master functions: the ZM function in the even sector and the Cunningham-Price-Moncrief-normalized odd function in the axial sector for dynamical Chern-Simons gravity. Abstract: We study quasi-bound states of a charged massive Dirac field in the exact electrically charged Einstein--Born--Infeld (BI) geometry and in a leading-order Euler--Heisenberg-type (EH) approximation. The two descriptions agree in the weak-field limit. We compare families in which either the mass measured at infinity or the outer-horizon radius is held fixed, and examine several field charges and levels. We integrate the radial equations outward from the horizon and inward from large radius, then match the solutions. The resulting frequencies show that the effect of the nonlinear terms depends on both the electrodynamic theory and the chosen comparison. For the fundamental like-charge mode, the small change in its real frequency has opposite signs in the two theories when the mass is fixed, whereas it is upward in both when the horizon radius is fixed. The fundamental mode decays more slowly as the nonlinear term grows along either family. The narrowest level, however, decays more rapidly when the mass is fixed and more slowly when the horizon radius is fixed. The spectra of the two theories approach one another as the nonlinear term is reduced. The nonlinear coefficient is used as an effective parameter and is not matched to the microscopic coupling in quantum electrodynamics (QED).

Critical scales, ray classes, orbit numbers and transfer functions are combined with three emission profiles and matched black-hole controls. Face-on and inclined images use the same prescribed emitter motion: circular timelike geodesics outside the innermost stable circular orbit and an inward plunge conserving its specific energy and angular momentum inside. Although the core is excised, regularization changes the retained exterior and throat matching. For the sampled parameters, the observer critical screen impact decreases from $10.392μ_1$ to $9.642μ_1$, where $μ_1$ is the observer-side asymptotic mass scale. Regularizing the second exterior then contracts the reflected interval from $3.890μ_1$ to $1.248μ_1$ without changing ordinary observer-side rays. Returned brightness depends on source support and velocity-dependent frequency shifts: the regular--regular image nearly coincides with its black-hole control for the outer-edge emission model face-on, while inner emission reveals returned structures. ### [71] [A Multipole Technique for the Simulation and Analysis of Gravitational Waveforms from Extreme Mass Ratio Inspirals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27153)
*Courtney McIntosh,Jeremy Schnittman*

Main category: gr-qc

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型由能量与轴向角动量的领头阶通量（leading-order fluxes）直接确定，无法直接给出 Carter 常数的演化，而该常数对描述偏心与倾斜轨道（eccentric and inclined trajectories）至关重要，因此需要构建可灵活替换辐射反作用（radiation-reaction）处方的计算框架。

Conclusion: EMRICode 的模块化结构使辐射反作用处方可与底层测地线演化独立替换，为纳入更精确的通量模型与额外物理效应提供框架，并可作为研究 EMRI 轨道动力学及超越理想化真空、点粒子近似（vacuum, point-particle approximation）扩展的灵活计算平台。

Abstract: We introduce EMRICode, a Python-based numerical framework for evolving generic extreme mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) in Kerr spacetime. The code combines adaptive numerical integration of test-particle geodesics with a prescribed model for the secular evolution of the orbital constants of motion, allowing eccentric and inclined trajectories to be followed over long inspiral timescales. We focus in particular on the treatment of the Carter constant, whose evolution is not directly determined by the leading-order fluxes for energy and axial angular momentum used in the present model. We investigate several prescriptions for determining its evolution and find that a radial-potential-based prescription provides the most stable behavior among those considered, while also exhibiting a systematic residual bias for nearly planar orbits. We compare the resulting inspirals with established radiation-reaction approximations, including the weak-field treatment of Peters and Kerr-based prescriptions from the literature, and use these comparisons to assess the qualitative behavior and limitations of the implementation. The modular structure of EMRICode allows the radiation-reaction prescription to be replaced independently of the underlying geodesic evolution, providing a framework for incorporating more accurate flux models and additional physical effects. We present two simple examples of how interactions with accretion disks and partial tidal disruptions can drive the evolution of a stellar EMRI orbit. EMRICode therefore provides a flexible computational framework for studying the orbital dynamics of EMRIs and for exploring extensions beyond the idealized vacuum, point-particle approximation.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞微扰理论（black-hole perturbation theory）在小质量比之外仍高度精确，但其精度上限的物理来源尚不明确，需要区分耗散通量、进动动力学与背景黑洞差异各自贡献。

Method: 发展模块化时域 Teukolsky 框架（modular time-domain Teukolsky framework），在辐射保持一阶微扰阶的前提下单独引入有限质量比轨道修正，并与数值相对论波形逐段比对；对进动双星分析吸积时间标度依赖，对并合阶段比较初始 Kerr 背景与残余黑洞。

Result: 非自旋双星中二阶耗散通量（second-order dissipative flux）消除 87% 的旋进速率偏差，残余约 1%；进动双星出现 ν²sin²ι 形式的时间标度依赖，无法由领头轨道平均后牛顿项（post-Newtonian）解释；并合阶段同一修正仅消除 1–2% 偏差，而并合偏移可由初始 Kerr 背景与残余黑洞之差定量说明，96% 的领头频率移动源于自旋而非质量变化。

Conclusion: 波形不同区域受不同物理效应主导：旋进段主要由耗散通量精度决定，并合段则受背景时空选择支配；结果提示进动动力学存在有限质量比修正，并发布 Vishveshwara Catalog（348 个双星的 1571 条时域波形）。

Abstract: Black-hole perturbation theory remains remarkably accurate beyond its formal small-mass-ratio regime, but what limits this accuracy remains unclear. We develop a modular time-domain Teukolsky framework that isolates finite-mass-ratio corrections to the orbital dynamics while keeping the radiation at first perturbative order. Comparisons with numerical relativity show that the dominant source of discrepancy changes across the waveform. For nonspinning binaries, the available second-order dissipative flux removes $87\%$ of the inspiral rate discrepancy, bringing it to within $\sim1\%$ of numerical relativity. For precessing binaries, we identify a $ν^2\sin^2ι$ dependence in the inspiral time rescaling that is not explained by the leading orbit-averaged post-Newtonian terms, suggesting finite-mass-ratio corrections to precessional dynamics. Near merger, the same dissipative correction removes only $1$--$2\%$ of the discrepancy. We show instead that the merger offset is quantitatively accounted for by the difference between the initial Kerr background used in the perturbative evolution and the remnant black hole, with $96\%$ of the leading predicted frequency shift arising from the change in spin rather than mass. We release $1571$ time-domain waveforms for $348$ binaries as the Vishveshwara Catalog.

</details>

*Giorgio Mentasti,Carlo Contaldi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文预测一台快速自转的专用天体测量（astrometry）巡天卫星对微赫兹（microhertz）频段随机引力波背景（stochastic gravitational-wave background, SGWB）的探测灵敏度，该频段位于脉冲星计时阵列（pulsar timing arrays, PTAs）与LISA之间的空白区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微赫兹频段是PTA与LISA灵敏度之间的探测空白，需评估天体测量手段能否在此频段探测SGWB，并确定实现该目标所需的观测配置。

Method: 利用卫星加速自转提高采样率与奈奎斯特频率（Nyquist frequency），论证白噪声底与采样节奏无关；采用对N颗参考星的互相关估计量（cross-correlation estimator）及远源极限下的天体测量重叠函数进行计算。

Result: 类Gaia卫星扫描黄道极、10年内观测约10^4颗亮星时，灵敏度较PTA测得背景差约60倍；8米级口径或覆盖全天卫星星座可达到该灵敏度，并连续覆盖μHz频段。

Conclusion: 天体测量在高频端最具竞争力（应变噪声为白噪声，而PTA等效噪声随f^2增长），更大口径或卫星星座方案有望填补μHz频段空白。

Abstract: We forecast the sensitivity of a dedicated, fast-spinning astrometric survey to a stochastic gravitational-wave background (SGWB) in the microhertz gap between pulsar timing arrays (PTAs) and LISA. Spinning the satellite faster samples each star more often, raising the Nyquist frequency and opening higher frequencies at no cost in noise: the shorter, noisier exposures are exactly compensated by their number, so the white-noise floor of each position stream is independent of cadence. High frequencies are where astrometry is most competitive, because the deflection measures the strain directly: its strain noise is white, whereas the equivalent noise of pulsar timing grows as $f^2$. With a cross-correlation estimator over $N$ reference stars and the astrometric overlap computed in the distant-source limit, we find that a Gaia-like satellite scanning the ecliptic poles, with $N\sim10^4$ bright stars over $10$~yr, falls short of the background measured by PTAs by a factor $\sim\!60$. A larger, $8$-m-class aperture, or a constellation of satellites covering the whole sky, reaches it, while extending the reach continuously across the $μ$Hz band.

</details>


### [74] [Local obstructions to trapped-region escape in modified gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27225)
*Francisco S. N. Lobo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在球对称下提出“几何优先”的能量条件框架，用面积加权输运定律把零聚焦、视界多重性与陷阱区消失从“有效”应力张量中解耦；在f(R)引力中借助熵分辨层级证明标量子（scalaron）导数项精确抵消，使熵陷阱由物理物质条件T_nn≥0与代数混合阈值决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 修正引力（modified gravity）中能量条件通常施加于混合物理物质与额外引力自由度的“有效”应力张量（effective stress tensor），因而难以辨别究竟是哪个扇区真正控制零聚焦（null focusing）、视界多重性（horizon multiplicity）与陷阱区（trapped region）的消失。

Method: 在球对称（spherical symmetry）下取归一化的未来指向径向零矢量对（future-directed radial null vectors），推导两个纵向Ricci投影与一个独立横向Einstein投影所满足的精确面积加权输运定律（area-weighted transport laws），据此建立陷阱持久性定理（trapping-persistence theorem）；在度规f(R)引力且F≡df/dR>0条件下，以局部Wald密度变量Fr²构造熵分辨层级（entropy-resolved hierarchy）。

Result: 纵向熵聚焦定律中标量子二阶导数精确抵消，仅剩非正标量梯度平方与物理物质投影；独立横向熵输运定律中全部标量子导数项抵消。由此熵陷阱由物理物质条件T_nn≥0与一个代数混合阈值（algebraic mixed threshold）控制，而非虚拟有效流体。

Conclusion: 所得层级清晰区分了物质正性（matter positivity）、几何聚焦、边际面恢复（marginal-surface recovery）与引力熵演化，并给出正则内视界（inner horizon）、喉（throat）及陷阱—反陷阱转变（trapped-to-antitrapped transition）所需的精确局部反转条件。

Abstract: Energy conditions in modified gravity are commonly imposed on an ''effective'' stress tensor that mixes physical matter with additional gravitational degrees of freedom, obscuring which sector actually controls null focusing, horizon multiplicity, and the disappearance of trapped regions. We develop a geometry-first alternative in spherical symmetry. For a normalized pair of future-directed radial null vectors, twolongitudinal Ricci projections and one independent transverse Einstein projection obey exact area-weighted transport laws. These imply a trapping-persistence theorem: after a nondegenerate future-outer marginal sphere, a regular ingoing generator cannot exit the trapped region while the ingoing longitudinal and transverse convergence channels remain nonnegative. In metric $f(R)$ gravity, with $F\equiv df/dR>0$, we then construct an entropy-resolved hierarchy based on the local Wald-density variable $Fr^2$. In the longitudinal entropy-focusing law, the scalaron second derivative cancels exactly, leaving a nonpositive scalar-gradient square together with the physical matter projection; in the independent transverse entropy-transport law, all scalaron derivative terms cancel. Entropy trapping is consequently governed by the physical matter condition $T_{nn}\geq 0$ and an algebraic mixed threshold rather than by a fictitious effective fluid. The resulting hierarchy cleanly separates matter positivity, geometric focusing, marginal-surface recovery, and gravitational-entropy evolution, and identifies the precise local reversals required for regular inner horizons, throats, and trapped-to-antitrapped transitions.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 引入里奇流（Ricci flow）辅助在这些流形上构造全局均匀（globally homogeneous）初始数据，再通过数值求解爱因斯坦方程（Einstein's equation）演化初始数据。

Result: 在三个示例流形——Seifert-Weber 空间、透镜空间（lens space）L(5,2) 与 Sixth-Turn 空间——上构建了均匀各向同性宇宙学模型。

Conclusion: 所发展的数值方法可在非平凡空间拓扑流形上实现均匀各向同性宇宙学模型的构建，并在三个具体流形上得到验证。

Abstract: This study develops numerical methods for computing homogeneous and isotropic cosmological models on manifolds with non-trivial spatial topologies. The use of Ricci flow is introduced here to aid in the construction of globally homogeneous initial data on these manifolds. The initial data produced in this way are evolved by solving Einstein's equation numerically to construct homogeneous and isotropic cosmological models on three exemplar manifolds in this study: Seifert-Weber space, the lens space $L(5,2)$, and Sixth-Turn space.

</details>


### [76] [Thermal and optical signatures of a magnetic AdS black hole in dark matter and quintessence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27503)
*Mohsen Fathi,Marco Olivares,Carlos E. Romero-Figueroa,J. R. Villanueva*

TL;DR: 该文研究由完美流体暗物质（perfect-fluid dark matter）与精质（quintessence）共同包围的静态反德西特黑洞（anti-de Sitter black hole），沿单参数路径η系统计算视界、霍金温度（Hawking temperature）、稀疏度（sparsity）、弱场偏折、光子球、阴影及强偏折系数等可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于刻画暗物质与精质两类背景场对黑洞几何及可观测光学性质的联合影响，并将多参数模型压缩为可清晰呈现的一条基准路径以便系统比较。

Result: 沿该路径，外视界、光子球、临界阴影尺度与相对论爱因斯坦环均向外移动。形式化的类太阳临边比较给出 η≲2.06×10⁻⁵（依赖于源，非普适界限）；以文中所用中心质量与距离，M87* 直径允许 0≤η≤1，而所采用的 Sgr A* 区间给出 η≲0.665。

Abstract: We study a static magnetic anti-de Sitter black hole surrounded by perfect-fluid dark matter and quintessence. We follow the geometry from the horizon to the observer. We first examine the horizons and curvature. We then calculate the Hawking temperature and sparsity, weak light bending, the photon sphere, the shadow, strong-deflection coefficients, relativistic images, and simple Event Horizon Telescope size intervals. Because the model has several parameters, we use a one-dimensional benchmark path labelled by $η$. The point $η= 0$ is Schwarzschild, while $η= 1$ is the selected full configuration. This path is only a clear way to present the combined changes; it is not a new coupling. We derive the main relations step by step and test the weak- and strong-field approximations with direct numerical geodesics. Along the path, the outer horizon, photon sphere, critical shadow scale, and relativistic Einstein rings move outward. A formal solar-limb comparison gives $η\lesssim 2.06 \times 10^{-5}$ if the same local environment is placed around the Sun, but this is a source-dependent analogue rather than a universal bound. With the central mass and distance values used here, the M87* diameter allows $0 \leq η\leq 1$, while the adopted Sgr A* interval gives $η\lesssim 0.665$. Longer derivations, error propagation, and numerical checks are collected in the appendices.

</details>


### [77] [Stability Analysis of Axion Dark Energy Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27514)
*Ankan Mukherjee,Nandan Roy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文将周期性势（伪 Nambu-Goldstone 玻色子启发）的轴子暗能量模型（axion dark energy model）重构为自治动力系统（autonomous dynamical system），证明辐射、物质主导期对应鞍点（saddle point），晚期暗能量主导解为稳定吸引子（stable attractor），从而保证宇宙加速的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此类模型虽能驱动晚期宇宙加速并呈现暗能量状态方程（equation-of-state parameter）振荡等丰富动力学，但其可行性关键取决于是否存在稳定的宇宙学解（stable cosmological solutions），因此需系统检验其稳定性。

Method: 把背景演化方程改写为自治动力系统，识别对应辐射主导（radiation-dominated）、物质主导（matter-dominated）与暗能量主导时期的固定点（fixed points），并在观测允许的参数空间区域内结合解析与数值方法分析其稳定性性质。

Result: 辐射主导与物质主导阶段自然表现为鞍点，保证标准宇宙学演化；晚期暗能量主导解为稳定吸引子，使宇宙加速对初始条件变化具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究确立轴子暗能量模型为动力学自洽（dynamically consistent）且观测可行的晚期加速膨胀候选解释。

Abstract: We investigate the stability of an axion dark energy model characterized by a periodic scalar-field potential motivated by pseudo Nambu Goldstone bosons. While such models are well suited to drive late-time cosmic acceleration and can exhibit rich dynamical features, including oscillatory behavior of the dark-energy equation-of-state parameter, their viability crucially depends on the existence of stable cosmological solutions. By reformulating the background evolution equations as an autonomous dynamical system, we identify the fixed points corresponding to radiation-dominated, matter-dominated, and dark energy dominated epochs. Using both analytical and numerical techniques, we analyze the stability properties of these fixed points within the observationally allowed region of parameter space. Our results show that the radiation- and matter-dominated phases emerge naturally as saddle points, ensuring a standard cosmological evolution, while the late-time dark energy dominated solution acts as a stable attractor. This guarantees the robustness of cosmic acceleration against variations in initial conditions. Overall, our study establishes the axion dark energy model as a dynamically consistent and observationally viable candidate for explaining the late-time accelerated expansion of the Universe.

</details>


### [78] [Thin-Shell Wormholes in Born--Infeld-Type Magnetic Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28112)
*Jonathan A. Rebouças,Edson Otoniel,Muhammad Israr Aslam,Rabia Saleem*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究由 Born–Infeld 型磁外场（magnetic exteriors）构建的对称薄壳虫洞（thin-shell wormhole），分析其径向稳定性、热力学平衡与双侧吸积盘成像，并区分几何、材料与运动学效应对壳层支撑和盘照明的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探讨非线性电磁外场下薄壳虫洞的可稳定性与可观测性，将稳定性判据、能量条件、吸积盘辐射图像与热力学一致性纳入统一框架加以检验。

Method: 采用精确 lapse 与 junction conditions（连接条件）定义固定质量族，用线性径向微扰分析校准的正压样本及广义与修正 cosmic Chaplygin 气体律；并以解析视界与大半径极限解释稳定性转变，同时计算静态观察者视角下 Maxwell 光线穿过透明连接面的盘成像。

Result: 壳层能量密度为负，但内部取样区域满足切向零能量条件与内禀强能量条件；校准正压样本不稳定，而广义与修正 cosmic Chaplygin 律在特定实分支上存在稳定扇区。圆形与俯冲运动降低正面强度，倾斜运动产生强方位角对比；增大次临界电荷使临界圆收缩并增加近临界圆的盘穿越次数；在可逆第一定律下通量抵消给出正温等熵运动。

Conclusion: 稳定性转变可由视界与大半径极限解析说明，修正线性压强项可将外部稳定替换为有限径向区间；虫洞—黑洞强度差可分离透射盘光与被喉部切除的辐射；熵的具体形式仍待本构补充（constitutive completion）确定。

Abstract: We investigate the radial stability, thermodynamic balance, and bilateral disk images of symmetric thin-shell wormholes built from Born--Infeld-type magnetic exteriors. The exact lapse and junction conditions define a fixed-mass family in which magnetic self-energy ties the nonlinear scale to the charge. The shell has negative energy density, although an inner sampled region satisfies the tangential null and intrinsic strong energy conditions. Linear radial perturbations yield unstable calibrated barotropic samples and stable sectors for the generalized and modified cosmic Chaplygin laws on selected real branches. Analytic horizon and large-radius limits explain the stability transitions and show how the modified linear pressure term can replace outer stability with finite radial intervals. Minimally coupled Maxwell rays traverse transparent junctions between exteriors with a common arctangent emission profile, viewed by a static observer. The wormhole-black-hole intensity difference separates transmitted disk light from emission removed by throat excision. Compared with static emitters, circular and plunging motion reduce the face-on intensities in the studied cases; inclined motion generates strong azimuthal contrast. Increasing the subcritical charge contracts the critical circle and permits more disk crossings near it in the optical samples. Under a reversible first law, flux cancellation gives isentropic motion at positive temperature, with entropy dependence left to a constitutive completion. These results distinguish geometric, material, and kinematic effects on shell support and disk illumination.

</details>


### [79] [On the Stability of Tolman Oppenheimer Volkoff Fireholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28140)
*Pablo Laguna,Octavio Guerrero,Kostas Kokkotas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用 Prüfer 相位法（Prüfer phase method）研究具有辐射态方程（radiation equation of state）的奇异 Tolman–Oppenheimer–Volkoff firehole 的径向稳定性（radial stability），发现其基本径向模的本征频率平方在外边界超过临界半径后由正变负，临界半径与平衡 firehole 半径近似同标度，且除刚性壁（rigid wall）约束外构型普遍径向不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异 firehole 构型包含裸的负点质量奇点（negative point-mass singularity）与正密度流体，其质量轮廓出现类 Schwarzschild 平台并趋于延展的自相似大气；此类构型的径向稳定性此前缺乏系统判定，本文旨在确定其稳定与不稳定的边界。

Method: 采用 Prüfer 相位法识别基本径向模（fundamental radial mode），并考虑两类外边界条件：拉格朗日压强扰动为零（开放 firehole，open firehole）与径向位移为零（盒式 firehole，boxed firehole）；同时借助零模（zero mode）与平衡族对内 lapse 归一化导数的关系、TOV 方程的标度对称性（scaling symmetry）以及变分界（variational bounds）进行分析。

Result: 两类边界条件下，基本模的平方频率均在外边界超过临界半径 R_c 时由正变负；R_c 随中心质量参数的标度与平衡 firehole 半径 R 近似一致，但阈值差异显著：开放情形 R_c^O < R，盒式情形 R_c^B ≃ R；正则零模为平衡族在固定中心质量下对内 lapse 归一化的导数，由此给出盒式阈值的转折点（turning-point）解释，并结合标度对称性推出大中心质量极限下 R_c^B → R。

Conclusion: 变分界表明刚性壁是最稳定的可分自伴（self-adjoint）外边界条件，而无界构型不稳定；因此，除非被约束在约等于其 firehole 半径的范围内，TOV firehole 在径向方向上是不稳定的。

Abstract: We investigate the radial stability of singular Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff fireholes with a radiation equation of state. These configurations contain a naked, negative point-mass singularity surrounded by a positive-density fluid, whose mass profile develops a Schwarzschild-like plateau before approaching an extended, self-similar atmosphere. Using the Prüfer phase method to identify the fundamental radial mode, we consider two outer boundary conditions: vanishing Lagrangian pressure perturbation (open firehole) and vanishing radial displacement (boxed firehole). For both, the fundamental squared frequency changes from positive to negative as the outer boundary exceeds a critical radius $R_c$. We find that $R_c$ follows approximately the same scaling with the central mass parameter as the equilibrium firehole radius $R$. However, the stability thresholds differ significantly: $R_c^{\rm O}<R$ for the open firehole, whereas $R_c^{\rm B}\simeq R$ for the boxed one. The regular zero mode is the derivative of the equilibrium family with respect to the inner lapse normalization at fixed central mass. This gives the boxed threshold a turning-point interpretation and, together with a scaling symmetry of the TOV equations, implies $R_c^{\rm B}\to R$ in the limit of large central mass. Variational bounds show that the rigid wall is the most stabilizing separated, self-adjoint outer boundary condition and that the unbounded configuration is unstable. Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff fireholes are therefore radially unstable unless confined within approximately their firehole radius.

</details>


### [80] [Topological ModMax--AdS Black Holes: Extremality, Charge Normalization, and Limits of WGC Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28359)
*Saeed Noori Gashti,Behnam Pourhassan,Saheb Soroushfar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究由 ModMax 电动力学（ModMax electrodynamics）及其符号反演对应物作为源的（sourced）四维拓扑反德西特黑洞（topological anti-de Sitter black hole），给出电荷归一化、势与第一定律，并对球面、平面与双曲视界的极端解进行分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机在于将 ModMax 非线性电动力学推广至四维拓扑 AdS 黑洞背景，并澄清电荷归一化中场强振幅（field-strength amplitude）与守恒位移通量（conserved displacement flux）的区别，同时为后续检验各类猜想提供经典诊断工具。

Result: 结果显示：双曲 ModMax 分支（hyperbolic ModMax branch）不存在电荷上界的判别式约束（upper-charge discriminant bound），并包含一个有限电荷的零质量点（finite-charge zero-mass point）；同时给出了不稳定球面零圆轨道与近视界 AdS₂ Breitenlohner–Freedman 指数。

Conclusion: 上述结果属于经典诊断（classical diagnostics），并非弱引力猜想（WGC, weak gravity conjecture）的裁定：模型未指定带电粒子谱（charged-particle spectrum）、紫外完备（ultraviolet completion）或全局微扰问题（global perturbation problem）；且固定耦合（fixed-coupling）的 AdS 数据不足以定量检验距离猜想（distance conjecture）与德西特猜想（de Sitter conjecture）。

Abstract: We study four-dimensional topological anti-de Sitter black holes sourced by ModMax electrodynamics and its sign-reversed counterpart. Distinguishing the field-strength amplitude from the conserved displacement flux fixes the charge normalization, potential, and first law. We classify the exact extremal roots for spherical, planar, and hyperbolic horizons; in particular, the hyperbolic ModMax branch has no upper-charge discriminant bound and contains a finite-charge zero-mass point. We also derive the unstable spherical null circular orbit and evaluate the local near-horizon AdS$_2$ Breitenlohner--Freedman index for an explicitly specified charged-scalar probe. These are classical diagnostics, not a WGC verdict: the model does not specify a charged-particle spectrum, an ultraviolet completion, or the global perturbation problem. The distance and de Sitter conjectures cannot be quantitatively tested with the fixed-coupling AdS data considered here.

</details>

At order M to 0, the internal freedom of the BornOppenheimer factorization separates quantum field theory on curved spacetime from the anisotropy dynamics, described by timedependent harmonic oscillators. Abstract: We study linear perturbations about an arbitrary time-dependent, spherically symmetric background in the most general Horndeski theory. The odd-parity sector of such perturbations, analyzed previously, contains no scalar field perturbation and, hence, leaves the related degree of freedom unconstrained. In this paper, we address this issue by analyzing even-parity monopole ($\ell = 0$) perturbations, which isolate this degree of freedom. We derive the quadratic master action for the only propagating physical mode associated with the scalar field and formulate stability criteria which ensure the absence of ghost and radial gradient instabilities for the high-energy modes. These conditions are necessary for the stability of the background and complement the odd-parity ones. We show that they reproduce the known results in the static and cosmological limits. We also demonstrate that, in the cubic subclass of Horndeski theory on a dynamical background, the monopole criteria impose no constraints beyond those previously obtained for modes with large multipoles ($\ell \gg 1$). As an illustration, we apply the derived stability conditions for the monopole mode to a non-minimally coupled scalar field theory and to a black hole model with linearly time-dependent scalar hair; in the latter case, the monopole conditions further restrict the parameter space allowed by odd-parity stability.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 三个模拟中世界管几何与准局部标量均遵循预测标度：含高阶项的自由指数拟合将表面几何与准局部泛函的指数恢复为 $1/2$（误差在半百分点内），各诊断对形成时刻的一致性达 $5\times10^{-5}M$；共同视界位于与形成切片相切的平滑边缘外陷俘管（marginally outer trapped tube）上，后续切片交于外、内两支，间距随 $(t-t_*)^{1/2}$ 变化。

Conclusion: 共同视界形成具有不依赖对称性的普适局部结构，表现为平方根分支分离与共享线性项；视界剪切（shear）与引力波 news 的相关性提示该形成过程可能在并合波形的短片段中留下特征信号。

Abstract: We derive the local structure of the first common apparent horizon in a generic binary-black-hole merger. This event occurs in the fully nonlinear regime, outside the standard regimes of post-Newtonian inspiral theory and perturbations of a stationary black hole, yet it admits a universal description. Without assuming symmetry, we show that the stability operator of the marginally outer trapped surface must lose invertibility at formation. Outermost stability then implies that the vanishing eigenvalue is the principal one, with a strictly positive eigenfunction. Lyapunov-Schmidt reduction yields square-root branch separation with a shared linear drift. Together these terms give a tilted parabola through linear order in time. The common horizon lies on a smooth marginally outer trapped tube tangent to the formation slice, and nearby later slices intersect it in outer and inner branches whose separation scales as $(t-t_*)^{1/2}$. Horizon quantities with a nonzero first response along the zero mode inherit the square-root separation and a shared linear term. We test these predictions in three binary black hole simulations, including an eccentric, precessing, unequal-mass system. In all three, the worldtube geometry and quasilocal scalars follow the predicted scaling. With the next-order term included, free-exponent fits to the surface geometry and quasilocal functionals recover $1/2$ to within half a percent, and diagnostics agree on the formation time within $5\times10^{-5}M$. The correlation between horizon shear and gravitational-wave news suggests that common horizon formation could have a signature in a short segment of the merger waveform.

</details>

### [86] [Gamma-ray Bursts as Cosmological Probes Calibrated with Strongly Lensed Quasars and Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27009)

Main category: astro-ph.CO

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRB 可将距离测量延伸至 Ia 型超新星（Type Ia supernovae）常规探测范围之外的更高红移，但其宇宙学应用受定标循环性（calibration circularity）与可能的红移依赖效应限制，需要独立外部定标源。

Result: 平坦 ΛCDM 下 Amati 给出 H0=78.26(+17.87/−16.30) km/s/Mpc、Ωm=0.670(+0.223/−0.276)，Combo 给出 H0=78.71(+13.83/−11.29) km/s/Mpc、Ωm=0.635(+0.235/−0.242)；Amati 对唯象红移项较不敏感，Combo 依赖更强，且未被建模的正向 Combo 趋势会使 H0 与 Ωm 偏高。

Abstract: Gamma-ray bursts (GRBs) extend distance measurements to redshifts beyond those routinely probed by Type Ia supernovae, but their use in cosmology is limited by calibration circularity and possible redshift-dependent effects. We investigate strongly lensed quasars as external calibrators of the Amati and Combo GRB relations. We reconstruct the luminosity-distance relation using an artificial neural network trained on TDCOSMO strong-lensing distances, with the absolute scale inherited from the lensing-only calibration. To avoid extrapolation, only GRBs within the lens-supported redshift interval are used for calibration. We then infer cosmological parameters through a hierarchical Bayesian analysis of flat $Λ$CDM, with $w_0w_a$CDM examined as a consistency test. For flat $Λ$CDM, Amati yields $H_0=78.26^{+17.87}_{-16.30}\,\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$ and $Ω_m=0.670^{+0.223}_{-0.276}$, while Combo yields $H_0=78.71^{+13.83}_{-11.29}\,\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$ and $Ω_m=0.635^{+0.235}_{-0.242}$. Amati is relatively insensitive to the phenomenological redshift term, whereas Combo shows a stronger dependence. Injection--recovery tests indicate that an unmodelled positive Combo trend can bias $H_0$ and $Ω_m$ upward. Strong-lens calibration therefore offers a route to extending the GRB Hubble diagram, but the inferred $H_0$ is not an independent GRB measurement: it retains the TDCOSMO distance scale. The precision is limited by GRB scatter, redshift-dependent systematics, and lens-modelling uncertainties.
