### [87] [Slow-Roll Inflation at Arbitrary Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28608)
*William Giarè*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文在慢滚展开（slow-roll expansion）的任意有限阶 $p$ 上给出原初标量谱与张量谱的显式表达式，适用于正则单场暴胀（canonical single-field inflation）与 $k$-暴胀（$k$-inflation），并据此导出闭合形式的张量倾斜（tensor tilt）与张量—标量比（tensor-to-scalar ratio）等观测量之间的自洽关系（consistency relations）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有结果多停留在较低阶（至 N3LO），缺乏对任意有限阶谱与自洽关系的统一、无需递推的闭合表述，尤其在 $k$-暴胀情形还需纳入标量声速（sound speed）及其标度导数的影响。

Method: 指出任意阶谱可由慢滚参数（slow-roll parameters）的已知组合乘以普适系数得到，这些系数完全由自由的 de Sitter 解确定，无需微扰修正的模式函数（mode functions）；并给出可对任意 $(n,p)$ 生成谱与自洽关系的算法。

Result: 获得联系 $n_t$ 及其跑动（runnings）$\alpha_n^t$ 与 $r$、$n_s$ 及其跑动 $\alpha_n^s$ 的闭合自洽关系，对任意 $n\geq 0$ 与任意有限 $p\geq 0$ 成立且不需对低阶关系递推；$k$-暴胀情形额外依赖标量声速及其标度导数。结果复现至 N3LO 的已知结论，并将显式计算推进至 N10LO。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了任意有限阶上谱与自洽关系的统一闭式框架与自动生成算法，把显式计算结果从 N3LO 推广至 N10LO，并为更高阶暴胀观测量的理论预言提供系统工具。

Abstract: We derive explicit expressions for the primordial scalar and tensor spectra at arbitrary finite order $p$ in the slow-roll expansion, valid both for canonical single-field inflation and $k$-inflation. At any order, the spectra are determined by known combinations of slow-roll parameters weighted by universal coefficients entirely fixed by the free de Sitter solutions, without requiring perturbatively corrected mode functions. Using these spectra, we obtain closed-form consistency relations that connect the tensor tilt $n_t$ and its runnings $α_n^t\equiv d^n n_t/d(\ln k)^n$ to the tensor-to-scalar ratio $r$, the scalar spectral index $n_s$, and its runnings $α_n^s\equiv d^n(n_s-1)/d(\ln k)^n$. The consistency relations are valid for arbitrary $n\geq 0$ and any finite $p\geq 0$, without recursion over lower-order relations. For $k$-inflation, they additionally depend on the scalar sound speed and its scale derivatives. We develop an algorithm that generates both the spectra and consistency relations for any pair $(n,p)$. We reproduce known results through next-to-next-to-next-to-leading order (N3LO) and extend the explicit calculations up to N10LO.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [88] [LEAF: LUT-aware Efficient Attention on FPGAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27078)
*Arianna Cox,Chang Sun,Lauri Laatu,Benedikt Maier,Aadeesh Sharma,Leo Rozanov,Wayne Luk,Maria Spiropulu,Alexander Tapper,Zhiqiang Que*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LEAF 是首个面向 FPGA 的纳秒级查找表感知（LUT-aware）Transformer，通过在注意力模块中将常量矩阵-向量乘法制表、其余部分保留算术形式，实现超低延迟推理，并在喷注标记（jet tagging）基准上兼顾精度与资源效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时系统（如 CERN 场景）要求亚微秒乃至纳秒级延迟，而 Transformer 的表达能力源于注意力机制（attention mechanism），该机制在硬件上实现代价高昂；同时需兼顾模型规模足够大以保性能、足够小以适配 FPGA。

Method: 采用 LUT-aware 训练，将 query、key、value 投影及最终输出前馈层转换为 FPGA 原生层，构建首个 LUT-aware Transformer 与 linformer 层；针对注意力中两个激活相乘无法制表的问题，仅对注意力模块的常量矩阵-向量乘法进行制表（tabulation），其余保留算术计算形式。

Result: 在标准喷注标记（jet tagging）基准上，相比此前最优的亚微秒级 Transformer（相同输入规模），精度提升 1.8 个百分点，且 LUT 用量减少 2.9 倍；最高效的 64 粒子输入 LEAF-based linformer 在 28k LUTs 下达到 80.4% 分类精度，延迟 35ns。

Conclusion: LEAF 证明 LUT-aware 设计可在 FPGA 上实现纳秒级 Transformer 推理，在实时科学计算等超低延迟场景中具备可行性。

Abstract: We introduce Lookup-table (LUT)-aware Efficient Attention on FPGAs (LEAF). LEAF is the first nanosecond-scale LUT-aware transformer for FPGAs. Deploying transformers that are large enough to perform well, small enough to fit on an FPGA, and fast enough to meet the sub-microsecond timescales required by real-time systems such as those at CERN remains difficult, since the same attention mechanism that gives these models their high expressivity also makes them costly to implement in hardware. LEAF converts the query, key, and value projections and the final output feed-forward layer into FPGA-native layers with LUT-aware training, creating the first LUT-aware transformer and linformer layers built for ultra-low-latency inference. Attention resists LUT-aware training because it multiplies two activations where the method needs a constant weight to tabulate; LEAF tabulates the attention block's constant matrix-vector multiplications and leaves the rest in arithmetic form. On a standard jet tagging benchmark this improves accuracy by 1.8 percentage points while using 2.9 times fewer LUTs than the previous state-of-the-art sub-microsecond transformer at the same input size. Our most efficient LEAF-based linformer with 64 input particles reaches 80.4% classification accuracy at 28k LUTs and 35ns.

</details>


### [89] [Geometry-Agnostic Data-Driven Discovery of Extreme Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28487)
*Angeline Hu,Ava K. Tse,Kaitlyn S. Yasumura,Luciana Sarabia,Malcolm Yeung,Kavin K. Prasanna,Kai Wong,William Hammond,Ioannis G. Keverkidis,Shengkai Li,Trung V. Phan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两阶段数据驱动神经框架，从观测数据推断有效动力学并搜索动态可行的极端场景，在Duffing系统、非线性薛定谔系统、Liouville系统上成功发现同宿脉冲、孤子与瞬子等相干结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统极端场景识别依赖对控制方程的先验知识，而许多系统缺乏已知的支配方程；因此需要一种直接从数据推断动力学约束、并能外推出罕见高影响相干结构的方法。

Method: 第一阶段直接从观测数据推断有效动力学约束，在系统具有适当对称性时以几何无关（geometry-agnostic）形式表示，不依赖固定网格、坐标系、维度或内禀曲率；第二阶段采用物理信息神经搜索（physics-informed neural-search），利用所学动力学搜寻未在原始观测中出现的罕见相干结构。

Result: 依次在Duffing动力学中发现同宿脉冲（homoclinic pulse）；借助非线性薛定谔（nonlinear Schrödinger）系统将坐标解释为空间后，该脉冲对应驻波孤子；用几何无关表示把动力学提升至更高维空间得到Townes孤子；在Liouville系统中实现从(1+1)维洛伦兹准流形（Lorentzian quasi-manifold）到(2+0)维欧几里得流形（Euclidean manifold）的动力学迁移并识别瞬子（instanton）。

Conclusion: 该框架可推广至扭结（kinks）、畸形波（rogue waves）、紧致子（compactons）、峰子（peakons）乃至奇点等相干时空结构，在风险分析、非线性预测与异常检测中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: We introduce a two-stage data-driven neural framework for discovering dynamically admissible extreme scenarios. Rather than assuming prior knowledge of the governing equations, the first stage infers the effective dynamical constraints directly from observational data. When the underlying physical system possesses suitable symmetries, the learned dynamics can be represented in a geometry-agnostic form, independent of any fixed grid, coordinate system, dimensionality, and intrinsic curvature. In the second stage, a physics-informed neural-search uses this learned dynamics to uncover rare, high-impact coherent structures that may not have appeared in the original observations. We demonstrate this framework through representative examples of increasing dimensionality and geometric complexity. Starting from a traditionally learned Duffing dynamics, we find a homoclinic pulse as a plausible localized extreme scenario. Next, since the same equation also appears in the stationary nonlinear Schrodinger system, with the coordinate interpreted as space instead of time, this pulse becomes a standing soliton. We then retrain the first stage using a geometry-agnostic neural representation, enabling the learned dynamics to be lifted to higher-dimensional space to obtain higher-dimensional solitary profile, known as Townes soliton. Finally, with a Liouville system, we demonstrate that dynamics learned from data on a (1+1)-dimensional Lorentzian quasi-manifold can be transferred to a (2+0)-dimensional Euclidean manifold to identify an instanton. Our approach can be applied to other coherent spatiotemporal structures, including kinks, rogue waves, compactons, peakons, and potentially even singularities, with applications in risk analysis, nonlinear forecasting, and anomaly detection.

</details>


<div id='physics.class-ph'></div>

# physics.class-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [5D Runge-Lenz Vector from Symplectic Reduction of 8D Harmonic Oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27750)
*Hitoshi Ikemori,Shinsaku Kitakado,Yoshimitsu Matsui,Toshiyuki Okubo,Toshiro Sato*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过辛约化（symplectic reduction）从八维谐振子（eight-dimensional harmonic oscillator）导出五维开普勒问题（five-dimensional Kepler problem）的龙格-楞次矢量（Runge-Lenz vector）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在建立八维谐振子与五维开普勒问题之间的约化联系，并给出龙格-楞次矢量可由谐振子的矩映射（moment map）经约化得到的构造性说明。

Method: 将谐振子的矩映射用五维伽马矩阵（gamma matrices）与辛约化相关的 𝔰𝔲(2) 生成元重写，以识别预期给出龙格-楞次矢量的矩映射；随后针对 SU(2) 对称性执行约化，并推导五维开普勒哈密顿量（Kepler Hamiltonian）。

Result: 明确显示所识别的矩映射在约化后归结为龙格-楞次矢量，从而得到五维开普勒问题的该守恒量。

Conclusion: 结果表明，五维开普勒问题的龙格-楞次矢量可自然地源于八维谐振子的矩映射在 SU(2) 辛约化下的约化，验证了该约化框架的有效性。

Abstract: We derive the Runge-Lenz vector of the five-dimensional Kepler problem from the eight-dimensional harmonic oscillator through symplectic reduction. By rewriting the moment maps of the harmonic oscillator in terms of five-dimensional gamma matrices and the $\mathfrak{su}(2)$ generators associated with the symplectic reduction, we identify the moment map expected to yield the Runge-Lenz vector. We then perform the reduction with respect to the SU(2) symmetry, derive the five-dimensional Kepler Hamiltonian, and show explicitly that the identified moment map reduces to the Runge-Lenz vector.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [91] [A Transport-Information Framework for Continuous-Fiber-Aligned Coarse-Graining Representation Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27811)
*Koretaka Yuge*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文提出一个“输运-信息（transport-information）”框架，用于系统刻画粗粒化（coarse-graining, CG）所关联的信息结构，通过纤维结构将表征与连续实现相联系，并将CG视为局部映射输运操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗粒化可视为丢弃未被保留信息以缩减表征的操作，但关于CG所关联信息结构的系统性描述尚未建立，缺乏非任意的信息论刻画方式。

Method: 针对连续纤维对齐（continuous-fiber-aligned）的表征集，将每个表征经由纤维结构与连续实现关联，CG定义为到表征的投影；CG由局部映射（local-map）操作描述，把连续概率质量输运至代表点，输运代价表示为连续表征二阶矩结构与输运度量的收缩。

Result: 借助既有不可避规范非线性（unavoidable canonical nonlinearity, UCN）框架，所构造的输运代价获得了非任意的信息论解释，即以与CG几何相关的期望KL散度（Kullback-Leibler divergence）来量化信息不可区分性。

Conclusion: 该框架为描述单个CG操作的信息代价、以及在考虑其底层连续实现的前提下比较不同表征集提供了基础。

Abstract: Consider representation sets of interest whose elements are associated with continuous realizations related to them through coarse-graining (CG). CG can be regarded as an operation that reduces a representation by discarding information that is not retained in its output. However, a systematic description of the information structure associated with CG in general remains to be established. Here, we propose a transport-information framework for continuous-fiber-aligned representation sets, in which each representation is associated with a continuous realization through a fiber structure, with CG defining the projection onto the representation. Within this framework, CG is described through a local-map operation that transports continuous probability mass onto representative points. The resulting transport cost is expressed as the contraction of the second-moment structure of the underlying continuous representation with the transport metric. Building on the previously proposed framework of unavoidable canonical nonlinearity (UCN), this construction provides a non-arbitrary information-theoretic interpretation of the CG-associated transport cost in terms of information indistinguishability, quantified by an expected Kullback-Leibler divergence associated with the CG geometry. The present framework provides a basis for describing information costs associated with individual CG operations and for comparing representation sets while accounting for their underlying continuous realizations.

</details>


### [92] [Classical-to-Quantum Transition in Blackbody Radiation Thermodynamics: Planck's Law without Statistical Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27858)
*J. Wang,G. Casati,G. Benenti*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究封闭有限能量系统中物质与电磁场耦合的经典动力学，发现能量向高频模式级联转移导致有效温度长期趋于零，与经典黑体热力学的有限温度平衡矛盾；在特定特征时间 t_L 处经典谱坍缩为普朗克分布，并据此推测量子动力学局域化止住了这一级联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受费米（Fermi）辐射-物质相互作用模型启发，考察经典黑体热力学假定有限温度平衡是否与封闭有限能量耦合系统的经典动力学相容。

Method: 数值/理论分析封闭有限能量系统中物质与电磁场的经典耦合动力学，追踪能量在各频率场模式间的转移；并要求结果与斯特藩-玻尔兹曼定律（Stefan-Boltzmann law）及维恩定律（Wien law）同时相容，以确定特征时间 t_L。

Result: 能量逐步转移至越来越高频的场模式，物质与辐射的有效温度在长时间极限下趋于零，与经典黑体热力学的有限温度平衡相矛盾；在特征时间 t_L 处，不同能量与腔尺寸所对应的经典谱坍缩到普适的普朗克分布（Planck distribution）上，且两条黑体定律同时得到满足。

Conclusion: 作者推测量子动力学局域化（dynamical localization）在 t_L 处中止经典能量级联，普朗克分布作为该中止的动力学后果出现，而无需引入任何先验统计假设。

Abstract: Inspired by Fermi's model of radiation-matter interaction, we investigate the classical dynamics of a closed, finite-energy system, in which matter is coupled to an electromagnetic field. We find that energy is progressively transferred to increasingly high-frequency field modes, so that the effective temperature of both matter and radiation approaches zero in the long-time limit - in sharp contradiction with the finite-temperature equilibrium assumed by classical blackbody thermodynamics. Requiring consistency with the Stefan-Boltzmann and Wien laws identifies a characteristic time $t_L$, at which both laws are simultaneously satisfied. Strikingly, classical spectra obtained for different energies and cavity sizes collapse onto the universal Planck distribution at $t_L$. We conjecture that quantum dynamical localization arrests the classical energy cascade at $t_L$, and that the Planck distribution emerges as a dynamical consequence of this arrest - without invoking any a priori statistical assumption.

</details>


### [93] [Thermodynamic Stability of Classical $\mathbb Z_2$ Lattice Gauge Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27938)
*Shunsuke Sengoku,Weiguang Cao,Haruki Watanabe*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文证明：对固定空间维度 d 中自旋位于 m 维胞腔上的经典 Z_2 格点规范模型（classical Z_2 lattice gauge models），当 1≤m≤d−2 时，低温下自由能密度（free-energy density）对温度与微扰强度联合解析（joint analyticity），且微扰无需保持任何内部或规范对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限温度相（finite-temperature phases）依赖自发对称破缺（spontaneous symmetry breaking）界定，但对称破缺场可消除相的区别（如 Ising 模型）。因此需要寻找不依赖对称性保护（symmetry protection）的稳定非平凡热相，本文即为这一目标提供理论证据。

Method: 对固定空间维度 d、自旋定义在 m 维胞腔上的经典 Z_2 格点规范模型，在足够低的正温度与足够弱的局域微扰（local perturbations）下证明热力学自由能密度（thermodynamic free-energy density）对温度和微扰强度的联合解析性；微扰可破坏内部/规范对称性并包含非对易量子相互作用（noncommuting quantum interactions）。另以独立的高温展开（high-temperature expansion）给出在任意固定有限微扰强度下延伸至无穷温度的解析区域。

Result: 在 1≤m≤d−2 条件下，低温区存在对称性无关的解析稳定区；高温展开亦给出解析区。两者结合支持低温与高温区域之间由热相变（thermal transition）分隔的猜想（conjecture）。

Conclusion: 研究确立了无对称性保护情形下的热力学稳定性（thermodynamic stability），并为“低温与高温regime之间存在无需对称性保护的热相变”这一猜想提供支持。

Abstract: Finite-temperature phases are traditionally studied through spontaneous symmetry breaking, but symmetry-breaking fields can remove their distinction, as in the Ising model. This motivates the search for stable nontrivial thermal phases without symmetry protection. For classical $\mathbb Z_2$ lattice gauge models with spins on $m$-dimensional cells in a fixed spatial dimension $d$, we prove joint analyticity of the thermodynamic free-energy density in temperature and perturbation strength at sufficiently low positive temperatures under sufficiently weak local perturbations, whenever $1\le m\le d-2$. Perturbations need not preserve internal or gauge symmetries and may include noncommuting quantum interactions. A separate high-temperature expansion yields an analytic region reaching infinite temperature at any fixed finite perturbation strength. These results establish thermodynamic stability and support the conjecture that low- and high-temperature regimes remain separated by a thermal transition without symmetry protection.

</details>


### [94] [Initial-State-Dictated Dynamical Universality Classes in Quantum Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28365)
*Yu-Rong Shu,Shuai Yin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文提出“初态决定的动力学普适类（initial-state-dictated dynamical universality classes）”概念，通过在二聚化海森堡模型（dimerized Heisenberg model）的虚时间弛豫动力学中展示：同一平衡普适类内，不同初态可导致不同的动力学临界行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相变物理以平衡普适类（universality class）及对应临界指数刻画临界现象，但非平衡量子临界动力学中初态是否也影响普适分类尚不明确，作者旨在探究初态对动力学普适类的决定作用。

Method: 研究二聚化海森堡模型（dimerized Heisenberg model）的虚时间弛豫动力学（imaginary-time relaxation dynamics），对比“经典”顺磁（随机自旋）初态与“量子”顺磁（二聚化与反二聚化）初态下的体（bulk）与边界（boundary）临界动力学行为。

Result: 不同初态给出显著不同的体临界初始滑移指数（critical initial slip exponent）；初态依赖同样延伸至边界临界动力学，即使在同一边界普适类内，不同初态也产生不同的表面临界初始滑移指数。对反二聚化初态，还发现违反体与表面临界初始滑移指数常规关系的反常标度律（anomalous scaling law）。

Conclusion: 结果表明存在一种由初态控制的量子临界性动力学分类（initial-state-controlled dynamical classification），其超越传统平衡普适类范式，扩展了对普适类概念的认识。

Abstract: The concept of universality class lies at the core of phase transition physics, with each class uniquely characterized by a dedicated set of critical exponents. Here, we introduce the concept of initial-state-dictated dynamical universality classes in nonequilibrium quantum critical dynamics. By studying the imaginary-time relaxation dynamics of the dimerized Heisenberg model, we demonstrate that distinct kinds of initial states can give rise to different dynamical universality classes within the same equilibrium universality class. Specifically, we find that "classical'' paramagnetic (random-spin) and "quantum'' paramagnetic (dimerized and antidimerized) initial states yield drastically different critical initial slip exponents in the bulk. This initial-state dependence also extends to boundary critical dynamics, where different initial states yield different surface critical initial slip exponents even within the same boundary universality class. Remarkably, for the antidimerized initial state, we uncover an anomalous scaling law violating the conventional relation between the bulk and surface critical initial slip exponents. Our results establish an initial-state-controlled dynamical classification of quantum criticality that extends beyond the conventional equilibrium universality paradigm.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [Vortex matter as a magnetically programmable metasurface for tuning near-field radiative heat transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26874)
*Siddharth Sangi,Pramod Reddy,Shanhui Fan*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文提出利用第二类超导体（type-II superconductor）的磁通涡旋晶格（vortex lattice）作为自组装超表面（metasurface），通过磁场调节其晶格尺寸，从而在纳米尺度上大范围调节近场辐射热传递（NFRHT）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明超表面可增强近场辐射热传递并重塑其光谱特性，但多数超表面的结构参数固定、难以动态调控，而磁场可调的调控方案多局限于量级为一的调制幅度，因而需要一种可大范围、原位可调的近场热传递调控机制。

Method: 将第二类超导体的涡旋晶格视为一种自组装超表面，利用磁场改变其晶格尺寸以调控近场辐射热传递；以两块温度 4.2 K、间距 100 nm 的铌（Nb）表面为对象进行数值/理论分析，并进一步考察降温至 2 K 时的调控效果。

Result: 在 4.2 K、100 nm 间隙下，近场辐射热传递可通过磁场调节超过 200 倍；随温度降低调控范围进一步扩大，在 2 K 时超过 10^5，远大于许多已有调控方案中量级为一的调制幅度。

Conclusion: 研究确立了涡旋物质（vortex matter）作为一种磁场可编程的热超表面（magnetically programmable thermal metasurface），为纳米尺度热流调控提供了新途径。

Abstract: Near-field radiative heat transfer (NFRHT) is of great interest for energy conversion and thermal management. Recent work on NFRHT has shown that metasurfaces can enhance NFRHT and reshape its spectral characteristics. Here, we show that the vortex lattice of a type-II superconductor represents a self-assembled metasurface whose lattice dimensions, in contrast to most other metasurfaces, can be tuned by magnetic fields, allowing NFRHT to be varied by orders of magnitude. Specifically, we show that for two Nb surfaces at 4.2 K and separated by a 100 nm gap, NFRHT can be magnetically tuned by a factor >200. Further, we show that the tuning range grows upon cooling, exceeding 10^5 at 2 K, which is far beyond the order-unity modulation of many proposed schemes for tuning NFRHT. Our results establish vortex matter as a magnetically programmable thermal metasurface and open new opportunities for tuning the flow of heat at the nanoscale.

</details>


### [96] [High-Field and Light-Dependent Magnetization Provides Evidence for Coexisting Granular Superconductivity and Superparamagnetism in Nitrogen-Doped Diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26880)
*E. Osmic,J. Barzola-Quiquia,S. Winnerl,T. Herrmannsdoerfer,M. Uhlarz,J. Meijer*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 氮掺杂 Ib 型金刚石在约 25 K 以下暗态中呈现超顺磁性与超导性共存的非常规磁滞行为，光照可逆地抑制超导相关特征，去除超顺磁贡献后表现为颗粒超导体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究氮掺杂 Ib 型金刚石（nitrogen-doped type Ib diamond）在低温下的磁性质，并考察光（低光强约 160 μW cm⁻²）与温度对磁矩的影响，以厘清其中可能共存的磁性相与超导相。

Method: 测量磁矩（magnetic moment）随场、温度和时间的依赖关系，分别在暗态与单色光照射下进行；并进行直至 ±14 T 的磁化测量以扣除超顺磁贡献，辅以同一批样品的拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）与双折射光谱（birefringence spectroscopy）测量。

Result: 暗态下约 25 K 以下出现非常规磁滞回线、不可逆性以及 0.5 T 下类磁通蠕变（flux-creep-like）的时间依赖磁化；光照后磁滞基本消失、不可逆性与蠕变行为消失，仅保留超顺磁（superparamagnetism）贡献。扣除超顺磁贡献后得到颗粒超导体特征：穿透场 H_p ≈ 2.6 T、临界场 H_c2 ≈ 10.6 T。所有光致变化在加热至约 50 K 以上后可完全恢复。

Conclusion: 观测现象被归因于局域区域（可视为晶粒）内内应力（internal stress）与氮浓度局域涨落的共同作用，该解释得到拉曼与双折射光谱测量的支持；结果表明氮掺杂金刚石中可存在超顺磁性与颗粒超导性（granular superconductivity）的共存及光可调控行为。

Abstract: We investigated the field-, temperature-, and time-dependent magnetic moment of a nitrogen-doped type Ib diamond in darkness and under monochromatic light with a low laser intensity of about 160 $μ$W cm$^{-2}$. In darkness, below approximately 25 K, the magnetic moment exhibits unconventional hysteresis loops that can be explained by the coexistence of superparamagnetism and superconductivity in certain regions of the sample. Under illumination, this hysteresis largely disappears, leaving mainly the superparamagnetic contribution. Temperature-dependent measurements reveal an irreversibility below 25 K in darkness, which vanishes under illumination. Similarly, at 0.5 T and below 25 K, the time-dependent magnetization shows flux-creep-like behavior that disappears when the sample is illuminated. All light-induced changes are fully reversible: the magnetic properties measured in darkness are restored after the sample is heated above roughly 50 K. Removing the superparamagnetic contribution from magnetization measurements up to $\pm 14$ T reveals clear characteristics of a granular superconductor, namely, a well-defined penetration field $H_p \approx 2.6$ T and a critical field $H_{c2} \approx 10.6$ T. Based on theoretical considerations, we attribute the observed phenomena to the combined effects of internal stress and local variations in nitrogen concentration within localized regions that can be regarded as grains. This interpretation is supported by Raman and birefringence spectroscopy measurements on the same sample.

</details>


### [97] [Rashba-controlled emergence and reversal of critical-field anisotropy in p-wave magnet--superconductor hybrids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27499)
*Rui-Xiang Cheng,Zhao Gong,Dong-Hui Xu,Chui-Zhen Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过调节Rashba自旋轨道耦合（Rashba spin-orbit coupling），可在不改动磁织构取向的情况下，将二维s波超导体最易发生Zeeman去配对的面内场方向旋转90°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文探究面内临界场的各向异性是否可被电学调控，即是否存在一个可调方向、且不依赖重新取向磁织构的顺磁响应通道。

Method: 考虑受p波磁体诱导奇宇称交换场（odd-parity exchange field）作用的二维s波超导体，对均匀单重态配对（uniform singlet pairing）下的Bogoliubov–de Gennes巨势（grand potential）作最小化。

Result: 无Rashba耦合时，面外交换场提升面内临界场但其角响应严格各向同性；增大Rashba耦合先产生再反转主轴临界场对比，使最小临界场方向跳变90°，补偿点处两主轴场相等且仍有残余角调制。

Conclusion: 该反转源于磁织构与Rashba织构的动量依赖去配对贡献相互竞争（在位单重态配对通道不变），并伴有方向依赖的序参量不连续塌缩；在界面Rashba耦合电学可调的异质结构中可实现易去配对轴的门控。

Abstract: We show that tuning Rashba spin-orbit coupling can change the in-plane field direction along which an $s$-wave superconductor is most susceptible to Zeeman depairing. We consider a two-dimensional $s$-wave superconductor subject to an odd-parity exchange field induced by a $p$-wave magnet. Without Rashba coupling, the out-of-plane exchange field enhances the in-plane critical field but leaves its angular response exactly isotropic, despite the anisotropic magnetic spin texture. Minimizing the Bogoliubov--de Gennes grand potential over uniform singlet pairing, we find that increasing Rashba coupling first generates and then reverses the principal-axis critical-field contrast, switching the minimum-critical-field direction by $90^{\circ}$. Equal principal-axis fields at the intervening compensation point coexist with residual angular modulation. We interpret the reversal through competing momentum-dependent depairing contributions from the magnetic and Rashba spin textures, while the on-site singlet pairing channel remains unchanged. Direction-dependent discontinuous collapses of the order parameter further shape the critical-field curves. These results identify a tunable directional paramagnetic response within the uniform-pairing sector. In heterostructures with sufficient electrostatic tunability of interfacial Rashba coupling, this reversal would enable gate control of the easy-depairing axis without reorienting the magnetic texture.

</details>


### [98] [Non-uniform grids to accelerate electron-phonon coupling calculations for superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27718)
*Kang Wang,Siyu Chen,Sun-Woo Kim,Kieran Bozier,Chris Pickard,Bartomeu Monserrat*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 提出一种结合双delta截断（double delta truncation）与非均匀Farey网格的加速框架，用于高效计算电子-声子相互作用（electron-phonon interactions），在保证精度前提下将超导预测的计算成本降低最多两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确预测超导电性（superconductivity）需要高效评估电子-声子相互作用，但密集布里渊区（Brillouin zone）采样带来严重计算瓶颈，均匀密集网格的采样开销难以承受。

Method: 在k空间与q空间中同时采用双delta截断方案与非均匀Farey网格：截断策略仅保留费米能级（Fermi level）附近满足能量约束的态以降低积分成本，Farey网格则动态消解局域散射引起的采样误差并稳定收敛。

Result: 在元素铝（aluminium）及多种高压氢化物（high-pressure hydrides）上的测试表明，计算成本最多降低两个数量级，电子-声子耦合常数（electron-phonon coupling constants）与超导临界温度（critical temperature, Tc）通常与全网格基准（full-grid benchmarks）偏差约5%以内。

Conclusion: 该组合方法克服了密集均匀网格相关的采样瓶颈，使复杂量子材料（quantum materials）中高精度、低成本的超导预测成为可行。

Abstract: Efficient and accurate evaluation of electron-phonon interactions is essential for predictive superconductivity calculations, but dense Brillouin zone sampling requirements pose a major computational bottleneck. To overcome this, we introduce an accelerated, highly scalable framework that combines a double delta truncation scheme with non-uniform Farey grids in both $\mathbf{k}$ and $\mathbf{q}$ space. The truncation strategy significantly reduces computational cost by restricting integration to states satisfying energy constraints near the Fermi level, while the Farey grids dynamically resolve sampling errors from localized scattering and stabilize convergence. Applied to elemental aluminium and several high-pressure hydrides, this combined methodology reduces computational cost by up to two orders of magnitude and yields electron-phonon coupling constants and superconducting critical temperatures Tc typically within about 5% of full-grid benchmarks. This approach overcomes sampling bottlenecks associated with dense uniform grids, making accurate, low-cost predictions of superconductivity in complex quantum materials feasible.

</details>


### [99] [Superconducting weak links at substrate steps in bilayer nickelate films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27729)
*Yidi Liu,Yaoju Tarn,Lopa Bhatt,Puhua Zhang,Joonseo Song,Berit H. Goodge,David A. Muller,Harold Y. Hwang,Yijun Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 双层镍酸盐（bilayer nickelate）薄膜在40 K以上的常压超导性已实现，但其电阻测量常出现零电阻不完整或两步超导转变（two-step superconducting transition）。该文将这一现象归因于衬底台阶-平台结构（step-terrace structure）诱导的超导弱连接（weak link），并通过角度依赖关联、原子尺度成像与有限元模拟加以证实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层镍酸盐薄膜在SrLaAlO$_4$(001)衬底上呈现40 K以上常压超导，但电阻测量中零电阻态不完整及两步转变的物理起源未明；厘清该现象对理解其输运性质与器件应用具有重要意义。

Method: 结合方位角依赖的两步转变与台阶-平台取向的相关性分析、台阶处结构破坏区域的原子尺度可视化，并将薄膜建模为具有较高（较低）转变温度的完整（弱连接）区域，采用简单模型与有限元模拟（finite-element simulation）进行复现；同时考察平均平台宽度对转变特性的影响。

Result: 方位角依赖的两步转变与台阶-平台取向呈直接相关，原子尺度成像显示台阶处存在结构破坏区域；完整/弱连接两区域模型可复现两步转变的主要特征，且转变特性乃至超导是否出现均强烈依赖平均平台宽度。

Conclusion: 台阶诱导的弱连接是双层镍酸盐薄膜两步超导转变的微观起源，为理解各向异性（anisotropic）与逾渗（percolative）输运提供了框架，并为超导器件制备提供了途径。

Abstract: Ambient-pressure superconductivity above 40 K has been realized in bilayer nickelate thin films on SrLaAlO$_4$ (001), yet resistive measurements often exhibit incomplete zero-resistance states and/or two-step superconducting transitions of unresolved origin. We show that these features arise from superconducting weak links at substrate step-terrace structures. A direct correlation between the azimuthal-angle-dependent two-step transition and step-terrace orientation establishes this connection, further supported by atomic-scale visualization of structurally disrupted regions at step terraces. Modeling the films as pristine (weak-link) regions with higher (lower) transition temperature reproduces essential features of the two-step transition using both a simple model and finite-element simulations. Transport measurements further reveal a strong dependence of transition characteristics - and even the occurrence of superconductivity itself - on average terrace width. These results identify step-induced weak links as the microscopic origin of two-step transitions in bilayer nickelate films, providing a framework for understanding anisotropic and percolative transport, and a route to superconducting devices.

</details>


### [100] [Saturating $T^2$ resistivity in iron-based superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28240)
*M. Lourdes Amigó,Jorge I. Facio*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文提出一个简单的唯象框架（phenomenological framework），用以分离导致电阻率（resistivity）高温饱和趋势的贡献，从而区分声子散射（phonon scattering）与电子-电子关联（electron-electron correlations）的作用，并在多种铁基超导体（iron-based superconductors）中检验该框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关联金属（correlated metals）中，电阻率由低温 ρ∝T² 行为向高温下较缓慢增长（饱和趋势）的转变（crossover）来源不明；实验中区分声子散射与电子-电子关联对该转变的贡献存在困难，需寻找可操作的判别手段。

Method: 引入一个简单的唯象框架，专门分离出造成高温饱和趋势的贡献；并将其应用于以电子-声子散射主导的已知体系与若干铁基超导体进行对比分析。

Result: 该框架显示出以电子-声子散射主导的体系与若干铁基超导体之间的清晰差异。对 LiFeAs、KFe₂As₂ 和 KCaFe₄As₄，电导率（conductivity）可表示为类费米液体项（Fermi-liquid-like term，∝1/T²）与温度无关成分之和，二者共同描述 ρ(T) 直至约 250 K。在 FeSe 中，该框架识别出向列相变（nematic transition）之上存在类似区间，而其下电阻率显著转变为近似线性于 T 的行为。

Conclusion: 研究结果在 Hund 金属（Hund's metals）中相干性（coherence）与电子关联的更广泛背景下得到讨论，为理解关联金属电阻率的高温行为提供了唯象判据与体系对比依据。

Abstract: Many correlated metals exhibit a crossover from low-temperature $ρ\propto T^{2}$ behavior to a slower increase of the resistivity at elevated temperatures. Distinguishing phonon scattering from electron--electron correlations as the origin of this crossover is experimentally challenging. We introduce a simple phenomenological framework that isolates the contribution responsible for the high-temperature saturating tendency. This approach reveals a clear contrast between systems well known for the predominance of electron--phonon scattering and several iron-based superconductors. For the latter, the conductivity can be expressed as the sum of a Fermi-liquid-like term ($\propto 1/T^{2}$) and a temperature-independent component, which together describe $ρ(T)$ up to $\sim 250$ K in LiFeAs, KFe$_2$As$_2$, and KCaFe$_4$As$_4$. In FeSe, the same approach identifies a similar regime above the nematic transition, with a pronounced change toward nearly linear-in-$T$ resistivity below it. We discuss these findings in the broader context of coherence and electronic correlations in Hund's metals.

</details>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [The optical force on a dielectric slab](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27254)
*Andrew Laeuger,Kevin Kuns,Yanbei Chen,Nancy Aggarwal*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文比较了洛伦兹力（Lorentz force）与外部场动量通量（momentum flux）两种方法计算有限厚度介质板所受辐射压力（radiation-pressure force），指出二者差异源自介质板内储存电磁动量的时间导数项，该项在腔（cavity）中导致速度相关力，并在外部真空环境下调和了Abraham与Minkowski动量表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上透射型光学元件（transmissive optics）的辐射压力及动力学反作用（dynamical backaction）由外部场动量通量计算，但对有限厚度介质而言，此做法忽略了板内储存电磁动量的时间依赖性，需澄清由此产生的力差异及其物理来源。

Method: 针对浸没于介电液体中、厚度为任意值 $L_s$ 的无损耗介质板（lossless dielectric slab），分别从束缚电荷所受洛伦兹力及外部动量通量两条路径推导任意载波（carrier）与边带（sideband）场下的受力；周围介质以给定线性介电响应（linear dielectric response）描述，不建模流体的力学响应（含压力与电致伸缩，electrostriction）。

Result: 两种方法在薄反射镜（thin reflector）极限下给出相同的预期力，一般情况下则相差板内储存场动量的变化率；该储存项（storage term）在静场下为零，低频时约化为 $(L_s/c^2)\,dP/dt$（$P$ 为穿过板面的净功率）。在腔中 $P$ 依赖板位置，故储存项产生速度相关力（velocity-dependent force）。

Conclusion: 在外部真空环境（无流体响应需建模）情形下，将储存项分别按Abraham动量与Minkowski动量表述处理后可得到一致的、无歧义的介质受力结果。

Abstract: Radiation-pressure forces on transmissive optics, including dynamical backaction, are usually computed from the momentum flux of the external fields. For a dielectric of finite thickness, this omits the time dependence of the electromagnetic momentum stored inside. We compute the force on a lossless dielectric slab of arbitrary thickness $L_s$ submerged in a dielectric fluid, proceeding from both Lorentz force on its bound charges and from the external momentum fluxes for arbitrary carrier and sideband fields. We treat the surrounding medium through a prescribed linear dielectric response and do not model the accompanying mechanical response of the fluid, including pressure and electrostriction. These two methods both recover the expected force in the limit of a thin reflector, but differ in the more general case by the rate of change of the field momentum stored in the slab. This storage term vanishes for static fields and at low frequencies reduces to $(L_s/c^2)\,dP/dt$, where $P$ is the net power through the slab. In a cavity, where $P$ depends on the slab position, the storage term produces a velocity-dependent force. The Abraham and Minkowski momentum formulations applied to the storage term are reconciled to give an unambiguous force on the dielectric in the case of an exterior vacuum environment, where there is no fluid response to model.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [CrystalAR: Autoregressive Modeling for Symmetric Crystal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27762)
*Ziqiao Meng,Rongsheng Chen,Zhi Zheng,Nikita Kazeev,Zhenkun Wang,Andrey Ustyuzhanin,Wee Sun Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CrystalAR 是一个统一的自回归框架，将 Wyckoff 对称性模板与完整晶体结构建模于单一生成序列中，通过将连续坐标生成重构为对称性条件下的离散分层空间定位决策，在保留对称性模板生成能力的同时，实现有竞争力的从头生成与无模板晶体结构预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体结构富含对称性，已有工作尝试将空间群（space-group）对称性显式引入生成模型以更好探索新颖对称性模板，但引入模板会加剧原子种类与晶体学位点之间化学计量关系的组合复杂性，并在离散晶体学模板的灵活探索与将其忠实实现为连续三维结构之间形成根本性张力。

Method: 提出统一的生成式自回归（autoregressive）框架 CrystalAR，在单一生成序列中同时建模 Wyckoff 对称性模板与完整晶体结构；将连续坐标生成重构为对称性条件下的离散空间定位决策层级，在各分辨率层级上自回归细化所有对称性不等价位点的位置，并联合建模同一层级内的空间构型。该离散分层表述进一步支持对晶体学模板与几何实现的约束感知搜索（constraint-aware search）。

Result: CrystalAR 保留了较强的对称性模板生成能力，同时取得有竞争力的从头生成（de novo generation）性能与无模板晶体结构预测（crystal structure prediction, CSP）准确率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对称性模板生成可与广泛具有竞争力的从头生成性能及有竞争力的 CSP 并存，且无需依赖从先验数据中检索的对称性模板，为保持对称性的晶体生成提供了一种有前景的替代范式。

Abstract: Deep generative modeling has achieved remarkable progress in crystal generation. Given the rich symmetries inherent in crystalline structures, recent work has sought to explicitly incorporate space-group symmetry into generative models, enabling more effective exploration of novel symmetry templates. Yet introducing symmetry templates increases the combinatorial complexity of stoichiometric relationships between atomic species and crystallographic sites, while creating a fundamental tension between flexible exploration of discrete crystallographic templates and faithful realization of these templates as continuous three-dimensional structures. To address these challenges, we propose CrystalAR, a unified autoregressive framework that models Wyckoff symmetry templates and complete crystal structures within a single generative sequence. Specifically, CrystalAR reformulates continuous coordinate generation as a symmetry-conditioned hierarchy of discrete spatial localization decisions, autoregressively refining the positions of all symmetry-inequivalent sites across resolution levels while jointly modeling their spatial configuration within each level. This discrete hierarchical formulation further enables constraint-aware search over both crystallographic templates and geometric realizations. As a result, CrystalAR retains strong symmetry-template generation capability while achieving competitive de novo generation performance and template-free crystal structure prediction (CSP) accuracy. These results suggest that symmetry-template generation can be retained alongside broadly competitive de novo generation performance and competitive CSP without reliance on symmetry templates retrieved from prior data, providing a promising alternative paradigm for symmetry-preserving crystal generation.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [TexFlow: learning crystallographic texture on SO(3) manifold via normalizing flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28363)
*Gowtham Nimmal Haribabu,Tarakram Ramgopal,Divakar Vetriveeran,Siddhant Kumar*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出 TexFlow，一个基于条件归一化流（conditional normalizing flow）的织构建模框架，通过在 SO(3) 上构造概率分布实现旋转取向的生成式建模，并结合 Hopf 纤维化（Hopf-fibration）可视化，在实验与模拟数据上验证了多峰织构及其条件演化的准确刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统织构分析依赖取向分布函数（orientation distribution function）与极图（pole figure），缺乏面向"工艺—结构—性能"关联预测建模的直接框架；而现有机器学习方法多面向欧氏数据，难以处理非欧氏群 SO(3) 中旋转的流形结构。

Method: 构建条件归一化流框架 TexFlow，通过构造方式学习 SO(3) 上旋转的概率分布：采用六维 Gram-Schmidt 正交化表示（Gram-Schmidt orthogonalization representation）编码旋转并映射回合法旋转 $\mathbf{R}\in\text{SO}(3)$，支持织构的条件概率建模 $p(\mathbf{R}|c)$，其中 $c$ 可表示温度或变形状态等工艺参数；同时引入 Hopf 纤维化可视化作为织构的紧凑全局表示。

Result: 在涵盖多种材料体系的无条件与条件实验及模拟织构数据集上验证，TexFlow 能够准确捕捉多峰织构（multimodal textures）及其条件演化。

Conclusion: TexFlow 提供了数据驱动的框架，可用于建立工艺—织构关联（process-texture linkages），弥补传统织构分析与预测性建模之间的差距。

Abstract: Crystallographic texture is an important microstructural feature that statistically describes the orientations of grains in polycrystalline materials. Conventional texture analysis relies on the orientation distribution functions and pole figures, which are essential for interpreting the texture of a given material specimen but do not, by themselves, provide a direct framework for predictive modeling of process-structure-property linkages. Data- and machine learning-based approaches offer a means to address this gap; however, a key challenge lies in developing representations and learning frameworks that account for the manifold structure of rotations. In particular, many existing machine learning techniques are designed for Euclidean data and therefore are not readily applicable to rotations in the non-Euclidean group $\text{SO}(3)$. Here we introduce TexFlow, a conditional normalizing flow framework for texture modeling that learns a probability distribution over rotations in $\text{SO}(3)$ by construction. Rotations are encoded in a six-dimensional Gram-Schmidt orthogonalization representation which is then mapped back to valid rotations $\mathbf{R}\in\text{SO}(3)$. The framework supports conditional probabilistic modeling of texture $p(\mathbf{R}|c)$, where $c$ may represent processing parameters like temperature or deformation state. To complement conventional texture-analysis visualizations, such as pole figures and sections of the orientation distribution function, we further introduce Hopf-fibration visualizations as a compact global representation of texture. TexFlow is validated on both unconditional and conditional experimental and simulated texture datasets spanning diverse material systems. We show that TexFlow can accurately capture multimodal textures and their conditional evolution, providing a data-driven framework for establishing process-texture linkages.

</details>

### [104] [Localizing binary neutron stars with the Einstein Telescope: a full Bayesian comparison of the triangular and 2L configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27081)
*Filippo Santoliquido,Jacopo Tissino,Marica Branchesi,Ulyana Dupletsa*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过全贝叶斯参数估计（full Bayesian parameter estimation）比较爱因斯坦望远镜（Einstein Telescope, ET）两种候选构型——三角构型（triangular design, Δ）与2L构型——对双中子星（binary neutron star, BNS）并合源的天区定位能力；结果显示2L构型在低、中等信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio, SNR）下显著优于Δ构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ET探测器的构型选择仍处开放状态，且对科学产出影响深远，因此需要为这一决策提供定量依据，尤其是此前缺乏超越Fisher矩阵（Fisher-matrix）预测的真实天区定位不确定性估计。

Method: 在各自拟建站点对比两种构型（Δ构型位于Euregio Meuse-Rhine站点；2L构型干涉仪分布于意大利撒丁岛与德国卢萨蒂亚），主要针对高频干涉仪单独进行全贝叶斯参数估计，并注入天空位置不同、光度距离40–2000 Mpc（SNR 12–720）的BNS信号；分析放松长波近似（long-wavelength approximation），并纳入地球自转与高阶模式（higher-order modes）。

Result: Δ构型对低、中等SNR源产生约八个退化天区模式，分布于南北半球及地球昼夜两侧，90%可信区域（90% credible areas）约达2×10^4 deg²；2L构型则恢复出单一主导模式，包含至少50%的后验概率（posterior probability），面积小一到两个数量级。

Conclusion: 2L构型在天区定位上具有实质优势，对多信使（multimessenger）后续观测 campaign 更为有利，为ET构型选择提供了定量支持。

Abstract: The Einstein Telescope (ET), the European next-generation gravitational-wave observatory, is at a decisive stage: the choice of its detector configuration is still open, with profound implications for its scientific output. We provide a quantitative basis for this choice by comparing two candidate configurations at their proposed sites: the triangular design ($Δ$) at the Euregio Meuse-Rhine site, and the 2L design, with interferometers in Sardinia, Italy, and Lusatia, Germany. We present the first realistic estimates of sky-localization uncertainties for binary neutron star (BNS) mergers, moving beyond Fisher-matrix forecasts to full Bayesian parameter estimation that captures the multimodal structure of the sky posterior. We focus mainly on the high-frequency interferometers alone, providing an expected scenario for the first years of ET operations, as the more challenging low-frequency instruments will come online later. BNS injections span different sky positions and luminosity distances between 40 and 2000 Mpc, corresponding to signal-to-noise ratios (SNRs) from 12 to 720. Our analysis relaxes the long-wavelength approximation and includes Earth's rotation and higher-order modes, all of which improve sky localization. We find that the $Δ$ configuration localizes low- and moderate-SNR sources in eight degenerate sky modes spread across both hemispheres and over the day and night sides of the Earth, with 90% credible areas reaching $\sim 2 \times 10^4 \, \mathrm{deg}^2$. In contrast, the 2L configuration recovers a single dominant mode that contains at least 50% of the posterior probability and is one to two orders of magnitude smaller in area. This represents a substantial advantage for multimessenger follow-up campaigns.

</details>


### [105] [The presence and order of QCD phase transitions from gravitational-wave observations of neutron star mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27287)
*Paul D. Lasky,Alexander Clevinger,Andre G. da Silva,Ricardo L. S. Farias,Veronica Dexheimer*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅利用双中子星并合旋近阶段（inspiral）引力波观测来检验中子星核心相变（phase transition）是否存在及其阶数（order）的形式化框架，并在GW170817、GW190425实测数据及A+、A#、Cosmic Explorer模拟注入上验证其能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星并合的引力波观测可探测核物质状态方程（nuclear equation of state）的高密度区域，需要一种无需依赖电磁对应体、仅凭引力波旋近信号即可判定核心相变存在性与阶数的方法。

Method: 构建检验中子星核心相变存在性与阶数的形式化框架（formalism），并应用于GW170817与GW190425数据；同时对A+、A#及Cosmic Explorer升级下的合成注入（synthetic injections）进行测试。

Result: A+或A#下的并合族群可在累计观测时长不足一个博士周期（不到半个美国博士周期）内测得一级相变（first-order phase transition）的存在；但仅凭旋近信号无法区分无相变族群与二级、三级相变族群，或转变密度（transition density）较大的一级相变族群。

Conclusion: 仅利用旋近阶段引力波观测即可在下一代探测器时代高效判定一级相变的存在性，但对高阶相变及高转变密度情形缺乏区分能力，表明该方法的检验范围存在明确边界。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave observations of neutron star mergers allows us to probe the dense portion of the nuclear equation of state. We develop a formalism for testing the existence and order of phase transitions in the cores of neutron stars using only gravitational-wave observations of their inspiral. We test our formalism on data from the first binary neutron star mergers GW170817 and GW190425, and use synthetic injections assuming the next and uber-next upgrades of the LIGO observatories, A+ and A#, as well as with Cosmic Explorer. We show that populations of mergers in either A+ or A# will be able to measure the existence of a first-order phase transition in observations lasting less than one PhD (less than half a US PhD!). On the other hand, with only the inspiral, we cannot distinguish populations of neutron stars with no phase transition from those with second- or third-order phase transitions or with first-order phase transitions with relatively large transition densities.

</details>


### [106] [An improved self-gravity treatment in general relativistic smoothed particle hydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27775)
*Pritam Banerjee,Kowsona Chakraborty,Niles Mondal,Tapobrata Sarkar*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文改进广义相对论光滑粒子流体动力学（GRSPH）中流体的自引力（self-gravity）处理：在流体元局部静止系推导弱引力场并变换至全局系，取代原先的牛顿源项（Newtonian source term）。该方案在洛伦兹因子（Lorentz factor）达2时把增强多项体（boosted polytrope）自引力误差中位数控制在1%以下，并保持了双多项体系统的稳定性；应用于潮汐瓦解事件（TDE），发现自引力处理会改变瓦解阈值、残余核质量与碎片能量分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 背景黑洞（black hole, BH）上的GRSPH适用于质量远小于BH的流体，自引力只能作为附加项；但标准实现以牛顿源项加入动量方程，当流体以接近光速的显著比例运动且靠近BH时该处理不再成立，因此需要更合理的自引力处方。

Method: 在流体元各自的局部静止系（local rest frame）中推导其弱引力场（weak gravitational field），再变换到求解动量方程的全局系（global frame）。所得作用力采用静止系距离与软化长度（softening length），并计入接收粒子的时间膨胀（time dilation）及其相对源粒子的运动。该推导适用于固定BH背景上任何弱自引力流动，前提是流体体尺度远小于其到BH的距离；并在基于树（tree-based）的GRSPH代码中实现。

Result: 在增强（boosted）多项体测试中，洛伦兹因子至2时自引力中位误差低于1%；双多项体组成的双星系统在十圈轨道上保持稳定。应用于超大质量BH对类太阳恒星的TDE时，自引力处理会使瓦解阈值发生偏移，并改变残余核质量与碎片能量分布；牛顿自引力在本方案预测为近乎完全瓦解的遭遇中会留下可观的残余核。

Conclusion: 相对论性自引力处方对高速、近BH流动是必要的，牛顿近似会系统性误判潮汐瓦解的结果（残余核与碎片能量分布）。方法具有一般性，可推广至固定BH背景下其他弱自引力流动。

Abstract: General relativistic smoothed particle hydrodynamics (GRSPH) on a fixed black hole (BH) spacetime is suitable for fluid bodies that are much less massive than the BH, but the self-gravity of the fluid is added as an extra term in the equation of motion. Standard GRSPH implementations add this as a Newtonian source term to the momentum equation, which is no longer valid when the fluid moves at a sizeable fraction of the speed of light, close to the BH. We develop an improved prescription by deriving the weak gravitational field of each fluid element in its local rest frame and transforming it to the global frame where the momentum equation is solved. The resulting force uses rest-frame distances and softening lengths, accounts for the time dilation of the receiving particle, and its motion relative to the source particle. The derivation applies to any weakly self-gravitating flow on a fixed BH background, provided the body is small compared with its distance from the BH. Implemented in a tree-based GRSPH code, the prescription reproduces the self-gravity of a boosted polytrope with a median error below 1\% up to a Lorentz factor of 2, and keeps a binary of two polytropes stable over ten orbits. As an application, we study tidal disruption events (TDEs) of solar-type stars by supermassive BHs. The treatment of self-gravity shifts the disruption threshold and changes both the remnant core mass and the debris energy distribution. Newtonian self-gravity leaves substantial surviving cores in encounters that our prescription predicts to be nearly complete disruptions.

</details>


### [107] [Dynamical stability of Magnetic Supramassive Neutron Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28609)
*Jorge L. Rodríguez-Monteverde,Sebastiano Bernuzzi,William Cook,Maximilian Jacobi,Brynmor Haskell*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文利用三维全广义相对论磁流体动力学（general-relativistic magnetohydrodynamic, GRMHD）模拟，研究接近质量脱落极限（mass-shedding limit）的强磁化超大质量中子星（supramassive neutron star）的动力学稳定性。结果表明，极端磁场不稳定性会显著重塑星体结构：质量较低的星体可进入新的准稳态，而接近最大质量转折点的星体则发生引力坍缩（gravitational collapse）形成黑洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是探究极端强磁场（可达约10^17 G）对接近质量脱落极限的超大质量中子星的动力学稳定性影响，并考察其对磁星（magnetar）及长寿命双中子星并合遗迹的潜在意义；该问题此前缺乏三维全相对论磁流体模拟的系统研究。

Method: 采用三维全广义相对论磁流体动力学模拟，结合微观物理状态方程（microphysical equation of state）构建平衡构型，初始极向磁场（poloidal magnetic field）强度最高约10^17 G；演化由阿尔芬时标（Alfvén timescale）与勒纳特数（Lehnert number）所设定的时标共同支配。

Result: 初始不稳定磁场在短于自转周期的阿尔芬时标内重排为极向-环向混合构型（mixed poloidal–toroidal configuration）。质量足够低于最大质量的星体通过磁应力重新分配角动量、产生较差自转（differential rotation），形成可稳定维持数个自转周期的准稳态；赤道面外层驱动出磁风，磁化率（magnetization）≲0.2，总静止质量约10^-3 M⊙，外流率可达每秒数倍太阳质量。接近或超过最大质量转折点的星体则因角动量被移出核心而发生引力坍缩，所得黑洞残余角速度最高增大约49%，角动量减少≲8%。

Conclusion: 极端磁场不稳定性可显著改变超大质量中子星的结构，并可能对磁星及长寿命双中子星并合遗迹的演化具有启示意义；但结论基于有限模拟时标与特定初始构型设定。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamical stability of strongly magnetized supramassive neutron stars close to the mass-shedding limit using fully general-relativistic magnetohydrodynamic simulations in three spatial dimensions. Equilibrium configurations are constructed using a microphysical equation of state and considering poloidal magnetic fields up to maximum strengths ${\sim} 10^{17}\,\mathrm{G}$. The initially unstable magnetic fields reorganize into mixed poloidal--toroidal configurations on Alfvén timescales shorter than the stellar rotation period. The subsequent dynamical evolution is governed by timescales set by the Lehnert number. For stars with masses sufficiently lower than the maximum mass, magnetic stresses redistribute angular momentum and drive the development of differential rotation, leading to a new quasi-stationary configuration that remains dynamically stable over several rotation periods. Magnetically-driven winds develop from the outer layers around the equatorial plane and remain baryon-matter dominated with magnetization ${\lesssim}0.2$. Over the simulated timescale, winds carry a total rest mass of order ${\sim}10^{-3}\mathrm{M}_\odot$ at outflow rates of up to a few $M_\odot/\mathrm{s}$. For stars close to (or beyond) the maximum-mass turning point along the stellar equilibrium sequence, strong magnetic fields trigger gravitational collapse by redistributing angular momentum away from the stellar core. The resulting black hole remnants are characterized by angular velocities up to ${\sim}49\%$ larger and angular momentum ${\lesssim}8\%$ smaller than those of the initial equilibrium stars. Our results show that extreme magnetic-field instabilities can significantly alter the structure of supramassive neutron stars, with possible implications for magnetars and long-lived binary neutron-star merger remnants.

</details>
