### [108] [Quantum Fluctuations and Wigner Dynamics of a Carbon Nanotube Oscillator Driven by an Atomic Force Microscope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27167)
*Conrad Silio,Cristian Staii*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文将原子力显微镜驱动的悬浮碳纳米管基频弯曲模建模为受经典力驱动的量子布朗谐振子（quantum Brownian oscillator），在相空间中以Wigner函数的高斯传播子形式给出约化动力学，并推导出有限记忆下协方差矩阵的解析求和表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 悬浮碳纳米管是研究受控纳米机械运动（nanomechanical motion）的平台，其动力学受环境涨落、记忆效应（memory effects）与量子相干性共同影响，需要能同时处理有限记忆与量子和经典涨落的解析框架。

Method: 从广义朗之万方程（generalized Langevin equation）出发，直接在相空间表述约化动力学，Wigner函数由耗散线性输运、确定性力致位移与热浴涨落构成的高斯传播子（Gaussian propagator）演化；对Drude记忆核，延迟响应由特征三次方程的three roots表示，并在经典噪声极限下将有限时间协方差矩阵的三个独立分量化为对这些响应极点的有限求和，进而推广至包含零点与低温涨落的完整量子涨落谱（热频率级数表示）。

Result: 获得避免数值双时协方差积分的协方差矩阵显式有限求和形式，并得到保留零点涨落与低温涨落的热频率级数表示；对初始双波包叠加态，分析了干涉衰减与Wigner函数负值积分这一非经典性（nonclassicality）相空间度量。

Conclusion: 研究为束缚振子的有限记忆量子布朗运动提供了显式解析框架，并给出可用于未来量子态制备（quantum state preparation）与量子层析（quantum tomography）实验的、可处理的驱动碳纳米管相空间描述。

Abstract: Suspended carbon nanotubes provide a useful platform for studying controlled nanomechanical motion in regimes where environmental fluctuations, memory effects, and quantum coherence can all influence the dynamics. We model the fundamental flexural mode of a carbon nanotube driven by an atomic force microscope as a harmonic quantum Brownian oscillator subject to a prescribed classical force and coupled linearly to a thermal environment. Starting from the generalized Langevin equation, we formulate the reduced dynamics directly in phase space, where the Wigner function evolves through a Gaussian propagator determined by dissipative linear transport, a deterministic force-induced displacement, and bath-induced fluctuations. For a Drude memory kernel, the retarded response is expressed through the three roots of a characteristic cubic equation. In the classical-noise limit, the three independent entries of the finite-time covariance matrix reduce to explicit finite sums over these response poles, eliminating numerical double-time covariance integration. We extend the construction to the full quantum fluctuation spectrum, obtaining a thermal frequency series representation that retains zero-point and low-temperature fluctuations. For an initial two-packet superposition, we examine interference attenuation and the integrated negative part of the Wigner function, which provides a phase space measure of nonclassicality. These results provide an explicit analytical framework for finite-memory quantum Brownian motion of bound oscillators and a tractable phase space description of driven carbon nanotube dynamics relevant to future quantum state preparation and quantum tomography experiments.

</details>


### [109] [Rényi multi-entropy of graph states and the $n -> 1$ limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27080)
*Sriram Akella,Abhijit Gadde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子比特图态（qubit graph states）上Rényi多熵（Rényi multi-entropy）的复本收缩（replica contraction），证明在奇数整数复本数n处收缩等于2^{-k/2}，其中k为有限域上矩阵秩之和；并定义"碰撞受控（collision-controlled）"图类使秩和为n的多项式，从而给出n→1的解析延拓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rényi多熵将Rényi熵推广到多于两方的纯态，在全息（holography）中其n→1极限被猜想对应体内最小皂膜网络（minimal soap-film web）的面积。然而一般态的复本收缩不是密度矩阵的矩，n=1延拓需另寻方法，这构成研究的核心困难。

Method: 以量子比特图态为对象，将复本收缩表示为有限域（finite field）上逐量子比特的行/列矩阵秩之和k，得到奇数n处的收缩值；并定义碰撞受控（collision-controlled）图类，使秩和k成为n的多项式，进而用自然的n→1多项式延拓，化为对各方划分的有限求和。

Result: 该图类包含无偶环图与所有完全二部图（complete bipartite graphs）。在任意奇数n>1下Rényi多熵在单量子比特Pauli测量下非增；但n=1延拓不具备此性质——对2r+1≥7的奇数环进行单量子比特Z测量，多熵由(r+1)log2升至(2r-1)log2。

Conclusion: 该n→1延拓在Pauli测量下可增大，故不构成纠缠单调量（entanglement monotone），提示多熵的全息延拓与一般态延拓在单调性上存在本质差异。

Abstract: Rényi multi-entropy extends the Rényi entropy to pure states of more than two parties. It is defined by a replica contraction at integer replica number $n$, and the multi-entropy is its $n\to1$ limit. In holography this limit is conjectured to compute the area of a minimal soap-film web in the bulk. Understanding multi-entropy for general states, however, is harder. Unlike the bipartite case, the replica contraction is not a moment of a density matrix, and the continuation to $n=1$ has to be found by other means. We study this problem for qubit graph states. We show that at every odd $n$ the contraction equals $2^{-k/2}$, where $k$ is a sum of ranks of matrices over a finite field, one row and one column per qubit. We define a class of graphs that we call collision-controlled, for which the rank sum is a polynomial in $n$. This class includes graphs without even cycles and all complete bipartite graphs. The natural polynomial $n \to 1$ continuation is a finite sum over partitions of the parties. While the Rényi multi-entropy is non-increasing under single-qubit Pauli measurements at every odd integer $n>1$, the continuation to $n=1$ is not. A $Z$ measurement on one qubit of an odd ring with $2r + 1 \geq 7$ qubits raises it from $(r+1)\log 2$ to $(2r-1) \log 2$. Thus the continuation is not an entanglement monotone.

</details>


### [110] [Relativistic Strong Quantum Mpemba Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28017)
*Zhilong Liu,Zehua Tian,Hui Jing,Jieci Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作证明在Unruh热化中可存在相对论性强量子Mpemba效应：当加速度超过阈值时，原本不相交的等谱初态弛豫曲线发生Mpemba交叉，并在精细调节的加速度下使"更远"初态获得更快渐近指数弛豫率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Mpemba效应（Mpemba effect）从非相对论开放量子系统推广到相对论情境，借Unruh效应（Unruh effect）把加速度与热化联系，探索加速度能否作为调控反常量子弛豫（anomalous quantum relaxation）的参数。

Method: 研究耦合无质量标量场（massless scalar field）的两能级探测器（two-level detector）在Unruh热化中的弛豫行为；分析跃迁与退相位通道（transition and dephasing channels）竞争对弛豫谱的支配作用，推导谱切换条件（spectral-switching condition），并构造等谱初态制备协议（isospectral preparation protocols）。

Result: 对固定的一对等谱初态（isospectral initial states），惯性运动下互不相交的弛豫曲线在超过阈值加速度后出现Mpemba交叉；在更高且精细调节的加速度下，初始更远态与最慢衰减模式零重叠，从而以更快的渐近指数率弛豫。足够强的纵向耦合可使加速度在布居与相干模式之间切换弛豫瓶颈。

Conclusion: 加速度可作为反常量子弛豫的控制参数，将相对论量子动力学与非平衡热力学（nonequilibrium thermodynamics）相联系；制备协议还揭示在固定纯度下相干性对慢模式重叠的抑制或增强取决于底层谱结构。

Abstract: The Unruh effect links acceleration to thermalization, providing a relativistic setting for anomalous quantum relaxation. We demonstrate a {\it relativistic} strong quantum Mpemba effect in the Unruh thermalization of a two-level detector coupled to a massless scalar field. For a fixed pair of isospectral initial states, relaxation curves that never cross in inertial motion exhibit a Mpemba crossing above a threshold acceleration. At a higher, precisely tuned acceleration, the initially farther state has zero overlap with the slowest decay mode and relaxes with a faster asymptotic exponential rate. We identify how competition between transition and dephasing channels governs the relaxation spectrum, where sufficiently strong longitudinal coupling enables acceleration to switch the relaxation bottleneck between population and coherence modes. We derive the spectral-switching condition and construct isospectral preparation protocols that eliminate slow-mode contributions within their physical domains. These protocols reveal how coherence can suppress or enhance slow-mode overlap at fixed purity, depending on the underlying spectral structure. Our results establish acceleration as a control parameter for anomalous quantum relaxation, connecting relativistic quantum dynamics with nonequilibrium thermodynamics.

</details>


### [111] [Stabilizer Rényi moments beyond entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27596)
*Vishnu Jejjala,Joydeep Naskar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究临界量子链中稳定子 Rényi 矩（stabilizer Rényi moments）的空间衰减，指出其依赖算符基选择而不由纠缠谱决定，并借助二维共形场论（2D CFT）给出基准与选择规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定子 Rényi 矩依赖算符基（operator basis）选择，且未必由纠缠谱（entanglement spectrum）决定，其空间衰减行为缺乏理论刻画，需要建立与共形场论对应的分析框架。

Method: 针对由二维共形场论（two-dimensional conformal field theory）描述的临界量子链，推导纯稳定子态（pure stabilizer states）约化态的精确纠缠基准，以及固定支撑（fixed support）上带电贡献的对称性选择规则（symmetry selection rule）；并对指标二（index two）的 Ising 区间给出精确 Pfaffian 表示，与候选共形边界（conformal boundary）计算比较。

Result: 精确 Ising 实例显示，单格点旋转（on-site rotation）会改变整体衰减指数（decay exponent），却保持所有子系统的纠缠谱不变；指标二增长 Ising 区间的带电振幅与候选共形边界预言数据相符。

Conclusion: 数值数据支持共形边界预言，但将其等同于晶格极限（lattice limit）仍需要端点匹配（endpoint matching）与极限次序（order of limits）的控制。

Abstract: Stabilizer Rényi moments depend on a choice of operator basis and need not be determined by entanglement spectra. In this paper, we study their spatial decay in critical quantum chains described by two-dimensional conformal field theories. We derive an exact entanglement baseline for reductions of pure stabilizer states and a symmetry selection rule for the charged contribution at fixed support. An exact Ising example shows that an on-site rotation changes the full decay exponent while preserving every subsystem entanglement spectrum. For growing Ising intervals at index two, we obtain an exact Pfaffian representation and compare the charged amplitude with a candidate conformal boundary calculation. The data support this prediction, but its identification with the lattice limit still requires endpoint matching and control of the order of limits.

</details>


### [112] [Complete Modified Logarithmic Sobolev Inequalities for one-dimensional quantum Spin Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28600)
*Li Gao,Jingyu Guo,Sihao Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了一维自旋链上Chen–Kastoryano–Gilyén Gibbs采样器在任意固定正温度下满足常数与系统尺寸无关的完整修正对数Sobolev不等式（CMLSI），并由此得到O(log N + log(1/ε))量级的trace与diamond混合时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gibbs采样器的混合时间是否随系统尺寸N增长而恶化，是量子与经典采样算法的核心问题；已有分析通常难以给出与系统尺寸无关的常数，尤其对非对易、空间非均匀的相互作用缺乏保证。

Method: 针对有界有限程相互作用（含非对易与非均匀情形）的一维自旋链，通过建立完整空间熵因子化（complete spatial entropy factorization），并将准局域误差（quasi-local errors）吸收为原始联合态与参考态下的条件相对熵（conditional relative entropy）。

Result: 所证CMLSI常数与系统尺寸无关，直接导出trace与diamond混合时间均为O(log N + log(1/ε))；静态估计还给出单站点Petz热浴（Petz heat-bath）及正则化热浴动力学的尺寸无关CMLSI。

Conclusion: 结果表明一维有限程自旋链上的Gibbs采样器具有接近最优的对数混合时间，空间熵因子化与条件相对熵吸收是获得尺寸无关常数的关键技术，并可推广至单站点热浴动力学。

Abstract: We prove a complete modified logarithmic Sobolev inequality with a system-size-independent constant for Chen--Kastoryano--Gilyén Gibbs samplers on one-dimensional spin chain at every fixed positive temperature. The result holds for Hamiltonians with bounded finite-range interactions, including noncommuting and spatially nonuniform interactions. It yields trace and diamond mixing times of order $O(\log N+\log(1/ε))$. The proof establishes complete spatial entropy factorization and absorbs quasi-local errors as conditional relative entropy at the original joint state and reference. The static estimate also yields a system-size-independent CMLSI for the single-site Petz heat-bath and regularised heat-bath dynamics.

</details>


### [113] [Systematic Truncated Wigner Approximation for Open Spin Systems Reveals Long-Range Order Far from Equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27462)
*Igor Timofeev,Dawid A. Hryniuk,Marzena H. Szymańska*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出连续的 SU(2) 协变截断 Wigner（truncated-Wigner）框架，可将 Lindblad 型耗散自旋问题映射为每格点一个 Bloch 球的随机动力学，并在 400 格点二维长程相互作用各向异性 XY 晶格上给出超越平均场的稳态相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程相互作用（long-range interactions）有可能保护耗散序（dissipative order）抵抗涨落，但二维情形下现有方法无法判定其成立条件，因此需要可扩展的非微扰方法。

Method: 从 Lindblad 方程的精确 Stratonovich-Weyl 演化系统性导出 SU(2) 协变截断 Wigner 框架，对任意自旋 S 同时生成漂移项与库噪声；并以精确解与张量网络（tensor-network）计算作为基准检验。

Result: 在 400 格点二维耗散各向异性 XY 晶格上，最近邻耦合下涡-反涡（vortex-antivortex）增殖破坏预测的 stagger-XY 相，而代数衰减相互作用稳定出扩展的 Z2 有序相；得到首个超越平均场的稳态相图，显示强耦合下序的重入性（reentrant）丧失，以及 α≈3.5 之外有序区关闭。

Conclusion: 该方法为长程相互作用、高维几何、自旋-玻色耦合及 S>1/2 的强关联驱动耗散系统提供可扩展路径，并与光晶格与光镊阵列中磁性原子、极性分子的实验相关。

Abstract: Long-range interactions can protect dissipative order against fluctuations, but determining when they do so in two dimensions remains out of reach for existing many-body methods. We develop a continuous SU(2)-covariant truncated-Wigner framework that maps the problem onto stochastic dynamics with one Bloch sphere per site. Derived systematically from the exact Stratonovich-Weyl evolution of the Lindblad equation, it generates both drift and reservoir noise for arbitrary spin $S$. Benchmarks against exact and tensor-network calculations validate its description of non-equilibrium dynamics and steady states. Applied to 400-site two-dimensional dissipative anisotropic XY lattices, our method reveals physics missed by mean-field and perturbative approaches: vortex-antivortex proliferation destroys the predicted staggered-XY phase for nearest-neighbour coupling, whereas algebraically decaying interactions stabilize an extended $\mathbb{Z}_2$-ordered phase. We obtain the first beyond-mean-field steady-state phase diagram of the long-range interacting model, revealing reentrant loss of order at strong coupling and closure of the ordered region beyond $α\approx 3.5$. Our new method provides a scalable route to strongly correlated driven-dissipative systems with long-range interactions, higher-dimensional geometries, spin-boson couplings, and spins beyond one half, relevant to modern experiments with magnetic atoms and polar molecules in optical lattices and tweezer arrays.

</details>


### [114] [Hybrid Lattice Surgery between Smooth Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27556)
*Grace Ding,Yanzhu Chen,Sheng-Jie Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出面向群曲面码（group surface codes）的平滑混合晶格手术（smooth hybrid lattice surgery），通过沿平滑边界实施合并与拆分操作实现联合逻辑测量，并证明其对应逻辑 K-陪集测量（logical K-coset measurement）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通曲面码（surface codes）的联合逻辑宇称测量（joint logical parity measurement）已成熟，但面向群曲面码、尤其是非阿贝尔群（non-Abelian groups）情形下的平滑边界混合晶格手术及其可实现的逻辑操作范围尚待拓展。

Method: 采用张量网络表示（tensor-network representation）推导显式测量电路，并给出非恒等测量结果（nonidentity measurement outcomes）的纠错流程；框架可处理相同或不同的有限群（finite groups）码块。

Result: 核心结论为平滑合并与拆分实现逻辑 K-陪集测量，其中 K 为定义界面的子群（subgroup）；并以群曲面码间的广义受控乘法门（generalized controlled multiplication gate）及 T、CZ、CCX、CS 魔态（magic states）制备协议为例，输出编码于一个或多个 Z₂ 曲面码块中的资源态。

Conclusion: 平滑混合晶格手术拓宽了群曲面码可用的逻辑操作范围，将普通曲面码的联合逻辑宇称测量推广至更一般的群结构与非阿贝尔情形。

Abstract: We develop smooth hybrid lattice surgery for group surface codes, enabling joint logical measurements through merge and split operations along smooth boundaries. The code patches may correspond to identical or different finite groups, including non-Abelian groups. Our central result is that smooth merge and split realize a logical $K$-coset measurement, where $K$ is the subgroup defining the interface. This generalizes the joint logical parity measurement of ordinary surface codes. Using a tensor-network representation, we derive explicit measurement circuits and develop procedures for correcting nonidentity measurement outcomes. We illustrate the framework through a generalized controlled multiplication gate between group surface codes and protocols for preparing the $T$, $CZ$, $CCX$, and $CS$ magic states, producing resources encoded in one or more $\mathbb{Z}_2$ surface code patches. Our results show how smooth hybrid lattice surgery broadens the range of logical operations available for group surface codes.

</details>


### [115] [Fate of Discrete Time Crystals in Finite-Size Cavity QED Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27589)
*Haimi Nguyen,David R. Reichman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在有限原子数的开放 Dicke 模型中，非平均场涨落导致瞬态离散时间晶体（DTC）的衰减率与振幅随原子数呈非单调变化；随机截断 Wigner 近似（TWA）能准确刻画有限尺寸动力学，并揭示三种动力学区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究仅在热力学极限（thermodynamic limit，N→∞）下预言周期驱动开放 Dicke 模型可出现离散时间晶体（Discrete Time Crystal, DTC）行为，但有限原子系综中的有限尺寸效应尚不清楚，需澄清其对实验可观测性的影响。

Method: 作者分析有限 N 下非平均场（non-mean-field）涨落对瞬态 DTC 的影响，并比较二阶累积量展开（second-order cumulant expansion）、随机截断 Wigner 近似（Truncated Wigner Approximation, TWA）与蒙特卡洛波函数方法（Monte Carlo Wave Function, MCWF）的适用性。

Result: 瞬态 DTC 的衰减率与振幅随原子数增加呈非平凡的非单调行为；二阶累积量展开无法捕捉有限尺寸动力学，而随机 TWA 在大系综极限下尤为准确；据此识别出三种不同的瞬态 DTC 动力学区间。

Conclusion: 论文阐明了有限尺寸 DTC 序非单调动力学衰减的机制，并讨论其对有限尺寸原子系综实验的意义。

Abstract: It has been shown that Discrete Time Crystal (DTC) behavior may arise in the periodically driven, open Dicke model in the thermodynamic limit ($N \rightarrow \infty$). In this work, we demonstrate that finite $N$ non-mean-field fluctuations in this model can lead to decay rates and amplitudes of transient DTCs that display a non-trivial non-monotonic behavior with an increasing number of atoms. We show that the second-order cumulant expansion fails to capture the finite-size dynamics, while a stochastic version of the Truncated Wigner Approximation (TWA) is remarkably accurate, particularly in the large atom ensemble limit. Using the TWA and the Monte Carlo Wave Function (MCWF) approach, we identify three distinct dynamical regimes of transient DTC behavior in the finite-size Dicke model. We outline the mechanism behind the non-monotonic finite size dynamical decay of DTC order and discuss the implications for experiments performed on finite-sized atomic assemblies.

</details>


### [116] [Local-Channel Stability of Classical $\mathbb Z_2$ Lattice Gauge Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27939)
*Guanxi Xun,Weiguang Cao,Haruki Watanabe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文为定义在 $d$ 维格点 $m$ 维胞腔自旋上的经典 $\mathbb Z_2$ 格点规范模型（lattice gauge model）建立了双向局域量子信道稳定性（two-way local quantum-channel stability）的充分条件。结果表明：在无内部或规范对称性要求下，足够弱的局域微扰在低正温度下保持与正则平坦系综（canonical flat ensemble）等价，而足够高温态则与乘积态双向等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画无保护对称性时格点规范模型低温柔性（stability）的鲁棒性：该模型族在完整自旋希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space）上定义、不施加高斯定律约束（Gauss-law constraint），涵盖 Ising 模型（$m=0$）与环面码（toric code）磁扇区（$m=1$），需明确何种条件下其低温柔性在弱局域微扰下依然成立。

Method: 低温柔性证明依赖两个要素：局域不可区分的平坦扇区（locally indistinguishable flat sectors）与维数为 $d-m-1$ 的延展磁缺陷（extended magnetic defects）。对弱非对易（noncommuting）微扰，先将对角概率约化为弱经典相互作用，再用局域信道恢复相干性（coherences）；所用转换信道采用乘积辅助输入，具有多对数因果半径（polylogarithmic causal radius）。此外引入两个平行 $m$-环面远端邻域间的最优协方差（covariance）作为判据。

Result: 对 $1\le m\le d-2$，足够弱的局域经典微扰在足够低的正温度下保持与正则平坦系综等价，且不要求微扰具备内部或规范对称性；对弱非对易微扰，在更小的充分窗口内得到相同结论，并达到任意预设的幂次逆全系统迹范数误差（trace-norm error）。足够高温态则与乘积态双向等价。最优协方差在单向局域转换下不增、在双向转换下不变，其取值 $\mathcal F_m=1$ 与 $0$ 可在无保护对称性时区分所证的低温和高温区域。

Conclusion: 该族模型在弱局域微扰下具有低温柔性，且无需内部或规范对称性保护；协方差判据 $\mathcal F_m$ 可区分所证明的低温和高温域。作者明确指出这些充分条件域并不决定完整相图（phase diagram），所得结论仅为充分性刻画。

Abstract: We establish sufficient conditions for two-way local quantum-channel stability in classical $\mathbb Z_2$ lattice gauge models with spins on $m$-dimensional cells of a $d$-dimensional lattice. Each interaction multiplies the spins on the boundary of an $(m+1)$-cell. This family, defined on the full spin Hilbert space without a Gauss-law constraint, includes the Ising model at $m=0$ and the magnetic sector of the toric code at $m=1$. The low-temperature proof combines two ingredients: locally indistinguishable flat sectors and extended magnetic defects of dimension $d-m-1$. For $1\le m\le d-2$, sufficiently weak local classical perturbations at sufficiently low positive temperature preserve equivalence to the canonical flat ensemble. No internal or gauge symmetry is required of the perturbation. For weak noncommuting perturbations, we prove the same conclusion in a smaller sufficient window by reducing the diagonal probabilities to a weak classical interaction and recovering the coherences with a local channel. The conversion channels use product ancillary inputs, have polylogarithmic causal radius, and achieve any prescribed inverse-power whole-system trace-norm error. Sufficiently high-temperature states are instead two-way equivalent to a product state. Independent finite-dimensional onsite systems can be included with weak local couplings. An optimized covariance between two distant neighborhoods of parallel $m$-tori is nonincreasing under one-way local conversion and invariant under two-way conversion. Its values $\mathcal F_m=1$ and $0$ distinguish the proved low- and high-temperature domains without a protecting symmetry. These sufficient domains do not determine the full phase diagram.

</details>


### [117] [Microscopically informed self-consistent multi-resolvent closure for dense many-body spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28031)
*Zhiqiang Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出多预解自洽方程（multi-resolvent self-consistency, MRSC），将稠密多体谱的多预解层级（multi-resolvent hierarchy）严格划分为精确代数层级与有限参数近似闭合两部分，并在自由费米子与相互作用 Ising 链上验证其预测性闭合能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密多体谱的多预解层级（multi-resolvent hierarchy）编码高阶谱权重关联，但对相互作用哈密顿量（interacting Hamiltonian）难以预测性地闭合该层级。

Method: 借助精确的 Feshbach 几何恒等式（Feshbach geometric identity）将 r-预解累积量组织为不可约顶点 Gamma^(r) 的阶梯，并以投影层级将顶点表示为累积量的泛函；闭合在有限参数下进行：连通基（connectivity basis）把一般哈密顿量的种子归为类振幅，因果 r 重 Stieltjes 表示（causal r-fold Stieltjes representation）结合矩场恒等式将矩匹配化为有限维、在预测性不动点处闭合的问题。

Result: 自由费米子（free-fermion）基准上重现所测精确恒等式，并在 r=1 处恢复 Faddeeva 闭合；在隐藏精确双预解数据的相互作用 Ising 链盲测中，由哈密顿量导出的闭合对测试探针预测误差为 0.35%–0.97%；精确双预解部分对非对角本征态热化假设（ETH）方差给出符号正确、能量分辨的修正，量级约为单预解基线的一阶。

Conclusion: 闭合误差可层级化组织，包含逐能级亏欠分解、可隔离项锚定测量与显式传播界（propagation bound）；同一组矩还给出局域旋转不变性窗口条件的诊断链（漂移核恒等式、单态可辨识边界、可迁移性普查），构成有限阶框架而非普适涌现的证明。

Abstract: The exact multi-resolvent hierarchy of dense many-body spectra encodes higher-order spectral-weight correlations, but closing it predictively for interacting Hamiltonians remains a challenge. We introduce the multi-resolvent self-consistency (MRSC) equations, which separate an exact algebraic hierarchy from an approximate finite-parameter closure. The exact Feshbach geometric identity organizes r-resolvent cumulants into a ladder over irreducible vertices Gamma^(r), and the projected hierarchy expresses those vertices as functionals of the cumulants. The closure works in finite parameters: a connectivity basis organizes a general Hamiltonian's seed into class amplitudes, and a causal r-fold Stieltjes representation with a moment-field identity reduce moment matching to a finite-dimensional problem closed at a predictive fixed point. On the free-fermion benchmark the tested exact identities are reproduced and the Faddeeva closure is recovered at r=1. In a blind test on the interacting Ising chain, with the exact two-resolvent data withheld, the Hamiltonian-derived closure predicts the tested probes to 0.35%-0.97%. At the observable level the exact two-resolvent sector supplies a sign-correct, energy-resolved correction to the single-resolvent baseline for the off-diagonal eigenstate-thermalization-hypothesis (ETH) variance, of order one relative to it. Closure errors organize hierarchically: a level-by-level deficit decomposition, anchor measurements of the isolatable terms, and an explicit propagation bound. The same moments further yield a diagnostic chain for the window conditions of local rotational invariance -- an exact drift-kernel identity, a proven single-state identifiability boundary, and a transferability census over couplings and realizations -- a finite-order framework, not a proof of universal emergence.

</details>


### [118] [Dynamical Mean-Field Theory for Real Materials using Variational Quantum Compressed Time Evolution on a Quantum Computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28231)
*Stefan Wolf,Johannes Selisko,Timo Eckstein,Ludwig Nützel,Maximilian Amsler,Christopher Wever,Georgy Samsonidze,Thomas Eckl,Michael J. Hartmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作构建了结合密度泛函理论与动力学平均场的混合量子-经典计算流程，并在 IBM Heron 量子设备上以变分量子压缩时间演化作为杂质求解器，成功分辨出 CaCuO2 谱函数的特征结构，但发现完整 DMFT 自洽所需精度超出当前 NISQ 硬件能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标是探索高温铜氧化物超导体母体结构 CaCuO2 的电子性质，并检验量子算法作为杂质求解器在真实材料 DMFT 计算中的可行性。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（density functional theory）与动力学平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory, DMFT）相结合的混合量子-经典流程；以基于变分量子压缩时间演化（variational quantum compressed time evolution）的量子算法作为杂质求解器（impurity solver），在 IBM Heron 量子设备上计算含时格林函数（time-dependent Green's function），并结合误差缓解（error mitigation）技术与 Lehmann 表示拟合（Lehmann representation fitting）从可及时间区间信号中提取态密度（density of states）。

Result: 尽管当前可及 NISQ 设备存在残余不精确性带来的显著挑战，仍成功分辨出谱函数（spectral function）的特征结构；但完整 DMFT 自洽（self-consistency）所需的数值精度超出当前硬件能力。

Conclusion: 面向真实材料的实用化 DMFT 应用需依赖后 NISQ（post-NISQ）硬件的发展；当前结果为量子辅助材料电子结构计算提供了阶段性验证。

Abstract: We design a hybrid quantum-classical computational workflow, combining density functional theory and dynamical mean-field theory, to investigate the electronic properties of CaCuO2, a parent structure of high-temperature cuprate superconductors. Our approach uses a quantum algorithm as an impurity solver that is based on variational quantum compressed time evolution to compute the time-dependent Greens function on an IBM Heron quantum device. Utilizing error mitigation techniques and a Lehmann representation fitting procedure, we extract the density of states from the signal in the accessible time regime. Despite the significant challenges rooted in the residual imprecision of currently accessible NISQ devices, we successfully resolve the characteristic features of the spectral function. However, we find that the required numerical accuracy for full DMFT self-consistency exceeds current hardware capabilities, indicating that practical real-material DMFT applications will necessitate post-NISQ hardware developments.

</details>


### [119] [Floquet ground state order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28336)
*Suman Mondal,Paul M. Schindler,Marin Bukov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出并计算周期驱动相互作用系统的"Floquet 基态"（Floquet ground state），证明其可承载稳定的量子有序（包括平衡态型序与本征非平衡的"基态时间晶体"），并由有限平均能隙保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 干净、相互作用的周期驱动系统通常被认为会热化到无特征的无限温态，从而排除稳定有序态的形成；作者希望寻找区别于预热化序（prethermal order）的真正稳定量子序及其相变。

Method: 将时间演化分解为几何（geometric）与平均能量（average-energy）动力学，运用几何 Floquet 理论（Geometric Floquet theory）定义 Floquet 基态；推导一般变分原理以在大规模相互作用驱动系统中计算该基态，并据此发展矩阵乘积态（matrix product state）算法，对超过 100 个相互作用自旋的非可积链进行数值模拟。

Result: 数值模拟显示在广泛参数区间存在有能隙、面积律纠缠（area-law entangled）的 Floquet 基态；其基态流形可支持缀饰的平衡序（如 Floquet 反铁磁体）与本征非平衡序（如基态时间晶体）。该量子序受有限平均能隙保护，改变参数时能隙在相变点闭合。

Conclusion: Floquet 物相可分解为平衡与非平衡要素：固定时刻的 Floquet 基态代表某一平衡物相，非平衡内容完全由驱动的完整群（drive holonomy）几何地产生；作者进一步指出准局域反绝热驱动（quasilocal counterdiabatic driving）是有能隙 Floquet 基态抗加热稳定性的机制，并论证其规避本征态热化假说（Eigenstate Thermalization Hypothesis）。

Abstract: Clean, interacting, periodically driven systems are believed to thermalize to featureless, infinite-temperature states that preclude the formation of stable ordered states. Here, we show stable quantum order, distinct from prethermal order, and its phase transitions in the ground state of the average-energy operator. Decomposing the time evolution into geometric and average-energy dynamics, we apply Geometric Floquet theory to identify the Floquet ground state. We derive a general variational principle to compute the Floquet ground state in large interacting periodically driven systems, and use it to develop a matrix product state algorithm. In driven nonintegrable chains of more than 100 interacting spins, our numerical simulations exhibit a gapped, area-law entangled Floquet ground state in extended parameter regimes; the ground-state manifold can support both dressed equilibrium order (e.g., Floquet antiferromagnets) and inherently nonequilibrium order (e.g., ground-state time crystals). This quantum order is protected by a finite average-energy gap, which closes at phase transition points upon changing model parameters. We decompose Floquet phases of matter into their equilibrium and nonequilibrium elements: whereas the Floquet ground state at any fixed time is a representative of an equilibrium phase of matter, the nonequilibrium content is entirely produced by the drive holonomy and is geometric. Finally, we identify quasilocal counterdiabatic driving as a mechanism for the stability against heating of gapped Floquet ground states in nonintegrable quantum systems, and argue why Floquet ground states evade the Eigenstate Thermalization Hypothesis. Our work enables the study of interacting nonequilibrium matter deep into the many-body regime, and lays the foundations for the engineering of strongly correlated ordered quantum states.

</details>


### [120] [Capturing Open Quantum System Dynamics with Compact Dressed Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28358)
*Zachary Giles,Dvira Segal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作推进了紧凑缀饰哈密顿量（compact dressed Hamiltonian, CDH）框架，用于模拟强耦合于结构化玻色环境（structured bosonic environments）的开放量子系统动力学，在紧凑希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space）中高效复现参考动力学并可直接获取刘维尔谱（Liouvillian spectrum）与慢弛豫模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强耦合开放量子系统中，环境结构化使传统方法计算代价高昂；需要一种在保留强系统-环境杂化（system-environment hybridization）物理的同时显著压缩状态空间的描述方式。

Method: CDH将反应坐标映射（reaction-coordinate mapping）与极化子变换（polaron transformation）结合，把强耦合杂化纳入缀饰表示（dressed representation）后再截断集体玻色模，残余环境用弱耦合马尔可夫量子主方程（Markovian quantum master equation）处理。

Result: 在单自旋、耦合自旋与自旋系综（spin ensembles）的spin-boson型模型中，涵盖共振与非共振区、弱至强耦合，与层级运动方程（hierarchical equations of motion, HEOM）及收敛反应坐标计算对比显示：振荡频率、弛豫行为与长时布居被准确复现，并捕获浴介导相互作用、自旋同步（synchronization）及集体发射特征。

Conclusion: CDH可在紧凑希尔伯特空间中系统趋近参考动力学，且刘维尔慢模在CDH表示下快速收敛，无需拟合含时观测量即可识别弛豫时间尺度，为强耦合开放量子系统提供高效且具物理性的描述。

Abstract: We advance the compact dressed Hamiltonian (CDH) framework to simulate the dynamics of open quantum systems strongly coupled to structured bosonic environments. The CDH approach combines reaction-coordinate mapping with a polaron transformation, incorporating strong system-environment hybridization into a dressed representation before truncating the collective bosonic mode. As a result of this truncation, dynamical features are captured with substantially fewer states than in the original representation. The residual environment is treated using a weak-coupling Markovian quantum master equation. We benchmark the method for spin-boson-type models including a single spin, coupled spins, and spin ensembles, considering both resonant and off-resonant regimes and weak to strong system-bath coupling. Comparisons with hierarchical equations of motion and converged reaction-coordinate calculations demonstrate that the CDH representation systematically approaches the reference dynamics with a compact Hilbert space, accurately reproducing oscillation frequencies, relaxation behavior, and long-time populations over a broad parameter regime. The method further captures cooperative phenomena, including bath-mediated interactions, synchronization between spins, and signatures for collective emission. Beyond efficient time propagation, the CDH framework provides direct access to the spectrum and eigenmodes of the Liouvillian. We demonstrate that slow dynamical modes converge rapidly in the CDH representation, enabling identification of relaxation timescales without fitting time-dependent observables. As a whole, the CDH framework provides an efficient and physical description of strongly coupled open quantum-system dynamics.

</details>


### [121] [Efficient Ground-State Manifold Amplification for Rydberg Atom Arrays via Houdayer Cluster Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28528)
*Andrew J. Ochoa,Martin J. A. Schuetz,Helmut G. Katzgraber*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出量子-经典混合方法：量子硬件提供初始解，经典后处理（Houdayer 簇更新）系统扩展解集，将量子采样的解多样性提升最高约11923倍（中位数24.3倍），并能部分甚至完全恢复基态简并流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子优化设备虽能产生高质量解，但存在根本性的采样局限（sampling limitations），对需要大量互不相关最优解的应用尤为不利，因此需要提升解的多样性与覆盖度。

Method: 采用量子-经典混合（hybrid quantum-classical）框架：以量子硬件输出最大独立集（maximum independent set）问题的初始解，再以纯经典的 Houdayer 簇更新算法（Houdayer cluster update algorithm）对解集进行后处理扩展。在 QuEra Aquila 模拟退火设备上的43个问题实例上进行验证。

Result: 多样性放大因子（给定解集规模下不同解数量的增幅）最高达11923倍，跨问题规模与难度参数的中位数为24.3倍；因这些实例的精确基态简并度（ground-state degeneracy）已知，可测得后处理中位恢复21%的基态流形，并在6个实例上可证完全恢复；部分情形下还能发现比量子硬件更大的独立集。

Conclusion: 该方法为非遍历的（non-ergodic），无法保证完整覆盖流形，但始终能发现更多高质量、有时严格更优的构型；表明简单的经典后处理可有效提升量子优化结果的实用价值。

Abstract: We demonstrate a hybrid quantum-classical approach for exploring ground-state manifolds of maximum independent set problems, in which quantum hardware supplies the initial solutions and an entirely classical post-processing algorithm expands them, increasing the solution diversity of quantum hardware measurements by up to several orders of magnitude. Quantum optimization devices, while capable of generating high-quality solutions, can suffer from fundamental sampling limitations. This is particularly problematic for applications that strongly depend on a large variety of uncorrelated optimal solutions. We apply the Houdayer cluster update algorithm to systematically expand quantum-generated solution sets through classical post-processing. We validate our approach on a test bed of 43 problem instances from QuEra's Aquila analog annealing device, achieving diversity amplification factors, defined as the increase in the number of distinct solutions at a given set size, of up to 11923x with a median of 24.3x across different problem scales and hardness parameters. Because exact ground-state degeneracies are known for these instances, the recovered fraction of the manifold can be measured rather than bounded: post-processing recovers a median of 21% of the ground-state manifold, and provably all of it for six instances. We also demonstrate that, in some cases, the classical post-processing can discover independent sets larger than those found by the quantum hardware, thus improving on solution quality and not just diversity. While the method is non-ergodic and cannot guarantee complete manifold coverage, it consistently discovers a larger number of high-quality, and in some cases strictly better, configurations than quantum sampling alone. This work demonstrates how simple classical post-processing can enhance the utility of quantum optimization results.

</details>


### [122] [Cumulative locality governs collective quantum charging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28614)
*Sebastián V. Romero,Xi Chen,Yue Ban*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明量子电池（quantum batteries）的可扩展超越广延充电（superextensive charging）不能仅靠全局算符（global operators）实现，而需要多体算符的累积层级结构（cumulative hierarchy of many-body operators），并将充电功率与对易子范数及有效参与数（participation number）关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电池可借助集体量子效应超越经典对应物，但支撑该优势所需的条件尚不明确，本文旨在厘清何种算符结构能够产生可扩展的充电优势。

Method: 针对一类通用的自由费米子充电器（free-fermionic chargers），将充电功率（charging power）表达为电池与充电器哈密顿量（Hamiltonians）对易子（commutator）的范数，并推导仅涵盖非对易项（noncommuting terms）的上界；同时重新定义参与数以反映各贡献项的相对强度。

Result: 全局算符不足以实现可扩展的超越广延充电，需依赖多体算符的累积层级；电池自身结构决定优势程度，且有效参与数与充电功率的标度行为（scaling）存在关联。

Conclusion: 结论不限于二次哈密顿量（quadratic Hamiltonians），确立了累积局域性（cumulative locality）作为量子充电优势的关键资源。

Abstract: Quantum batteries can outperform their classical counterparts through collective quantum effects, yet the conditions enabling such advantage remain unclear. Using a generic class of free-fermionic chargers we prove that global operators are insufficient for scalable superextensive charging and that a cumulative hierarchy of many-body operators is required, with the structure of the battery determining the degree of advantage. This is obtained by relating the charging power to the norm of the commutator between the battery and charger Hamiltonians and deriving an upper bound only capturing noncommuting terms. Moreover, we revisit the concept of participation number and propose an effective definition that accounts for the relative strength of the contributing terms, correlating it with the charging power scaling. Not limited to quadratic Hamiltonians, our results establish cumulative locality as a key resource for quantum charging advantage.

</details>

### [123] [Ammonia-Hydrogen Phase Equilibria in Planetary Envelopes from First-Principles-Based Machine-Learning Molecular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27136)
*Akash Gupta,Austin Guo,Jie Deng*

Main category: astro-ph.EP

TL;DR: 该研究利用基于密度泛函理论训练的机器学习原子间势，对氨-氢体系开展大规模两相共存模拟，绘制了0.1–100 GPa、500–4000 K范围内的共存组成与临界曲线，并据此讨论了天王星、海王星及暖亚海王星中氨-氢的混溶行为与行星演化含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氨与氢是太阳系内外行星的重要组成成分，但二者在行星包层（planetary envelopes）中如何相互作用尚不清楚，缺乏宽温压范围内的相行为数据。

Method: 采用基于密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算训练的机器学习原子间势（machine-learning interatomic potentials），在500–4000 K、0.1–100 GPa条件下进行大规模两相共存（two-phase coexistence）模拟，确定富氢相与富氨相的共存组成及临界曲线（critical curve）。

Result: 临界温度随压力升高由0.1 GPa时的500 K增至约30 GPa时的2000 K；在3000 K与4000 K下直至100 GPa均未发现相分离（phase separation）。临界曲线斜率在0.3–30 GPa间显著下降，在约30 GPa、2000 K以上趋于平坦，此处分子的作用开始让位于原子物种。共存曲线呈非对称性，临界组成偏向富氨。

Conclusion: 对预期包层组成而言，相边界接近并穿越已发表的天王星与海王星热剖面（thermal profiles），暗示氨可能正在这两颗行星中“降雨”；而在TOI-270 d等暖亚海王星（warm sub-Neptunes）中，氨与氢可能完全混溶。结合已有研究，硅酸盐、水与氨或随行星冷却依次与氢分离，这对行星的组成、热与结构演化具有意义。

Abstract: Ammonia and hydrogen are major constituents of planets within and beyond the solar system, yet how they interact in planetary envelopes is poorly known. We perform large-scale two-phase coexistence simulations of the ammonia-hydrogen system from 500 to 4000 K and 0.1 to 100 GPa, driven by machine-learning interatomic potentials trained on density functional theory calculations. From these we map the compositions of coexisting hydrogen-rich and ammonia-rich phases, and the critical curve that separates conditions of complete miscibility from those of phase separation. The critical temperature increases with pressure, from 500 K at 0.1 GPa to 2000 K near 30 GPa, and we find no phase separation at 3000 and 4000 K up to 100 GPa. The slope of the critical curve decreases substantially between 0.3 and 30 GPa, and the curve nearly flattens above about 30 GPa and 2000 K, where molecules begin to give way to atomic species. The coexistence curves are asymmetric with an ammonia-rich critical composition. For the expected envelope compositions, the phase boundary lies close to, and intersects, published thermal profiles of Uranus and Neptune, implying ammonia may be raining out in these planets today. In contrast, in warm sub-Neptunes such as TOI-270 d, ammonia and hydrogen are likely miscible in all proportions. Together with previous studies, our results suggest that silicates, water and ammonia separate from hydrogen in sequence as planets cool, with implications for their compositional, thermal and structural evolution.

</details>


<div id='nlin.SI'></div>

# nlin.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Painlevé II connection problem revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28227)
*Nikolai Iorgov,Oleg Lisovyy,Noam Tamarin,Yurii Zhuravlov*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 该文针对一般单值数据（monodromy data）研究非齐次 Painlevé II 方程，借助 Kapaev 渐近公式给出 tau 函数在复时间平面六条特殊射线上的 Zak 变换表示参数，并求解这些射线间的连接问题（connection problem）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非齐次 Painlevé II 方程 tau 函数在不同渐近区域之间的连接问题（connection problem）此前缺少对一般单值数据的完整刻画；该文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用 Kapaev 渐近公式（Kapaev's asymptotic formulas），将 tau 函数在复时间平面六条特殊射线 arg t = πp/3（p = 0,…,5）上的 Zak 变换表示（Zak transform representations）参数，用关联线性系统的 Stokes 参数（Stokes parameters）表示，并据此求解射线之间的连接问题。

Result: 得到 tau 函数在六条特殊射线之间的连接公式，其参数完全由 Stokes 参数表达；并通过解析与数值两方面对该结果进行了验证。

Conclusion: 研究给出了基于 Stokes 参数的非齐次 Painlevé II tau 函数连接问题的显式描述，解析与数值检验支持该结果的正确性。

Abstract: We study the inhomogeneous Painlevé II equation for generic monodromy data. Using Kapaev's asymptotic formulas, the parameters of Zak transform representations of the Painlevé II tau function on six special rays $\arg t=\frac{πp}{3}$ ($p=0,\ldots,5$) in the complex time plane are expressed in terms of Stokes parameters of the associated linear system. We then solve the connection problem for the tau function between these special rays, and provide analytical and numerical checks of our result.

</details>


### [125] [Toda chain solves large BKP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28492)
*Anton Zabrodin*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 本文证明Toda链梯队（Toda chain hierarchy）的任意解均满足大BKP梯队（large BKP hierarchy），这是Alexandrov关于KdV解满足（小）BKP梯队结论的差分（difference）类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果中，Alexandrov证明了KdV方程（KdV equation）的解满足（小）BKP梯队，而Toda链梯队作为2D Toda格点（2D Toda lattice）的一维约化，其与BKP型可积梯队的关系尚未明确，本文旨在填补这一对应关系中的差分版本空缺。

Method: 采用可积梯队（integrable hierarchy）之间的解映射证明方式：将Toda链梯队视为2D Toda格点的一维约化（1D reduction），论证其任意解自动构成大BKP梯队的解，从而建立两个梯队之间的包含关系。

Result: 证明了Toda链梯队的任意解都是大BKP梯队的解，即Toda链梯队被大BKP梯队所包含；该结论与Alexandrov的KdV–BKP结果在结构上平行，构成其差分类比。

Conclusion: Toda链梯队与大BKP梯队之间存在解集包含关系，表明KdV–BKP对应在差分可积系统（difference integrable systems）框架下具有自然推广，为后续研究梯队间的约化与嵌入提供了依据。

Abstract: We prove that any solution of the Toda chain hierarchy (the 1D reduction of the 2D Toda lattice) is a solution of the large BKP hierarchy. This is a difference analogue of Alexandrov's result that KdV solves (small) BKP.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.IM'></div>

# astro-ph.IM [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [A Radiation-Hardened, Fault-Aware Quantized Neural Network That Self-Heals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27621)
*Peter Xiangyuan Ma,Joshua S. Bloom*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 提出 Projection Healing 运行时修复方法：在 Fault-Aware Training 中加入对齐惩罚（alignment penalty），使未受损激活与权重集中于低秩子空间（low-rank subspace），再从未损坏坐标重建被标记的损坏权重，无需额外副本即可在 COTS 硬件上修复量化神经网络权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型被部署到航天器、粒子加速器等辐射环境，持久内存中的数值易被高能粒子翻转而持续退化；推理时新数据会打破静态模型的离线假设，因此需要针对比特翻转的容错能力，且希望在商用现货（COTS）硬件上不存储额外副本。

Method: 扩展 Fault-Aware Training（FAT）范式，增加对齐惩罚项，将未损坏的激活与权重压缩到低秩子空间；运行时对被标记的损坏坐标，利用未损坏的幸存坐标进行重构（reconstruction），即 Projection Healing。方法在六个量化神经网络回归基准上验证。

Result: 在 FAT 检查点固定、仅切换运行时修复的条件下，原生单位超额积分（native-unit excess integral）进一步降低约 23%。与经典 SECDED 相比，该方法在 RMSE 退化 +5% 时将运行清洗间隔（scrub interval）中位数延长 2.04×（SECDED 为 1.62×），在 +50% 时延长 1.96×（SECDED 为 1.07×）。

Conclusion: Projection Healing 为设计抗辐射的数据驱动模型提供了新路径，可与现有硬件冗余、纠错码（ECC）及 FAT 类技术互补，而非替代。

Abstract: Deep learning (DL) models are increasingly being deployed into radiation-challenging environments in spacecraft, particle accelerators, and, soon, space-based data centers. Instantiated as a learned orchestration of numbers stored in persistent memory, models are susceptible to continual degradation as high-energy particles corrupt data. At inference time, new data passed through the network fundamentally break the off-line assumptions and guarantees of a static model determined during training. To preserve the quality and efficacy of the models, fault tolerance against bit flips is a clear objective. In this work we developed a runtime method for repairing the quantized weights of neural networks (NNs) after bit flips on commodity, commercial off-the-shelf (COTS) hardware, without storing extra replicas. Our work extends the well-established Fault-Aware Training (FAT) paradigm by adding an alignment penalty to concentrate undamaged activations and weights into a low-rank subspace, and we then reconstruct flagged corrupted coordinates from the uncorrupted survivors. We call this method {\it Projection Healing} and apply it to six regression benchmarks on quantized NNs. Holding a FAT checkpoint fixed and toggling only the runtime heal gives a further ${\sim}23\%$ reduction in the native-unit excess integral. Across those datasets the method extends the operational scrub interval by a median of $2.04\times$ at a $+5\%$ RMSE degradation and $1.96\times$ at $+50\%$, compared with $1.62\times$ and $1.07\times$ for classical Single Error Correction, Double Error Detection (SECDED). This introduces a new path towards designing resilient data-driven models, complementing established hardware redundancy, ECC, and FAT-based techniques.

</details>


<div id='physics.atom-ph'></div>

# physics.atom-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Parallel Omnidirectional Atom Rearrangement with Dynamic Holograms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27053)
*Samuel A. Engel,Dewei Gong,Ziguang Lin,Lysander Christakis,Kang-Kuen Ni,Norman Y. Yao*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 该工作利用单个千赫兹速率空间光调制器（spatial light modulator, SLM）生成的动态全息图（dynamic holograms），实现并行全向原子输运与重排，并将镱（ytterbium）原子装配为2078格点、平均填充率99.0%的笼目晶格（kagome lattice）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学镊子阵列（optical tweezer arrays）中进行量子科学研究依赖于对单个原子的装配与重构能力，因此需要更快速、可扩展的原子输运与重排手段。

Method: 采用单一千赫兹速率SLM生成动态全息图实现并行全向输运，并开发无需训练的温启动加权Gerchberg-Saxton算法（warm-start, weighted Gerchberg-Saxton algorithm）用于实时全息图生成；同时表征存活率与加热效应。

Result: 即使在前一幅全息图液晶尚未稳定时即下达后续全息图指令，原子仍保持囚禁，从而实现显著更快的输运；该算法性能与神经网络方法相当且无需训练。最终装配出2078格点笼目晶格，平均填充率达99.0%，并演示了阵列层间的并行输运与三维重构。

Conclusion: 该方法易于与现有镊子平台兼容，为基于SLM的大规模原子装配及可编程三维控制提供了途径。

Abstract: The ability to assemble and reconfigure individual atoms is central to performing quantum science using optical tweezer arrays. Here, we demonstrate parallel omnidirectional atom transport and rearrangement using dynamic holograms generated by a single kilohertz-rate spatial light modulator. By carefully characterizing survival and heating, we show that atoms remain confined even when successive holograms are commanded before the liquid crystal has settled, enabling significantly faster transport. To generate holograms in real time, we develop and implement a warm-start, weighted Gerchberg-Saxton algorithm that achieves performance comparable to neural-network methods without requiring any training. Combining these techniques, we assemble ytterbium atoms into a 2078-site kagome lattice with 99.0% mean filling. In addition, we demonstrate parallel transport between array layers, combining axial and transverse motion to reconfigure atoms in three dimensions. Our approach is readily adaptable to existing tweezer platforms and provides a method for large-scale SLM-based atom assembly as well as programmable three-dimensional control.

</details>

### [128] [Generalized Spin Groups and Applications to Magnons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28024)
*Xinhai Tu,Di Wang,Yuxuan Mu,Xiangang Wan*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该文提出“广义自旋群（generalized spin groups）”系统对称性框架，统一描述含与不含自旋轨道耦合（SOC）的磁振子（magnon）性质，并对磁振子热导与自旋导张量给出全部对称性允许形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自旋群（spin groups）仅适用于自旋与晶格自由度可分离的各向同性海森堡模型（isotropic Heisenberg model），而实际磁振子哈密顿量还含DM相互作用、Gamma交换与单离子各向异性（SIA）等各向异性项；交错磁体（altermagnets）中部分自旋-晶格锁定也常被以常规磁群处理，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 先对海森堡、DM、Gamma与SIA相互作用的对称性进行分类，得到6个纯自旋群（spin-only groups）及共161个非平凡自旋群；再通过自旋哈密顿量母群的自发对称性破缺（spontaneous symmetry breaking）获得磁有序相的对称性，并据此对磁振子热导率与自旋导率张量作对称性约束分析。

Result: 母群分类给出6个纯自旋群与161个非平凡自旋群；有序态对应6个纯自旋群，分别生成90、264、157、264、387与598个非平凡自旋群；并导出磁振子热导与自旋导张量的全部对称性允许形式。

Conclusion: 广义自旋群为含与不含SOC的磁振子输运分析提供统一对称性框架，对磁振子响应给出更精细的约束。

Abstract: Spin groups provide the natural symmetry framework for the isotropic Heisenberg model, in which spin and lattice degrees of freedom are separable. They have recently found broad applications in describing electronic band structure, magnon band topology, and transport phenomena. However, a typical spin Hamiltonian describing magnons contains not only isotropic Heisenberg exchange but also anisotropic interactions induced by spin-orbit coupling (SOC), including the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya (DM) interaction, the Gamma exchange, and the single-ion anisotropy (SIA), which strongly influence magnon properties. More recently, some studies have also revealed relativistic magnon transport phenomena in altermagnets. Such systems typically exhibit partial spin-lattice locking, but are commonly assumed to be described by conventional magnetic groups. Here, we develop a systematic symmetry framework, generalized spin groups, for magnons. We first classify the symmetries of Heisenberg, DM, Gamma, and SIA interactions. The resulting classification contains 6 spin-only groups, together with a total of 161 nontrivial spin groups. The symmetries of magnetically ordered phases are then obtained through spontaneous symmetry breaking of the corresponding parent groups for spin Hamiltonians. We also identify 6 spin-only groups for ordered states, which generate 90, 264, 157, 264, 387, and 598 nontrivial spin groups, respectively. Finally, we apply generalized spin groups to the magnon thermal conductivity and spin conductivity tensors and derive all the symmetry-allowed forms. Our results provide a unified symmetry framework for analyzing magnon transport both with and without SOC, yielding more refined constraints on magnon responses.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Zeeman Quantum Geometry of the Layer Pseudo-spin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26982)
*Bingxuan Jiang,Shihao Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文引入“层赝自旋量子几何（quantum geometry of the layer pseudo-spin）”，即由带间Berry联络与层矩阵元构成的混合张量，并指出层赝自旋作为Ising型标量在三次旋转下的选择定则与赝矢量自旋不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 位置依赖层间隧穿耦合的二维层体系具有可调控的层赝自旋极化，但其对外场响应的几何描述尚不明确，需要建立相应的量子几何框架来刻画其内禀自由度。

Method: 构造由带间Berry联络（interband Berry connection）与层矩阵元（layer matrix element）组成的混合张量，用于描述对振荡面内电场的响应；在最小两带模型中计算层赝自旋量子度规（quantum metric）与Zeeman–Berry曲率，并对扭转MoTe$_2$双层进行定量分析。

Result: 在最小两带模型中，层赝自旋量子度规与Zeeman–Berry曲率非零，但其布里渊区积分消失；面内驱动在线性阶不改变空间平均极化，但留下可被层分辨隧穿直接成像的莫尔周期胞内图案。对扭转MoTe$_2$双层量化了局域偶极调制，并识别出整流、螺旋性奇（helicity-odd）与三次谐波通道，使净响应在高阶出现。

Conclusion: 层赝自旋量子几何为二维层状体系中光-物质耦合提供几何化描述框架，其Ising型标量特性导致独特选择定则，并给出可通过层分辨隧穿与高阶非线性通道探测的实验预言。

Abstract: Two-dimensional layers coupled by position-dependent interlayer tunnelling host a layer pseudo-spin whose polarization is a controllable internal degree of freedom. We introduce the quantum geometry of this layer pseudo-spin: a mixed tensor formed from the interband Berry connection and the layer matrix element, which governs the response to an oscillating in-plane electric field. Because the layer pseudo-spin is an Ising-type scalar invariant under threefold rotation, its selection rules differ from those of a pseudovector spin. In a minimal two-band model, the layer-pseudo-spin quantum metric and Zeeman--Berry curvature are nonzero, while their zone integrals vanish. An in-plane drive therefore leaves the spatially averaged polarization unchanged at linear order but imprints a moiré-periodic intracell pattern, directly imageable by layer-resolved tunnelling. For twisted MoTe$_2$ bilayer, we quantify the local dipole modulation and identify the rectified, helicity-odd, and third-harmonic channels through which a net response appears at higher order.

</details>


### [130] [Stacking-Induced Tuning of a Flat-Band Surface State in NbIrTe4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27096)
*Jiabin Yu,Mohamed Oudah,Giang Nguyen,Hsianghsi Kung,V. King,Rysa Greenwood,Adrian F. Rumson,Jisun Kim,Matteo Michiardi,Giorgio Levy,Andrea Damascelli,E. R. Johnson,Meigan Aronson,Lukas Muechler,Sarah A. Burke*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究结合扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunnelling microscopy, STM）、角分辨光电子能谱（angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, ARPES）与密度泛函理论（density-functional theory, DFT），揭示NbIrTe₄表面约8 meV的强共振源于对表面堆叠序敏感的局域平坦带，且该表面态色散可通过局域结构操作调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三元层状过渡金属硫族化合物（transition metal dichalcogenides, TMDCs）性质对堆叠序（stacking order）与层间耦合高度敏感，需明确其电子结构与表面态的微观起源，而NbIrTe₄作为块体Weyl半金属候选与单层量子自旋霍尔绝缘体（quantum spin Hall insulator）预测材料具有研究价值。

Method: 采用STM/扫描隧道谱（tunnelling spectroscopy）、ARPES与DFT相结合，并辅以准粒子干涉（quasiparticle interference, QPI）测量，对NbIrTe₄的电子结构与表面态进行表征与理论对照。

Result: 费米能级以下约8 meV的强共振被归因于表面来源的平坦带（flat band），其对表面局域堆叠序敏感；在顶层发生横向位移的区域，隧道谱转为带隙样（gap-like）特征且费米能级附近强度发生能量移动；DFT与QPI表明该近准一维（quasi-1D）平坦表面态附近谱学的显著变化源于顶部两层态密度空间分布的改变，并伴随色散出现。

Conclusion: NbIrTe₄可作为一个通过局域结构操作有效调控表面能带色散的平台。

Abstract: Ternary layered transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDCs) offer high tunability and access to correlated and topological states. However, like the broader family of layered TMDCs, their properties are sensitive to stacking order and interlayer coupling. Here, we investigate the electronic structure and surface states of NbIrTe$_4$, a candidate Weyl semimetal in bulk, and predicted to be a quantum spin Hall insulator in monolayer form. Using scanning tunnelling microscopy (STM), angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES), and density-functional theory (DFT), we attribute the strong resonance in tunnelling spectroscopy located approximately 8 meV below the Fermi level to a surface-derived flat band which shows sensitivity to the local stacking order at the surface. In regions where the top layer is laterally shifted, tunnelling spectroscopy instead shows a gap-like feature with intensity near the Fermi level shifted in energy. Combining DFT and quasiparticle interference (QPI) measurements, we find this dramatic change in the spectroscopy in the vicinity of the quasi-1D flat-band surface state becomes dispersive due to a change in the spatial distribution of the density of states in the top two layers. These findings establish NbIrTe$_4$ as a platform where surface band dispersion can be effectively tuned through local structural manipulation.

</details>


### [131] [Quantum Oscillations across Hole Crystallization in Rhombohedral Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27134)
*Daniele Guerci,Enrico Rossi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出，菱形堆叠石墨烯（rhombohedral graphene）中量子振荡频率的突增可由环形费米海（annular Fermi sea）内空穴口袋的两种竞争归宿解释：塌缩为单连通费米液体（Fermi liquid）或结晶为反晶体（anticrystal）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期量子振荡（quantum-oscillation）测量显示，载流子密度降低至超导（superconductivity）附近时主频突增并出现附加频率；非相互作用能带模型（noninteracting band model）虽能解释多频，却无法解释频率的向上跃变，需引入相互作用机制。

Method: 基于环形费米海中内层空穴口袋（inner hole pocket）的两种竞争命运进行理论分析：口袋塌缩（pocket collapse）与空穴结晶（hole crystallization）；并比较二者对外层费米等能线（Fermi contour）及磁击穿轨道（magnetic-breakdown orbit）的影响。

Result: 口袋塌缩在固定密度下使外层费米等能线收缩；空穴结晶则保留较大的等能线并支持磁击穿轨道，从而解释频率跃变，附加的重构轨道（reconstructed orbit）产生多音频谱（multitone spectrum）。

Conclusion: 该对比为频率跃变提供了可能解释，并支持将反晶体视为菱形堆叠石墨烯中超导附近正常态的候选相。

Abstract: Recent quantum-oscillation measurements in rhombohedral graphene reveal an abrupt increase in the dominant frequency and the emergence of additional frequencies as the carrier density is lowered toward superconductivity. Noninteracting band models can account for multiple frequencies but do not explain the upward jump. We show that these features can arise from two competing fates of the inner hole pocket in an annular Fermi sea: collapse into a simply connected Fermi liquid or crystallization into an anticrystal, in which localized holes coexist with itinerant electrons. Pocket collapse contracts the outer Fermi contour at fixed density, whereas hole crystallization preserves a larger contour that supports magnetic-breakdown orbits. This contrast provides a possible explanation for the frequency jump, while additional reconstructed orbits produce a multitone spectrum. Our results support the anticrystal as a candidate normal state near superconductivity in rhombohedral graphene.

</details>


### [132] [Ballistic transport and crystallographic control in altermagnetic quantum rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27173)
*Edilberto O. Silva*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究了有限宽度 d 波交错磁（altermagnetic）量子环的两端相干输运，指出受限 Aharonov–Bohm 通量保持严格的 h/e 周期性并在一模对称几何下给出精确半通量透射零点，晶体取向可反转透射自旋，且自旋极化在有限偏压、温度与无序下比单个相干共振幅度更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探索交错磁体（altermagnet）在介观干涉结构中的相干输运特征，寻找可用于实验辨识的干涉节点位置、通量周期性与晶体调控的自旋极化等可测信号。

Method: 采用相干两端输运计算，考察有限宽度 d 波交错磁量子环与非磁引线的耦合；引入垂直磁场、晶体旋转，并进行有限偏压、热展宽、无序、Zeeman 项及自旋守恒电压探针（voltage-probe）计算。

Result: 受限通量保持严格 h/e 周期并在对称单模几何中产生精确半通量透射零点；垂直场按有效面积 A_eff=1.0342πr_0^2 移动干涉图样，交错磁劈裂将共同零点解析为邻近的自旋相关节点；晶体旋转可反转透射自旋，镜面取向 θ_A=π/4 严格自旋简并；对 50 nm 参考环，亚特斯拉场即可给出 |P_T|>0.99 的窗口，最易实现者仅需均匀场而无需受限通量。

Conclusion: 自旋极化在有限偏压、温度、无序、Zeeman 与电压探针条件下比单个相干共振幅度显著更稳定，因而干涉节点位置、通量周期性与晶体控制的自旋极化可作为交错磁介观干涉仪的实验可用特征。

Abstract: We study coherent two-terminal transport through a finite-width $d$-wave altermagnetic quantum ring with nonmagnetic leads. A confined Aharonov--Bohm flux remains exactly $h/e$ periodic and, in the symmetric one-mode geometry, produces an exact half-flux transmission zero. A perpendicular field shifts the interference pattern according to an effective area $A_{\rm eff}=1.0342\,πr_0^2$ and the altermagnetic splitting resolves the common zero into nearby spin-dependent nodes. Rotating the crystal reverses the transmitted spin, whereas the mirror orientation $θ_A=π/4$ is exactly spin degenerate. For a $50$~nm reference ring, sub-tesla fields yield windows with $|P_T|>0.99$, the most accessible of which requires a uniform field alone and no confined flux. Finite-bias, thermal, disorder, Zeeman, and spin-conserving voltage-probe calculations show that the polarization is appreciably more stable than individual coherent-resonance amplitudes. These results identify interference-node positions, flux periodicity, and crystal-controlled spin polarization as experimentally useful signatures of an altermagnetic mesoscopic interferometer.

</details>


### [133] [Diffusive Description of Superconducting Single-Photon Detectors Using Quantum Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27189)
*Krzysztof Pomorski,Eryk Halubek*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文采用非厄米量子力学（non-Hermitian quantum mechanics）的方法，对超导单光子探测器（superconducting single-photon detectors）中描述低能与高能准粒子（quasiparticle）的耦合经典扩散方程（coupled classical diffusion equations）的动力学进行参数化，维度覆盖一至三维空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机，仅指出所处理的对象为超导单光子探测器中低能与高能准粒子的耦合扩散动力学。

Method: 核心方法是借用非厄米量子力学（non-Hermitian quantum mechanics）的形式体系，对耦合的经典扩散方程（coupled classical diffusion equations）进行参数化处理，并在一至三维（one to three spatial dimensions）空间中展开分析。

Result: 摘要仅说明实现了对上述耦合扩散方程动力学的参数化，未给出具体定量结果或验证结论。

Conclusion: 非厄米量子力学方法可作为参数化超导单光子探测器中准粒子扩散动力学的可行框架，且适用于一至三维空间情形。

Abstract: The methodology of non-Hermitian quantum mechanics is employed to parameterize the dynamics of coupled classical diffusion equations describing low- and high-energy quasiparticles in superconducting single-photon detectors in one to three spatial dimensions.

</details>


### [134] [Steering of topological criticality in the Su-Schrieffer-Heeger chain via non-Hermitian substitutional impurities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27436)
*Juan A. Cañas,Guo-Qing Zhang,A. Martín-Ruiz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究在一维Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）链中引入有限浓度的非厄米替代杂质（non-Hermitian substitutional impurities），提出通过位点依赖的非互易跃迁幅度不对称性，实现对拓扑相边界的确定性重构，并用有限尺寸标度验证其临界点位移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作多关注非厄米杂质对拓扑相的扰动效应，缺乏一种可主动、确定性调控拓扑相边界的机制；本文旨在寻找通过局部缺陷不对称性实现宏观拓扑相边界可控移动的方案。

Method: 在一维SSH链中引入有限浓度非厄米替代杂质，构建位点依赖的非互易（non-reciprocal）胞内与胞间跃迁幅度，并打破杂质跃迁的内部结构对称性；通过有限尺寸标度分析（finite-size scaling analysis）提取关联长度临界指数（correlation length critical exponents），并借助非厄米趋肤效应（Non-Hermitian Skin Effect）刻画谱的实-复转变。

Result: 破坏非互易杂质跃迁的内部结构对称性可引起临界点的定向宏观位移，从而通过局部缺陷不对称性实现拓扑相的受控扩张或压缩。对称与非对称构型下提取的临界指数分别为ν≈1.455与ν≈1.397，表明杂质导致偏离原始SSH普适类（universality class），且高缺陷密度下指数发散；同时观察到杂质诱导的实谱到复谱转变，并由非厄米趋肤效应给出几何刻画。

Conclusion: 孤立非厄米不对称性可协同作用，确定性地移动宏观拓扑保护边界，为利用局部缺陷工程主动调控非厄米拓扑相提供机制性依据。

Abstract: We investigate the topological phase in a one-dimensional Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) chain in the presence of a finite concentration of non-Hermitian substitutional impurities. Our primary focus is the discovery of a deterministic mechanism to actively reconfigure the topological phase boundaries by introducing site-dependent, non-reciprocal intracell and intercell hopping amplitudes. Specifically, breaking the internal structural symmetry of the non-reciprocal impurity hoppings induces a targeted macroscopic displacement of the critical point. This mechanism allows for the controlled expansion or compression of the topological phase strictly via local defect asymmetry. We validate this boundary displacement through finite-size scaling analysis. The extracted correlation length critical exponents ($ν\approx 1.455$ and $ν\approx 1.397$ for symmetric and asymmetric configurations, respectively) confirm a disorder-induced departure from the pristine SSH universality class, with the exponent diverging at high defect densities. We also report an impurity-induced real-to-complex spectral transition geometrically characterized by the Non-Hermitian Skin Effect. Ultimately, our results establish how isolated non-Hermitian asymmetries cooperatively interact to deterministically shift macroscopic topological protection.

</details>


### [135] [Magnetic-Field-Induced Darkening of Bright Excitons in a MoWSe$_2$ Monolayer Alloy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27469)
*Katarzyna Olkowska-Pucko,Artur O. Slobodeniuk,Mehdi Arfaoui,Elena Blundo,Natalia Zawadzka,Łucja Kipczak,Jan Szpakowski,Diana Vaclavkova,Dipankar Jana,Piotr Kapuściński,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Clement Faugeras,Marek Potemski,Adam Babiński,Antonio Polimeni,Maciej R. Molas*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作在Mo0.68W0.32Se2中展示了面内磁场诱导亮激子变暗的互补现象，并用量子混合模型定量解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁场增亮自旋禁戒暗激子（dark exciton）已被广泛研究，但磁场对光学亮激子（bright exciton）的反向效应尚不清楚，本文旨在探索这一互补过程。

Method: 在4.2 K下对Mo0.68W0.32Se2施加最高25 T的面内磁场，进行磁光光谱测量，并构建亮-暗激子混合模型（bright-dark mixing model）拟合能量与强度随磁场的变化。

Result: 面内磁场激活了位于亮激子下方14.2±1.3 meV的暗激子谱线，同时使亮激子发光强度被抑制约85%；模型定量再现了能量与强度的耦合演化，给出有效面内g因子|g∥|=5.2±0.2。

Conclusion: 合金化（alloying）使体系接近亮-暗交叉点，兼具小亮-暗劈裂与强面内磁耦合；磁场诱导的能级排斥（level repulsion）抬升亮支并显著耗尽其热布局，由此确立了通过调控亮-暗层级实现激子布居磁控制的一般途径。

Abstract: Magnetic fields are widely used to brighten spin-forbidden dark excitons, but the inverse effect on an optically bright exciton has remained largely unexplored. We demonstrate the complementary phenomenon of magnetic-field-induced darkening of a bright exciton in Mo$_{0.68}$W$_{0.32}$Se$_2$. At 4.2 K, an in-plane magnetic field up to 25 T activates a dark-exciton line 14.2$\pm$1.3 meV below the bright exciton while suppressing the bright-exciton emission by about 85\%. A bright-dark mixing model quantitatively captures the coupled energy and intensity evolution with an effective in-plane $g$ factor of $|g_{\parallel}|=5.2\pm0.2$. Alloying is crucial to the effect, as it brings the system close to the bright-dark crossover, where a small bright-dark splitting is combined with unusually strong in-plane magnetic coupling. This places the system in a regime where field-induced level repulsion raises the bright-like branch and strongly depletes its thermal population. These results identify a general route to magnetic control of exciton populations through engineering of the bright-dark hierarchy.

</details>


### [136] [Magnetic-Field Effects on the Electronic States of the K$_4$ Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27539)
*Katsuhiro Takahashi,Shouya Yoshida,Katsunori Wakabayashi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在最近邻紧束缚模型中研究均匀磁场下K₄晶格的电子态，通过固定平行于磁场的Bloch动量分量将Peierls紧束缚问题约化为有效二维问题，得到反映晶格投影几何的Hofstadter谱；弱场下由三狄拉克锥的低能有效哈密顿量导出赝自旋平坦支与两条相对论性朗道阶梯并存的朗道能级结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K₄晶格是非中心对称的三维网络，在Γ和H点具有三狄拉克锥（triple-Dirac cones），其磁场下的电子结构尚需系统刻画，尤其是Hofstadter谱的自相似结构与螺旋轴带来的特殊磁响应。

Method: 采用最近邻紧束缚模型（nearest-neighbor tight-binding model）并作Peierls替换；通过固定平行于磁场的Bloch动量分量，将三维问题约化为有效二维问题，计算Hofstadter谱；弱场极限下由Γ点附近的低能有效哈密顿量（low-energy effective Hamiltonian）导出朗道能级（Landau levels）。

Result: Hofstadter谱的自相似子图案反映晶格在垂直于磁场平面上的投影几何：B∥[100]时在磁通标度φ=1/8出现类方形子结构，B∥[111]时在更细的φ=1/32标度出现类蜂窝与类三角子结构（φ为投影晶格原胞一个格子的磁通，对应每个投影单胞一个磁通量子）。由于手性K₄网络的螺旋轴（screw axes），场平行动量充当附加磁通，在共度值处使谱沿磁通轴移动，并将H点的第二个三狄拉克锥引入磁谱。弱场下三组分性质给出三次久期方程，其解表明赝自旋平坦支与两条相对论性朗道阶梯共存，并存在单一手性n=0能级沿磁场线性色散，结果与晶格计算一致。

Conclusion: K₄晶格在磁场下的谱结构由晶格投影几何与螺旋轴对称性共同决定；三狄拉克锥的三组分特征导致平坦支、相对论性朗道阶梯与手性n=0能级并存的独特朗道能级结构，且与直接晶格计算相符。

Abstract: We investigate the electronic states of the K$_4$ lattice in a uniform magnetic field within a nearest-neighbor tight-binding model. The K$_4$ lattice is a \rev{noncentrosymmetric three-dimensional network with triple-Dirac cones at the $Γ$ and H points}. By fixing the Bloch momentum component parallel to the field, the Peierls-substituted tight-binding problem reduces to an effective two-dimensional problem and yields a Hofstadter spectrum \rev{whose self-similar subpatterns reflect the geometry of the lattice projected onto the plane perpendicular to the field}. A square-like substructure emerges on the flux scale $φ= 1/8$ for $\bm{B}\parallel[100]$, and honeycomb- and triangular-like substructures emerge on the finer scale $φ= 1/32$ for $\bm{B}\parallel[111]$\rev{. Here $φ$ is the flux through the elementary plaquette of the projected lattice, and these scales correspond to one flux quantum per projected unit cell. Owing to the screw axes of the chiral K$_4$ network, the field-parallel momentum acts as an additional flux, which, for commensurate values of this momentum, shifts the spectrum along the flux axis and brings the second triple-Dirac cone at the H point into the magnetic spectrum}. In the weak-field limit, we derive the Landau-level structure from a low-energy effective Hamiltonian around the $Γ$ point. The three-component nature of the triple-Dirac cone gives rise to a cubic secular equation, whose solutions describe how a pseudospin-flat branch coexists with two relativistic Landau ladders \rev{and a single chiral $n=0$ level dispersing linearly along the field, in agreement with the lattice calculation}.

</details>


### [137] [Time-Reversal Symmetry in Artificial Topological Graphene Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28055)
*J. A. da Silva Neto,G. Q. Garcia,A. M. de M. Carvalho,C. Furtado*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文精确求解了带质量项的双谷狄拉克环在Aharonov-Bohm通量与垂直磁场下的能谱，给出持续电流与磁化强度的解析与数值结果，并阐明谷分裂及时间反演的含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人工石墨烯环（artificial graphene ring）中狄拉克准粒子的量子环效应，尤其是磁场如何通过狄拉克结构引入谷依赖项并改变持续电流（persistent current）与磁化行为。

Method: 以低能双谷狄拉克哈密顿量（two-valley Dirac Hamiltonian）加均匀质量项描述环；加Aharonov-Bohm通量时约化为Bessel函数求解的二阶径向方程，并给出精确久期方程（secular equation）；加垂直均匀磁场后用合流超几何函数（confluent hypergeometric functions）求解径向问题，从去除伪根（spurious roots）的久期行列式导数得到持续电流与磁化强度。

Result: 对窄环给出能谱与持续电流的闭式表达；弱场展开（精确至B^4量级修正）显式给出谷分裂（valley splitting），并分离出磁化率（susceptibility）的抗磁与顺磁贡献；物理时间反演（交换谷）给出相反磁场下能级间的精确关系，而有效单谷时间反演被质量项与边界条件破坏，但在零场谱中不留痕迹；数值结果验证了分析。

Conclusion: 磁场通过狄拉克结构产生的谷依赖项解除谷简并；物理时间反演保持严格对称关系，而有效单谷时间反演虽被破坏却不影响零场能谱；所给解析框架可定量描述环的能谱、持续电流与磁化性质。

Abstract: We study an artificial graphene ring described by the low-energy two-valley Dirac Hamiltonian with a uniform mass term. When the ring is threaded by an Aharonov-Bohm flux, the problem reduces to a second-order radial equation solved by Bessel functions. We give the exact secular equation and, for narrow rings, closed expressions for the spectrum and the persistent current. We then add a uniform magnetic field perpendicular to the plane. The radial problem is solved with confluent hypergeometric functions, and the persistent current and magnetization are written as derivatives of a secular determinant from which the spurious roots have been removed. Besides the usual orbital coupling, the field enters through a valley-dependent term that comes from the Dirac structure and lifts the valley degeneracy. A weak-field expansion, accurate up to corrections of order B^4, gives this valley splitting explicitly and separates the diamagnetic and paramagnetic contributions to the susceptibility. Finally, we work out what time reversal implies for the spectrum. Physical time reversal, which exchanges the valleys, leads to an exact relation between levels at opposite fields. The effective single-valley time reversal, on the other hand, is broken by the mass term and by the boundary conditions, yet leaves no trace in the zero-field spectrum. Numerical results for the spectrum, the persistent current, and the magnetization illustrate the analysis.

</details>


### [138] [Collective Hall quantization in 3D Chern vector systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27802)
*Zhi-Qiang Zhang,Shuguang Cheng,Hongfang Liu,Hailong Li,Hua Jiang,X. C. Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文预测了一种由陈向量（Chern vector）C=(0,m,n) 保护的独特三维量子霍尔效应（3D quantum Hall effect），其霍尔电导量子化为 G_xy=±(mL_y+nL_z)e²/h，体现了整体拓扑而非单个边缘模式的鲁棒输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维量子霍尔效应由陈向量而非单一陈数（Chern number）刻画，现有量子化霍尔输运仅在单个非零陈分量的特殊情形下被确立；对于更一般的陈向量态，拓扑如何保护量子化输运这一基本问题尚未解决。

Method: 通过理论预测，研究具有维度 L_y 与 L_z 的样品中由 C=(0,m,n) 保护的三维量子化霍尔效应，分析整体拓扑（global topology）对边缘模式集体行为的约束，并提出可供实验观测的候选材料。

Result: 整体拓扑强制 mL_y+nL_z 的集体量子化，尽管单个边缘模式对无序（disorder）脆弱；该响应表现为霍尔电导 G_xy=±(mL_y+nL_z)e²/h，同时依赖于陈向量分量与样品尺寸。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了一种新的输运机制：鲁棒量子化是统一手性网络（chiral network）的整体属性，而非其组成模式的属性，并给出了可用于实验验证的候选材料。

Abstract: Understanding the three-dimensional (3D) quantum Hall effect, characterized by a Chern vector rather than a single Chern number, has been a central pursuit in topological physics. While quantized Hall transport is established for the special case with one nonzero Chern component, recent realizations of more general Chern-vector states raise a fundamental question: how does such topology protect quantized transport? Here, we predict a distinct 3D quantized Hall effect protected by $\mathbf{C}=(0,m,n)$. For samples with dimensions $L_y$ and $L_z$, global topology enforces collective quantization of $mL_y+nL_z$, despite the fragility of individual edge modes to disorder. This reveals a novel transport regime in which robust quantization emerges as a holistic property of a unified chiral network,rather than of its constituent modes. Crucially, this collective response manifests as a quantized Hall conductance $G_{xy}=\pm(mL_y+nL_z)e^2/h$, with a distinctive dependence on both Chern-vector components and sample dimensions. We propose candidate materials to facilitate experimental observation.

</details>


### [139] [On the origin of nonreciprocal charge transport in topological insulator BiSbTeSe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27849)
*Fengyi Guo,Ziqi Wang,Xiubing Li,Zheng Dai,Congcong Li,Yufan Ju,Shuai Zhang,Bin Cheng,Zhe Ying,Fengqi Song*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在双栅控 BiSbTeSe2 拓扑绝缘体器件中观测到高度可栅控的非互易电荷输运（nonreciprocal charge transport），其二次谐波纵向电阻对电流与磁场均呈线性，在电荷中性点（charge neutrality point）附近最强并可室温保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易电荷输运可用于探测方向依赖的电荷动力学与自旋-动量锁定（spin-momentum locking）电子态，因此需要寻找可有效调控该响应、且能在室温工作的材料平台。

Method: 制备双栅控（dual-gated）BiSbTeSe2 拓扑绝缘体器件，测量二次谐波纵向电阻（second-harmonic longitudinal resistance）随电流与磁场的变化，并通过栅压调节费米能级位置。

Result: 二次谐波纵向电阻对电流和磁场均为线性；非互易响应在电荷中性点附近达到最大，在其两侧发生符号反转（sign reversal），费米能级进入体态（bulk states）后被强烈抑制；中性点附近信号比无栅调制时约大一个数量级，且在 300 K 仍保持稳健。

Conclusion: 非互易响应被归因于拓扑表面态（topological surface states）中自旋-动量锁定不均匀性（inhomogeneity）；BiSbTeSe2 被视为室温非线性电子学（nonlinear electronics）的有前景平台。

Abstract: Nonreciprocal charge transport provides an effective approach for probing direction dependent charge dynamics and spin-momentum-locked electronic states. Here, we report highly gate-tunable nonreciprocal charge transport in dual-gated BiSbTeSe2 topological insulator device. The second-harmonic longitudinal resistance is linear in both current and magnetic field. The nonreciprocal response reaches its maximum near the charge neutrality point, exhibits sign reversals on either side, and is strongly suppressed once the Fermi level enters the bulk states. We suggest that the nonreciprocal response is attributed to the inhomogeneity of spin-momentum locking in the topological surface states. The nonreciprocal signal near the charge neutrality point is about one order of magnitude larger than that without gate modulation and remains robust up to 300 K, highlighting BiSbTeSe2 as a promising platform for room-temperature nonlinear electronics.

</details>


### [140] [Polarity-selective domain wall propagation driven by spin-orbit torque](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28052)
*Nobuyuki Umetsu,Michael Quinsat,Masaki Kado,Hiroki Tokuhira*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文指出，二维磁各向异性（magnetic anisotropy）景观的局部几何结构可选择在给定电流下哪种畴壁极性（wall polarity）能够传播，并结合微磁学模拟（micromagnetic simulations）与扩展的集体坐标模型（collective-coordinate model）阐明其机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 垂直磁化纳米线（perpendicularly magnetized nanowires）中，磁各向异性的空间变化会阻碍自旋轨道矩（spin-orbit torque）驱动的手性畴壁（chiral domain wall）运动，因此需要理解无序景观如何影响畴壁传播。

Method: 采用基于扩散平滑（diffusion-based smoothing）生成的空间相关随机各向异性景观进行微磁学模拟，并以单一倾斜各向异性边界（inclined anisotropy boundary）隔离机制；同时构建扩展的集体坐标模型。

Result: 在同一无序实现中，上-下（up-down）与下-上（down-up）畴壁呈现不同的传播结果；在Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya相互作用（Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya interaction）下，相反极性产生相反的电流诱导倾斜（current-induced tilts），与边界对齐程度不同，导致钉扎强度（pinning strengths）差异；反转边界倾斜方向可交换被钉扎与传播的极性。

Conclusion: 二维各向异性景观的几何结构可几何性地筛选畴壁极性，扩展的集体坐标模型能够描述这一选择，并区分传播阈值（propagation threshold）与静态钉扎极限（static pinning limit）。

Abstract: Spatial variations in magnetic anisotropy impede the spin-orbit-torque-driven motion of chiral domain walls in perpendicularly magnetized nanowires. We show that the local geometry of a two-dimensional anisotropy landscape can select which wall polarity propagates under a given current. Micromagnetic simulations of spatially correlated random anisotropy landscapes generated by diffusion-based smoothing reveal different propagation outcomes for up--down and down--up walls within the same disorder realization. A single inclined anisotropy boundary isolates the mechanism: in the presence of the Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya interaction, opposite wall polarities develop opposite current-induced tilts, producing different alignment with the boundary and hence different pinning strengths. Reversing the boundary inclination interchanges the pinned and propagating polarities. An extended collective-coordinate model captures this geometric selection and distinguishes the propagation threshold from the static pinning limit.

</details>


### [141] [Adiabatic confinement and number-theoretic spectra of massless Dirac fermions in twisted trilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28063)
*Kunihiro Yananose,Changwon Park,Young-Woo Son*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究揭示扭角三层石墨烯中，无质量费米子可被无质量势垒、体隙与物理边界地限制在三角形 Bernal 畴内，形成无隙无能边的量子腔，并在态密度振荡中体现 Eisenstein 整数算术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯中电子如无质量相对论粒子，Klein 隧穿（Klein tunnelling）使其可无阻碍穿过静电势垒，导致难以俘获，并波及原子坍缩（atomic collapse）与强子禁闭（hadron confinement）问题；现有机制依赖质量势垒、能隙或物理边界，亟需新途径。

Method: 以扭角三层石墨烯（twisted trilayers）为平台，其 Bernal 与菱方（rhombohedral）畴由平滑边界相接；结合数值模拟（simulations）与解析建模（analytical modeling），分析无质量载流子向菱方畴的绝热连续性及非绝热耦合逃逸行为。

Result: 无质量载流子不存在向菱方畴的绝热延续（adiabatic continuation），经非绝热耦合（nonadiabatic coupling）逃逸至有质量载流子的概率因平滑堆垛变化而指数抑制；绝热极限下实现完全反射，Bernal 畴成为无隙、无能边金属中的量子腔。态密度（density-of-states）振荡编码 Eisenstein 整数算术，将能量与简并度联系到范数（norms）与素因子分解（prime factorizations）。

Conclusion: 研究提出区别于既有方案的无质量费米子限制机制，将相对论性限制（relativistic confinement）与数论（number theory）引入实验可及的电子谱，为石墨烯中量子腔与数论—电子谱关联研究提供新方向。

Abstract: Electrons in graphene move like massless relativistic particles, making them exceptionally mobile but difficult to trap because Klein tunnelling permits unimpeded passage through electrostatic barriers. This challenge extends to atomic collapse and hadron confinement. Twisted trilayers form Bernal and rhombohedral domains joined by smooth boundaries, bringing their carriers into contact. Here we show that a new mechanism, distinct from established approaches, confines massless fermions in equilateral triangular Bernal domains without mass barriers, bulk gaps or physical edges. Simulations and analytical modeling reveal that massless carriers have no adiabatic continuation into rhombohedral domains, while escape through nonadiabatic coupling to massive carriers is exponentially suppressed by smooth stacking variation. Perfect reflection in the adiabatic limit turns Bernal domains into quantum cavities in a gapless, edgeless metal. Striking density-of-states oscillations encode Eisenstein-integer arithmetic, linking energies and degeneracies to norms and prime factorizations. These findings bring relativistic confinement and number theory into experimentally accessible electronic spectra.

</details>


### [142] [Density Saturation and Autocorrelation Characteristics of Interlayer Exciton Ensembles in MoSe$_2$/WSe$_2$ Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28158)
*Mirco Troue,Johannes Figueiredo,Alina Scheuermann,Sebastian Loy,Jonas Kiemle,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,Ursula Wurstbauer,Alexander W. Holleitner*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究通过时间分辨光致发光（time-resolved photoluminescence）与二阶自相关（second-order autocorrelation）测量，在宽激发功率与池温范围内考察 MoSe₂/WSe₂ 异质结中的层间激子（interlayer excitons），提取密度—温度相图，观测到约 10¹³ cm⁻² 的激子密度饱和，并与莫特转变（Mott transition）预言相符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究范德华异质结（van der Waals heterostructures）中层间激子系综的密度上限及其物理起源，并检验该密度极限是否伴随光子统计性质（photon statistics）的变化。

Method: 采用时间分辨光致发光与二阶自相关测量，在宽范围激发功率与池温（bath temperature）下，通过分析最低发射能层间激子态的光致发光幅度与寿命，提取激子密度—温度图。

Result: 提取的激子密度在约 10¹³ cm⁻² 处出现饱和，与范德华异质结中层间激子系综莫特转变的预测值一致；二阶自相关结果显示，从低密度至可达到的最大密度，发光始终保持 g⁽²⁾(0) ≈ 1。

Conclusion: 密度饱和可能与莫特转变相关，但文中亦讨论了其他解释；最低能激子系综的光子统计符合泊松分布（Poissonian statistics），即密度极限未伴随对经典光子统计的偏离。

Abstract: We investigate interlayer excitons in MoSe$_2$/WSe$_2$ heterostructures using time-resolved photoluminescence and second-order autocorrelation measurements across a wide range of excitation powers and bath temperatures. By analyzing the photoluminescence amplitude and lifetime of the interlayer exciton state with the lowest emission energy, we extract a corresponding density-temperature diagram. The extracted exciton density exhibits a saturation at approximately $10^{13}$cm$^{-2}$, consistent with values predicted for a Mott transition of interlayer exciton ensembles in van der Waals heterostructures. Moreover, possible interpretations beyond the Mott transition are discussed. We further test whether the density limit is accompanied by a departure from classical photon statistics. The second-order autocorrelation measurements reveal that the photoluminescence maintains an emission characteristic of $g^{(2)}(0) \approx 1$ from low exciton densities up to the maximum reachable density in the investigated exciton ensembles consistent with the lowest-energy exciton ensemble obeying Poissonian statistics.

</details>


### [143] [Steady-state cooperative emission of interlayer excitons in atomically thin light-emitting diodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28304)
*Andrés M. Mier Valdivia,Pavel E. Dolgirev,Grace H. Chen,Nadine Leisgang,Andrew Y. Joe,Stefan Ostermann,Deepankur Thureja,Yongxin Zeng,Dapeng Ding,Daniel Rhodes,Bumho Kim,Song Liu,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,James C. Hone,Susanne F. Yelin,Hongkun Park,Mikhail D. Lukin,Philip Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作在原子级薄的二维（2D）过渡金属二硫族化物（TMD）发光二极管中，利用边缘增强的 fringe 电场局域电注入的层间激子（IXs），观测到稳态协同发光（cooperative emission）的显著光子聚束与延迟反聚束、聚束复现等关联特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当发射体集合被限制在光波长尺度内时，其辐射可变得相互关联并产生协同发光；二维 TMD 电子-空穴双层中的强光-物质耦合可形成长寿命偶极层间激子，为涌现集体现象提供理想平台，但电驱动下的稳态协同发光尚待实现。

Method: 构建原子级薄 LED，通过边缘增强的 fringe 电场将电注入的层间激子局域在样品边缘并产生电致发光（EL）；采用 Hanbury-Brown-Twiss 光子-光子关联测量，并辅以多体量子主方程（many-body quantum master equation）建模非相干注入 IXs 的稳态超辐射（superradiant）光子统计。

Result: 观测到集体衰减的显著特征：大幅光子聚束（photon bunching）之后出现有限延迟的反聚束（antibunching）与聚束复现（bunching revivals）。当自由载流子掺杂形成简并费米海（degenerate Fermi sea）时这些关联特征消失，而引入局域电子后则被增强，结果与量子主方程模型一致。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了二维 TMD LED 中的稳态协同发光，可作为研究电驱动、非平衡玻色-费米混合物（Bose-Fermi mixtures）相中强光-物质相互作用的平台。

Abstract: When an ensemble of emitters is confined within an optical wavelength, its radiation can become correlated, leading to cooperative emission. In two-dimensional (2D) transition metal dichalcogenide (TMD) electron-hole bilayers, strong light-matter coupling enables the formation of interlayer excitons (IXs), long-lived dipolar excitations that provide an ideal setting for emergent collective phenomena. Here, we engineer an atomically thin light-emitting diode (LED) that generates electroluminescence (EL) from electrically injected IXs that are confined near the sample edges by an edge-enhanced fringing electric field. By measuring Hanbury-Brown-Twiss photon-photon correlations, we find pronounced signatures of collective decay in the form of large photon bunching followed by finite delay antibunching and bunching revivals. These emitter correlation signatures disappear when free charge carriers are doped to form a degenerate Fermi sea but are enhanced by the introduction of localized electrons. The results are consistent with a many-body quantum master equation that models the steady-state superradiant photon statistics of incoherently injected IXs. Our work establishes steady-state cooperative emission in a 2D TMD LED as a platform for studying strong light-matter interactions in electrically driven, non-equilibrium phases of Bose-Fermi mixtures.

</details>
