### [144] [Electronic driving of torsional modes in carbon nanotubes via orbital-phonon coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28326)
*Koji Yamada,Wataru Izumida,Mamoru Matsuo,Takeo Kato*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文从理论上研究悬浮碳纳米管量子点（suspended carbon nanotube quantum dot）中扭转模式（torsional mode）的电子驱动机制：谷轨道角动量与扭转角速度之间的轨道旋转耦合（orbital-rotation coupling），在谷态劈裂与扭转声子能量匹配时可将该模式驱离平衡态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米管中的谷态（valley states）沿管轴携带相反轨道磁矩，区间谷混合（intervalley mixing）形成的本征态劈裂可由轴向磁场调控，这为用电子输运驱动机械扭转自由度提供了可能，但相关耦合与驱动机制尚缺乏理论刻画。

Method: 建立扭转角速度与谷轨道角动量之间的轨道旋转耦合；采用顺序隧穿（sequential tunneling）通过正常金属电极的主方程（master equation），计算稳态电流与扭转模式的声子分布（phonon distribution）。

Result: 当谷态劈裂与扭转声子能量匹配时，谷轨道跃迁可驱动该模式偏离平衡；电极接触同时控制声子泵浦（phonon pumping）与电学读出，可实现显著激发，并对应正、零或负的电流响应。

Conclusion: 该机制不依赖电极的本征自旋或谷极化（spin or valley polarization），而是利用接触处的态依赖隧穿（state-dependent tunneling），从而实现扭转模式的电学驱动与探测。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate electronic driving of a torsional mode in a suspended carbon nanotube quantum dot. The valley states in a nanotube carry opposite orbital magnetic moments along the tube axis, and intervalley mixing forms valley-mixed eigenstates whose splitting can be controlled by an axial magnetic field. We formulate an orbital-rotation coupling between the torsional angular velocity and the valley orbital angular momentum. Using a master equation for sequential tunneling through normal-metal electrodes, we calculate the steady-state current and the phonon distribution of the torsional mode. When the valley-state splitting matches the torsional phonon energy, orbital transitions can drive the mode out of equilibrium. The contacts control both phonon pumping and its electrical readout, allowing substantial excitation with a positive, vanishing, or negative current response. The mechanism requires no intrinsic spin or valley polarization of the electrodes, but uses state-dependent tunneling at the contacts.

</details>


### [145] [Spin-wave circulation in unbounded ferromagnetic film](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28334)
*Jan Klima,Daniel Stoeffler,Andres Sarmiento,Kevin Weber,Loic Temdie,Aurélie Solignac,Roberto Fenollosa,Yves Henry,Matthieu Bailleul,Vincent Vlaminck*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作实验证明了利用硬磁性球形微粒的杂散场在连续YIG薄膜中构建径向对称磁化景观，使自旋波沿绕粒子的闭合圆形路径传播，并通过微磁模拟加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋波计算（spin-wave computing）的关键难题在于引导自旋波通过电路时避免波形畸变或反射，这通常导致一维传播（1D propagation）情形，限制了其在二维磁子电路（2D magnonic circuits）中的应用。

Method: 在平坦YIG薄膜（YIG film）上放置硬磁性球形微粒（hard magnetic spherical microparticle），利用其杂散场（stray field）产生径向对称的磁化景观（magnetization landscape）；同时开展微磁模拟（micromagnetic simulations）复现实验条件。

Result: 实验观察到自旋波被引导至绕微粒的闭合路径；微磁模拟确认自旋波可围绕微粒循环数圈（several loops）。

Conclusion: 研究表明利用非均匀磁化景观（inhomogeneous magnetic landscape）可将自旋波导向圆形路径，并为可扩展的自旋波信息处理技术（scalable spin-wave-based information processing technologies）提供支持。

Abstract: A key challenge in spin-wave computing is steering spin waves through circuits without wave distortion or reflections. This often results in 1D propagation scenarios, which hinder applicability in 2D magnonic circuits. We demonstrate experimentally a novel approach using a hard magnetic spherical microparticle to guide spin-waves onto a closed path in a plain thin YIG film. The particle stray field induces a radially symmetrical magnetization landscape, which allows spin-waves to loop around. In parallel, we conducted micromagnetic simulations reproducing the experimental conditions, which confirm the circulation of spin-waves over several loops around the particle. This work demonstrates the possibility to steer spin-waves onto a circular path using inhomogeneous magnetic landscape, as well as advancing scalable spin-wave-based information processing technologies.

</details>


### [146] [Orbital-angular-momentum control of localized Floquet states and photocurrents in Weyl semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28396)
*Kyle S. Netherwood,Juan I. Aranzadi,Nils Jacobsen,Michael A. Sentef,Sang Soon Oh*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文基于实空间理论，研究涡旋光束驱动的外尔半金属（Weyl semimetal）中由光轨道角动量（orbital angular momentum, OAM）诱导的局域 Floquet 涡旋态及其光电流，表明 OAM 可作为调控局域载流态的参量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化光（structured light）不仅通过偏振，还可通过空间分布与 OAM 调控量子材料，但缺乏描述此类空间结构光驱动外尔半金属的实空间理论。

Method: 构建涡旋光束驱动外尔半金属的实空间理论：利用柱对称性导出类 Bogoliubov-de Gennes（Bogoliubov-de Gennes-like）径向方程，按角动量分解态；并以保留完整二维空间结构的 Wilson 晶格（Wilson-lattice）形式补充连续描述，用于直接计算局域光电流。

Result: 两种方法一致识别出 OAM 诱导、局域于光束轴附近的涡旋态；拉盖尔-高斯（Laguerre-Gaussian）光束下空间变化驱动引起局域涡旋态与体态的强杂化；晶格计算显示其环流电流密度显著强于扩展的表面态，且最强电流密度对应最局域化、低角动量的涡旋模。

Conclusion: 光 OAM 可作为在外尔体系中产生与操控局域载流态的可调控制参量，并提供了研究空间结构光产生光电流的实空间框架。

Abstract: Structured light provides a route to controlling quantum materials not only through its polarization, but also through its spatial profile and orbital angular momentum (OAM). We develop real space theories of a Weyl semimetal driven by a vortex beam and show that optical OAM generates highly localized Floquet vortex states with pronounced circulating photocurrents. Exploiting cylindrical symmetry, we derive a Bogoliubov-de Gennes-like radial equation that resolves the states by angular momentum and reveals their evolution between the light-dressed Weyl cones. For Laguerre-Gaussian beams, the spatially varying drive produces strong hybridization between localized vortex states and bulk states. We complement this continuum description with a Wilson-lattice formulation that retains the full two-dimensional spatial structure and enables direct calculation of local photocurrents. Both approaches consistently identify OAM-induced vortex states localized around the beam axis. The lattice calculation further shows that these states support substantially stronger circulating current densities than the more extended surface states, with the largest current densities associated with the most strongly localized, low-angular-momentum vortex modes. Our results establish optical OAM as a tunable control parameter for creating and manipulating localized current-carrying states in Weyl systems and provide a real-space framework for studying photocurrents generated by spatially structured light.

</details>


### [147] [Quantum Formulation of the Spinterface Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28406)
*Seif Alwan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在非平衡格林函数（NEGF）框架下构建了手性诱导自旋选择性（CISS）效应的 spinterface 模型全量子表述，将自旋流（spin current）严格分解为偶（非手性）与奇（手性）两部分，并指出奇部在少于四个位点时精确为零，从而提供无需可调参数的判据以区分真正手性信号与非手性自旋阀机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CISS 效应虽已有大量实验报道，但缺乏区分"真正手性贡献"与"非手性自旋阀（spin-valve）机制"的清晰理论判据；作者希望借助界面磁矩（interfacial magnetic moment）与手性 Rashba 自旋轨道耦合（Rashba spin-orbit coupling）的协同作用，给出可检验的定量划分。

Method: 构建由承载局域磁矩的金原子、具 Rashba 自旋轨道耦合的手性分子链及两个非磁性电极组成的 junction，在 NEGF 形式下由非平衡电子分布自洽确定磁矩，并由平均场哈密顿量（mean-field Hamiltonian）计算自旋分辨透射（spin-resolved transmission）。

Result: 界面磁矩与手性 Rashba 项协同产生大电流极化，复现高极化、四位点阈值出现、随链长受干涉调制的增长、热抑制（thermal suppression）及手性反转时符号反号等 CISS 关键实验特征；核心新结果为自旋流分解 $I_{S_z}(\phi)=I_{S_z}^{\rm even}(\phi)+I_{S_z}^{\rm odd}(\phi)$，其中奇部在固定 $m_z$ 下于少于四位点的链上精确为零，四位点几何（一个金原子加三个分子位点）为最小非零情形。

Conclusion: 奇部源于同时满足手性、磁性非对称（磁矩仅在金端）且含两条及以上 Rashba 活性键的器件中的互易性破缺（reciprocity breaking）；该分解提供了一个直接、无需可调参数的判据，用于判定观测到的自旋信号是否真正源于手性而非非手性自旋阀机制。

Abstract: We present a fully quantum formulation of the spinterface model for the chirality-induced spin selectivity (CISS) effect, implemented within the non-equilibrium Green's function (NEGF) formalism. The model consists of a gold atom hosting a localised magnetic moment, coupled to a chiral molecular chain with Rashba spin-orbit coupling, and contacted by two non-magnetic leads. The magnetic moment is determined self-consistently from the non-equilibrium electronic distribution, and the spin-resolved transmission is computed from the mean-field Hamiltonian. We show that the interfacial magnetic moment and the chiral Rashba term cooperate to produce large current polarisations that reproduce the key experimental signatures of CISS: high polarisation, a threshold emergence at four total sites followed by interference-modulated growth with chain length, thermal suppression, and sign reversal upon chirality inversion. The central new result of this work is a clean separation of the CISS effect into an even (achiral) and an odd (chiral) contribution to the spin current, $I_{S_z}(φ) = I_{S_z}^{\rm even}(φ) + I_{S_z}^{\rm odd}(φ)$. We demonstrate that $I_{S_z}^{\rm odd}$ vanishes exactly at fixed $m_z$ for chains of fewer than four sites and becomes non-zero for chains of four or more sites, with the smallest junction exhibiting a non-zero odd part being the four-site geometry (one gold atom plus three molecular sites). The odd part arises from reciprocity breaking in a device that is simultaneously chiral, magnetically asymmetric (moment on the gold end only), and long enough to support two or more Rashba-active bonds. This provides a direct, parameter-free test of whether an observed spin signal is genuinely chiral or arises from an achiral spin-valve mechanism.

</details>


### [148] [Grain-Boundary Scattering between Chiral Domains in Chiral Topological Semimetal CoSi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28426)
*Nicholas A. Lanzillo,Ion Garate,Ching-Tzu Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 采用第一性原理量子输运计算，比较CoSi薄膜中孪晶（异手性）与非孪晶（同手性）晶界对电子散射的影响，发现孪晶晶界电阻更高，且非孪晶晶界的表面导电贡献更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性拓扑半金属（chiral topological semimetals）在下一代纳米互连（nano-interconnects）中具有应用潜力，但其晶界处电子输运的复杂性尚待阐明，尤其是不同手性晶界对散射的作用。

Method: 基于第一性原理量子输运计算（first-principles quantum transport calculations），评估CoSi薄膜中孪晶（twin，异手性/heterochiral）与非孪晶（non-twin，同手性/homochiral）晶界的电子散射性质，并结合透射本征态（transmission eigenstate）分布进行分析。

Result: 跨越孪晶晶界的散射比非孪晶晶界更具电阻性；非孪晶晶界的表面导电（surface conduction）占比高于孪晶晶界。透射本征态显示，表面费米弧（Fermi-arc）电子可跨越同一表面上同手性畴间的非孪晶晶界而无显著背散射；而在异手性畴间的孪晶晶界处则被散射至对侧表面，产生部分前向透射与部分背散射（backscattering），导致更高晶界电阻率。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了手性拓扑半金属中电子输运的复杂性，为其在下一代纳米互连中的潜在应用提供了依据。

Abstract: We evaluate the electron scattering properties in CoSi thin films having either twin (heterochiral) or non-twin (homochiral) grain boundaries using first-principles quantum transport calculations. We find that scattering across a twin grain boundary is more resistive than scattering across a non-twin grain boundary and that non-twin grain boundaries have a greater percentage of surface conduction than twin grain boundaries. This is corroborated by the transmission eigenstate profiles, which show that the surface Fermi-arc electrons can transmit past the non-twin grain boundaries between homochiral domains on the same surface without significant backscattering. In contrast, these surface electrons get scattered along the twin grain boundaries between heterochiral domains to the opposite surface, resulting in partial forward transmission and partial backscattering that yields higher grain-boundary resistivity. This work helps elucidate the complexities of electron transport in chiral topological semimetals for potential application in next-generation nano-interconnects.

</details>


<div id='physics.ins-det'></div>

# physics.ins-det [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [Pulse Shape Model for a Light Enhanced Phonon Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28161)
*G. Angloher,M. R. Bharadwaj,A. Böhmer,S. Braun,M. R. Cababie,I. Colantoni,I. Dafinei,N. Di Marco,C. Dittmar,F. Ferella,F. Ferroni,S. Fichtinger,A. Filipponi,M. Friedl,D. Fuchs,L. Gai,M. Gapp,M. Heikinheimo,K. Heim,M. N. Hughes,K. Huitu,M. Kellermann,A. Mancuso,L. Pagnanini,F. Petricca,C. Petrucci,S. Pirro,F. Pröbst,G. Profeta,A. Puiu,F. Reindl,K. Schäffner,J. Schieck,P. Schreiner,C. Schwertner,P. Settembri,K. Shera,M. Stahlberg,A. Stendahl,M. Stukel,C. Tresca,S. Yue,V. Zema,Y. Zhu,L. Ziegele,N. Zimmermann,O. Schulz*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: COSINUS 提出新的脉冲形状模型，解释复合 NaI 低温闪烁量热计（cryogenic scintillating calorimeter）的异常脉冲形状，并据此发展出远程 TES（remoTES）声子探测器设计，首次实现单通道声子探测器的粒子甄别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合设计的 NaI 原型探测器测得脉冲形状偏离已知低温量热计描述，且中子刻度（neutron calibration）中未显现可辨识的核反冲（nuclear recoil）群体，需要物理解释以指导最终探测器设计。

Method: 建立新的脉冲形状模型，指出原型中存在由载流子晶体（carrier）光吸收导致信号增强的声子通道，即光增强声子探测器（light enhanced phonon detector）；并采用基于贝叶斯推断软件包 BAT.jl 的脉冲形状拟合方法分析数据。

Result: 新模型可解释此前的脉冲形状偏离与核反冲群体缺失现象，并构成远程 TES（remoTES）最终声子探测器设计的基础；该设计首次提供以单通道声子探测器进行粒子甄别（particle discrimination）的可能。

Conclusion: 复合探测器实为光增强声子探测器，其信号增强机制解释了既往观测；该认识支撑了 remoTES 设计，并配合 BAT.jl 贝叶斯拟合方法给出最新结果。

Abstract: COSINUS has tested prototype NaI-based cryogenic scintillating calorimeters with a composite design, in which the transition edge sensor (TES) is fabricated on a separate carrier crystal coupled to the NaI target. Measurements with the composite detectors showed deviations from the known pulse shape description for cryogenic calorimeters and did not reveal an identifiable nuclear recoil population during a neutron calibration. In this work we present a new pulse shape model which provides an explanation to these observations and that laid the foundation for the development of the final COSINUS phonon detector design named remote TES (remoTES). We demonstrate that the first COSINUS detector prototypes featured a phonon channel with signal enhancement by light absorption in the carrier, namely a light enhanced phonon detector. This design offers for the first time the chance to use a single channel phonon detector for particle discrimination. We present recent results using a novel pulse shape fitting method based on the Bayesian inference software package BAT.jl.

</details>


### [150] [Variable-Resolution Scattering Reveals Ensemble Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28215)
*Uri Steinitz,Mathar Kravikass,Gil Koren,Roy Beck*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: TENOR-SAXS 通过对单张二维散射图像施加两次各向异性数字展宽（anisotropic digital smearing），从展宽图像间比较中提取传统径向曲线无法给出的曲率信息，从而在一次测量中同时估算回转半径（radius of gyration, Rg）分布的散射加权均值与宽度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统低角散射仅能给出表观回转半径，其值混入了集合方差（ensemble variance）贡献，无法分别解析散射加权均值与分布宽度；且要还原真实均值本身也必须已知分布宽度，因此需要一种不依赖显式仪器去展宽（desmearing）即可获得宽度的方法。

Method: 对单张二维 SAXS 图像施加两种各向异性数字展宽并比较展宽后图像，所得到的可观测量无需天然仪器点扩散函数（point-spread function）的显式模型；在低角与窄分布极限下，针对已知或独立约束的单粒子形状因子（form factor）推导解析表达式，并用多种形状因子与不同尺寸分布集合的模拟散射数据加以验证。

Result: 模拟结果与解析理论吻合良好；主可观测量在最低阶上只依赖散射加权均值与方差，而非分布函数形式或更高阶矩；在对应生物样品可行同步辐射曝光时间的模拟数据中，分布宽度（以均值分数表示）恢复的典型偏差约 0.1，散射加权平均 Rg 的相对偏差约 2%；且对典型天然分辨率范围内仅呈弱敏感性。

Conclusion: TENOR-SAXS 为从现有二维散射数据估算集合宽度提供了无需显式仪器去展宽的框架，并可用于校正方差对表观回转半径的贡献。

Abstract: SAXS/SANS determine the size and structure of particles and macromolecules in solution. However, the conventional low-angle determination of the radius of gyration, Rg, cannot separately resolve its scattering-weighted mean and the width of its distribution. Because the apparent Rg includes a contribution from ensemble variance, the distribution width is required even to recover the mean Rg. Here, we introduce TENOR-SAXS, a method for estimating the scattering-weighted width of an Rg distribution by applying two anisotropic digital smearings to a single 2D scattering image. Comparing the smeared images provides curvature information unavailable from the conventional radial profile. The resulting observables require no explicit model of the native instrumental point-spread function and show only weak sensitivity over the range of native resolutions typical of SAXS measurements examined here. Analytical expressions are derived in the low-angle and narrow-distribution limits for ensembles described by a known or independently constrained single-particle form factor. For verification, we simulated scattering by particles with several form factors and ensembles with different size distributions, all agree well with the analytical theory. To leading order, the principal observable depends on the scattering-weighted mean and variance rather than on the distribution's functional form or higher moments. For simulated data corresponding to feasible synchrotron exposure times for biological samples, the distribution width and, expressed as a fraction of its mean, was recovered with a typical discrepancy of ~0.1, while the scattering-weighted mean Rg was recovered with ~2% relative discrepancy. TENOR-SAXS thus provides a framework for estimating ensemble width from existing 2D scattering data without explicit instrumental desmearing and for correcting the variance contribution to the apparent radius

</details>


<div id='math.CA'></div>

# math.CA [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [Spherical harmonics and infinitesimal projective transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28320)
*Yury A. Neretin*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 研究二维球面 $S^2$ 上光滑函数空间按极小旋转不变子空间 $\oplus\mathcal H_m$ 的分解，将元素视为 $(m,u)$ 两个变量的函数，并构造了对应于 $\mathrm{GL}(3,\mathbb R)$ 李代数作用的无穷次可微算子类 $\mathcal W^\infty$ 中的算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\mathrm{GL}(3,\mathbb R)$ 在李代数层面通过沿向量场的微分作用于 $C^\infty(S^2)$，需要在这些微分作用与球谐空间分解的坐标表示之间建立显式的算子实现。

Method: 以球面函数空间的极小旋转不变空间直和分解为基础，把元素表示为非负整数 $m$ 与复变量 $u$ 的函数（$m=k$ 时 $u$ 的次数不超过 $2k$ 的多项式），进而构造 $\mathcal W^\infty$ 中对应上述微分作用的算子。

Result: 得到了 $\mathcal W^\infty$ 中的一族微分-差分算子（differential-difference operators），其表达式包含平移 $m\mapsto m\pm 2$ 以及关于 $u$ 的四阶导数。

Conclusion: 该构造将 $\mathrm{GL}(3,\mathbb R)$ 李代数在球面光滑函数上的微分作用转化为 $(m,u)$ 坐标下的显式微分-差分算子，给出了球谐分解与李代数作用之间的算子对应。

Abstract: Consider the space of $C^\infty$-functions on the two-dimensional sphere $S^2$ and its decomposition $\oplus\mathcal H_m$ into a direct sum of minimal rotation-invariant spaces. We consider elements of $\oplus\mathcal H_m$ as functions of two variables, a nonnegative integer variable $m$ and a complex variable $u$ (a restriction of such function to a set $m=k$ is a polynomial in $u$ of degree $\le 2k$). The group $\mathrm{GL}\,(3,{\mathbb R})$ of all matrices of order 3 acts on $S^2$, so its Lie algebra acts in $C^\infty(S^2)$ by differentiations along vector fields. We construct operators in $\mathcal W^\infty$ corresponding such differentiations and obtain a collection of differential-difference operators in $\mathcal W^\infty$. The expressions include shifts $m\mapsto m\pm 2$ and derivatives of order 4 in $u$.

</details>


<div id='nlin.CD'></div>

# nlin.CD [[Back]](#toc)

### [152] [Physics-Inspired Optimizers beyond the Edge of Stability: Period-Two Orbits and Delayed Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27321)
*Alexander Itin*

Main category: nlin.CD

TL;DR: 论文将动量梯度下降视为损失景观（loss landscape）中的含摩擦粒子运动，研究以动量的动能梯度作为步长的动理学映射（kinetic map）在稳定性边缘（edge of stability, EoS）之外的行为，并结合慢扫掠分岔理论解释训练动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习常用较大学习率，训练呈现离散动力系统特征，sharpness 常增长至优化器稳定极限（EoS）并在该处持续训练；物理启发式优化器（如 VRAdam）会改变这一运动，但其在 EoS 之外的机制尚不清楚。

Method: 在约化模型中分析 kinetic map，引入弹性（elasticity，步长对动量的双对数斜率）刻画稳定性；用 Neishtadt 慢扫掠分岔（slowly swept bifurcations）理论解释 sharpness 缓升导致的失稳延迟；并在精确二次问题、全批量 MLP、ResNet-32 与 ResNet-18 上做实验验证。

Result: 约化模型中 kinetic map 在边缘外产生对称二周期轨道，当且仅当弹性介于 0 与 1 之间时严格线性稳定；VRAdam 在临界学习率两倍以上失效（动能非凸），而其拉格朗日量的 Legendre 对偶（Legendre dual）为凸且在各学习率下均通过；受控扫掠网络中的动力学与理论无拟合参数吻合，含扫掠起点记忆及噪声擦除；带动量时大动量处的弹性决定能否进入振荡，β1=0.9 时步长有界映射已存在周期四与混沌吸引子，而三次-Legendre 映射在 β1 达 0.99 的各起点均可到达振荡。

Conclusion: 实验支持该理论，但 ResNet-18 上映射的表观收益很大程度可由匹配有效学习率（effective learning rate）复现，提示部分增益并非来自映射本身。

Abstract: Gradient descent with momentum can be viewed as a particle moving with friction in the loss landscape. In deep learning the learning rate is usually large, so training is a discrete dynamical system: in many full-batch experiments the sharpness grows to the optimizer's stability limit, the edge of stability (EoS), and training continues there. Physics-inspired optimizers change this motion; Velocity-Regularized Adam (VRAdam), for example, shrinks the step at high speed. We study kinetic maps, in which the step is the gradient of a kinetic energy of the momentum, and ask what they do beyond the EoS. (i) In a reduced model, a kinetic map creates a symmetric period-two orbit beyond the edge, strictly linearly stable if and only if the map's elasticity (the log-log slope of step length versus momentum) lies between 0 and 1; on it, training sits at an effective edge of stability. VRAdam's damper fails above twice the critical learning rate, where its kinetic energy is not convex; the Legendre dual of VRAdam's own Lagrangian is convex and passes at every learning rate. (ii) Because the sharpness rises gradually, the loss of stability is delayed, as in Neishtadt's theory of slowly swept bifurcations. In a network with a controlled sweep, the dynamics match this theory without fitted parameters, including the memory of where the sweep started and its erasure by noise. (iii) With momentum, reaching the oscillation also requires elasticity at large momentum: maps that bound the step have competing period-four and chaotic attractors already at beta1 = 0.9, while the cubic-Legendre map reached the oscillation from every sampled start up to beta1 = 0.99. Experiments on an exact quadratic problem, a full-batch MLP and ResNet-32 support the theory; on ResNet-18, much of the apparent benefit of the maps is reproduced by matching the effective learning rate.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.soft'></div>

# cond-mat.soft [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [Density Accelerated Relaxation in Active Passive Colloidal Networks with Competing Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27207)
*Pronay Dutta,Hima Nagamanasa Kandula*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究考察具有电场介导非同类相互作用（异种吸引、同种排斥）的准二维活性-被动态胶体混合物（active-passive colloidal mixtures），给出活性渗流网络（percolated network）形成区间，并发现该区间内弛豫随密度增加而加快的反常现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致密颗粒凝胶（particulate gels）通常表现为密度越高弛豫越慢，而活性与被动组分共存、且相互作用非对称的体系其稳态与动力学规律尚不明确，故需厘清其状态图与弛豫行为。

Method: 构建准二维（quasi-2D）活性-被动态胶体混合物，施加电场介导的非同类相互作用（异种吸引、同种排斥），在总面积分数与活性颗粒面积分数两个维度上映射稳态，并结合颗粒分辨动力学（particle-resolved dynamics）与微观结构分析。

Result: 状态映射识别出活性流体（active fluid）、团聚态（clustered states）以及活性渗流网络形成的参数区间；在渗流网络区间内，弛豫速率随密度升高而增大，与致密颗粒凝胶的预期相反。

Conclusion: 颗粒分辨的动力学与微结构证据表明，该加速源于非对称相互作用所促成的活性驱动重排（activity-driven rearrangements），而非致密化导致的动力学阻滞。

Abstract: We investigate quasi-2D active-passive colloidal mixtures with electric field-mediated non-identical interactions: unlike species attract and like species repel. Mapping the steady states across total area fraction and active particle area fraction identifies the regime of active percolated network formation among active fluid and clustered states. Within the percolated network regime, relaxation speeds up with increasing density, contrary to expectations for dense particulate gels. Particle-resolved dynamics and microstructure link this acceleration to activity-driven rearrangements enabled by asymmetric interactions.

</details>


### [154] [Soft elasticity of soft shells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27263)
*Andre M. Sonnet,Epifanio G. Virga*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文识别出Sonnet与Virga（2025）变分理论所描述的软壳（soft shells）的全部软模（soft modes），并证明其与Bonnet族（Bonnet families）严格相关，同时给出软模存在的判据与基于路径积分级联（cascade of path integrations）的构造方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软壳的软模是保持弹性能不变的单参数非平凡变形族，可描述理想情况下无需能量激活的软壳涨落（fluctuations）的产生方式；连续介质力学（continuum mechanics）关注软变形模式及其遍历的构形，与微分几何（differential geometry）将同一Bonnet族内曲面视为等价的立场不同。

Method: 在Sonnet与Virga（2025）提出的变分理论框架内，识别软壳的全部软模，并将其与Bonnet问题（即是否存在保持平均曲率（mean curvature）的非平凡等距（isometries））的解类——Bonnet族相联系；进而提出软模存在的一般判据及基于路径积分级联的构造方法，并应用于若干示例。

Result: 软模被证明与Bonnet族严格关联；所提出的判据与构造方法在若干示例情形中得到应用与验证（摘要未给出具体数值或定量结果）。

Conclusion: 软壳的软变形模式与Bonnet族存在本质联系，所建立的判据与路径积分级联方法为软模的判定与构造提供了系统途径，有助于理解软壳中零能激活的涨落机制。

Abstract: Soft modes of a shell are one-parameter families of non-trivial deformations that preserve the elastic energy. We identify all soft modes of the soft shells described by the variational theory of put forward by Sonnet and Virga (2025). They are seen to be strictly connected with the Bonnet families, a special class of solutions to the problem posited by Bonnet, who asked whether there exist non-trivial isometries that preserve the mean curvature. Differential geometry deems as equivalent all surfaces in one and the same Bonnet family, but continuum mechanics is interested more specifically in soft deformation modes and the shapes they traverse, as they may describe ways to ignite fluctuations of soft shells that ideally require no energy to be activated. We give a general criterion for the existence of soft modes and a method to construct them based on a cascade of path integrations. We apply both criterion and method to some exemplary cases.

</details>


### [155] [Structure and Dynamics of Defects in Two-Dimensional Nematic Liquid Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27278)
*Cody D. Schimming,Daniel A. Beller*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文综述二维向列相液晶中向错（disclination）结构与动力学的解析理论，重点介绍近期在复杂边界条件、各向异性弹性（anisotropic elasticity）及流动存在等复杂情形下的分析进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向列相液晶中的拓扑缺陷（topological defects）在非平衡活性物质（active materials）与发育中的生物系统中具有重要作用，因而重新受到关注；已有研究多集中于非平衡情形下的缺陷行为模拟，而复杂区域中缺陷形貌与行为的解析理解近期亦有进展，故有必要予以综述。

Method: 采用综述（review）方式，梳理二维向列相液晶中向错结构与动力学的解析理论（analytical theory），并突出近期发展的方法与结果。

Result: 总结指出近期在若干复杂情形下——包括复杂边界条件（intricate boundary conditions）、各向异性弹性、以及流动（flows）的存在——对缺陷形貌与行为的解析认识取得了进展，并归纳了相应新方法与新结果。

Conclusion: 二维向列相液晶中向错结构与动力学的解析理论已在复杂边界、各向异性弹性与流动等复杂区域取得新进展，为理解非平衡活性体系与生物系统中的缺陷行为提供了解析基础。

Abstract: Topological defects in nematic liquid crystals have recently received renewed interest due to their important role in non-equilibrium active materials and developing biological systems. While many studies have focused on simulating defect behaviors in these non-equilibrium situations, there has also been recent progress in our analytical understanding of defect morphology and behavior in a number of complicated regimes, such as intricate boundary conditions, anisotropic elasticity, or the presence of flows. Here, we review the analytical theory of disclination structure and dynamics in two-dimensional nematic liquid crystals, highlighting recent methods and results.

</details>


### [156] [Intrinsic Curvature Controls Spontaneous Entanglement of Elastic Rods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27342)
*Fani Derveni,Javier Sabater,Pedro M. Reis*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 实验研究了内禀曲率（intrinsic curvature）对翻滚弹性杆自发缠结（spontaneous entanglement）的影响：自然曲率对打结（knotting）呈非单调的主导作用，而锁死（locking）主要随杆长变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究本征曲率在弹性杆自发缠结过程中的作用，并区分打结与锁死两种缠结机制，为DNA等微观丝状体系的缠结过程提供几何参数依据。

Method: 对直杆与螺旋杆构成的统计系综（statistical ensemble）施加受控翻滚（controlled tumbling），比较不同弧长与自然曲率下的两种缠结机制。

Result: 自然曲率对打结具有主导且非单调的影响，存在抑制成结的临界曲率（critical curvature），使成结概率显著低于等效直杆；锁死对曲率基本不敏感，主要随杆长变化。

Conclusion: 识别出控制不同缠结模式（entanglement modes）的几何参数，结论限于所研究的宏观实验体系，或可启发对DNA等微观丝状体系缠结过程的理解。

Abstract: We report results from an experimental investigation on the role of intrinsic curvature in the spontaneous entanglement of elastic rods subjected to controlled tumbling. We examine two distinct entanglement mechanisms, knotting and locking, in a statistical ensemble of straight and helical rods with varying arc length and natural curvature. Our primary finding is that natural curvature exerts a dominant, non-monotonic influence on knotting, with a critical curvature that suppresses knot formation and significantly reduces the probability of knotting relative to equivalent straight rods. In contrast, locking is largely insensitive to curvature and scales primarily with rod length. These results, obtained for the macroscopic experimental system studied here, identify geometric parameters that govern distinct entanglement modes in tumbled elastic rods and may inform entanglement processes in microscopic filamentary systems such as DNA.

</details>


### [157] [Emergent Active-Blob Length Scale Governs the Collapse Transition of Tangentially Driven Polymers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27787)
*Baptiste Parage,Sara Jabbari-Farouji*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文利用布朗动力学模拟（Brownian dynamics simulations）研究切向驱动（tangentially driven）活性聚合物在链内吸引强度与活性变化下的线圈-小球转变（coil-to-globule transition），发现极性活性引入新的折叠长度尺度并给出普适主曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡态下线圈-小球转变可由在Θ条件处发散的熱团长度（thermal blob length）解释，但活性（activity）如何修正该构象转变及相关基本长度尺度仍不清楚。

Method: 采用布朗动力学模拟（Brownian dynamics simulations），对切向驱动聚合物系统性改变链内吸引强度（intrachain attraction strength）与活性强度。

Result: 极性活性（polar activity）使从溶胀到球状构象的交叉过渡变得平滑；在自回避（self-avoiding）与Θ条件之间，活性主导并引入作为活性团长度（active blob length）的新兴折叠长度尺度；热长度与活性长度之比构成新的标度变量（scaling variable），给出活性聚合物塌缩的普适主曲线（universal master curve）。

Conclusion: 活性引入的额外长度尺度重构了聚合物塌缩的标度描述，热/活性长度之比可作为统一刻画活性聚合物构象转变的标度变量。

Abstract: The coil-to-globule transition is a paradigmatic phenomenon in polymer physics and biophysics, in which polymer chains evolve from swollen to globular conformations upon decreasing solvent quality. At equilibrium, this transition can be rationalized in terms of a thermal blob length that diverges at the so-called $Θ$ condition, marking the crossover from self-avoiding to globular conformations. How activity modifies this conformational transition and affects the fundamental length scales at play remains poorly understood. Here, we address this question for tangentially driven polymers using Brownian dynamics simulations, systematically varying both intrachain attraction strength and activity. We find that polar activity smears out the crossover from swollen to globular conformations. Between self-avoiding and $Θ$ conditions, activity dominates by introducing an emergent folding length scale, which acts as an active blob length. In this regime, we demonstrate that the ratio of thermal to active length scales provides a new scaling variable that leads to a universal master curve for active polymer collapse.

</details>


### [158] [Emergent non-reciprocal pair interactions in catalytically propelled active polyhedra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27821)
*Silvana A. Caipa Cure,Sander C. Kammeraat,Jerome Hem,Silke Henkes,Daniela J. Kraft*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究构建了可实验观测的活性胶体多面体体系，结合随机力推断（Stochastic Force Inference）测得主要为径向、非互易的粒子对相互作用，并用最小活性布朗粒子模型揭示其物理起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子形状对活性粒子动力学与对相互作用至关重要，但针对各向异性活性粒子的实验测量严重缺乏，亟需建立可定量测量其有效对相互作用的实验体系。

Method: 通过3D微打印制备边长数N=3,4,5,6及无穷（圆盘）的准二维多面体胶体，部分包覆铱催化帽并分散于肼中实现自驱动；利用随机力推断（Stochastic Force Inference）提取对相互作用，并以圆盘形最小泳动活性布朗粒子模型进行对照。

Result: 所有形状的单粒子位置动力学相似；在中心距约两个粒子直径处，对相互作用以径向、非互易为主，兼具吸引与排斥区间。模型复现实验行为并识别出物理机制：浓度依赖的减速决定主导角对称性，化学耦合的空间几何与泳动吸引决定相对幅度和角度依赖；形状各向异性仅调制这些相互作用的幅度与角度依赖，且各向异性越强变化越显著。

Conclusion: 结果展示了在中等相互作用距离上化学介导相互作用与粒子几何诱导效应之间的相互竞争与耦合。

Abstract: Particle shape plays a fundamental role in the dynamics and pair interactions of active particles. Yet, experimental measurements of anisotropic active particles are largely lacking. Here, we introduce an experimental system that allows us to observe and measure the effective pair interactions of self-propelled active colloidal polyhedra. We realize such a system by 3D micro-printing quasi- 2D polyhedral colloids with edge number N= 3,4,5,6 and infinity and induce propulsion by partially coating them with an iridium catalytic cap and dispersing them in hydrazine. Despite their different geometries, all shapes exhibit similar single-particle positional dynamics. Stochastic Force Inference reveals predominantly radial, non-reciprocal pair interactions with similar attractive and repulsive regimes for all particle shapes at center-to center distances of about two particle diameters. A minimal plausible phoretic active-Brownian-particle model with a disc shape reproduces the experimentally observed behavior and identifies the physical origins: concentration-dependent slowing down sets their dominant angular symmetry, while the spatial geometry of the chemical coupling and phoretic attraction recover their relative magnitudes and angular dependence. We find that the shape anisotropy present in experiments only modulates the magnitudes and angular dependence of these interactions, and that these variations become more pronounced the stronger the anisotropy. Our results demonstrate the interplay of chemically mediated interactions and particle geometry-induced effects at intermediate interaction distances.

</details>


### [159] [Programmable nonlinear rotation of acoustically levitated chiral stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27927)
*Sue Shi,Maximilian C. Hübl,Aarnav Panda,Thorfinn Barton,Carl P. Goodrich,Scott R. Waitukaitis*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究以"手性星形（chiral stars）"为模型体系，揭示了几何形状（手性、臂数）与声场幅值如何共同决定声悬浮（acoustic levitation）物体的自发旋转，并建立了一个可预测旋转方向反转的极简力矩模型（minimal torque model）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声悬浮中的物体（液滴、薄膜、粒子聚集体）常发生自发旋转，但其旋转是否发生、方向如何、随声幅如何变化缺乏一般性理解；现有认识未能说明物体形状如何决定这些行为。

Method: 构建"手性星形"形状族，系统改变手性与臂数；在对称声场中测量旋转响应，并结合独立的旋转阻力（rotational drag）测量约束、建立极简力矩模型。

Result: 保持面内镜像对称（in-plane mirror symmetry）的星形不旋转，破坏该对称性者按手性决定方向自发旋转；臂数编码的几何与声幅耦合产生非线性动力学，包括旋转方向的自发反转；模型可覆盖所有星形几何的观测响应，并可据此设计具有指定幅值依赖响应与反转电压的"混合星形（hybrid star）"。

Conclusion: 几何与声幅共同支配声悬浮体系的旋转，并存在与声场的意外高阶耦合（higher-order couplings）；该结果一方面解释了自发旋转机制，另一方面为声悬浮物质的可编程旋转动力学（programmable rotational dynamics）提供了路径。

Abstract: Acoustic levitation enables objects to be suspended and manipulated without physical contact, providing a versatile platform for studying the dynamics of freely suspended matter. Yet levitated objects, from droplets and thin films to particle assemblies, frequently rotate spontaneously, and this axial rotation often remains difficult to predict or control. There is no general understanding of how the shape of a levitated object determines whether it rotates, in which direction, or how its rotation changes with acoustic amplitude. Here, we establish how particle geometry and acoustic amplitude govern spontaneous rotation of acoustically levitated objects. We work with a family of shapes we call ``chiral stars'', within which we systematically vary handedness and number of arms. In a symmetric acoustic field, stars that preserve in-plane mirror symmetry do not rotate, while those that break it spontaneously rotate with a direction set by handedness. Beyond this symmetry requirement, we demonstrate that geometry, as encoded by the number of star arms, couples with acoustic amplitude to produce complex, nonlinear dynamics, including spontaneous reversal of rotation direction. To explain these behaviors, we develop a minimal torque model constrained by independent measurements of rotational drag, which captures the observed responses across all star geometries. We then demonstrate predictive power by designing a ``hybrid star'' with a prescribed amplitude-dependent response and reversal voltage. Our results not only establish how particle geometry and acoustic amplitude combine to govern rotation in acoustically levitated systems, but also reveal unanticipated higher-order couplings with the acoustic field. Beyond explaining spontaneous rotation, they open a route toward programmable rotational dynamics in acoustically levitated matter.

</details>


### [160] [Effect of interfacial water structure on the adsorption of weakly hydrated anions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27964)
*Swaminath Bharadwaj*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究通过计算机模拟（computer simulations）考察界面水网络（interfacial water network）破坏作为弱水化阴离子（weakly hydrated anions）吸附的间接驱动力，并揭示其与直接溶质-离子及离子-离子相互作用的耦合关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱水化阴离子倾向于富集于大分子表面并在低盐浓度下引起盐溶（salting-in），近期研究将其归因于界面水网络的破坏，但该间接驱动力与直接相互作用的相对作用及相互影响尚不清楚。

Method: 采用计算机模拟研究 SCN⁻ 与 I⁻ 在仅通过范德华相互作用（van der Waals interactions）与水和离子作用的模型延展溶质上的吸附；通过增大有效溶质尺寸并同时减弱溶质-水与溶质-离子吸引作用，逐步增加纯水溶液中的界面水网络无序度。

Result: 碘离子（iodide）吸附随界面水网络破坏加剧而增加，尽管其直接溶质-碘相互作用减弱，但总体上碘离子仍呈贫化（depleted）；相反，SCN⁻ 的优先吸附下降，但在更高界面水网络无序度下下降速率变小，表明间接贡献部分补偿了直接溶质-SCN⁻ 相互作用减弱所致的吸附减少。

Conclusion: 界面水网络破坏相关的间接贡献促进弱水化阴离子在大分子表面的吸附，但吸附程度还取决于直接溶质-离子相互作用与阴离子-阳离子配对（anion-cation pairing）；该相互作用在混合盐溶液（mixed-salt solutions）中可能尤为重要，因为强水化阴离子同样可破坏界面水网络。

Abstract: Weakly hydrated anions tend to partition to macromolecular surfaces, leading to salting-in at low salt concentrations. Recent studies have linked this partitioning to disruption of the interfacial water network. Here, we investigate the role of interfacial-water-network disruption as an indirect driving force for the adsorption of weakly hydrated anions and its interplay with direct solute-anion and anion-cation interactions using computer simulations. We study the adsorption of SCN$^{-}$ and I$^{-}$ ions on a model extended solute that interacts with water and ions only via van der Waals interactions. To probe the indirect contribution, we increase the effective solute size while simultaneously decreasing the attractive solute-water and solute-ion interactions, resulting in progressively greater disorder in the interfacial water network in pure aqueous solutions. Our results show that iodide adsorption increases despite weaker direct solute-iodide interactions and correlates with greater disruption of the interfacial water network, although iodide remains depleted overall. In contrast, preferential adsorption of SCN$^{-}$ decreases, but the rate of decrease becomes smaller at higher levels of interfacial-water-network disorder. This suggests that the indirect contribution associated with interfacial-water-network disruption partly compensates for the decrease in SCN$^{-}$ adsorption caused by weaker direct solute-SCN$^{-}$ interactions. Our results highlight that the indirect contribution associated with interfacial-water-network disruption promotes the adsorption of weakly hydrated anions on macromolecular surfaces, while the extent of adsorption also depends on direct solute-ion interactions and anion-cation pairing. This interplay may be particularly important in mixed-salt solutions, where the presence of strongly hydrated anions can also disrupt the interfacial water network.

</details>


### [161] [Tracking multiscale liquid crystal order with high harmonic spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28128)
*Marta L. Murillo-Sánchez,Andrea Annunziata,Simon Vendelbo Bylling Jensen,Ángel Rubio,Caterina Vozzi,Thomas Pfeifer,Davide Faccialà,Nicolas Tancogne-Dejean,Laura Cattaneo*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究以热致液晶 4-氰基-4'-正戊基联苯（5CB）为模型体系，利用偏振分辨高次谐波产生（HHG）探测分子间相互作用如何导致宏观有序，发现向列相中谐波产率呈四瓣各向异性并在各向同性相消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文力图回答高次谐波产生能否追踪微观分子间相互作用如何产生宏观有序这一问题，并借助具有明确向列相—各向同性相变的液晶模型体系加以检验。

Method: 采用温控平板射流（flat jet）样品，以中红外强场脉冲驱动偏振分辨高次谐波产生；结合第一性原理参数化的紧束缚模型（tight-binding model）以及耦合微观响应、Landau-de Gennes 取向关联与相位匹配（phase matching）的多尺度模型进行解释。

Result: 向列相中谐波产率呈二重旋转对称的四瓣角分布，且在驱动场偏振垂直于分子指向矢（molecular director）时最强，该各向异性在各向同性液体中消失；跨多个谐波阶次的温度依赖测量均分辨出同一一级相变，且各阶次光学灵敏度不同。模型将响应归因于短程反平行分子排列中的分子间轨道杂化（orbital hybridization）。

Conclusion: 高次谐波产生将局域电子耦合、介观有序与宏观传播联系起来，可作为软物质（soft matter）多尺度组织的一种全光学探针（all-optical probe）。

Abstract: Can high-harmonic generation track how microscopic intermolecular interactions give rise to macroscopic order? We address this question in the thermotropic liquid crystal 4-cyano-4'-n-pentylbiphenyl, a model system with a well-defined nematic-to-isotropic transition. Using a temperature-controlled flat jet, we show that polarization-resolved high-harmonic generation driven by mid-infrared strong-field pulses is highly sensitive to liquid-crystal order. In the nematic phase, the harmonic yield forms a four-lobed angular pattern with twofold rotational symmetry and is strongest when the driving-field polarization is perpendicular to the molecular director; this anisotropy vanishes in the isotropic liquid. A tight-binding model parameterized by first-principles calculations attributes this response to intermolecular orbital hybridization in short-range antiparallel molecular arrangements. Temperature-dependent measurements across multiple harmonic orders resolve the same first-order phase transition, with each harmonic order exhibiting a distinct optical sensitivity. A multiscale model combining the microscopic response, Landau-de Gennes orientational correlations and phase matching explains this behaviour through the interplay between orientational correlation lengths and harmonic coherence lengths. High-harmonic generation therefore links local electronic coupling to mesoscopic order and macroscopic propagation, providing an all-optical probe of multiscale organization in soft matter.

</details>


### [162] [Dramatic pressure-induced slowdown and the soft glassy rheology of water at 2D material interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28254)
*Guilhem Mariette,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Jean Comtet*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究利用音叉式原子力显微镜（tuning-fork-based atomic force microscopy）测量了不同二维材料界面间分子受限水的全范围（线性至非线性）剪切依赖黏弹流变学，揭示其行为类比于宏观软玻璃态屈服材料，并受剪切诱导动力学加速的非线性模型描述；同时发现压力诱导的动力学显著减慢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纳流控器件（nanofluidic devices）仅能探测线性响应区（linear-response regime）内的分子水流动，而基于纳米力学力（nanomechanical force）的方法结果明显不一致，亟需突破线性区限制并解决数据矛盾。

Method: 采用音叉式原子力显微镜（tuning-fork-based atomic force microscopy），在多种二维材料界面处测量分子受限水的完整线性至非线性、剪切依赖的黏弹流变学（viscoelastic rheology）。

Result: 观测到水流动响应中存在显著的速率效应（rate effects）；流变行为与宏观软玻璃态屈服材料（soft glassy yielding materials）高度类似，并可由考虑剪切诱导动力学加速的非线性模型完整描述，这一特征是无序过冷与亚稳系统（supercooled and metastable systems）的标志；此外发现压力诱导的纳米受限水动力学明显减慢，弛豫时间（relaxation times）延伸至微秒量级且具有显著的材料依赖性。

Conclusion: 非线性、玻璃态流变学（glassy rheology）是界面水的普遍特征，并为可调谐的非线性纳流控元件（non-linear nanofluidic elements）提供了实现路径。

Abstract: The flow of molecularly confined water has attracted considerable interest in recent years. Yet despite their exquisite character, current nanofluidic devices cannot probe molecular water flow beyond the linear-response regime, while nanomechanical force- based approaches have produced markedly inconsistent results. Here, using tuning- fork-based atomic force microscopy, we tackle these limitations by measuring the full linear-to-nonlinear, shear-dependent viscoelastic rheology of molecularly confined water at various two-dimensional material interfaces -- demonstrating pronounced rate effects in water flow response. The observed rheological behaviors are remarkably analogous to that of macroscopic soft glassy yielding materials and fully captured by a non-linear model accounting for the shear-induced acceleration of the dynamics -- a hallmark of disordered supercooled and metastable systems. We further unveil a pronounced pressure-induced slowdown in nanoconfined water dynamics, with relaxation times extending into the microsecond range and marked material dependence. These results reveal non-linear, glassy rheology as a general feature of interfacial water and offer a route to tunable, non-linear nanofluidic elements.

</details>


### [163] [A terminal-group and fluorination matrix for designing polar nematic liquid crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28312)
*Calum J. Gibb,Jordan Hobbs,William C. Ogle,Richard. J. Mandle*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究构建了含40种介晶基元（mesogen）的双苯基酯苯基分子基体，独立改变侧向核心氟化（lateral core fluorination）与端基（terminal-group）身份，发现侧向氟化在抑制非极性介相（apolar mesophases）与清亮点（clearing points）的同时，配合适当端基可诱导极性向列相（polar nematic phase）行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 侧向氟化已成为极性液晶（polar liquid crystals）设计的重要变量，但其与端基身份的相互作用机制尚不清楚，缺乏对两者独立贡献的系统认识。

Method: 合成并研究40个双苯基酯苯基介晶构成的分子基体，独立调控侧向核心氟化与端基；采用构象平均原子电荷（conformer averaged atomic charges）的图傅里叶分析（graph Fourier analysis）表征电荷谱空间特征。

Result: 侧向氟化通常抑制非极性介相并降低清亮点，但结合合适端基可促成极性向列相；图傅里叶分析显示极性材料位于电荷谱空间的高频区域，与键尺度静电基元（bond-scale electrostatic motif）相符，端基可将该特征推向更高或更低频率，从而调控极性有序的出现。

Conclusion: 侧向氟化与端基身份共同决定极性有序的形成，电荷谱分析为理解并设计极性液晶提供了一种可量化的描述框架。

Abstract: Lateral fluorination has emerged as a powerful design variable in the design of polar liquid crystals, but its interplay with terminal-group identity remains poorly understood. Here we report a 40-member matrix of biphenyl-ester-phenyl mesogens that independently varies lateral core fluorination and terminal-group identity. Increasing lateral fluorination generally suppresses apolar mesophases and clearing points, however this same change can enable polar nematic phase behaviour in conjunction with suitable terminal units. Graph Fourier analysis of conformer averaged atomic charges shows polar materials occupy a high-frequency region of charge-spectral space, consistent with a bond-scale electrostatic motif. Different terminal units can bias this towards higher or lower frequencies, and in doing so can enable polar order to emerge.

</details>


<div id='math.SP'></div>

# math.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Polynomial Spectral Gap Estimates for Finitely Differentiable Quasi-Periodic Schrödinger Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26977)
*Ao Cai,Yang Gao,Jiawei He*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该论文证明：对小 $C^{k}$ 势与 Diophantine 频率的准周期 Schrödinger 算子，其谱隙（spectral gap）具有多项式上界，且衰减指数 $k-34\tau$ 中 $k$ 的系数 $1$ 在渐近意义下最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准周期 Schrödinger 算子的谱隙大小与其正则性（regularity）之间的关系需要定量刻画；现有结果对 $C^{k}$ 光滑性下谱隙的衰减速率缺乏最优性判断，本文旨在给出多项式上界并检验其指数的最优性。

Method: 采用定量可约化（quantitative reducibility）方法，并结合 Moser–Pöschel 论证（Moser–Pöschel argument）完成证明。

Result: 对足够大的 $k$，得到谱隙的多项式上界，其衰减指数为 $k-34\tau$（$\tau$ 为 Diophantine 指数）；同时沿无穷多个谱隙标号（gap labels）构造了相应下界反例。

Conclusion: 这些例子表明 $k$ 的领先系数 $1$ 是渐近最优的（asymptotically optimal），从而确认所得上界在指数层面不可实质性改进。

Abstract: We prove polynomial upper bounds on spectral gaps of quasi-periodic Schrödinger operators with small $C^{k}$ potentials and Diophantine frequencies. For sufficiently large $k$, the decay exponent is $k-34τ$, where $τ$ is the Diophantine exponent. We construct examples with lower bounds along infinitely many gap labels, showing that the leading coefficient $1$ of $k$ is asymptotically optimal. The proof uses quantitative reducibility and the Moser-Pöschel argument.

</details>


<div id='nucl-th'></div>

# nucl-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [Hybrid Stars with Different Amount of Quark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28408)
*Milena Albino,Tuhin Malik,Márcio Ferreira,Constança Providência*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 本研究放宽了“最大质量中子星须具有大夸克核心”的约束，重新通过贝叶斯推断（Bayesian inference）构建状态方程（equation of state, EOS）集合（SmallQ集），并与先前NJL-GW集对比，考察夸克核心尺度对中子星性质与观测相容性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前作探索了大夸克核心中子星（neutron star）的可能性，但该强约束可能人为限制EOS空间；本文旨在检验仅要求最大质量中子星含有夸克核心（不限尺寸）时，EOS集合与观测数据的相容性及物理性质差异。

Method: 采用与先前相同的约束（核物质性质、NICER的mass-radius数据、Virgo-LIGO的GW170817数据、微扰QCD（perturbative QCD）与因果性（causality）），仅放宽最大质量星夸克核心尺寸约束，通过贝叶斯推断获得新的SmallQ EOS集。强子相使用含非线性介子相互作用的相对论平均场模型（Relativistic Mean Field model），夸克相使用含四夸克与八夸克相互作用项的Nambu--Jona-Lasinio模型。

Result: SmallQ集的相变密度（phase transition density）更高，为3–4.5 ρ₀，而NJL-GW集为2–2.5 ρ₀；新集描述的中子星半径更小、潮汐形变（tidal deformability）更小，与NICER和GW170817数据更相容。SmallQ集中夸克奇异组分（strange fraction）更大，2 M⊙中子星可能仅含极少或不含夸克物质；该集内有不可忽略比例的EOS在1.8 M⊙处质量-半径曲线呈正斜率，而核子星（nucleonic star）中该比例可忽略，超子星（hyperonic star）中斜率恒为负。

Conclusion: 放宽夸克核心尺寸约束后所得EOS集具有更高相变密度，且在半径、潮汐形变与观测数据相容性方面更优；夸克物质组分与质量-半径曲线斜率行为随星体组成显著变化，表明夸克核心尺度的设定对EOS集合的物理预言有实质影响。

Abstract: In a previous work, we explored the possibility of neutron stars with large quark cores. Through Bayesian inference, we obtained large sets of equations of state imposing constraints regarding the properties of nuclear matter, mass-radius data from NICER, the GW170817 data from Virgo-LIGO collaboration, perturbative QCD and causality. We also imposed that the maximum mass neutron star (NS$\text{max}$) must have a large quark core. In this work, we obtain a new set of equations of state (SmallQ set) with the same constraints as before. However, we relaxed the last constraint, imposing only that NS$\text{max}$ must have quark core, regardless of its size. We compare the results with the set from our previous paper constrained by NICER and GW170817 observations NJL-GW. For both sets, we use the Relativistic Mean Field model with nonlinear meson interactions for the hadron phase and the Nambu--Jona-Lasinio model with four- and eight-quark interaction terms for the quark phase. The SmallQ set has higher values of phase transition density, with 3 -- 4.5 $ρ_0$, compared to the NJL-GW, with 2 -- 2.5 $ρ_0$. The new set describes NSs with smaller radius and smaller tidal deformability, and is more compatible with NICER and GW170817 data. A difference in the quark matter composition is observed, with a larger quark strange fraction occurring in the SmallQ set. Within this set, $2 M_\odot$ NSs may contain little to no quark matter. It is shown that there is a non negligible fraction of EOS whose mass-radius curve presents a positive slope for 1.8 $M_\odot$ stars, while for nucleonic stars this fraction is negligible and for hyperonic stars the slope is always negative.

</details>


### [166] [Direct Urca process facilitated by non-Fermi-liquid quantum criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28023)
*Hao-Fu Zhu,Guo-Zhu Liu*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 该文指出，超流与超导量子临界性（quantum criticality）引起的非费米液体（non-Fermi liquid）行为可大幅放宽直接 Urca 过程（direct Urca process）的质子分数阈值，使该过程在低质量中子星中即可发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统费米液体（Fermi-liquid）理论预言直接 Urca 过程需质子分数（proton fraction）超过 \(Y_p \approx 0.11\)–\(0.15\) 的阈值，而低质量中子星（low-mass neutron stars）中极少达到该条件，因而该过程长期被认为受限。

Method: 基于中子星中超流（superfluid）与超导（superconducting）量子临界性所产生的非费米液体区域进行分析：该区域核子动量占据（momentum occupation）平滑、无尖锐费米面（Fermi surface），化学势出现对数温度依赖。

Result: 这些内禀特征根本改变了标准 \(β\) 平衡（\(β\)-equilibrium）关系，使直接 Urca 中微子发射（neutrino emission）可在较低核密度下被激活。

Conclusion: 研究修正了长期存在的 Urca 阈值范式（Urca-threshold paradigm），表明直接 Urca 过程有机会在低质量中子星中发生。

Abstract: Conventional Fermi-liquid theories of dense nuclear matter predict that the direct Urca process requires a proton fraction above a threshold of $Y_p \approx 0.11$--$0.15$, which is rarely reached in low-mass neutron stars. Here we show that this stringent kinematic constraint is drastically relaxed by the non-Fermi liquid behavior arising from superfluid and superconducting quantum criticality in neutron stars. In the non-Fermi-liquid regimes, nucleons have a smooth momentum occupation without a sharp Fermi surface, and their chemical potentials develop a logarithmic temperature dependence. These intrinsic features fundamentally alter the standard $β$-equilibrium relation. Consequently, direct Urca neutrino emission can be activated even at low nuclear densities. This modifies the long-standing Urca-threshold paradigm and indicates that direct Urca process has the opportunity to occur in low-mass neutron stars.

</details>


<div id='math.PR'></div>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [Central Limit Theorem for continuous $N$-particle systems in fixed- and high-temperature: single level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27166)
*Jiaming Xu*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 论文刻画了连续 N 粒子系统多项式线性统计量的中心极限定理，给出固定温度与高温两种机制下的充要条件、极限协方差的组合公式，并用带状图曲面展开给出自由累积量的拓扑解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项式线性统计量（polynomial linear statistics）的涨落刻画是该领域核心问题，而现有结论对连续粒子系统在固定温度与高温机制下缺乏统一的充要条件与协方差公式，尤其高温下的自由累积量结构尚未明确。

Method: 通过 Dunkl 算子（Dunkl operators）提取矩，并对对数多元 Bessel 生成函数（logarithmic multivariate Bessel function）做以上曲面带状图（ribbon graphs on surfaces）为指标的拓扑展开。

Result: 以对数 Bessel 生成函数系数给出两机制下 CLT 的充要条件及极限协方差的显式组合公式；固定温度下复现经典二阶自由矩–累积量关系（至多差因子 1/θ），高温下给出新的二阶自由 γ-累积量及其协方差关系。

Conclusion: 拓扑展开表明仅亏格零（genus zero）在固定温度下贡献、高温下任意高亏格均贡献，从而赋予自由累积量拓扑解释；应用上证明 β-加法（β-addition）在两机制下保持 CLT 并可由输入自由累积量确定其极限协方差，且 γ→∞ 时高温协方差在适当重标度下收敛至固定温度对应量。

Abstract: We characterize central limit theorems for polynomial linear statistics of continuous $N$-particle systems on $\mathbb R$, in both the fixed-temperature regime $θ=β/2>0$ and the high-temperature regime $Nθ\toγ>0$. In each regime, we give necessary and sufficient conditions in terms of the coefficients of the logarithmic Bessel generating function, together with an explicit combinatorial formula for the limiting covariance. At fixed temperature, this formula recovers the classical second-order free moment--cumulant relation, up to the factor $1/θ$; at high temperature, it yields new second-order free $γ$-cumulants and their covariance relations. The proof combines moment extraction through Dunkl operators with a topological expansion of the logarithmic multivariate Bessel function indexed by ribbon graphs on surfaces. This expansion gives a topological interpretation of the free-cumulants: only genus zero contributes at fixed temperature, whereas arbitrarily high genera contribute at high temperature. As applications, we prove that $β$-addition preserves the CLT in both regimes and determine its limiting covariance from the input free cumulants, and establish a suitably rescaled transition from the high-temperature covariance to its fixed-temperature counterpart as $γ\to\infty$.

</details>


### [168] [From Hermite Chaos to an Attractive Log-Gas: Explicit High-Rank Limits of Iterated-Logarithm and Tail Constants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27261)
*Elina Moldavskaya*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文研究标准 Hermite 过程（Hermite process）加权积分所定义的随机变量 Y 的右尾渐近，其常数 Λ_m 具有无穷维变分表示；本文给出 m→∞ 时 √(m!)Λ_m 的显式极限、误差阶、与对数气体熵—能量变分问题的联系，以及 H↑1/2 边界处极限交换性等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常数 Λ_m 的有限阶闭式求值尚不可知，但其决定了右尾渐近与长记忆高斯序列非线性函数加权和的重对数律（law of the iterated logarithm）中的精确常数，因此需要在高阶（m→∞）与边界（H→1/2）等极限下刻画其行为。

Method: 建立每一阶的精确 Rényi 变分表示（Rényi variational representation），在高阶极限下转化为吸引型对数气体（attractive logarithmic gas）的熵—能量变分问题，并借助 Selberg 积分（Selberg integral）与 Barnes G 函数求极限自由能；同时对 d≤0 情形分析 H=1/2 记忆边界与 m→∞ 极限的交换性。

Result: 当 m→∞（H、d 固定）时，√(m!)Λ_m 递减趋于 H、d 的显式正函数，相对误差为 O(m^{-1})，该函数亦为各有限阶归一化常数的下界；d≤0 时收敛在 H=1/2 处一致，除以 √(2H−1) 后两极限可交换，公共值显式，常权情形等于 2^{3/2}π^3G(1/2)^8=2^{11/6}πe 𝖠^{-12}（𝖠 为 Glaisher–Kinkelin 常数）。

Conclusion: 固定阶展开中归一化常数在 H↑1 时的二次、三次系数随 m→∞ 收敛到显式极限函数的对应系数；并补充了全局有限阶界与集中权极限（concentrating-weight limit）等结果，形成对 Λ_m 的多极限完整刻画。

Abstract: Let $Z$ be a standard Hermite process of order $m\ge2$ and Hurst index $H\in(1/2,1)$. For $d<H-1/2$, let $Y$ be the variance-normalized version of the weighted integral $\int_0^1 x^{-d}\,\mathrm dZ(x)$. Its right tail satisfies $
  \log\Pr\{Y>t\}\sim
  -\frac12\left(\frac{t}{Λ_m}\right)^{2/m}, t\to\infty, $ where the positive constant $Λ_m$ admits an infinite-dimensional variational representation. The same constant governs the sharp law of the iterated logarithm for weighted sums of nonlinear functions of long-memory Gaussian sequences. A general closed-form evaluation at finite order is not known.
  As $m\to\infty$ with $H$ and $d$ fixed, the normalized constants $\sqrt{m!}\,Λ_m$ decrease to an explicit positive function of $H$ and $d$, with relative error $O(m^{-1})$. This function also gives a lower bound for the normalized constant at every finite order. An exact Rényi variational representation at each order yields, in the high-rank limit, the entropy--energy variational problem of an attractive logarithmic gas. The limiting free energy is evaluated through the Selberg integral and the Barnes $G$ function.
  For $d\le0$, convergence remains uniform up to the memory boundary $H=1/2$. After division by the square-root boundary factor $\sqrt{2H-1}$, the high-rank and boundary limits commute. Their common value is explicit; for constant weights it equals $2^{3/2}π^3G(1/2)^8=2^{11/6}πe\,\mathsf A^{-12}$, where $\mathsf A$ is the Glaisher--Kinkelin constant. The quadratic and cubic coefficients of the fixed-order expansion of the normalized constant as $H\uparrow1$ converge, as $m\to\infty$, to those of the explicit limiting function. Global finite-order bounds and a concentrating-weight limit complement the main result.

</details>


### [169] [Invariant pseudo-unitary matrix ensembles and local statistics and conductance fluctuations of the DMPK ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27532)
*Dong Wang,Thomas Wolfs*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究 DMPK 系综（DMPK ensemble）在粒子数趋于无穷时的局部统计极限，在金属区与两类交叉区分别得到正弦、Bessel、Airy 型统计，在绝缘区证明带高斯涨落的局域化，并通过一类不变伪酉矩阵（invariant pseudo-unitary matrices）的乘积解释其与 Ginibre 矩阵乘积的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DMPK 系综（DMPK ensemble）是无序导线中量子输运的标准模型，物理文献已预测其普适与局域化行为，但缺乏严格的极限刻画与定量描述。

Method: 取粒子数趋于无穷的极限，考察体部与硬边、软边的局部统计；构造在乘积下封闭的一类不变伪酉矩阵（invariant pseudo-unitary matrices），将 DMPK 系综解释为此类随机矩阵乘积谱变量的联合密度，并归入导数型多项式系综（polynomial ensemble of derivative type）；此外推导电导均值与方差的均匀渐近展开。

Result: 金属区及交叉 I 得到体部正弦统计（sine statistics）、硬边 Bessel 统计与软边 Airy 统计；绝缘区证明在显式确定性位置附近局域化并具高斯涨落；交叉 II 得到体部与两边界的显式极限核，其中硬边核涉及 Legendre 函数且为新结果，体部与软边核与临界标度 Ginibre 矩阵乘积奇异值平方的相应核一致；并证明电导方差在整个金属区趋于 1/15。

Conclusion: 结果严格证实并量化了物理文献关于普适与局域化行为的预测，揭示了 DMPK 系综与 Ginibre 矩阵乘积之间的结构联系，并确立了 DMPK 系综的普适电导涨落（universal conductance fluctuations）。

Abstract: We consider the DMPK ensemble, which models quantum transport in a disordered wire, and describe its limiting local statistics as the number of particles tends to infinity. In the metallic regime, as well as at crossover I, we obtain sine statistics in the bulk, Bessel statistics at the hard edge and Airy statistics at the soft edge. In the insulating regime, we prove localization around certain explicit deterministic positions with Gaussian fluctuations. This confirms and quantifies the predicted universal and localized behavior in the physics literature. At crossover II, we obtain explicit limiting kernels in the bulk and at both edges, which interpolate between these regimes. The hard edge kernel involves Legendre functions and, to the best of our knowledge, is new, while the bulk and soft edge kernels agree with those derived for the squared singular values of critically scaled products of independent Ginibre matrices. To explain these similarities, we construct a class of invariant pseudo-unitary matrices that is closed under taking products and use it to interpret the DMPK ensemble as the joint density of the spectral variables of a product of such random matrices. More generally, the latter takes the form of a polynomial ensemble of derivative type involving a second order differential operator compatible with the Legendre functions. Finally, we obtain uniform asymptotic expansions of the mean and variance of the conductance and prove that the DMPK ensemble admits universal conductance fluctuations, i.e. the variance of the conductance tends to $1/15$ throughout the whole metallic regime. This again confirms the predictions in the physics literature.

</details>


### [170] [Improved rapid phase ordering for Ising dynamics on random regular graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27673)
*Zhangsong Li,Allan Sly*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文研究随机 d-正则图上低温 Ising 模型的 Glauber 动力学（Glauber dynamics），证明在 β≥C/d 且 d 充分大时，若初始构型具有至少 ε_{C,d}·n 的正自旋超额，则动力学在 O(log n) 时间内对条件于正磁化的 Gibbs 测度（Gibbs measure）准平衡，且偏差 ε_{C,d} 可随 d、C 增大趋于 0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在该低温区域内，全混合时间（mixing time）因正、负主导相之间的瓶颈而呈 n 的指数级，故需考察从偏向某一相的初始化出发的准平衡行为。已有结果（GSS25+）在 Ising 与大 d 情形下要求 β≳(log d)/d，本文旨在将该阈值放宽至 β≥C/d。

Method: 采用大耦合（grand coupling）框架，并发展出一种新的自举（bootstrap）方法，用以证明不一致团簇（disagreement clusters）的亚临界性（subcriticality）。关键在于控制强局部关联（strong local correlations）低温模型中负信息在时空上的传播。

Result: 对充分大的 d，若初始正自旋相对负自旋的超额至少为 ε_{C,d}·n，则动力学在 O(log n) 时间内准平衡至条件于正磁化的 Gibbs 测度；且所需偏差 ε_{C,d} 可选取为在 d、C→∞ 时趋于 0。

Conclusion: 结果表明在 β≥C/d 的更宽低温参数区间内，偏离均衡相的初始偏差可被压缩至极小量（随 d、C→∞ 趋于零），并给出了基于自举法分析不一致团簇亚临界性的技术路线。

Abstract: We study Glauber dynamics for the Ising model on random $d$-regular graphs with $n$ vertices at low temperature, in the regime $β\geq C/d$ where $C,d$ are sufficiently large constants. In this regime, the full mixing time is exponential in $n$, because of the bottleneck between the two dominant phases: configurations with a majority of plus spins and configurations with a majority of minus spins. We show that, for sufficiently large $d$, if the dynamics is initialized with an excess of at least $ε_{C,d} n$ plus spins over minus spins, then it quasi-equilibrates in time $O(\log n)$ to the Gibbs measure conditioned on positive magnetization. In addition, the required bias $ε_{C,d}$ can be chosen so that $ε_{C,d}\to 0$ as $d,C\to\infty$.
  Previous bound of \cite{GSS25+} in the Ising and large-$d$ setting required $β\gtrsim (\log d)/d$. A key difficulty is that, even from the all-plus initialization, one must control the propagation of negative information in spacetime in a low-temperature model with strong local correlations. We address this by developing a new bootstrap approach to proving subcriticality of disagreement clusters under the grand coupling.

</details>


### [171] [Large Deviations in Stochastic Dynamics: A Hamiltonian Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27179)
*Lorenzo Cirigliano,William D. Piñeros,Anna Poggialini,Grant M. Rotskoff,Eric Vanden-Eijnden*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该讲义以向后Kolmogorov方程的WKB分析为统一方法，系统介绍随机动力系统中的大偏差理论，涵盖小噪声、长时间与有限噪声三类渐近区域，并以Schlögl化学双稳态模型贯穿全篇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大偏差理论（large deviation theory）在各渐近区域的表述往往彼此割裂，缺乏统一推导框架；讲义旨在通过单一方法学揭示各类大偏差原理（large deviation principle）背后共同的Hamiltonian结构，并提供从理论到计算的系统入门。

Method: 采用向后Kolmogorov方程（backward Kolmogorov equation）的WKB分析作为统一方法学，按三种渐近区域展开：随机微分方程的小噪声极限与Markov跳跃过程的大体积极限；长时间与小噪声联合极限下的准势（quasipotential）形式；有限噪声下长时间极限的倾斜生成元（tilted generator）谱方法。

Result: 建立了大偏差原理从大数定律（law of large numbers）出发、通过逐步精细的涨落刻画而涌现的推导路径；给出平衡与非平衡系统中亚稳态（metastability）与跃迁速率的准势表述；并实现与谱方法及多尺度系统约化的衔接。

Conclusion: 讲义综述了最小作用量方法（minimum action method）及其准势计算的几何变体与神经网络技术等计算途径，以Schlögl模型在标量与空间扩展情形下作为贯穿示例，并在附录中提供随机微积分与Markov跳跃过程的自包含背景。

Abstract: These lecture notes present an introduction to large deviations in stochastic dynamical systems, adapted from a lecture series given at the Les Houches school ``Large Deviations: Theory and Applications'' in July 2024. The exposition develops the theory through a unified methodology: WKB analysis of backward Kolmogorov equations, which reveals the Hamiltonian structure underlying all large deviation principles considered here. We treat three asymptotic regimes systematically. First, the small-noise limit for stochastic differential equations and the large-volume limit for Markov jump processes, where the Large Deviation Principle emerges from the Law of Large Numbers through progressively finer characterizations of fluctuations. Second, the long-time limit combined with small noise, yielding the quasipotential formalism for metastability and transition rates in both equilibrium and nonequlibrium systems. Third, the long-time limit at finite noise, connecting to spectral methods via tilted generators and enabling reduction of multiscale systems.We survey computational approaches including minimum action methods, their geometric variants for quasipotential calculations, and neural network techniques. The Schlögl model of chemical bistability serves as a running example, illustrating both scalar and spatially extended settings. Appendices provide self-contained background on stochastic calculus and Markov jump processes.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [Field Theory of the Gutzwiller Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27050)
*Abhijat Sarma,Cenke Xu,Chong Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文用场论方法厘清了Gutzwiller投影与parton规范理论的关系：投影态普适物理可由局域于宽度ε→0的"时间平板"上的规范场描述；当规范场有能隙时两者普适物理一致，无能隙情形下Gutzwiller投影并非好的长程拟设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gutzwiller投影（Gutzwiller projection）是自旋液体与量子霍尔波函数（如Laughlin波函数）的著名拟设，但其与紧密相关的parton规范理论（parton gauge theory）之间的精确关系长期未明。本文旨在澄清这一问题。

Method: 采用路径积分（path integral）描述，将投影态的普适物理表述为局域于宽度ε→0的时间平板（temporal slab）上、与体材料物质场（bulk matter fields）耦合的规范场（gauge field）；解析推导各类Gutzwiller投影态的普适物理，并将构造推广至离散对称性（discrete symmetries）以分析投影SPT态，进一步推广描述G-intertwining缺陷。

Result: Gutzwiller投影对U(1)狄拉克自旋液体（U(1) Dirac spin liquids）、spinon费米面自旋液体及复合费米液体（composite Fermi liquids）的长程性质并非好拟设；量子自旋霍尔态的Gutzwiller投影仅有准长程序（quasi-long range order）。而在规范场有能隙的情形（Laughlin态、Z2自旋液体、Luttinger液体）中，平板规范理论复现了完整parton规范理论的正确普适物理。

Conclusion: Gutzwiller投影与parton规范理论的对应依赖于规范场是否有能隙：有能隙时二者普适物理一致，无能隙时投影态不能正确刻画长程性质。该场论框架亦可推广至离散对称性与G-intertwining缺陷（包括自对偶缺陷）的描述。

Abstract: The Gutzwiller projection produces a well-known ansatz for spin-liquid and quantum Hall ground-state wavefunctions that is amenable to numerical simulation, with the Laughlin wavefunction being the most celebrated example. A long-standing issue is the precise relation between the Gutzwiller projection and the closely related parton gauge theory. Here, we clarify this issue by providing a field-theoretic description of the Gutzwiller projection. In a path integral description, the universal physics of the projected state is described in terms of a gauge field supported on a temporal slab of vanishing width $ε\rightarrow 0$ coupled to the bulk matter fields. We analytically derive the universal physics of various Gutzwiller projected states. Our construction is also applicable to discrete symmetries, and we use it to analyze various projected SPT states. Among our results, we show that the Gutzwiller projection is not a good ansatz for the long-distance properties of $U(1)$ Dirac spin liquids, spinon Fermi surface spin liquids or composite Fermi liquids, and that the Gutzwiller projection of a quantum spin Hall state has only quasi-long range order. In contrast, in examples where the gauge field is gapped, including Laughlin states, $\mathbb{Z}_2$ spin liquids and Luttinger liquids, we show that the slab gauge theory from Gutzwiller projection reproduces the correct universal physics of the full parton gauge theory. We also present a generalization of our field theory that describes $G$-intertwining defects, with application to self-duality defects of recent theoretical interest.

</details>


### [173] [Spectral signatures of hidden order and hidden quantum critical points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27060)
*Reja H. Wilke,Henning Schlömer,Sebastian Paeckel,Annabelle Bohrdt,Fabian Grusdt*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出并论证：掺杂自旋链中的隐藏序（hidden order）及其临界点与无能隙对称保护拓扑相（gapless SPT），可通过共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS）与中子散射可测的动力学两点响应函数直接探测，并给出数值指纹与微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐藏序相自发破缺对称性却对常规局域两点关联函数不可见，通常需非局域（弦）序参量刻画；量子气体显微镜实验已实现此类探测，但固体系统中多点观测量难以获取，隐藏序的实验signature基本未被探索，故需寻找实际可行的探针。

Method: 以最小模型（掺杂自旋链）为对象，通过数值计算动力学两点响应函数，识别隐藏序相、隐藏量子临界点及一类无能隙SPT相的光谱指纹，并结合解析分析追溯其微观起源。

Result: 数值上获得隐藏序相、隐藏量子临界点及特定无能隙SPT相的清晰光谱指纹；解析部分阐明这些指纹源于自旋与电荷自由度的相互作用，并给出其微观来源。

Conclusion: 光谱学（RIXS、中子散射）可作为隐藏序与无能隙SPT相的实用探针，并为其低能物理提供统一理解。

Abstract: Hidden-order phases elude the conventional Ginzburg-Landau framework: although they spontaneously break symmetry, their order remains invisible to standard local two-point correlators. Instead, they are typically characterized by nonlocal (string) order parameters and are believed to underlie a range of exotic quantum states, such as symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phases. While hidden correlations have previously been detected through nonlocal observables in quantum gas microscope experiments, in realistic solid-state systems multipoint observables are typically inaccessible and the signatures of hidden order remain largely unexplored. In this work, we show that hidden order in doped spin chains can be directly probed through dynamical two-point response functions measurable by resonant inelastic X-ray scattering (RIXS) and neutron scattering. Focusing on a minimal model, we numerically identify clear spectral fingerprints of both the hidden-order phase, its associated hidden quantum critical point as well as a specific group of gapless SPT phases. Complementing our numerical results with analytical insights, we uncover the microscopic origin of these spectral signatures and demonstrate how they emerge from the interplay between spin and charge degrees of freedom. Together, our results establish spectroscopy as a practical probe of hidden order and gapless SPT phases and provide a unified understanding of the low-energy physics governing these unconventional quantum phases.

</details>


### [174] [Chiral magnetic excitations in the RMn6Sn6 kagome metals (R=Tb, Ho, Er, and Y)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27430)
*Christopher J. Ranlett,Tianxiong Han,S. X. M. Riberolles,Dhurba R. Jaishi,Tyler J. Slade,Bing Li,A. Sapkota,D. L. Abernathy,Rebecca L. Dally,J. W. Lynn,B. G. Ueland,R. J. McQueeney*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在 RMn6Sn6（R=Tb、Ho、Er、Y）系列中，非弹性中子散射（inelastic neutron scattering, INS）观测到手性磁激发（chiral magnetic excitations）与磁结构无关地普遍存在，其强度随磁结构取向变化，并仅出现在布里渊区 L=odd 位置、层间呈 π 相位差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前在 kagome 金属 TbMn6Sn6 中发现的手性磁激发具有巡游特征（itinerant character）、能量尺度大（>100 meV）且阻尼强，无法由局域矩线性自旋波理论（local moment linear spin-wave theory）预言，其起源不明，需要更多材料体系加以检验。

Method: 采用非弹性中子散射（INS）测量多种 RMn6Sn6 化合物（R=Tb、Ho、Er、Y），这些材料分别具有单轴（uniaxial）、倾斜（tilted）、面内铁磁（planar ferrimagnet）与面内双螺旋（planar double-spiral）磁结构，并比较手性激发的强度与分布。

Result: 手性激发在所有磁结构中均存在，说明其与磁结构无关；R=Er 与 Y 显示面内磁结构下手性模式强度较弱，提示激发横向于局域 Mn 矩方向；该激发仅出现在布里渊区 L=odd 位置，对应各 kagome 层内同手性的手性自旋激发，且层间相位相差 π。

Conclusion: 手性磁激发是 RMn6Sn6 家族中不依赖磁结构的普遍现象，其偏振方向与层间相位关系为探讨该巡游型激发的微观起源提供了关键约束。

Abstract: Recent inelastic neutron scattering (INS) investigations of the kagome metal TbMn6Sn6 reveal novel chiral magnetic excitations associated with the kagome layer. These excitations appear to have an itinerant character with a large energy scale (> 100 meV) and heavy damping; however, the origin of these excitations, which are not predicted by local moment linear spin-wave theory, is not understood. Here, we use INS to show that chiral excitations are also found in RMn6Sn6 compounds with R=Tb, Ho, Er, and Y having uniaxial, tilted, planar ferrimagnet, and planar double-spiral magnetic structures, respectively. Thus, the chiral excitations exist regardless of the magnetic structure. Additionally, R=Er and Y demonstrate that the chiral mode intensity is weaker in planar magnetic structures, suggesting that excitations are transverse to the local Mn moment direction. The chiral excitations appear only at L=odd positions in the Brilloun zone, and correspond to chiral spin exciations of the same handedness within each kagome layer, and pi out-of-phase between layers.

</details>


### [175] [Finite-temperature spectral functions of lattice polarons with process tensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27582)
*Qi Gao,Yuan Wan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文将有限温度声子影响泛函表示为低秩矩阵乘积态的逐元素指数，结合嵌入构造实现单次计算获得全时域传播子，从而高效计算晶格极化子的有限温度谱函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限温度下晶格极化子（lattice polaron）谱函数的数值计算存在困难，需要一种能高效求和电子世界线（worldline）的方法。

Method: 提出过程张量（process tensor）方法：将有限温度声子影响泛函（phonon influence functional）表达为低秩矩阵乘积态（matrix product state）的逐元素指数；并通过嵌入构造（embedding construction）把不同传播时间的世界线嵌入单一矩阵乘积态，使全模拟时间区间的传播子一次计算得到。

Result: 在一维 Holstein 极化子上与已有的有限温度密度矩阵重整化群（density-matrix renormalization group）结果进行基准对比；进一步获得二维 Holstein 极化子数值收敛的谱。谱显示随温度升高，谱权重（spectral weight）由相干谱特征逐步转移至非相干背景。

Conclusion: 该过程张量方法可高效、数值收敛地计算一维与二维晶格极化子的有限温度谱函数，并揭示温度驱动的谱权重由相干向非相干转移行为。

Abstract: We develop a process tensor approach for calculating the finite-temperature spectral functions of lattice polarons. We show that the finite-temperature phonon influence functional can be expressed as the elementwise exponential of a low-rank matrix product state, enabling an efficient summation of electron worldlines. An embedding construction embeds worldlines of different propagation times into a single matrix product state. This construction yields the propagator over the full simulated time interval in a single calculation. We benchmark this approach against available finite-temperature density-matrix renormalization group results for the one-dimensional Holstein polaron. We further obtain numerically converged spectra for the two-dimensional Holstein polaron. The spectra reveal a progressive transfer of spectral weight from coherent spectral features into an incoherent background with increasing temperature.

</details>


### [176] [Good-$Γ$ Numbers: A Shell-Optimized Route to the Thermodynamic Limit in Quantum Monte Carlo Calculations of the High-Density Two-Dimensional Electron Gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27761)
*S. Azadi,T. D. Kühne,A. Principi,M. S. Bahramy*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作提出"Good-Γ（GG）粒子数"概念，即Γ点Hartree–Fock能量异常接近热力学极限的体系尺寸N，并验证其可在高密度二维均匀电子气（2D UEG）中替代扭转平均（twist averaging, TA）以减小单粒子有限尺寸误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子蒙特卡洛（quantum Monte Carlo, QMC）模拟周期体系时，单粒子有限尺寸误差（single-particle finite-size errors）是核心障碍，常规手段为扭转平均（twist averaging, TA），本文寻求更经济的替代方案。

Method: 在高密度费米液体区（r_s = 1–4）的二维均匀电子气（2D UEG）中，系统性考察四个密度与两种自旋极化下GG粒子数对应的一体动能与交换能误差ΔKE(N)、ΔEX(N)；并将由GG-N序列外推到热力学极限（thermodynamic limit, TL）的扩散蒙特卡洛（diffusion Monte Carlo, DMC）能量与同密度、同极化下扭转平均外推结果对比。

Result: 两种外推方案在所有测试情形下吻合至亚毫哈特里（millihartree）量级，且不依赖于采用1/N还是N^(-5/4)的外推形式。

Conclusion: GG粒子数序列可作为扭转平均的一种有效且低成本替代方案，用于消除DMC中的单粒子有限尺寸误差；结论限于高密度（r_s = 1–4）二维均匀电子气体系。

Abstract: Single-particle finite-size errors remain a central obstacle in quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) simulations of periodic systems, conventionally reduced via twist averaging (TA). Here we introduce Good-$Γ$ (GG) particle numbers as system sizes $N$ at which the $Γ$-point Hartree--Fock (HF) energy of the high-density two-dimensional uniform electron gas (2D UEG) lies anomalously close to its thermodynamic-limit (TL) value. All results in this work concern the 2D UEG in its high-density Fermi-liquid regime, $r_s=1$--$4$. We systematically investigate the kinetic- and exchange-energy errors, $ΔKE(N)$ and $ΔEX(N)$, that underlie this one-body finite-size-error cancellation across four densities and both spin polarizations. We then compare diffusion Monte Carlo (DMC) energies extrapolated to the TL from GG-$N$ sequences against twist-averaged extrapolations at the same densities and polarizations, finding agreement to within a fraction of a millihartree in every case, independent of whether a $1/N$ or an $N^{-5/4}$ extrapolation form is used.

</details>


### [177] [Spin fluctuations and nearly isotropic quasiparticles in the doped three-dimensional Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27779)
*Pengcheng Hou,Kun Chen,Youjin Deng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作利用连通行列式图解蒙特卡洛（connected-determinant diagrammatic Monte Carlo）方法，在偏离半满（away from half filling）且高于奈尔温度（Neel temperature）的区域内，首次给出三维哈伯德模型（three-dimensional Hubbard model）动量分辨的自能（self-energy）与全布里渊区自旋磁化率（spin susceptibility）的可控结果，揭示强反铁磁自旋涨落（antiferromagnetic spin fluctuations）与近各向同性准粒子（quasiparticles）共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维哈伯德模型在偏离半满、高于奈尔温度时表现为带有反铁磁自旋涨落的顺磁金属（paramagnetic metal），但此前缺乏可控的动量分辨（momentum-resolved）结果，且需为动力学平均场理论（DMFT）的扩展与自旋涨落理论提供基准。

Method: 采用连通行列式图解蒙特卡洛，在 n = 0.4–0.95、T/t = 0.05–0.33、U/t = 4–8 参数区间计算动量分辨自能，并在其中十二个参数点计算全布里渊区自旋磁化率（重点为 U/t = 4）；同时将结果与两粒子自洽理论（two-particle self-consistent theory）及动力学平均场理论（DMFT）进行对比。

Result: 在 U/t = 4 且趋近半满时自旋涨落增强：n = 0.7–0.9、T/t = 0.14 时磁化率峰值最高增强约七倍，散射率（scattering rate）动量依赖部分升至二阶值的四至五倍；涨落由屏蔽至约 2U/3、在整个布里渊区近均匀的有效自旋相互作用驱动，但峰值高度由峰值波矢处略小的相互作用值决定，近磁序时该微小差异引起峰值高度的大幅变化。涨落虽强却为短程，关联长度（correlation length）至多约两个晶格常数；在 n = 0.7–0.9、T/t ≥ 0.14 时，固定能带能量下费米面附近散射率变化不超过约 1%，无热点（hot spots）。两粒子自洽理论能再现峰值（其求和规则将单一常数相互作用固定于该值附近），但高估局域散射率、低估动量依赖部分；DMFT 在数个百分点内再现局域散射率与准粒子权重，其缺失的非局域部分近似为能带能量的函数。

Conclusion: 强自旋涨落与近各向同性的准粒子可以共存；所给结果可作为 DMFT 扩展方法与自旋涨落理论的定量基准。

Abstract: Away from half filling and above its Neel temperature, the three-dimensional Hubbard model is a paramagnetic metal with antiferromagnetic spin fluctuations, where controlled momentum-resolved results have been lacking. Using connected-determinant diagrammatic Monte Carlo, we compute the momentum-resolved self-energy for n = 0.4-0.95, T/t = 0.05-0.33 and U/t = 4-8, and the full-zone spin susceptibility at twelve of these points, focusing on U/t = 4. At U/t = 4 and toward half filling the spin fluctuations become strong: for n = 0.7-0.9 at T/t = 0.14 they enhance the susceptibility peak up to sevenfold and raise the momentum-dependent part of the scattering rate to four to five times its second-order value. They are driven by an effective spin interaction screened to about 2U/3 and nearly uniform over the zone. Yet the peak height is set by the slightly smaller value of this interaction at the peak wave vector, and near magnetic order this small difference changes the height by a large factor. Two-particle self-consistent theory reproduces the peak because its sum rule fixes its single constant interaction near this value, but for n = 0.7-0.9 at T/t = 0.14 its self-energy overestimates the local scattering rate and underestimates the momentum-dependent part. Though strong, the fluctuations are short ranged, with a correlation length of at most about two lattice spacings, and for n = 0.7-0.9 and T/t >= 0.14 the scattering rate near the Fermi surface varies by at most about 1% at fixed band energy, without hot spots. At U/t = 4 dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT) reproduces the local scattering rate and quasiparticle weight within a few percent, and the nonlocal part it misses is approximately a function of the band energy. Strong spin fluctuations thus coexist with nearly isotropic quasiparticles, and the results provide a benchmark for extensions of DMFT and for spin-fluctuation theories.

</details>


### [178] [Suppression and reemergence of directional transport across the ferromagnetic transition in SrRuO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27791)
*Smriti Bhatia,Neha Sharma,Srishti Bhardwaj,Shruti Roy,Dirk Fuchs,Tulika Maitra,Vivek Kumar Malik,Kaushik Sen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在DyScO₃衬底上生长(110)取向SrRuO₃薄膜，利用面内正交方向的晶体各向异性实现对称性分辨的输运测量，发现居里温度以上的各向异性电阻、以及磁场下线性磁阻（MR）符号随方向相反等方向性磁输运现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SrRuO₃是研究关联氧化物金属中自旋轨道耦合输运与拓扑响应的典型巡游铁磁体（itinerant ferromagnet）；(110)取向薄膜的面内晶体学各向异性为区分不同对称性的电荷输运提供平台，进而厘清各向异性磁输运的来源。

Method: 制备DyScO₃衬底上的(110)取向外延SrRuO₃薄膜，沿正交面内方向进行对称性分辨的电荷输运测量（温度依赖电阻、磁阻、普通霍尔与反常霍尔效应）。

Result: T_c,on≈165 K以上电阻呈强各向异性，符合方向依赖的自旋涨落散射（spin-fluctuation scattering）；其下各向异性几乎消失，归因于各向同性的电子-磁振子（electron-magnon）与电子-电子散射。线性磁阻（linear MR）在[001]方向为负且饱和，在[1̄10]方向于T₀=145 K以下变号为正；二次磁阻项两方向相同并在T*_OHE=155 K（普通霍尔系数过零）附近达峰，符合两带（two-band）图像下的载流子补偿；反常霍尔信号在T*_AHE=150 K变号，与费米面附近贝里曲率（Berry curvature）贡献的温度依赖重分布一致。

Conclusion: 结果确立铁磁有序（ferromagnetic ordering）是SrRuO₃中所观测方向性磁输运的主要驱动因素。

Abstract: SrRuO$_3$ is a canonical itinerant ferromagnet that continues to serve as a fertile platform for exploring spin-orbit coupled transport and topological responses in correlated oxide metals. Here, we investigate a (110)-oriented epitaxial SrRuO3 thin film on DyScO$_3$, where crystallographic anisotropy allows for symmetry-resolved charge transport along orthogonal in-plane directions. We uncover two principal findings. First, the resistance above the Curie temperature $T_{c,on}\approx 165$ K exhibits strong anisotropy, consistent with directionally dependent spin-fluctuation scattering. Below $T_{c,on}$, this anisotropy nearly vanishes, despite persistent structural inequivalence, due to isotropic electron-magnon and electron-electron scattering processes. Second, the application of a magnetic field reveals a striking directional contrast in the linear magnetoresistance (MR): it remains negative and saturates along [001], but changes sign and becomes positive along $[1\bar{1}0]$ below $T_0=145$ K. In contrast, the quadratic MR term is identical for both directions and peaks near $T_{\mathrm{OHE}}^*=155$ K, where the ordinary Hall coefficient crosses zero, consistent with carrier compensation under a two-band scenario. A sign change in the anomalous Hall signal at $T_{\mathrm{AHE}}^*=150$ K is consistent with a temperature-dependent redistribution of Berry-curvature contributions near the Fermi level. These results establish ferromagnetic ordering as the primary driver of the observed directional magnetotransport in SrRuO$_3$.

</details>


### [179] [Field theories and critical excitations at anyon condensation transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28148)
*Luisa Eck,Xie Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文针对任意子凝聚（anyon condensation）相变，尤其涉及非阿贝尔任意子（non-abelian anyons）的情形，提出两种互补方法，从母拓扑序（parent topological order）与凝聚体的范畴数据（categorical data）中提取相变信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
