Motivation: 任意子凝聚相变，特别是凝聚任意子为非阿贝尔时的刻画仍具挑战性；现有方法难以仅凭范畴数据判定相变性质，故需发展可从母拓扑序数据直接推断临界行为的手段。

Method: 第一种方法将用于SymTFT框架下1+1维相变的Ginzburg-Landau程序推广至2+1维任意子凝聚相变，适用于参与凝聚的任意子具有整数量子维度（integer quantum dimensions）的情形；第二种方法给出判定哪些点激发与圈激发在连续相变中临界的判据，关键在于确定哪些任意子圈（anyon loops）始终保持凝聚。

Result: 判据表明：保持凝聚的圈之外的其余圈均变为临界；点激发（point excitation）若与每个凝聚圈均平凡编织（braid trivially），则变为临界。两种方案在若干阿贝尔与非阿贝尔实例中得到展示。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了仅依赖母拓扑序与凝聚体范畴数据来刻画任意子凝聚相变的两条互补途径，可识别连续相变中的临界激发，并在阿贝尔与非阿贝尔示例中验证其适用性。

Abstract: Anyon condensation transitions remain challenging to characterize especially when the condensing anyons are non-abelian. We develop two complementary approaches for extracting information about the transition from the categorical data of the parent topological order and the condensate. The first adapts a Ginzburg-Landau procedure developed for $1+1$D transitions in the SymTFT setting to $2+1$D anyon condensation transitions. The construction applies when the anyons participating in the condensate have integer quantum dimensions. The second gives a criterion for identifying which point and loop excitations become critical at a continuous transition. The key step is to determine which anyon loops remain condensed throughout. All other loops become critical, while a point excitation becomes critical if it braids trivially with every condensed loop. We illustrate both proposals in several abelian and non-abelian examples.

</details>


### [180] [Proliferation Transitions for Quantum Doubles of Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28253)
*Yuhan Gai,Sakura Schafer-Nameki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文在量子二重态（quantum double）D(G, ω) 的框架下研究增殖相变（proliferation transition），利用对称拓扑场论（SymTFT）的构造从凝聚代数（condensable algebra）的分类数据直接推断相变性质，并证明"孪生代数"（twin algebras）虽任意子内容相同却导致不同的相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增殖相变是 2+1 维拓扑序（topological order）之间经由任意子凝聚（anyon condensation）实现的一类相变，但此前缺乏从代数数据出发系统刻画相变性质的框架，尤其对同一任意子内容是否足以决定相变这一问题尚不清楚，因此需要建立代数分类与相变物理之间的直接联系。

Method: 研究对象为有限群（含三上闭链 three-cocycle ω）的量子二重态 T = D(G, ω)，其可凝聚代数已被完全分类。给定可凝聚代数 A，将 T 表示为专门针对该 A 增殖相变定制的对称拓扑场论（SymTFT），其体（bulk）由代数所生成子范畴的 Müger 中心（Müger center）即透明线（transparent lines，可为玻色子或费米子）范畴构造；对于规范化的 Landau-Ginzburg（LG*）型相变，还分析代数乘法结构如何确定三次相互作用（cubic interactions）。

Result: 相变的性质可由代数分类数据直接读出。对 LG* 型相变，相变不仅通过场内容依赖 A 中的任意子，其三次相互作用由 A 的乘法结构（multiplication structure）完全固定。应用于具有相同任意子内容但乘法结构不同的孪生代数时，孪生体给出彼此不同的增殖相变。

Conclusion: 代数分类数据（而非仅任意子内容）足以且必要地决定增殖相变的物理性质；SymTFT 表示提供了从凝聚代数出发系统推断相变性质的统一工具，并揭示了乘法结构在区分相变中的关键作用。

Abstract: Proliferation transitions are phase transitions between a 2+1d topological order $\mathcal{T}$ and another $\mathcal{T}/\mathcal{A}$, obtained by condensing anyons in an algebra $\mathcal{A}\subset \mathcal{T}$. We consider such transitions in quantum doubles of groups, $\mathcal{T}=D(G, ω)$, for finite groups $G$ possibly with three-cocycle $ω$, for which condensable algebras are fully classified. Given a condensable algebra $\mathcal{A}$, we represent $\mathcal{T}$ in terms of a Symmetry Topological Field Theory (SymTFT), that is tailored to the $\mathcal{A}$ proliferation transition. The SymTFT bulk is constructed from the Müger center of the subcategory generated by the algebra, i.e. the category of transparent lines, which can be bosons or fermions. Properties of the transition can then be inferred directly from the algebra classification data. For gauged Landau-Ginzburg (LG*) type transitions, we show that they do not only depend on the anyons in $\mathcal{A}$ through their field content, but the cubic interactions are fixed in terms of the multiplication structure of $\mathcal{A}$. Applied to twin algebras, which have the same anyon content but different multiplication structures, we show that twins result in distinct proliferation transitions.

</details>


### [181] [Rare-earth tuning of magnetic entropy and interaction scales in K$_3$$Ln$(PO$_4$)$_2$ ($Ln$ = Yb, Gd) for adiabatic demagnetization refrigeration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28038)
*Yonglin Wang,Chuandi Zhang,Jianglong Zhang,Zihang Gao,Ziqi Li,Feiran Shen,Lunhua He,Peijie Sun,Hao Deng,Junsen Xiang,Wentao Jin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文研究稀土双磷酸盐 K3Ln(PO4)2（Ln = Yb, Gd）的低温磁性与绝热去磁制冷（adiabatic demagnetization refrigeration, ADR）性能，揭示 Yb 与 Gd 化合物在最低制冷温度与低温保持时间上的显著差异，并将其归因于磁熵与相互作用尺度的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于阻挫稀土磁体（frustrated rare-earth magnets）的 ADR 是实现无液氦亚开尔文（sub-Kelvin）制冷的可行途径，而优化 ADR 功能需在磁熵（magnetic entropy）与相互作用尺度（interaction scales）之间取得平衡。

Method: 选取单斜 P2_1/m 结构、含畸变等腰三角形稀土晶格的 K3Ln(PO4)2（Ln = Yb, Gd），通过磁化与比热测量确认磁性基态，并开展准 ADR（quasi-ADR）直接测量，辅以晶体电场（crystal-electric-field）计算与基于磁熵和相互作用尺度的热力学分析。

Result: 两种化合物在 0.1 K 以上均无长程磁有序（long-range magnetic ordering）；K3Yb(PO4)2 从 2 K、6 T 初态可降至 29.8 mK，而 K3Gd(PO4)2 因磁熵储库更大而在 150 mK 以下表现出更长的保持时间；晶体电场计算确认 K3Yb(PO4)2 基态为 J_eff = 1/2 双峰（ground-state doublet）。

Conclusion: K3Ln(PO4)2 可作为化学稳健（chemically robust）、稀土可调的固态 ADR 平台。

Abstract: Adiabatic demagnetization refrigeration (ADR) based on frustrated rare-earth magnets provides a promising route toward helium-free sub-Kelvin cooling, while balancing magnetic entropy and interaction scales is essential for optimizing the ADR functionalities. Here we investigate the low-temperature magnetic and ADR properties of the alkali rare-earth double phosphates K$_3$$Ln$(PO$_4$)$_2$ ($Ln$ = Yb, Gd), which crystallize in a monoclinic $P2_1/m$ structure containing distorted isosceles triangular lattices of rare-earth ions. Magnetization and specific heat measurements confirm the absence of long-range magnetic ordering down to 0.1 K in both compounds. Direct quasi-ADR measurements demonstrate strongly contrasting refrigeration behaviors: K$_3$Yb(PO$_4$)$_2$ reaches a minimum temperature of 29.8 mK from an initial state of 2 K and 6 T, whereas K$_3$Gd(PO$_4$)$_2$ exhibits extended hold times below 150 mK associated with its larger magnetic entropy reservoir. Crystal-electric-field calculations confirm a $J_{\mathrm{eff}} = 1/2$ ground-state doublet in K$_3$Yb(PO$_4$)$_2$, while thermodynamic analysis based on magnetic entropy and interaction scales accounts for the distinct ADR performance of the two compounds. These results identify K$_3$$Ln$(PO$_4$)$_2$ as a chemically robust platform for rare-earth-tunable solid-state ADR.

</details>


### [182] [Contrasting Crystal-Field and Dipolar Anisotropies in the Triangular Antiferromagnet NaBaEr(BO$_3$)$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28041)
*Cheng Su,Yonglin Wang,Zihang Gao,Xinyang Liu,Jianglong Zhang,Chin-Wei Wang,Michael Marek Koza,Juping Xu,Wen Yin,Hao Deng,Wei Li,Junsen Xiang,Wentao Jin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 结合磁化率、比热与中子散射，确定三角格子反铁磁体 NaBaEr(BO$_3$)$_2$ 的晶体结构为低对称 $R\bar{3}$，其低温磁性可用强易轴各向异性的有效自旋-1/2 描述，并在 $T_{\rm N}=0.1$ K 以下形成磁矩主要位于 $ab$ 面的 A 型反铁磁序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土阻挫磁体（rare-earth frustrated magnets）的磁基态受晶体场各向异性、交换相互作用与长程偶极相互作用（dipolar interactions）等多种竞争能量尺度共同支配，本文旨在研究这些机制在三角格子反铁磁体中的相互竞争。

Method: 对 NaBaEr(BO$_3$)$_2$ 开展磁化测量、比热测量与中子散射（neutron scattering）测量；其中中子粉末衍射（neutron powder diffraction）用于确定结构，非弹性中子散射用于建立自旋模型，单晶中子衍射用于确定磁有序，并辅以经典偶极能量计算。

Result: 中子粉末衍射分辨出 ErO$_6$ 八面体旋转，支持低对称 $R\bar{3}$ 结构而非此前的 $R\bar{3}m$ 模型；磁性与非弹性中子散射给出有效自旋-1/2 图像，Kramers 基态双重态与第一激发晶体电场能级间隔约 4 meV，易轴各向异性显著；在 $T_{\rm N}=0.1$ K 以下观测到 A 型反铁磁序，有序磁矩主要位于 $ab$ 面内。

Conclusion: 估计的偶极相互作用尺度与 $T_{\rm N}$ 相当，经典偶极能量计算对所观测堆垛模式支持面内磁矩取向；结果表明晶体场各向异性与偶极各向异性在决定 NaBaEr(BO$_3$)$_2$ 磁基态时呈现出相互对立的效应。

Abstract: The magnetic ground states of rare-earth frustrated magnets can be governed by competing energy scales arising from crystal-field anisotropy, exchange interactions, and long-range dipolar interactions. Here we investigate this interplay in the triangular-lattice antiferromagnet NaBaEr(BO$_3$)$_2$ by combining magnetization, specific heat, and neutron scattering measurements. Neutron powder diffraction resolves rotations of the ErO$_6$ octahedra and supports the lower-symmetry $R\bar{3}$ structure over the previously proposed $R\bar{3}m$ model. Magnetization measurements and inelastic neutron scattering establish an effective spin-1/2 description of its low-temperature magnetism with pronounced easy-axis anisotropy, arising from a well-isolated Kramers ground-state doublet separated from the first excited crystal-electric-field level by approximately 4 meV. Below $T_{\rm N}=0.1$ K, single-crystal neutron diffraction reveals A-type antiferromagnetic order with the ordered moment predominantly oriented within the $ab$ plane, despite the pronounced paramagnetic easy-axis anisotropy. The estimated dipolar interaction scale is comparable to $T_{\rm N}$, and classical dipolar-energy calculations for the observed stacking pattern favor an in-plane moment orientation. These results reveal contrasting crystal-field and dipolar anisotropies in determining the magnetic ground state of NaBaEr(BO$_3$)$_2$.

</details>


### [183] [Neural Network Fractional Quantum Hall States with Learnable Orbital Pair Matrix Factors: Balancing Physical Priors and Flexibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28095)
*Yubing Qian,Weiluo Ren,Ji Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出轨道对矩阵因子MHPO（MHPO-M）拟设，以可学习、依赖电子构型与轨道的对因子替代固定Laughlin结构，在保持神经网络波函数灵活性的同时恰当引入物理先验，在ν=1/5、1/3、2/5下取得最低变分能并扩展至35个电子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络分数量子霍尔（FQH）态有望刻画朗道能级（LL）混合并理解FQH物理，但物理先验的引入此前仅限于拟设内置的最小精确约束；仅提供极少量附加先验时网络难以优化，而刚性施加通量附着（flux attachment）等先验又会限制网络表达力。

Method: 在球面几何（spherical geometry）下分析先验引入方式对优化与表达力的影响，进而提出MHPO-M拟设：将固定的Laughlin结构替换为可学习的对因子（pair factors），并使其同时依赖电子构型与轨道；另构建基于该拟设的因子化轨道读出（factorized orbital readout）。

Result: 在ν=1/5、1/3与2/5下，MHPO-M在所测试变体中取得最低变分能（variational energy）；因子化轨道读出使其可扩展至35个电子，能量与由三朗道能级精确对角化（exact diagonalization）结果推断的趋势具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 结果表明确实可以在不牺牲神经网络波函数灵活性的前提下恰当纳入物理先验，为含LL混合的FQH系统提供准确且可扩展的建模路径。

Abstract: Neural network fractional quantum Hall (FQH) states offer a promising alternative for capturing Landau level (LL) mixing and for understanding FQH physics, yet the incorporation of physical priors has so far been limited to the minimal exact constraints built into the ansatz. Working in spherical geometry, we show that the network's flexibility suffers both when only minimal additional physical priors are supplied and when priors such as flux attachment are imposed rigidly. In the former case, the monopole harmonic product orbital (MHPO) ansatz is difficult to optimize, whereas in the latter, the expressivity of the neural network is restricted. We therefore propose the orbital pair matrix factor MHPO (MHPO-M) ansatz, which balances physical priors against flexibility by not only replacing the fixed Laughlin structure with learnable pair factors but also making them dependent on both the electron configurations and the orbitals. Across $ν=1/5$, $1/3$, and $2/5$, MHPO-M achieves the lowest variational energy among the tested variants, and a factorized orbital readout based on it enables scaling to 35 electrons, with energies competitive with trends inferred from three-LL exact-diagonalization results. These results demonstrate that physical priors can be appropriately incorporated without sacrificing the flexibility of neural network wavefunctions, opening a promising path toward accurate, scalable modeling of FQH systems with LL mixing.

</details>


### [184] [Classical spin liquids in the kagome Ising model, and their link with magnetoelasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28298)
*T. Vignau Costa,J. Colbois,G. L. Rossini,F. Mila,D. C. Cabra,S. A. Grigera,R. A. Borzi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作研究kagome晶格上最近邻Ising模型与弹性晶格畸变耦合的问题，通过积分掉晶格自由度得到kagome \(J_1\)-\(J_2\)-\(J_{3}\) Ising有效模型，并用张量网络方法与经典Monte Carlo重新审视其零温相图，发现具有广延零温熵、自旋关联呈代数衰减的多个扩展相区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁弹性耦合如何通过诱导长程有效交换相互作用改变阻挫磁体的基态相图与有序性质，尤其是对三十多年前已研究但特性未明的kagome Ising基态，需要重新刻画其是否有广延的临界相区。

Method: 对kagome晶格最近邻Ising模型与弹性畸变的耦合体系积分掉晶格自由度，得到含第三近邻的近似纯自旋\(J_1\)-\(J_2\)-\(J_{3}\) Ising模型；采用张量网络方法并结合经典Monte Carlo模拟，重新计算零温相图与基态性质，并追踪磁弹性耦合在有效模型相图中的轨迹。

Result: 发现三个具有广延零温熵与代数衰减自旋关联的相，它们不仅位于精细调节点或相边界，而占据参数空间的有限区域；其准长程序（quasi long-range order）体现为结构因子中的类Bragg峰（Bragg-like peaks），并与Ising及Heisenberg模型中的多种相相关联。

Conclusion: kagome \(J_1\)-\(J_2\)-\(J_{3}\) Ising模型的相图包含广延的代数关联相区；磁弹性耦合生成的参数轨迹可解释无阻挫铁磁磁弹性模型中自阻挫（self-frustration）的出现，并将该有效模型与Ising/Heisenberg模型的多种相联系起来。

Abstract: We investigate a nearest-neighbor Ising model on the kagome lattice coupled to elastic lattice distortions. By integrating out the lattice degrees of freedom, we derive an approximate spin-only description in terms of a kagome \(J_1\)-\(J_2\)-\(J_{3}\) Ising model with interactions extending to third nearest-neighbors. Using tensor network methods complemented by classical Monte Carlo simulations, we revisit its zero-temperature phase diagram and characterize the ground states studied more than thirty years ago. We discover three phases with extensive zero-temperature entropy and algebraically decaying spin correlations. Importantly, these states are not confined to fine-tuned points or frontiers between phases, but occupy extended areas of parameter space. Their quasi long-range order is reflected in Bragg-like peaks in the structure factors; we relate this, and other properties, to diverse phases found in Ising and Heisenberg models. Finally, we trace the trajectories generated by the magnetoelastic coupling through the effective-model phase diagram, explaining the emergence of self-frustration for the unfrustrated magnetoelastic model with ferromagnetic interactions.

</details>


### [185] [Sum-over-poles framework for quantum embedding to approach the real-frequency axis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28472)
*Alessandro Carbone,Massimo Capone,Nicola Marzari,Tommaso Chiarotti*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出基于求和极点表示（sum-over-poles, SOP）的固体量子嵌入实频框架，将晶格划分为周期性重复的相互作用碎片并自洽求解嵌入Dyson方程，避免了解析延拓的病态问题，并在一维半满Hubbard环上与DMFT的精确对角化实现进行了基准对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子嵌入方法常依赖解析延拓（analytic continuation），存在数值病态问题；本文旨在构建接近实轴求解的实频框架，以规避该困难。

Method: 将晶格划分为周期性重复的相互作用碎片，推导碎片的嵌入Dyson方程（embedded Dyson equation）并自洽求解；利用嵌入势（embedding potential）的紧凑极点展开在近实轴处执行自洽流程，并引入极点-残差动力学（pole-and-residue dynamics）；该方法将前期工作提出的算法反演法推广至相互作用哈密顿量，且与动力学平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory, DMFT）密切相关。

Result: 在一维半满Hubbard环（one-dimensional Hubbard ring at half filling）上，以DMFT的精确对角化实现作为基准进行验证。

Conclusion: 该框架为实空间动力学量子嵌入提供了自然路径，适用于周期体系以及材料中的强关联缺陷。

Abstract: We introduce a real-frequency framework for the quantum embedding of solids, based on sum-over-poles (SOP) representations. We partition the lattice into periodically repeated interacting fragments and derive an embedded Dyson equation for the fragment, to be solved self-consistently. The resulting scheme generalizes the algorithmic-inversion method of our previous work to interacting Hamiltonians and is closely related to dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT), especially when an extended impurity problem is introduced. By exploiting the compact pole expansion of the embedding potential, we carry out the self-consistent procedure close to the real axis, which avoids ill-posed analytic continuation. In addition, the SOP representation allows us to introduce a pole-and-residue dynamics, which we use to reach the self-consistent solution. After deriving the method, we benchmark it against exact-diagonalization implementations of DMFT on the one-dimensional Hubbard ring at half filling. The framework offers a natural route to real-space dynamical quantum embedding, for periodic systems as well as for strongly correlated defects in materials.

</details>


### [186] [Acceleration of CTQMC via Denoising Diffusion Models with Auxiliary Spectral Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28542)
*Rishi Rao,Li Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩散模型（diffusion model）的神经去噪器（neural denoiser），用于处理连续时间量子蒙特卡洛（Continuous-time Quantum Monte Carlo, CTQMC）杂质求解器产生的虚时格林函数（Green's function）噪声，在保证因果性的同时将所需测量步数降低 10-100 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTQMC 杂质求解器虽数值精确但计算昂贵，欠采样导致的统计噪声严重制约低温研究；采样得到的自能（self-energy）噪声尤为突出，代入戴森方程（Dyson equation）后可能破坏自洽循环的稳定性。

Method: 构建基于扩散模型架构、以合成数据训练的神经去噪器，从含噪虚轴 CTQMC 数据预测正定且归一化的辅助谱函数（auxiliary spectral function），并借助自动微分（autodifferentiation）使其与测得自能、前两个高频矩（high-frequency moments）及杂质占据数匹配，思路类似约束残差最小化（Constrained Residual Minimization, CRM）方法。

Result: 在 Bethe 晶格半满 Hubbard 模型（仅 5000 次测量重现莫特转变（Mott transition））、二维 Hubbard 模型的 DCA 双 patch 计算（仅 5×10⁵ 次测量重现赝能隙转变（pseudogap transition））以及 SrVO₃ 体系（测量量比参考少 50 倍仍匹配谱函数）上完成基准测试，整体测量步数降低 10-100 倍。

Conclusion: 该去噪框架所生成的格林函数与自能在设计上满足因果性（causal by design），并可免费获得体系的近似谱函数而无需执行解析延拓（analytic continuation），为降低 CTQMC 计算成本提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Continuous-time Quantum Monte Carlo (CTQMC) impurity solvers are a numerically exact, but expensive, option for solving the Dynamical Mean Field Theory (DMFT) equations, with statistical noise from undersampling presenting a major challenge to low temperature studies. This noise is especially egregious within the sampled self-energy, which can destabilize the self-consistency loop upon insertion into the Dyson equation. In this work, we introduce a neural denoiser for fermionic imaginary time Green's functions based on the diffusion model architecture trained on synthetic data. The network predicts positive and normalized auxiliary spectral functions consistent with the noisy imaginary axis CTQMC data, which is then refined via autodifferentiation to match the measured self-energy, its first two high-frequency moments, and impurity occupation similar to the Constrained Residual Minimization (CRM) method. This leads to Green's functions and self-energies that are causal by design. Through this denoising framework we are able to reduce the required CTQMC measurement steps by a factor of 10-100. We benchmark our method with a half-filled Hubbard model on a Bethe lattice, a two-patch Dynamical Cluster Approximation (DCA) of the two-dimensional Hubbard model, and the SrVO$_3$ system. We reproduce the Mott transition upon increasing Coulomb interaction for the half-filled Hubbard model with only 5000 CTQMC measurements, the pseudogap transition for the DCA two-dimensional Hubbard model with only 5$\times$10$^5$ measurements, and match the spectral function of SrVO$_3$ with 50 times fewer measurements than the reference. Since we generate auxiliary spectral functions, we recover the approximate spectral functions of the systems for free without performing analytic continuation.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [Exact spectral integrability of a finite-channel random Schrödinger operator: from fermionization of the Dyson--Riccati gas to Kontsevich integral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27154)
*Yan V. Fyodorov,Pierre Le Doussal*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文对 β=2 幺正对称类下带 Hermitian 白噪声势的准一维随机矩阵 Schrödinger 算符的 Riccati 动力学给出了任意有限通道数 N 的精确解，并通过单个 Airy Hankel τ 函数统一刻画态密度与 Lyapunov 增长率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 N=1 的 Halperin–Thouless 公式仅适用于单通道情形，缺乏对任意有限通道数 N 的精确多通道推广，且有限 N 密度尾与大 N 谱边行为难以统一处理。

Method: 将 Dyson–Riccati 本征值气体表示为精确的自由费米子（free-fermion）表示，在 L→∞ 极限用 Airy Hankel τ 函数表达态密度与总 Lyapunov 增长率，并给出外向围道 Kontsevich 矩阵 Airy 积分表示；再通过鞍点（saddle-point）与双标度（double-scaling）分析处理有限通道与大 N 极限。

Result: 统一得到有限通道密度的 Lifshitz 尾与大 N 谱边结果；对 N≫1，尖锐的 N=∞ 谱边在 N^{-4/5} 谱窗口内被软化，其剖面由 Painlevé I 的 tritronquée 解支配。

Conclusion: 作者猜想该准一维标度谱边剖面对于一大类 β=2 随机带矩阵（random band matrices）具有普适性。

Abstract: We solve exactly, for arbitrary finite channel number $N$, the Riccati dynamics of a quasi-one-dimensional random matrix Schrödinger operator with Hermitian white-noise potential in the $β=2$ unitary symmetry class. The associated Dyson--Riccati eigenvalue gas admits an exact free-fermion representation at any sample length $L$, which in the limit $L\to \infty$ expresses the density of states and the total Lyapunov growth rate exactly through a single Airy Hankel $τ$-function providing multichannel generalization of $N=1$ Halperin--Thouless formulas. The $τ-$function further admits an outgoing-contour Kontsevich matrix Airy integral representation. Its saddle-point and double-scaling analyses give, in a unified way, the finite-channel density Lifshitz tail, and the large-$N$ spectral edge. In particular, for $N\gg 1$ the sharp $N=\infty$ edge is softened within an $N^{-4/5}$ spectral window, with its profile governed by the tritronquée solution of Painlevé I. We conjecture universality of this quasi-one-dimensional scaling edge profile for a broad class of $β=2$ random band matrices.

</details>


### [188] [Moments of Random Permanents and Hafnians: A Replica Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27872)
*Paolo Facchi,Angelo Mariano,Giorgio Parisi,Saverio Pascazio,Antonello Scardicchio*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文利用积永（permanent）与哈夫尼安（Hafnian）的围道积分（contour-integral）表示，将随机矩阵相应矩的计算转化为复制（replica）计算，并求得主导鞍点解及分布的解析结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机矩阵的积永与哈夫尼安之矩在玻色采样（boson sampling）等场景中具有重要意义，但缺乏闭式解析结果，故需借助统计物理方法（如复制方法）加以处理。

Method: 采用积永与哈夫尼安的围道积分表示，把随机矩阵矩的计算转化为类似平均场自旋玻璃（mean-field spin glass）配分函数矩的复制计算，并对N×N独立元素矩阵的2k阶矩求解主导鞍点。

Result: k>2时主导鞍点为复制对称（replica symmetric），给出指数增长率、前因子及首个1/N修正的闭式结果，与精确枚举一致；增长率仅取决于矩阵元素为实或复，高阶累积量只影响前因子与1/N项；k=2时复制对称解失稳，分布分为幂律主体与大偏差尾部（large-deviation tail）。

Conclusion: 结果适用于单光子及压缩光（squeezed light）的玻色采样，为相关计数问题的统计性质提供了解析描述。

Abstract: Using contour-integral representations of the permanent and of the Hafnian, we turn the computation of their moments for random matrices into a replica calculation, analogous to that of the moments of the partition function of a mean-field spin glass. For the $2k$-th moment of $N\times N$ matrices with independent entries, the dominant saddle point is replica symmetric for $k>2$, and we obtain in closed form the exponential growth rate, the prefactor and the first $1/N$ correction, in agreement with exact enumerations. The growth rate depends only on whether the entries are real or complex; higher cumulants affect only the prefactor and the $1/N$ term. At $k=2$ the replica-symmetric solution becomes unstable, separating a power-law bulk of the distribution from a large-deviation tail, which we compute. The results apply to boson sampling with single photons and with squeezed light.

</details>


### [189] [Ubiquitous Flat Bands in Edge-Inflated Random Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28461)
*Richard Berkovits*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文证明平坦带（flat bands）并非周期性晶格（periodic lattice）专属，而可通过简单的“边膨胀（edge-inflation）”程序在多种非周期随机网络（nonperiodic random networks）中普遍产生，并揭示其退化度与图零度（graph nullity）及最大匹配数（maximum matching number）的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平坦带通常被认为依赖周期性晶格几何中的破坏性干涉（destructive interference）或手性对称性（chiral-symmetry）约束。作者的动机是检验平坦带是否必须依赖晶体序（crystalline order），还是可由更一般的图结构机制产生。

Method: 对随机正则图（random regular）、Voronoi 镶嵌（Voronoi tessellation）、Delaunay 三角剖分（Delaunay triangulation）、无标度图（scale-free）、小世界图（small-world）及 Erdös-Rényi 种子图，将每条边替换为有限一维紧束缚链（tight-binding chain），链长可为固定值 L 或均值 L̄ 的随机长度；更一般地，用相同的无环模体（loopless motif）替换种子键。

Result: 原随机图的邻接矩阵（adjacency matrix）通常满秩、几乎无零本征态，但其膨胀版本表现出大范围平坦带；平坦带能量由链上本征态或替代模体的本征值决定，其本征态为种子顶点处破坏性干涉所支持的链子集态。零能平坦带的退化度与图零度 η(G) 和最大匹配数 ν(G) 相关联，数值结果支持 η(G)=N−2ν(G)（存在小偏差），退化零能态比例主要由种子图平均度（average degree）控制。

Conclusion: 平坦带对种子位点无序与随机磁通（random magnetic flux）基本稳健，但键无序及链/模体位点无序会使其展宽；因此平坦带并不需要晶体序，而可作为局部图膨胀（graph inflation）与模体替换（motif substitution）的稳健结果涌现。

Abstract: Flat bands are usually associated with periodic lattice geometries, where destructive interference or chiral-symmetry constraints produce macroscopically degenerate eigenstates. Here we show that flat bands arise generically in a broad class of nonperiodic random networks through a simple edge-inflation procedure. Starting from random regular, Voronoi tessellation, Delaunay triangulation, scale-free, small-world, and Erdös-Rényi seed graphs, each edge is replaced by a finite one-dimensional tight-binding chain, either with fixed length $L$ or with random lengths of mean $\bar L$. Although the original random graphs typically have adjacency matrices of full rank and therefore few or no zero eigenstates, their inflated counterparts exhibit extensive flat bands. The flat-band energies are determined by the eigenstates residing on the chains or, more generally, by the eigenvalues of identical loopless motifs replacing the seed bonds. The corresponding eigenstates are states supported on subsets of the inflated chains formed by destructive interference at the original seed vertices. For zero-energy flat bands, we relate the degeneracy to the graph nullity $η(G)$ and the maximum matching number $ν(G)$. The numerical results support the correspondence $η(G)=N-2ν(G)$, up to small deviations, across the random graph ensembles studied. The fraction of degenerate zero-energy states in inflated random graphs is controlled primarily by the average degree of the seed graph. We further show that the flat bands exhibit characteristic robustness to disorder: disorder on the seed sites and random magnetic flux leave the flat bands essentially unchanged, while bond disorder and disorder on the chain or motif sites broaden them. These results show that flat bands do not require crystalline order, but can emerge as robust consequences of local graph inflation and motif substitution.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Boundary One-Point Functions in the Open XXZ Spin Chain with Generic Integrable Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27062)
*Till Bargheer,Tengis Batmunkh,Paul Ryan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用泛函分离变量法（Functional Separation of Variables），在具有最一般可积边界条件的开放 XXZ 自旋链中，于有限系统尺寸下精确计算了边界自旋的单点函数（one-point functions），并给出行列式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在最一般可积边界条件下，残余 u(1) 对称性被破坏，描述能谱的 Baxter TQ 方程变为非齐次形式，使该模型超出既有关联函数（correlation functions）与形状因子（form factors）方法的适用范围，故需寻找不受此类修改影响的新途径。

Method: 采用此前主要针对有理型、（准）周期自旋链发展的泛函分离变量法（Functional Separation of Variables），论证该方法对上述边界修改基本不敏感，并同时适用于对角边界与一般边界。

Result: 在任意哈密顿量本征态、有限系统尺寸下，获得开放边界系统形状因子典型例子——纵向与横向边界自旋单点函数——的精确行列式表达式；无需两种边界构型之间的约束关系，也无需参考态（reference state）。所得公式已通过与直接有限体积对角化（finite-volume diagonalisation）的比对得到验证。

Conclusion: 泛函分离变量法可推广至对称性破缺、TQ 方程非齐次的最一般可积开放边界情形，为开放 XXZ 链中谱外可观测量的精确计算提供可行框架。

Abstract: We consider the problem of computing observables beyond the spectrum in the open XXZ spin chain with the most generic integrable boundary conditions. For such boundaries, the residual u(1) symmetry is broken and the familiar Baxter TQ equation describing the spectrum becomes inhomogeneous, placing the model outside the realm of existing approaches to correlation functions and form factors. We show that the Functional Separation of Variables method, so far developed mainly for rational, (quasi-)periodic spin chains, is largely insensitive to these modifications, and works equally well for diagonal and generic boundaries. Using this method, we compute exact determinant expressions for a prototypical example of form factors in open-boundary systems, namely the longitudinal and transverse boundary-spin one-point functions in arbitrary Hamiltonian eigenstates at finite system size. No constraint relating the two boundary configurations and no reference state are required. We verify the resulting formulae against direct finite-volume diagonalisation.

</details>


### [191] [Analytical solution of electromagnetic heating in a stratified absorbing medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27274)
*Ulises Jaime-Yepez,Hongyun Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该研究推导了电磁波垂直入射分层皮肤组织时的解析解，其中仅电磁衰减为分层分布，热容与导热系数在全组织内保持均匀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤暴露于电磁波（electromagnetic wave）时，被吸收的功率在波衰减过程中转化为热；由于衰减特征深度（characteristic depth of attenuation）远小于电磁束横向尺寸，横向热传导（lateral heat conduction）在首阶可忽略，此前仅针对单一均匀层皮肤建立了首阶解析解与二阶渐近修正，缺乏对分层皮肤组织的解析处理。

Method: 将先前针对单层均匀皮肤、电磁波垂直入射情形的解析方法推广至分层（stratified）皮肤组织，允许电磁衰减具有层特定（layer-specific）的分布，从而得到相应的温度演化解析解（analytical solution），同时保持热容（heat capacity）与导热系数（conductivity）在整个皮肤组织内均匀。

Result: 在垂直入射（perpendicular incidence）条件下，得到分层皮肤组织中温度演化的解析解，其中电磁衰减按层分布并产生层特定的加热分布（heating profile）。

Conclusion: 该工作在仅电磁衰减分层的简化假设下，将电磁加热解析理论由单层均匀皮肤拓展到分层皮肤组织；热学参数仍取均匀值，分层效应仅通过加热分布体现。

Abstract: We consider the temperature evolution in a skin tissue when it is exposed to an electromagnetic wave. The heating is caused by the absorbed power as the electromagnetic wave attenuates inside the skin. In many applications, the characteristic depth of attenuation is small in comparison with the lateral size of the electromagnetic beam. Because of this separation of length scales, the lateral heat conduction is negligible at leading order. Previously, we studied electromagnetic heating in a skin of a single homogeneous layer. We derived the leading-term analytical solution and the asymptotic corrections up to the second order for lateral heat conduction and for oblique incidence. In this study, we derive the analytical solution in the case of perpendicular incidence on a stratified skin tissue with a layer-specific profile for electromagnetic attenuation. It should be emphasized that in the current study, the heat capacity and conductivity are still uniform over the entire skin tissue; only the electromagnetic attenuation is stratified resulting in a layer-specific heating profile.

</details>


### [192] [Counterexamples to the total-current Hohenberg-Kohn conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27294)
*Andre Laestadius,Mihály A. Csirik*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文针对“总电流 Hohenberg-Kohn 猜想（total-current Hohenberg-Kohn conjecture）”，在三维空间中构造三个双无自旋费米子（spinless fermions）反例，表明基态密度与总电流不能唯一确定标量势和矢量势（即使总电流恒为零、磁场非零），从而否定该猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 总电流 Hohenberg-Kohn 猜想声称基态粒子密度与总电流可将标量势确定至常数、矢量势确定至规范变换（gauge transformation）。若成立，将为含磁场的密度泛函理论（density functional theory）提供唯一性基础；本文旨在检验该猜想在物理相关三维情形下是否成立。

Method: 构造三个双无自旋费米子反例：均利用复共轭（complex conjugation）将哈密顿量 $H^{v,\mathbf{A}}$ 映射为 $H^{v,-\mathbf{A}}$，保持标量势与相互作用相同、基态密度相同且总电流为零，但磁场反向。前两例无相互作用，其一含显式光滑有界矢量势与高斯密度，其二标量势与矢量势属标准势空间中的光滑有界函数；第三例含单位耦合库仑排斥（Coulomb repulsion）、各向同性谐振标量势及磁荷 $\pm 1/2$ 的狄拉克单极子场（Dirac monopole），其矢量势需在不同空间片（patches）上分别定义。

Result: 三个反例均给出非简并基态、总电流恒为零而磁场非零，且存在另一个哈密顿量与之间密度、同零电流但磁场相反，构成对总电流 Hohenberg-Kohn 猜想的反例。

Conclusion: 总电流 Hohenberg-Kohn 猜想在三维双无自旋费米子情形下不成立；即便在总电流为零的特殊设定中，基态密度与总电流亦不足以确定磁场乃至矢量势的唯一性。

Abstract: The total-current Hohenberg-Kohn conjecture asserts that the ground-state particle density and total current determine the scalar potential up to an additive constant and the vector potential up to a gauge transformation. We give three counterexamples for two spinless fermions in the physically relevant setting of three spatial dimensions. In each, a Hamiltonian $H^{v,\mathbf{A}}$ has a nondegenerate ground state with identically zero total current but nonzero magnetic field. Complex conjugation then yields a second Hamiltonian $H^{v,-\mathbf{A}}$, with the same scalar potential and interaction, whose ground state has the same density and the same zero current, while the magnetic field is reversed. The first two examples are noninteracting. One is explicit, with a smooth bounded vector potential and a Gaussian density. The other has smooth bounded scalar and vector potentials in the standard potential spaces. The third example has Coulomb repulsion with unit coupling, an isotropic harmonic scalar potential, and Dirac monopole fields of magnetic charge $\pm 1/2$. The monopole example uses vector potentials defined on separate spatial patches, since these fields are not curls of globally defined smooth vector potentials.

</details>


### [193] [Continuous and Differentiable Neural Network Representations of the Surfzone Sea-Surface from Lidar Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27352)
*David R. Culver,Matthias Morzfeld,Falk Feddersen*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该研究提出用SIREN神经网络从时序激光雷达点云中构建连续、可微且免网格的海面表示，并在加州Monterey的破碎波数据上验证其精度、可靠性与方向波分析潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海浪分析依赖海面观测，多波束扫描激光雷达（multi-beam scanning lidar）可通过时序点云获得二维海面高程η(x,y)，但点云需经网格化（gridding）、插值或映射才能用于波分析，限制了分析的灵活性与导数估计。

Method: 采用特定神经网络架构SIREN表示海面，并以一架气电混合无人机（UAS，drone）在加州Monterey上空悬停采集的60秒时空激光雷达数据进行训练；训练后的SIREN可在任意位置与时间估计海面及其导数，并通过独立训练两个SIREN检验可靠性与可重复性。

Result: SIREN表示倾向于滤除最高波数变化（浪花飞溅splash-up与海雾sea-spray）；其误差与仪器误差相当且对局部数据密度不敏感；两个独立SIREN间差异与仪器误差相当，但在数据稀疏区域增大。通过计算时空导数及由SIREN生成的时空切片估计波速，展示了其在方向波分析（directional wave analysis）中的潜力。

Conclusion: SIREN为激光雷达点云提供了一种连续、可微、免网格的海面表示方法，精度接近仪器误差，具备方向波分析能力；其主要局限在于滤除高波数细节及数据稀疏区域可靠性下降。

Abstract: Ocean waves are often analyzed via sea-surface observations. Multi-beam scanning lidar enables two-dimensional measurements of the water surface $η(x,y)$ via sequenced point clouds of sea-surface elevation. Point cloud data, however, have to be gridded, interpolated, or mapped for use in wave analysis. We present and test a method for creating a continuous and differentiable (space and time), but grid-free, representation of the sea-surface from time sequenced lidar point clouds. We use a specific neural network architecture, a SIREN, to represent the sea-surface and to train the network on spatiotemporal 60~s of lidar data collected by a gas-electric UAS (drone) hovering over large (4 m), breaking waves in Monterey, California. The trained SIREN allows estimation of the sea-surface and its derivatives at any location and time. SIREN sea-surface representations tend to filter the highest wave number variability (splash-up and sea-spray). SIREN errors are comparable to instrument errors and insensitive to local data density. The reliability and reproducibility of our approach is tested by training two SIRENs independently on the same data. Differences between the two independent SIRENs are comparable to instrument error, but increase in data-poor regions. SIRENs' potential for directional wave analysis is demonstrated by computing spatial and temporal derivatives and by estimating wave velocity from a SIREN-generated space-time slice of the sea-surface.

</details>


### [194] [Two routes to the Hadamard condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27359)
*Eleanor March*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文强化、推广并技术性地澄清了哈达玛条件（Hadamard condition）的两类常见动机——其在定义 Wick 多项式与应力-能量算符期望值中的作用，以及其与等效原理（equivalence principle）的关联，从而厘清该条件的地位与内涵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弯曲时空量子场论（quantum field theory on curved spacetime）中，哈达玛条件常被视为线性量子场“物理上合理”“类真空”态的必要条件，并广泛用于重整化（renormalizability）证明与霍金温度（Hawking temperature）推导等基础应用；但其动机缺乏统一且严格的技术说明，本文旨在弥补这一空缺。

Method: 采用概念分析与技术论证相结合的方式，对两类流行动机进行强化与推广，并使之处于可技术控制（bring under technical control）的框架之下。

Result: 厘清了哈达玛条件与等效原理的关系、其与 Wick 多项式及应力-能量算符期望值良定义性（well-definedness）的关系，以及哈达玛态在何种意义上属于“类真空（vacuum-like）”态。

Conclusion: 哈达玛条件的作用与地位得到更清晰的界定，其作为弯曲时空量子场论中“物理上合理”态判据的合理性获得更有力的概念与技术支撑。

Abstract: In quantum field theory on curved spacetime, the Hadamard condition is often presented as a necessary condition on 'physically reasonable', 'vacuum-like' states of linear quantum fields, and plays a central role in many theoretical and foundational applications - ranging from proofs of the renormalizability of Wick polynomials to derivations of the Hawking temperature. In this paper, I strengthen, generalise, and bring under technical control two popular motivations for the Hadamard condition - one which appeals to the role of Hadamard states in defining physical quantities such as Wick polynomials and the expectation value of the stress-energy operator on general curved spacetime backgrounds, and one which appeals to the equivalence principle. This clarifies the role and status of the Hadamard condition, including its relationship to the equivalence principle, to well-definedness of physical quantities such as Wick polynomials and the expectation value of the stress-energy operator, and the sense in which Hadamard states are 'vacuum-like'.

</details>


### [195] [Phonon Dispersion Relation in Weakly Coupled Bose Gas Beyond Bogoliubov Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27810)
*Jan Dereziński,Lorenzo Pettinari*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究零温下均匀玻色气体（Bose gas）在周期性边界大立方盒中的准粒子色散关系（quasiparticle dispersion relation），在正则与巨正则系综下计算色散关系的首个非平庸修正，并指出小动量下修正与动量绝对值呈线性关系，从而给出声子（phonon）的确定速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色气体的色散关系在弱耦合区通常由 Bogoliubov 方法给出，但其修正项的性质（尤其小动量极限下是否保持线性）需在有限盒子中系统地检验；正则与巨正则系综的差异亦需澄清。

Method: 受 Bogoliubov 方法启发，将常规玻色气体哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）重排为适合弱耦合微扰理论（perturbation theory）的形式，分别在正则系综（canonical ensemble）与巨正则系综（grand-canonical ensemble）下计算色散关系的首个非平庸修正。

Result: 在两种系综设定下均得到色散关系的一阶非平庸修正；对小动量，该修正与动量的绝对值成正比，因此在该近似下声子具有良定义的速度，并给出了该速度的表达式。

Conclusion: 弱耦合玻色气体的准粒子谱在小动量下保持线性（声学型）行为，修正未破坏声子速度的良定义性；该结果为正则与巨正则两种系综下的色散修正提供了对照。

Abstract: We study the quasiparticle dispersion relation for a homogeneous Bose gas in a large cubic box with periodic boundary conditions at zero temperature. Inspired by the Bogoliubov method, we rearrange the usual Bose gas Hamiltonian in a form adapted to perturbation theory in the weak-coupling regime, both in the canonical and grand-canonical setting. We compute the first non-trivial corrections to the dispersion relation in both settings. We show that for small momenta they are linear in the absolute value of the momentum. Thus, in this approximation phonons have a well-defined velocity, which we compute.

</details>


### [196] [Half-delocalization for semiclassical measures of split higher-dimensional quantum cat maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28026)
*Vincent Ferrari-Dominguez*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究多维环面上的量子猫映射（quantum cat maps），在动力学于有理数上可分裂（splits over the rationals）的假设下，为其半经典测度（semiclassical measures）的Kolmogorov-Sinai熵证明了最优下界，这是首个针对具有多个正Lyapunov指数（Lyapunov exponents）的系统的此类界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于半经典测度熵下界的结果尚未覆盖具有多个正Lyapunov指数的动力学系统，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 证明基于新提出的条件量子动力学熵（conditional quantum dynamical entropy）概念，并结合对适应于该动力学的多重Ehrenfest时间（multiple Ehrenfest time）的细致分析。

Result: 在动力学于有理数上分裂的假设下，得到了量子猫映射半经典测度的Kolmogorov-Sinai熵的最优下界。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为具有多个正Lyapunov指数的系统建立了此类熵下界，所引入的条件量子动力学熵与多重Ehrenfest时间分析构成了证明的核心工具。

Abstract: We study quantum cat maps on multidimensional tori and prove an optimal lower bound for the Kolmogorov-Sinai entropy of their semiclassical measures under the hypothesis that the dynamics splits over the rationals. This is the first instance of such a bound for a dynamical system with multiple positive Lyapunov exponents. The proof is based on a new notion of conditional quantum dynamical entropy and a careful study of the multiple Ehrenfest time adapted to the dynamics.

</details>


### [197] [The concentration conjecture for Dirac--Coulomb operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28193)
*Habib Ammari,Jiayu Qiu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明：带质量 m>0 的三维 Dirac 算子（Dirac operator）在吸引 Coulomb 势、由有限多个点电荷生成时，其谱隙（spectral gap）内最低特征值至少为 m√(1−G²)，等号当且仅当全部电荷集中于一点时成立，并推广至任意非负有限电荷测度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对固定总电荷 G∈(0,1] 的多中心吸引 Coulomb 势，作者希望解决"电荷浓缩猜想"（concentration conjecture），即最低特征值下界以及等号刻画。

Method: 证明依赖加权自由 Dirac 预解式（weighted free Dirac resolvent）的严格双中心估计，以及 Birman-Schwinger 比较；双中心估计通过辅助轴向算子（auxiliary axial operator）获得，结合零能界（zero-energy bound）、标量预解式估计、径向分解（radial decomposition）、角向 Schur 补（angular Schur complement）与精确多项式不等式。

Result: 谱隙内最低特征值满足下界 m√(1−G²)，等号仅在所有电荷集中于一点时取得；单中心临界算子取其在临界情形下的区分扩张（distinguished extension）。结论也适用于任意非负有限电荷测度。

Conclusion: 该结果完整证实了三维 Dirac 算子多中心 Coulomb 势的浓缩猜想，并给出显式等号条件与向一般电荷测度的推广。

Abstract: We prove the concentration conjecture for the three-dimensional Dirac operator with mass $m>0$ and an attractive Coulomb potential generated by finitely many point charges. For a fixed total charge $G\in(0,1]$, the lowest eigenvalue in the spectral gap is at least $m\sqrt{1-G^2}$, with equality precisely when all the charge is concentrated at one point. The critical one-center operator is understood as its distinguished extension. The result also extends to arbitrary nonnegative finite charge measures. The proof uses a strict two-center estimate for the weighted free Dirac resolvent and a Birman-Schwinger comparison. We establish the two-center estimate through an auxiliary axial operator, combining a zero-energy bound and a scalar resolvent estimate with a radial decomposition, an angular Schur complement, and exact polynomial inequalities.

</details>


### [198] [Matrix divisors, Christoffel transformations and symmetric $d$-connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28497)
*Anton Selemenchuk*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该摘要提出用 Christoffel 变换（Christoffel transformation）$w(x)\mapsto x^2w(x)$ 将基本纤维丛修正（elementary bundle modifications）与间隙概率递推（gap probability recurrences）联系起来，并通过矩矩阵（moment matrix）确定归一化与分裂类型，进而在谱坐标下计算诱导变换及其递推系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于建立基本纤维丛修正与间隙概率递推之间的对应关系，并借助模问题（moduli problems）的局部数据刻画二者共享的稳定轨迹（stable loci）及其点加映射（point-addition maps）。

Method: 方法包括：用矩矩阵确定归一化与分裂类型（splitting type）；以局部数据定义源与目标模问题；针对度数（$°d$）为 1、2 的 Pearson 比（Pearson ratios）$d(-z-1)/d(z)$ 构造有理谱坐标（rational spectral coordinates）并计算诱导变换；对具有有限矩的正偶权重（positive even weights）通过重加权构型得到变换后的间隙概率递推。

Result: 所得变换后的间隙概率递推，其系数是变换后谱坐标的有理函数（rational function）；在所述假设下，该结论可通过局部公式推广至这些坐标卡（charts）之外。

Conclusion: Christoffel 变换可作为连接纤维丛修正与间隙概率递推的桥梁，其递推系数可由谱坐标有理表达，且局部公式在给定假设下具有超出特定坐标卡的适用性。

Abstract: The Christoffel transformation $w(x)\mapsto x^2w(x)$ relates elementary bundle modifications to gap probability recurrences. The moment matrix determines normalization and the splitting type. We define the source and target moduli problems by their local data. The modifications identify common stable loci and conjugate their point-addition maps. For Pearson ratios $d(-z-1)/d(z)$ with $°d=1,2$, we construct rational spectral coordinates and compute the induced transformation. For positive even weights with finite moments, reweighting configurations yields the transformed gap probability recurrence. Its coefficient is a rational function of the transformed spectral coordinates. A local formula applies outside these charts under the stated hypotheses.

</details>


<div id='physics.plasm-ph'></div>

# physics.plasm-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [199] [Dynamics of thermalization in classical three-dimensional many-body systems: Non-Maxwellian distributions and the role of anisotropic trapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27368)
*Roberto Onofrio,Bala Sundaram*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究两个热浴（baths）之间热化（thermalization）过程的三维特征，发现在趋向热化的途中会出现偏离麦克斯韦-玻尔兹曼分布（Maxwell-Boltzmann distributions）、更接近κ分布（κ-distributions）的能量分布，并考察各向异性囚禁（anisotropic trapping）对反常标度（anomalous scaling）出现的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相互作用热浴（interacting baths）的物理涉及超冷原子（ultracold atoms）到电离气体（ionized gases）等多种场景，一维模型虽能刻画诸多特征，但部分性质为二、三维系统所特有，故需针对三维情形展开研究。

Method: 沿用作者此前研究的两热浴热化模型，聚焦其三维特征；分析热化途中的能量分布形态，并进一步考察各向异性囚禁条件下热化行为，探讨其与粒子数（particle number）相关的反常标度之间的关系。

Result: 与一维情形不同，三维中热化途中的能量分布偏离麦克斯韦-玻尔兹曼分布、更接近κ分布，且这种偏离体现在分布峰值（peak）处的变化上；而一维中能量分布的单调行为使此类峰值变化无法出现。各向异性囚禁亦被证明在反常标度的出现中起作用。

Conclusion: 三维热化过程呈现一维模型无法捕捉的分布峰值特征与反常标度行为；相关结果可能有助于理解多种经典多体系统（many-body classical systems）中湍流行为（turbulent behavior）与非线性（nonlinearities）之间的相互作用。

Abstract: The physics of interacting baths is of interest in a variety of contexts, ranging from ultracold atoms to ionized gases. While many features can already be captured in a one-dimensional model, others are specific to the situation of two or three-dimensional systems. In this paper, we focus on three-dimensional features of a model for thermalization between two baths that we have explored in earlier publications. In contrast to the one-dimensional situation we show that, en route to thermalization, deviations from the Maxwell-Boltzmann energy distributions more akin to $κ$-distributions are observed. In three dimensions, these can be seen in changes at the peak of the distribution, which is precluded by the expected monotonic behavior of the energy distributions in one dimension. Furthermore, we consider thermalization for anisotropic trapping and explore its role in the onset of anomalous scaling with respect to the particle number. These results may be pertinent to the understanding of the interplay between turbulent behavior and nonlinearities for a variety of many-body classical systems.

</details>


### [200] [Generation of non-Maxwell-Boltzmann energy distributions during thermalization of interacting baths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27369)
*Roberto Onofrio,Bala Sundaram*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 该文通过高统计量数值实验，研究两个储库（reservoir）之间由高斯型距离依赖的种间相互作用（interspecies interaction）介导的热化（thermalization）过程，考察相互作用范围与强度、质量及囚禁频率等参数对偏离麦克斯韦-玻尔兹曼（Maxwell-Boltzmann）能量分布的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解相互作用介导的热化是否真正产生麦克斯韦-玻尔兹曼分布，并识别导致偏离的物理来源，这一问题与放热化学反应、超冷原子混合物（ultracold atomic mixtures）及自持燃烧等离子体（self-sustained burning plasmas）等体系相关。

Method: 采用高统计量的数值实验，相互作用取为粒子间距离的高斯型依赖（Gaussian dependence）的哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）；系统扫描相互作用范围与强度、两储库的质量与囚禁频率（trapping frequency）等强烈影响空间重叠的参数，并追踪初始印记的麦克斯韦-玻尔兹曼能量分布的演化偏差。

Result: 在强相互作用下，任意范围均出现偏离，归因于潜相互作用能（latent interaction energy）突然转化为粒子动能；长程（long range）相互作用在弱耦合区亦可产生偏离；非均匀囚禁（non-uniform confinement）引入额外偏离源，表现为瞬态的双温度麦克斯韦-玻尔兹曼分布，暗示从粒子间动力学到热化的逆级联（inverse cascading）路径。

Conclusion: 弱相互作用区给出了平衡温度（equilibrium temperature）的简单表达式并加以讨论；研究结果可为放热化学反应动力学、超冷原子混合物物理及自持燃烧等离子体提供参考。

Abstract: We report on numerical experiments of thermalization between two reservoirs in which interspecies interactions are mediated via a Hamiltonian with a Gaussian dependence upon the distance between the particles. By using relatively high statistics, we are able to study deviations from the initially imprinted Maxwell-Boltzmann energy distributions as a function of the range and strength of the interaction, as well as the parameters of the two baths such as masses, trapping frequencies, that strongly affect the spatial overlap of the two species. At large interaction strengths and for any range, deviations are attributable to the sudden conversion of latent interaction energy into kinetic energy of the particles. In addition, long range interactions generate deviations even for interaction strengths in the weak regime, and non-uniform confinement introduces a further source of deviation from the Maxwell-Boltzmann energy distribution. This latter results in transient two temperature Maxwell-Boltzmann distributions suggestive of an inverse cascading route from inter-particle dynamics to thermalization. In the weakly-interacting regime, a simple expression for the equilibrium temperature is also obtained and discussed. These findings may be of relevance in the dynamics of exothermic chemical reactions, in the physics of ultracold atomic mixtures, and in self-sustained burning plasmas.

</details>


<div id='math.DS'></div>

# math.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [201] [Stability and instability for particle dynamics in magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27893)
*Alberto Enciso,Daniel Peralta-Salas*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 该文研究牛顿-洛伦兹方程在零速平衡点处的稳定性，证明在磁场非零处（C^1情形）及实解析场的孤立零点处均为李雅普诺夫不稳定；但构造出一个在孤立零点处平坦的紧支撑无散度光滑场，使平衡点稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牛顿-洛伦兹方程描述带电粒子在磁场中的运动，其零速平衡点的稳定性此前缺乏系统刻画，且已有直觉可能默认无散度条件或光滑性足以保证稳定，本文旨在澄清稳定性与场的正则性、散度条件之间的关系。

Method: 在R^3中分析方程 ddot x = dot x × B(x)：对C^1正则且B非零的情形证明李雅普诺夫不稳定（Lyapunov instability）；对实解析（real-analytic）场证明每个孤立零点处不稳定；并通过显式构造一个紧支撑（compactly supported）、无散度（divergence-free）的C^∞场，其在孤立零点处平坦（flat），使对应平衡点稳定。证明不假设场无散度。

Result: 得到两方面的不稳定结论：磁场非零处（C^1）与实解析场的每个孤立零点处，零速平衡点均李雅普诺夫不稳定；同时存在反例：一个紧支撑无散度C^∞场，在孤立零点处平坦，其零速平衡点却李雅普诺夫稳定。

Conclusion: 零速平衡点的稳定性不能由一般的非零或孤立零点条件统一判定，正则性与零点处的平坦性起关键作用；无散度条件并不排除稳定性，说明稳定性行为依赖于场的局部几何与光滑结构。

Abstract: We study zero-speed equilibria of the Newton-Lorentz equation $\ddot x=\dot x\times B(x)$ in $\mathbf{R}^3$, which describes the motion of a charged particle in a magnetic field $B$. We prove Lyapunov instability wherever the field $B$ does not vanish (under $C^1$ regularity), and at every isolated zero of $B$ provided it is real-analytic. These results do not assume that the field is divergence-free. In contrast, we construct a compactly supported divergence-free $C^\infty$ field that is flat at an isolated zero and makes the corresponding equilibrium Lyapunov stable.

</details>


### [202] [Strictly convex ergodic billiards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28247)
*Wentao Fan,Boris Hasselblatt*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 该文构造了首个已知的严格凸遍历台球系统（strictly convex ergodic billiards），即足够不对称的"柠檬台球"（lemon billiards）；通过控制奇异性曲线（singularity curves）得到遍历性乃至伯努利性质（Bernoulli property）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前尚无严格凸的遍历台球（ergodic billiards）实例，该文旨在填补这一空白，并推进对柠檬台球这类两圆相交型台球桌的动力学理解。

Method: 研究对象为两圆相交、且其中一圆包含两圆心的"柠檬台球桌"（lemon billiards tables）。作者此前已证明其到适当截面（section）的回归映射具有一致双曲性（uniformly hyperbolic returns），本文则以一种看似新颖的方法控制奇异性曲线。

Result: 对足够不对称的柠檬台球，奇异性曲线的控制与一致双曲回归相结合，推出该系统具有遍历性（ergodicity），并进一步具有伯努利性质（Bernoulli property）。

Conclusion: 由此得到首个已知的严格凸遍历台球族，表明在严格凸且不对称的几何条件下，台球流的强随机性（伯努利性质）可以实现。

Abstract: We produce the first known strictly convex ergodic billiards: sufficiently asymmetric lemon billiards. These billiard tables are the intersection of two circles of which one contains the centers of both. We previously proved that they have \emph{uniformly} hyperbolic returns to a suitable section, and we here control singularity curves, with an approach that seems new. This implies ergodicity and indeed the Bernoulli property.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [The $m=2$ amplituhedron is a positive geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27903)
*Joris Koefler,Dmitrii Pavlov,Rainer Sinn*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文证明 $m=2$ 的幅面体（amplituhedron）是正几何（positive geometry），从而解决了正几何领域的核心猜想，其主要工具是一个源于 Hodge 理论的判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幅面体是粒子物理中具有根本重要性的半代数集（semi-algebraic sets），其发现开创了正几何（Positive Geometry）这一研究方向；判定其是否为正几何是该领域的中心猜想。

Method: 引入一个 Hodge 理论的判据（Hodge-theoretic criterion）来判断对象是否为正几何，并将 Brown–Dupont 的工作与 Arkani-Hamed–Bai–Lam 的原始框架联系起来。

Result: 证明 $m=2$ 的幅面体满足正几何的定义，即该中心猜想在 $m=2$ 情形下成立。

Conclusion: $m=2$ 幅面体被确认为正几何，所提出的 Hodge 理论判据为研究正几何性质提供了新工具。

Abstract: Amplituhedra are semi-algebraic sets of fundamental importance in particle physics. Their discovery initiated the field of Positive Geometry. We settle a central conjecture in this field: we show that the $m=2$ amplituhedron is a positive geometry. Our main new tool is a Hodge-theoretic criterion for being a positive geometry, connecting the work of Brown--Dupont with the original setup of Arkani-Hamed--Bai--Lam.

</details>


### [204] [Mass Deformations and Sheets in Logahoric Higgs Moduli](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27934)
*Georgios Kydonakis,Lutian Zhao*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文利用对数化 Hitchin 可积系统（logahoric Hitchin integrable system）与李代数叶层（sheets of the Lie algebra），描述六维 $\mathcal{N}=(2,0)$ 超共形理论在质量形变下的驯服缺陷（tame defects）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于借助既有对数化 Hitchin 框架刻画六维超共形理论的缺陷结构，并检验 Balasubramanian--Distler 提出的质量型形变判据。

Method: 方法基于作者先前引入的对数化 Hitchin 可积系统及李代数叶层，将 Hitchin 系统中固定维数叶（leaves of fixed dimension）的变化与固定维数留数轨道（residue orbits）的变化相对应。

Result: 结果表明：Hitchin 系统中固定维数叶的变化等价于固定维数留数轨道的变化；同时验证了质量型形变（mass-like deformations）中的周期条件与维数条件。

Conclusion: 由此在对数化 Higgs 扭量（logahoric Higgs torsors）的泊松模空间（Poisson moduli space）上实现李代数叶层，为理解六维理论中驯服缺陷的质量形变提供几何刻画。

Abstract: We use the framework of the logahoric Hitchin integrable system introduced in our earlier work and the sheets of the Lie algebra in order to describe the tame defects in a six dimensional $\mathcal{N}=(2,0)$ superconformal theory under mass deformations. We show that varying leaves of fixed dimension in the Hitchin system amounts to varying residue orbits of fixed dimension. Moreover, we verify the period and dimension conditions in mass-like deformations, as proposed by Balasubramanian--Distler, thus realizing the sheets of the Lie algebra on the Poisson moduli space of logahoric Higgs torsors.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [LatticeMind: A Validation-Gated Agentic Framework for Density Functional Theory Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26869)
*Nathaniel M. Vegh,Yu Zhu,Raphaël J. Prentki,Félix Beaudoin,Shuaishuai Yuan,Hong Guo*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出 LatticeMind，一个带验证门控的智能体框架（validation-gated agentic framework），将自然语言请求转化为可执行、可校验的密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）项目；借助工作流契约、确定性验证器与有界修复，在多工作流基准上稳定复现参考值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）实质是多阶段工作流，结构与保存的密度需在依赖阶段间正确传递；语言模型可写出合法单步输入，但阶段串联后易出现依赖丢失、文件交接错误或关键词幻觉，且错误往往在执行后才暴露，代价高昂。

Method: 工作流契约（workflow contract）规定必需阶段、文件与依赖；确定性验证器（deterministic validators）在任何求解器启动前检查项目；校验失败触发有界修复（bounded repair）；报告保持对项目文件的可追溯性。

Result: 在覆盖总能量、态密度、能带、磁性、声子工作流的 44 例 RESCU 基准上，三个非旗舰 LLM 后端均复现全部参考值；132 次运行中 119 次首次验证方案即通过，其余经一轮修复通过。消融表明工作流契约与执行前验证是主要可靠性机制；常规试错修复基线成功率 71% 且每次成功消耗约两倍 token。同一架构在 19 例 VASP 基准上亦复现全部参考值，并可迁移至 Rh 修饰 Rh₆/In_xGa_{1-x}N 光催化剂界面的 23 个输入多阶段研究。

Conclusion: 工作流契约、确定性验证、有界修复与可追溯记录构成可靠 AI 辅助科学计算（AI-assisted scientific computing）的实用基础：可预防错误在执行前被捕获，求解器不再是第一验证者。

Abstract: First-principles calculations are rarely single solver calls: they are multistage workflows in which structures, settings, and saved densities must pass correctly between dependent stages. Language models can write a valid single input, but reliability collapses once stages are chained: a dropped dependency, wrong file handoff, or hallucinated keyword can invalidate results even when the solver runs, and such errors surface only after costly execution. We introduce LatticeMind, a validation-gated agentic framework that converts natural-language requests into executable, checkable density functional theory (DFT) projects. A workflow contract specifies required stages, files, and dependencies; deterministic validators inspect the project before any solver launch; failed checks trigger bounded repair; and reports remain traceable to project files. On a 44-case RESCU benchmark spanning total-energy, density-of-states, band-structure, magnetic, phonon workflows, three non-flagship LLM backends reproduced every reference value; 119 of 132 runs passed on the first validated plan and the rest after one repair cycle. Ablations identify the workflow contract and pre-execution validation as the principal reliability mechanisms; a conventional trial-and-repair baseline reaches 71% at twice the token cost per success. The same architecture reproduces every reference value on a 19-case VASP benchmark, demonstrating transfer across solvers. Beyond these task families, LatticeMind generated and validated the 23 inputs of a multistage study of Rh-induced electronic responses at a Rh$_6$/In$_x$Ga$_{1-x}$N photocatalyst interface. These results establish workflow contracts, deterministic validation, bounded repair, and traceable records as a practical foundation for reliable AI-assisted scientific computing, where preventable errors are caught before execution and the solver is never the first validator.

</details>


### [206] [Tailoring Dielectric Relaxation and Charge Transport in Amorphous Ge-Te-Se Phase-Change Materials through Yttrium Incorporation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26996)
*Surbhi Agarwal,Jiří Vacík,D. K. Dwivedi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用熔体急冷法制备Te(1-x)(GeSe0.5)Yx (x=0.05, 0.10, 0.15)硫系玻璃合金，在1 Hz–1 MHz、302–422 K（低于玻璃转变温度）范围内研究介电常数、介电损耗与交流电导率的温度/频率依赖性，结果支持相关势垒跳跃（CBH）机制，并显示Y掺杂提升交流电导率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估所制备硫系玻璃合金在相变存储器（phase-change memory）及其他电子器件中的应用潜力，并阐明其介电弛豫与导电机制。

Method: 以熔体急冷法（melt-quenching method）制备合金，采用介电谱（dielectric spectroscopy）表征；在1 Hz–1 MHz、302–422 K范围内测量介电常数、交流电导率与介电损耗，并用Guintini介电色散模型、相关势垒跳跃（correlated barrier hopping, CBH）模型、取向极化（orientation polarization）及Cole-Cole分析解释结果。

Result: 介电损耗与介电常数随频率升高和温度降低而减小；Cole-Cole分析证实弛豫时间存在分布，所得激活能与CBH导电机理一致；交流电导率遵循Aω^s幂律（频率指数s<1且随温度升高而减小），进一步支持CBH机制。

Conclusion: Y的引入增加了定域带尾态（localized tail states）密度，从而提升交流电导率；该材料被视为未来相变存储器及其他电子器件的有前景候选材料。

Abstract: The Te(1-x)(GeSe0.5)Yx (x = 0.05, 0.10, 0.15) alloys were prepared by a melt-quenching method and characterized using dielectric spectroscopy. The dielectric parameters and electrical conductivity were evaluated to assess their potential for phase-change memory and other electronic applications. The temperature and frequency dependence of the dielectric constants, ac conductivity, and dielectric loss of prepared chalcogenide glassy alloys were investigated over the frequency range 1 Hz - 1 MHz, and the temperature range 302 - 422 K, below the glass transition temperature. Both dielectric loss and dielectric constant were found to decrease with increasing frequency and decreasing temperature. The observed dielectric loss change was interpreted using the Guintini model of dielectric dispersion, correlated barrier hopping (CBH) model, and orientation polarization. The Cole-Cole analysis confirmed a distribution of relaxation times and yielded activation energies consistent with the CBH conduction mechanism. The frequency dependence of the ac conductivity follows the power law (Aωs), where the frequency exponent (s < 1) decreases with increasing temperature, further supporting the CBH conduction mechanism. The incorporation of Y increases the density of localized tail states, resulting in enhanced ac conductivity. These findings suggest that the investigated materials are promising candidates for future phase-change memory and other electronic device applications.

</details>


### [207] [Heat-current-induced nonreciprocal phonon damping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27014)
*Tingwei Deng,Xianyong Ding,Rui Wang,Xiangang Wan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在中心对称非磁性晶体中，稳态热流可通过改变散射伙伴的非平衡占据数，产生对波矢反演为奇函数的非互易非谐阻尼（nonreciprocal anharmonic damping），从而在原本受时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry）约束的成对声子模式间造成线宽展宽/窄化对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间反演对称性通常要求相反波矢处互为时间反演对的声子模式具有相等的平衡线宽（equilibrium linewidth）。本文旨在探究非平衡条件下（如存在稳态热流时），这一等宽关系是否会被打破，以及其微观机制为何。

Method: 在单模弛豫时间近似（single-mode relaxation-time approximation）框架下，推导出与温度梯度（temperature gradient）成正比、在波矢反演下为奇函数的线宽修正；该修正源于散射伙伴的非平衡占据数改变了时间反演三声子通道（three-phonon channels）的统计权重，而通道的矩阵元平方仍保持相等。并对中心对称的 MgO 与金刚石（diamond）进行第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）。

Result: 第一性原理计算在 MgO 与金刚石中若干有限波矢处得到非零且依赖模式的线宽修正，在离子晶体与共价晶体中均验证了该机制；修正依赖于散射伙伴速度与平衡寿命，并保留了平衡线宽中所缺失的带符号通道权重。在固定局域温度下反转热流，成对模式的展宽与窄化随之互换。

Conclusion: 热流诱导的线宽对比具有电流可逆性（current-reversible），可作为占据反馈（occupation feedback）的光谱特征，并对非线性声子输运（nonlinear phonon transport）具有潜在意义。

Abstract: Time-reversal symmetry enforces equal equilibrium linewidths for time-reversed phonon modes at opposite wave vectors. Here we show that a steady heat current can produce nonreciprocal anharmonic damping even in centrosymmetric nonmagnetic crystals. The effect arises from nonequilibrium occupations of scattering partners, which change the statistical weights of time-reversed three-phonon channels while their squared matrix elements remain equal. Within the single-mode relaxation-time approximation, we derive a linewidth correction that is linear in the temperature gradient and odd under wave-vector reversal. The response depends on scattering-partner velocities and equilibrium lifetimes and retains signed channel weights absent from the equilibrium linewidth. First-principles calculations for centrosymmetric MgO and diamond yield nonzero, mode-dependent linewidth corrections at selected finite wave vectors, demonstrating the mechanism in both ionic and covalent crystals. Reversing the heat current at fixed local temperature exchanges the broadening and narrowing of paired modes. This current-reversible linewidth contrast provides a spectral signature of occupation feedback, with potential implications for nonlinear phonon transport.

</details>


### [208] [MOFFieldBench: A Machine Learning Benchmark for Continuous Electronic Fields in Metal-Organic Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27036)
*Zhaolin Hu,Hehe Fan,Chenhao Rao,Wangyihan Guo,Meng Xu,Qiwei Yang,Yi Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 论文提出 MOFFieldBench，一个包含约 46,000 个金属有机框架（MOFs）、并将晶体结构、电子密度（ED）场与静电势（ESP）场一一对齐的多模态基准，用于系统评估电子场作为可扩展学习表征的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MOF 机器学习模型多依赖原子结构，电子密度（electron density, ED）与静电势（electrostatic potential, ESP）场虽编码孔隙几何与客体—骨架相互作用的互补信息，但受限于昂贵的第一性原理（first-principles）计算、存储开销及对齐数据集稀缺而未被充分利用。

Method: 构建统一流程，整合场生成、误差可控压缩与稀疏点云（point-cloud）表示，并设计四项任务：场重构、13 种材料性质预测、多模态性质预测与成对跨模态检索（cross-modal retrieval）。

Result: ED 与 ESP 在孔隙几何、吸附与分离性质上具备竞争力；多模态融合（multimodal fusion）在若干任务上优于最佳单模态基线，表明原子结构与电子场具有互补预测作用；学习型 ED 重构接近第一性原理参考密度，且以预测密度初始化可将自洽场（self-consistent-field, SCF）计算时间平均减少 13.59%。

Conclusion: 电子场可作为实用且信息量丰富的学习模态，MOFFieldBench 为表征选择、多模态学习与电子场驱动的 MOF 建模研究提供了基准资源。

Abstract: Machine-learning models for metal-organic frameworks (MOFs) predominantly rely on atomic structures, whereas electron-density (ED) and electrostatic-potential (ESP) fields remain underexplored as scalable learning representations. These continuous fields encode complementary spatial and electronic information relevant to structure-property relationships, particularly pore geometry and guest-framework interactions, but costly first-principles calculations, storage requirements and the scarcity of systematically aligned datasets limit their broader use. Here we introduce MOFFieldBench, a multimodal benchmark comprising approximately 46,000 MOFs with one-to-one aligned crystallographic structures, ED fields and ESP fields. A unified workflow integrates field generation, error-controlled compression and sparse point-cloud representation, supporting field reconstruction, prediction of 13 material properties, multimodal property prediction and pairwise cross-modal retrieval. ED and ESP provide competitive representations for pore geometry, adsorption and separation properties. Multimodal fusion improves several prediction tasks relative to the best unimodal reference, supporting complementary predictive roles for atomic structures and electronic fields. Learned ED reconstruction closely approximates first-principles reference densities, while predicted-density initialization reduces self-consistent-field calculation time by 13.59\% on average in paired tests. These results support electronic fields as practical, informative learning modalities and establish MOFFieldBench as a resource for studying representation choice, multimodal learning and electronic-field-driven MOF modelling.

</details>


### [209] [An efficient approach to electron-phonon coupling calculations of heterostructures within an all-electron framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27124)
*Ignacio Gonzalez Oliva,Sebastian Tillack,Fabio Caruso,Pasquale Pavone,Claudia Draxl*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种从孤立层性质构建弱束缚异质结电子-声子矩阵（electron-phonon matrix）的高效方法，并在WSe2双层与吡啶/MoS2体系中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 界面处第一性原理电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling）计算需使用大超胞（supercell），计算成本过高，构成主要障碍。

Method: 以孤立层性质为基础，分两步构建动力学矩阵（dynamical matrices）与势响应（potential response）：扩张步骤通过精确傅里叶映射（Fourier mapping）由单胞折叠至超胞；叠加步骤按块由孤立层对应量组装异质结的原子间力常数（interatomic force constants）与势响应，忽略弱层间贡献。方法实现于全电子全势代码exciting的电子-声子模块。

Result: 针对双层WSe2与吡啶/MoS2的声子色散（phonon dispersion）与谱函数（spectral functions）进行了演示，并与精确参考计算对比。

Conclusion: 该方法可高效计算电子-声子自能（electron-phonon self-energy），为弱束缚异质结的电子-声子耦合研究提供可行途径。

Abstract: A major challenge in calculating electron-phonon coupling at interfaces from first principles is the prohibitive computational cost due to the large supercells involved. Here, we present an efficient approach, which builds the electron-phonon matrix of weakly bound heterostructures from the properties of the isolated layers. The two key ingredients, the dynamical matrices and the potential response, are constructed in two steps. In the expansion step, these quantities are folded from the unit cells onto the supercell through an exact Fourier mapping. In the addition step, the interatomic force constants and the potential responses of the heterostructure are assembled blockwise from the corresponding quantities of the isolated layers, neglecting the weak interlayer contribution. The method is implemented in the electron-phonon module of the all-electron full-potential code exciting, enabling efficient calculations of the electron-phonon self-energy. We demonstrate the approach for the phonon dispersion and spectral functions of bilayer WSe$_2$ and pyridine/MoS$_2$, in comparison with exact reference calculations.

</details>


### [210] [Nanoscale chemical engineering of Ti3C2Tx MXene multilayers through in situ electron beam irradiation in a TEM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27240)
*Ayoub Benmoumen,Marie-Laure David,Noé Condé,Eric Gautron,Sophie Morisset,Stéphane Célérier,Anupma Thakur,Babak Anasori,Vincent Mauchamp,Philippe Moreau*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用聚焦扫描透射电子显微镜（STEM）电子束对Ti3C2Tx多层膜进行原位辐照，通过调节束能、电子剂量与扫描参数实现原子尺度的结构与化学调控，可选择性地去除Cl或F表面端基、优先去除碳，并驱动稀薄氮杂质迁移聚集形成纳米级富氮碳氮化物畴，从而构建平面内MXene异质结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低维材料的缺陷工程（defect engineering）可在原子尺度调控结构与化学性质，但将该策略应用于MXene这类二维（2D）材料家族、实现超越常规化学合成的局域纳米尺度结构构筑仍具挑战。

Method: 采用高度聚焦的扫描透射电子显微镜（STEM）电子束，在80与300 keV下辐照Ti3C2Tx多层膜，并结合环形暗场成像（annular dark-field imaging）与电子能量损失谱（EELS）进行原位表征，同时与理论预测进行对照。

Result: 辐照引发渐进的的结构与化学修饰，主要源于原子物种与MXene层的选择性溅射（selective sputtering）；特定条件可选择性去除Cl或F表面端基而基本保留Ti3C2骨架，另一些条件则优先去除碳，与理论预测一致；电子辐照还驱动稀薄氮杂质迁移聚集，形成嵌入的富氮碳氮化物（Ti3CyNzTx）纳米畴。

Conclusion: 电子辐照可局域调控MXene成分并构建纳米尺度结构，所形成的横向图案化碳化物/碳氮化物构筑属于具有纳米级空间控制的平面内MXene异质结构（in-plane MXene heterostructures），其结构超出常规化学合成所能获得的范围。

Abstract: Defect engineering in low-dimensional materials is a powerful approach to tailor their structure and chemistry at the atomic scale, with high relevance for tuning physical properties. Here, we apply this strategy to MXenes, one of the largest families of two-dimensional (2D) materials, and demonstrate the use of a highly focused scanning transmission electron microscope (STEM) beam for the structural and chemical nanoengineering of titanium carbide (Ti3C2Tx) multilayers. By adjusting the beam energy, electron dose, and scanning parameters, Ti3C2Tx multilayers were irradiated at 80 and 300 keV and characterized in situ using annular dark-field imaging and electron energy-loss spectroscopy (EELS). The irradiation induces gradual structural and chemical modifications, largely driven by the selective sputtering of atomic species and MXene layers. We identify irradiation conditions that selectively remove Cl or F surface terminations while largely preserving the Ti3C2 framework, whereas other conditions promote preferential carbon removal, in agreement with theoretical predictions. Most notably, electron irradiation drives the migration and accumulation of initially dilute nitrogen impurities within the irradiated regions, leading to the formation of nitrogen-rich titanium carbonitride (Ti3CyNzTx) nanodomains embedded in the Ti3C2Tx multilayers. These laterally patterned carbide/carbonitride architectures constitute in-plane MXene heterostructures with nanometer-scale spatial control. These results demonstrate that electron irradiation can be used to locally tailor MXene composition and create nanoscale architectures beyond those accessible through conventional chemical synthesis.

</details>


### [211] [Reaction-induced Localized Annealing for On-chip Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27355)
*Nensi Toncich,Nerea Abando,Henning Galinski,Frans Spaepen,Ralph Spolenak*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种微加工热输运平台（microfabricated heat-delivery platform），利用埋入式反应多层膜（buried reactive multilayer）与纳米级扩散阻挡层（nanometric diffusion barrier），实现反应诱导的片上局部退火（on-chip annealing）最高1400°C及约10⁶°C s⁻¹的超快升降温速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 片上热加工（on-chip thermal processing）被视为自然科学各学科材料发现的关键技术，但实现高温、超快、局域化的热处理仍具挑战，需要一种材料无关（material-independent）的通用热输运方案。

Method: 设计微加工热输运平台，结合埋入式反应多层膜提供热源，并以纳米级扩散阻挡层调控热输运，从而获得反应诱导的局部退火与超快升降温（约10⁶°C s⁻¹）。

Result: 在快速凝固（rapid-solidification）过程中生成超高长径比（ultra-high aspect ratio）微结构；在室温下稳定无掺杂四方氧化锆（dopant-free tetragonal zirconia）；并观测到熔体中毛细驱动孔洞塌缩（capillary-driven hole collapse）的瞬态状态（transient states）。

Conclusion: 该工作拓展了局域高温加工路径，并提供一种材料无关的方法，用于在平衡态与非平衡态（on and off-equilibrium）条件下快速筛选多维纳米材料（multidimensional nanomaterials）。

Abstract: On-chip thermal processing is a key technology for materials discovery across all disciplines of the natural sciences. Here, we present a microfabricated heat-delivery platform that enables reaction-induced localized on-chip annealing up to 1400°C. A buried reactive multilayer combined with a nanometric diffusion barrier provide ultra-fast heating and quenching rates on the order of 10$^6$°C s$^{-1}$ as illustrated by the generation of ultra-high aspect ratio microstructures during rapid-solidification and the stabilization of dopant-free tetragonal zirconia at room temperature. Rapid kinetics also enable the observation of transient states of capillary-driven hole collapse in melts. Our work expands localized high-temperature processing routes and offers a material-independent approach for rapid screening of multidimensional nanomaterials on and off-equilibrium.

</details>


### [212] [Nanocrystalline cubic boron arsenide growth on GaAs(100) by gas source molecular beam epitaxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27460)
*Matthew A. Delmont,Yu Wu,Han-Hsuan Wu,Xiaoqing Pan,Christopher J. Palmstrom,Bolin Liao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究利用气源分子束外延（gas source molecular beam epitaxy）在晶格失配的GaAs(100)衬底上成功合成纳米晶立方砷化硼（c-BAs）薄膜，并确定了维持化学计量比的生长温度窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立方砷化硼（c-BAs）是极具潜力的超高导热半导体（ultrahigh thermal conductivity semiconductor），但在晶格失配衬底上的直接合成仍存在挑战，需探索可行的异质外延路径。

Method: 采用气源分子束外延（gas source molecular beam epitaxy）在GaAs(100)衬底上生长c-BAs薄膜；通过X射线光电子能谱（X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy）确定生长温度窗口，并结合原子力显微镜（atomic force microscopy）、反射高能电子衍射（reflection high-energy electron diffraction）、掠入射X射线衍射（grazing-incidence X-ray diffraction）和高分辨透射电子显微镜（high-resolution transmission electron microscopy）表征表面、结构与界面。

Result: 确定250–400 °C为最佳生长温度窗口，可维持接近化学计量比并抑制严重砷损失；薄膜呈Volmer-Weber三维岛状生长模式（3D island growth mode），与GaAs衬底存在择优晶体学取向对齐；界面化学上陡峭且无镓向c-BAs层互扩散。

Conclusion: 该工作为将c-BAs集成到先进热学与光电子平台（thermal and optoelectronic platforms）迈出了初步一步。

Abstract: Cubic boron arsenide (c-BAs) is a promising ultrahigh thermal conductivity semiconductor, but its direct synthesis on lattice-mismatched substrates remains challenging. Here, we demonstrate the synthesis of nanocrystalline c-BAs thin films on GaAs(100) substrates using gas source molecular beam epitaxy. X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy reveals an optimal growth temperature window of 250-400 C to maintain near stoichiometry and suppress severe arsenic loss. Surface and structural characterizations via atomic force microscopy, reflection high-energy electron diffraction, and grazing-incidence X-ray diffraction demonstrate a Volmer-Weber 3D island growth mode and a preferential crystallographic alignment with the GaAs substrate. High-resolution transmission electron microscopy confirms a chemically sharp interface without gallium interdiffusion into the c-BAs layer. These findings establish an initial step for the integration of c-BAs into advanced thermal and optoelectronic platforms.

</details>


### [213] [Charge-conserving creation and annihilation of topological phase singularities in atomically thin semiconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27486)
*Yongjing Wang,Yuhang Wang,Guoshuai Fan,Qingqing Luo,Yanning Li,Lidong Sun,Chunguang Hu,Wanfu Shen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作以蓝宝石衬底上的原子级薄MoS₂薄膜为模型体系，从理论与实验上证明介电色散（dielectric dispersion）的演化决定了光学相位奇点（phase singularities, PSs）在拓扑荷守恒下的产生、轨迹、合并与湮灭。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子级薄过渡金属硫族化物（transition metal dichalcogenides）的介电函数可快速变化，易产生拓扑相位奇点，是光学相位调控的潜在材料平台；但激子（exciton）驱动的相位奇点体系中，拓扑荷守恒（topological charge conservation）下的奇点演化尚未被研究。

Method: 采用蓝宝石衬底上的原子级薄MoS₂薄膜作为模型体系，通过改变MoS₂薄膜厚度实现介电色散的演化，使高能n-k色散环（n-k dispersion loop）逐渐收缩，从而改变其与零反射条件（zero reflection condition）的交汇情况。

Result: 理论与实验均表明，介电色散的演化在拓扑荷守恒约束下决定光学相位奇点的产生、轨迹、合并与湮灭；薄膜厚度调控所引发的n-k色散环收缩是实现该演化的实验途径。

Conclusion: 研究为探索与调控拓扑光学态（topological optical states）提供了通用策略。

Abstract: Atomically thin transition metal dichalcogenides provide a promising material platform for optical phase manipulation, as rapid variations in their dielectric functions can give rise to topological phase singularities (PSs). However, PS evolution while preserving topological charge conservation has not yet been investigated in exciton enabled PS systems. Here, using atomically thin MoS2 films on sapphire as a model system, we theoretically and experimentally demonstrate that the evolution of dielectric dispersion determines the creation, trajectory, merging, and annihilation of optical PSs under topological charge conservation. By varying the MoS2 film thickness, we experimentally realize this evolution through the gradual shrinking of the high energy n-k dispersion loop, which modifies its intersection with the zero reflection condition. Our results provide a general strategy for exploring and manipulating topological optical states.

</details>


### [214] [Accelerating real-time hybrid-functional calculations with learned ACE and symmetry-based FFT reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27798)
*Tomohito Otobe*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出两种加速混合泛函（hybrid functional）实时计算的方法：学习自适应压缩交换（ACE）因子的时间演化以预测更新，并利用晶体对称性在等价实空间点间复用交换FFT，组合后相对基准加速超63倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时混合泛函（real-time hybrid-functional）计算需反复重构非局域交换（nonlocal exchange），其成本随布里渊区（Brillouin-zone）采样迅速增长，限制了含激子效应（excitonic effects）的非线性与非平衡电子动力学研究。

Method: 方法一学习自适应压缩交换（adaptively compressed exchange, ACE）因子的时间演化并预测其更新，降低显式重构频率；方法二基于晶体对称性（crystal symmetry）在等价实空间点间复用交换快速傅里叶变换（FFT），即对称性FFT复用（SBFR），在保留完整k点求和的同时减少FFT次数。本文以HSE06泛函实现。

Result: 相对使用全行网格FFT的ACE基准，学习预测加速五倍以上，SBFR加速十四倍以上，二者结合加速六十三倍以上；组合方法展现良好并行扩展性（parallel scaling），学习后每步传播（propagation）耗时低于0.5秒。

Conclusion: 计算成本的降低使混合泛函可支持非线性与非平衡电子动力学研究，并可考察半导体与绝缘体中的激子效应作用；方法适用于多种混合泛函。

Abstract: Real-time hybrid-functional calculations require repeated reconstruction of nonlocal exchange, whose cost grows rapidly with Brillouin-zone sampling. We develop two methods to improve the efficiency of time evolution and spatial processing. The first learns the time evolution of adaptively compressed exchange (ACE) factors and predicts their updates, reducing the frequency of explicit reconstruction. The second uses crystal symmetry to reuse exchange fast Fourier transforms (FFTs) among equivalent real-space points. This symmetry-based FFT reuse (SBFR) reduces the number of FFTs while retaining the full $\bm k$-point sum. The methods can be used with a variety of hybrid functionals, and we use HSE06 in this work. Relative to an ACE reference using full-row mesh FFTs, learned prediction provides more than fivefold acceleration, SBFR more than fourteenfold, and their combination more than sixty-threefold. The combined method shows efficient parallel scaling. After learning, propagation takes less than 0.5 s per step. The reduced computational cost allows us to study nonlinear and nonequilibrium electron dynamics with hybrid functionals and to examine the role of excitonic effects in semiconductors and insulators.

</details>


### [215] [Atomic identification via the Prandtl-Tomlinson stick-slip mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27808)
*Sophia E. Schweiss,Lukas Hoermann,Franz J. Giessibl,Alfred J. Weymouth*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用CO终止针尖在部分氧化的Cu(110)表面进行横向力显微镜（lateral force microscopy, LFM）测量，实现了金属位与氧位的原子级辨识，并结合密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）揭示其短程轨道相互作用主导机制及单原子尺度的Prandtl-Tomlinson黏滑行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扫描探针显微镜（scanning probe microscopy）领域中原子种类辨识仍是持续存在的挑战，传统原子力显微镜（atomic force microscopy, AFM）多依赖静电相互作用（electrostatic interactions）进行原子识别，而对典型金属氧化物表面的原子辨识方法仍有待拓展。

Method: 采用CO终止针尖对部分氧化的Cu(110)表面开展横向力显微镜测量，并辅以基于密度泛函理论的计算，分析针尖-样品相互作用（tip-sample interaction）的组成与来源。

Result: 在较大z范围内金属位与氧位呈现清晰差异，实现典型金属氧化物上的原子辨识；DFT计算表明相互作用由短程轨道相互作用（short-ranged orbital interactions）主导，静电贡献可忽略；小针尖-样品距离下LFM可探测单原子尺度的Prandtl-Tomlinson黏滑（stick-slip）机制，表现为正反扫描方向的显著不对称。

Conclusion: 横向力显微镜可作为金属氧化物表面原子识别及纳米摩擦学（nanotribology）基础模型在单原子尺度实验验证的有效手段，并表明短程轨道相互作用在该体系中起决定性作用。

Abstract: Distinguishing atomic species is an ongoing challenge in the field of scanning probe microscopy. Typically, atomic force microscopy makes use of electrostatic interactions to perform atomic identification. Here we perform lateral force microscopy (LFM) measurements with a CO terminated tip of a partially oxidized Cu(110) surface and show clear differences between the metal and oxygen sites over a large z-range. This allows for atomic identification on a typical metal oxide. Density functional theory based calculations show that the tip-sample interaction is dominated by short-ranged orbital interactions and that the electrostatic contribution is negligible. At small tip-sample distances, LFM can probe the Prandtl-Tomlinson stick-slip mechanism on the single-atom scale. It is visible as a striking asymmetry in the forward and backward scan directions, experimentally revealing this fundamental model for nanotribology on the single-atom scale.

</details>


### [216] [Early-stage local melting during solid-state sintering of a Cu-Ni-Ti-Zr-Hf alloy: A combined thermodynamic and kinetic analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27825)
*Andrea Skolakova,Hana Lesakova,Frantisek Lukac,Marketa Jarosova,Jan Pinc*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究制备了非等原子比 Cu15Ni35Ti25Zr12.5Hf12.5 复杂浓缩合金（complex concentrated alloys, CCAs）粉末并开展放电等离子烧结（Spark Plasma Sintering, SPS），揭示 1100 °C 下的局部熔化并非宏观温度梯度所致，而是源于雾化过程继承的微观偏析与 Cu 富集区局部固相线（solidus）的显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cu-Ni-Ti-Zr-Hf 基形状记忆（shape memory）复杂浓缩合金功能性能优异，但粉末冶金（powder metallurgy）制备困难；且全局平衡相图（equilibrium phase diagrams）预测的固相稳定温度与实际烧结中的局部熔化现象存在矛盾，需要解释其机理。

Method: 采用感应熔炼（induction melting）结合雾化（atomization）制粉，并在 850–1100 °C 进行 SPS 致密化；结合回溯性 Scheil-Gulliver 凝固模拟（solidification simulations）与二维局部平衡热图（2D local equilibrium heat maps）分析局部熔化来源。

Result: 在 1100 °C 烧结时出现预期外的局部熔化，但并非由宏观热梯度驱动，而是源于雾化继承的微观偏析（microsegregation）。固态加热过程中，颗粒界面高缺陷密度促进的短程扩散（short-range diffusion）使 Ti 向颗粒间网络偏聚，颗粒内部形成 Cu 富集区（可达约 41 at. %）；因 Cu 与 Hf（ΔH_mix^l-l 为 -19.2 kJ/mol）及 Ti 的强负混合焓（mixing enthalpy），这些区域的局部固相线大幅降低，加热至 1100 °C 时越过局部固相线形成液相，其凝固导致 Cu-Hf 共偏析（co-segregation）。

Conclusion: 该框架可解释含低熔点组元且具强负混合焓的快速凝固（rapidly solidified）复杂浓缩合金粉末中的局部熔化现象，为粉末冶金工艺窗口设计提供机理依据。

Abstract: Complex concentrated alloys (CCAs) combining elements from 3d transition and refractory groups, specifically Cu-Ni-Ti-Zr-Hf-based shape memory systems, exhibit excellent functional properties but present significant manufacturing challenges via powder metallurgy. In this study, a non-equimolar Cu15Ni35Ti25Zr12.5Hf12.5 (at. %) alloy was successfully produced by induction melting, followed by atomization and subsequent consolidation via Spark Plasma Sintering (SPS) at temperatures ranging from 850 to 1100 C. Although global equilibrium phase diagrams predict solid-state stability up to 1260 C, localized melting unexpectedly occurred during SPS at 1100 C. By combining retrospective Scheil-Gulliver solidification simulations with 2D local equilibrium heat maps, we demonstrate that this early-stage melting is not driven by macroscopic thermal gradients, but by microsegregation inherited from atomization. During solid-state heating, thermally activated short-range diffusion, accelerated by high defect densities at particle interfaces, partitions Ti towards the interparticle network, leaving Cu-enriched regions (up to ~41 at. %) within the particle interiors.
