Owing to the strongly negative mixing enthalpy of Cu with Hf (-19.2 kJ/mol for $ΔH_{mix}^{l-l}$) and, to a lesser extent, with Ti, the local solidus of these Cu-rich regions is dramatically depressed. Upon heating to 1100 C, these regions cross their local solidus and form a liquid phase, whose solidification produces the Cu-Hf co-segregation. This framework accounts for localized melting phenomena in rapidly solidified CCA powders containing low-melting constituents with strong negative mixing enthalpies.

</details>


### [217] [Enhancement of Li and Na Storage in Janus HfSSe Monolayers by Co-Induced Electron-Deficient Chalcogen Sites: A First-Principles Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28152)
*Md. Muktadir Hasan,Md. Mafizul Islam,Ahmed Zubair*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用第一性原理密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）研究本征与Co掺杂Janus HfSSe单层作为锂/钠离子电池负极材料，Co掺杂显著增强碱金属结合并提升理论容量与倍率性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高容量、快充、耐久可充电池的需求推动先进电极材料探索，二维材料（two-dimensional materials）因大比表面积与短离子扩散路径而具潜力，需评估Janus HfSSe及Co掺杂对其储锂/储钠性能的调控作用。

Method: 基于第一性原理密度泛函理论（first-principles density functional theory）计算本征与Co掺杂Janus HfSSe单层的稳定性、电子结构、碱金属吸附能、层数容纳、理论容量、开路电压（open-circuit voltage）与迁移势垒（migration barrier）。

Result: 本征HfSSe动力学稳定、带隙0.85 eV；Co掺杂使费米能级移向价带区，首层Li/Na吸附后呈金属性。最优Li、Na吸附能由-2.6125、-1.9800 eV提升至-3.7405、-3.0529 eV；Co掺杂形成缺电子位点增强结合。Co掺杂体系可容纳4层Li与3层Na，理论容量Li由555.43升至776.19 mAh/g、Na由370.29升至582.14 mAh/g；平均开路电压Li为0.62 V、Na为0.29-0.30 V，最小迁移势垒分别为0.2153与0.1044 eV。

Conclusion: Co掺杂Janus HfSSe单层兼具增强的碱金属吸附、提高的理论容量与较低迁移势垒，被认定为有前景的锂离子与钠离子电池负极材料。

Abstract: The growing demand for high-capacity, fast-charging, and durable rechargeable batteries has intensified the search for advanced electrode materials, with two-dimensional materials offering large surface-to-mass ratios and short ion-diffusion pathways. Here, pristine and Co-doped Janus HfSSe monolayers were investigated as anode materials for Li-ion and Na-ion batteries using first-principles density functional theory. Pristine HfSSe was found to be dynamically stable and exhibited a 0.85 eV band gap, while Co substitution shifted the Fermi level toward the valence-band region. Adsorption of the first Li or Na layer induced metallic behavior, thereby improving charge transport. Co doping strengthened alkali-metal binding, with the most favorable Li and Na adsorption energies changing from -2.6125 and -1.9800 eV to -3.7405 and -3.0529 eV, respectively. Co doping created electron-deficient sites that enhanced Li/Na binding. Co-doped HfSSe accommodated up to four Li layers and three Na layers, increasing the theoretical capacities from 555.43 to 776.19 mAh/g for Li and from 370.29 to 582.14 mAh/g for Na. The average open-circuit voltages were 0.62 V for Li and 0.29-0.30 V for Na, with minimum migration barriers of 0.2153 and 0.1044 eV, respectively. These results identified Co-doped Janus HfSSe as a promising anode material for Li-ion and Na-ion batteries.

</details>


### [218] [Crucial role of magnetic dipole and anomalous field dependence in $\mathrm{NiPS_3}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28209)
*Do Hoon Kiem,Muhammad Nauman,Joonyoung Choi,David Graf,Junghyun Kim,Heejun Yang,Je-Geun Park,Younjung Jo,Myung Joon Han*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过高场磁转矩测量（high-field torque magnetometry）结合相对论密度泛函理论（relativistic density functional theory）计算与磁力响应理论（magnetic force response theory），构建了NiPS₃的有效低能自旋哈密顿量（effective low-energy spin Hamiltonian），揭示磁偶极相互作用（magnetic dipole interaction）及自旋翻转转变（spin-flop transition）在偏离二维自旋排列中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NiPS₃的奇异磁行为来源不明，主要障碍在于对其低能各向异性（low-energy anisotropy）的认识有限，现有标准XY或XXZ模型难以描述超出该框架的相互作用项。

Method: 采用高场磁转矩测量，发现此前未被识别的各向异性自旋响应（anisotropic spin response）；结合相对论密度泛函理论计算与磁力响应理论，构建有效低能自旋哈密顿量。

Result: 所构建的哈密顿量不仅解释了观测到的反常磁行为，也兼容近期其他实验结果；磁偶极相互作用及伴随的自旋翻转转变被确认为驱动体系偏离二维自旋排列的重要因素，从而直接揭示其各向异性能量层级（anisotropic energy hierarchy）。

Conclusion: 该广义哈密顿量（generalized Hamiltonian）可作为后续研究的重要基础，所采用的研究方法有望适用于其他相关材料体系。

Abstract: $\mathrm{NiPS_3}$ has attracted considerable attention, yet the origin of its intriguing magnetic behaviors remains elusive due to limited insight into its low-energy anisotropy. Here, high-field torque magnetometry measurements are performed, revealing a previously unrecognized anisotropic spin response that reflects interaction terms beyond the standard XY or XXZ models. In combination with relativistic density functional theory calculations and magnetic force response theory, an effective low-energy spin Hamiltonian is constructed, elucidating not only the observed anomalous magnetic behavior but also other recent experiments. Remarkably, the magnetic dipole interaction and the associated spin-flop transition play a crucial role in driving the deviation from two-dimensional spin alignment, providing direct insight into the underlying anisotropic energy hierarchy. The generalized Hamiltonian can serve as the important fundamental ground for future study, and our approach adopted here will hopefully be relevant to many other related material systems.

</details>


### [219] [High-throughput Down-selection of Fusion Candidate Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28232)
*Elena Botica-Artalejo,Josh Randolph,Kevin B. Woller,Gregory Wallace,Michael P. Short*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出一套高通量（high-throughput, HT）实验框架，将组合合成（combinatorial synthesis）、原位离子辐照（in situ ion irradiation）与多性能筛选整合于统一流程，用于聚变堆 Cu-Cr-Ta 射频天线材料的加速筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统辐照鉴定（irradiation qualification）方法速度缓慢，通常需要大量逐一进行的辐照实验与辐照后表征（post-irradiation characterization）；按现有发展速度，可能无法及时获得部署聚变电厂所需材料。

Method: 采用物理气相沉积（physical vapor deposition）制备二维 Cu-Cr-Ta 成分梯度晶圆，并用原位离子辐照瞬态光栅光谱（in situ ion irradiation transient grating spectroscopy, I3TGS）表征；以辐照诱导表面声波频率波动作为一次缺陷（primary defect）积累的快速代理指标，同时测量热扩散率（thermal diffusivity）。补充高通量表征包括 EDS 成分图谱、四探针电阻率测量及废物处置评级（waste disposal rating）计算。

Result: 该集成方法相对传统辐照路线实现超过 100 倍的加速，分析 78 种新材料所需时间由数月缩短至数小时。

Conclusion: 组合合成、原位离子辐照与多性能筛选的统一工作流可实现聚变相关材料的快速降选（down-selection），为辐照材料研发提速提供可行路径。

Abstract: Developing radiation-resistant fusion reactor materials is constrained by the slow pace of conventional irradiation qualification methodologies, which usually require extensive one-at-a-time irradiation campaigns and post-irradiation characterization. At the current pace of development, we may not discover the materials necessary to deploy fusion power plants at desired rates. In this work, we present a high-throughput (HT) experimental framework for the accelerated down-selection of fusion-relevant, Cu-Cr-Ta RF antenna materials by integrating combinatorial synthesis, in situ ion irradiation, and multi-property screening within a unified workflow. A 2D compositional Cu-Cr-Ta gradient wafer was fabricated by physical vapor deposition and characterized using in situ ion irradiation transient grating spectroscopy I3TGS. Radiation-induced surface acoustic wave frequency fluctuations were used as a rapid proxy for primary defect accumulation, while thermal diffusivity measurements were obtained simultaneously during irradiation. Complementary HT characterization included EDS compositional mapping, four-point probe electrical resistivity measurements, and waste disposal rating calculations to evaluate long-term activation behavior. This integrated methodology enabled an acceleration with respect to conventional irradiation approaches by more than 100 times, from months to hours to analyze 78 new materials.

</details>


### [220] [Multiscale Modeling of Zr Corrosion: Predicting Microstructures of Oxide Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28493)
*K. Starkey,L. Kahle,M. Christensen,V. Eyert,A. El-Azab,C. B. Geller,A. P. Shivprasad,E. Wimmer*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种第一性原理驱动的多尺度建模方法，用于模拟锆腐蚀过程中的氧化膜形成；模拟复现了柱状氧化膜微观结构、晶粒尺寸分布及波浪状金属/氧化膜界面，并识别出界面处六方ZrO层为显著的氧扩散屏障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从第一性原理出发、多尺度地理解锆腐蚀氧化膜的形成机制及其微观结构起源，特别是柱状晶结构、界面形貌以及腐蚀动力学偏离抛物线规律的物理原因。

Method: 结合基于DFT训练集的机器学习势分子动力学模拟与第一性原理密度泛函理论（DFT）计算获取ZrO_x基础性质，并将化学计量依赖的热化学数据、各向异性弹性、界面能、体相与晶界质量输运、多相异质晶粒形核及应变能依赖相稳定性输入先进的相场（phase field）模型（MedeA PhaseField模块）。

Result: 模拟复现了显微照片中的柱状腐蚀膜微观结构与晶粒尺寸分布；该结构源于紧邻α-Zr/ZrO_{2-x}界面的六方ZrO层，其由连续异质晶粒形核与晶界质量扩散动态稳定化，并被识别为显著的氧扩散屏障及亚抛物线（sub-parabolic）预转变腐蚀动力学的潜在贡献因素；模拟亦复现了金属/氧化膜界面的波浪状特征，其局部存在可诱发裂纹的拉应力区，尽管氧化膜整体处于压应力状态。

Conclusion: 第一性原理信息驱动的多尺度相场框架能够定量再现锆氧化膜的关键微观结构特征，并揭示界面ZrO层在扩散阻挡与腐蚀动力学中的作用，以及界面起伏导致的局部拉应力开裂风险。

Abstract: A first-principles-informed multiscale modeling approach is reported for oxide formation during corrosion of zirconium. The present simulations reproduce the columnar corrosion film microstructure and grain size distribution seen in micrographs of oxidized Zr surfaces. This structural feature emerges from a hexagonal ZrO layer adjacent to the $α$-Zr/ZrO$_{2-x}$ interface, which is dynamically stabilized by continuous heterogeneous grain nucleation and grain boundary mass diffusion. This ZrO layer is identified as a significant oxygen diffusion barrier and a potential contributor to sub-parabolic pretransition corrosion kinetics. The simulations also reproduce the undulatory nature of the metal/oxide interface, leading to regions with tensile stress components capable of nucleating cracks, despite the overall state of compressive hydrostatic stress within the oxide film. First principles density functional theory (DFT) computations of fundamental ZrO$_x$ properties were combined with molecular dynamics simulations using a machine-learned potential based on a DFT training set to provide extensive input to an advanced phase field model for oxide formation and its microstructure, as implemented in the new MedeA PhaseField module. The phase field simulations reported herein incorporated computed stoichiometry-dependent thermochemical data, anisotropic elastic properties, interface energies, bulk and grain boundary mass transport processes, multiphase heterogeneous grain nucleation, and strain energy-dependent phase stabilities.

</details>


### [221] [Symmetry-Guided Machine Learning of Spin-Dependent Nonlinear Hall Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28529)
*Jyotirmoy Sau*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文以蜂窝（honeycomb）模型研究贝里曲率偶极（Berry-curvature dipole）受结构对称性、磁矩取向与载流子占据共同调控的机制，并建立对称性约束多项式代理模型，对256个留出自旋构型的偶极分量预测R²>0.9995。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝里曲率偶极可在线性霍尔响应（linear Hall response）消失时产生非线性霍尔电流（nonlinear Hall current），但对称性分析本身无法确定其大小与磁可调性，故需厘清结构对称、磁矩取向与费米面附近占据窗口对偶极的定量影响。

Method: 在蜂窝模型中计算曲率图（curvature maps）与能带求和的偶极被积函数，分析速度与占据窗口如何筛选有符号贡献；通过移除三重旋转（threefold rotation）构造非磁性偶极，并以对称性约束多项式模型拟合磁取向依赖关系，在固定载流子密度与化学势扫描两种条件下检验预测精度。

Result: 磁矩重取向可激活第二偶极分量并使两分量在正负值间调节；全局反转保持偶极不变而反转本征线性霍尔电导率（intrinsic linear Hall conductivity），使等占据的反向畴（reversed domains）在均匀场平均下仍贡献有限体偶极。参考化学势处代理模型对256个留出构型预测达R²>0.9995；固定载流子密度时因化学势的取向依赖移动误差略增，化学势扫描中D_y精度下降更显著。

Conclusion: 在常数弛豫时间（relaxation time）的半经典输运框架下，所得偶极决定二次电流（quadratic current）及其整流（rectified）与二次谐波（second-harmonic）分量的角度依赖，从而给出磁取向依赖偶极的定量描述，并揭示载流子占据同时影响响应与可预测性。

Abstract: A Berry-curvature dipole can produce a nonlinear Hall current even when the linear Hall response vanishes, but symmetry alone does not determine its magnitude or magnetic tunability. The interplay of structural symmetry, moment orientation, and carrier occupation is examined in a honeycomb model. Curvature maps and the band-summed dipole integrand reveal how velocity and the occupation window near the Fermi level select signed contributions. Removing threefold rotation permits a nonmagnetic dipole perpendicular to the surviving mirror. Generic magnetic reorientation activates the second component and tunes both components through positive and negative values. Global reversal preserves the dipole while reversing the intrinsic linear Hall conductivity, allowing a finite bulk dipole contribution from equally populated reversed domains under uniform-field averaging.A symmetry-constrained polynomial model predicts both dipole components for 256 held-out spin configurations, achieving coefficients of determination $R^2 > 0.9995$ at the reference chemical potential. At fixed carrier density, orientation-dependent shifts of the chemical potential modify the response and yield slightly larger prediction errors for the chosen model. Across the chemical-potential sweep,the prediction accuracy decreases more strongly for $D_y$,revealing an energy dependence of the surrogate's performance. Within semiclassical transport with a constant relaxation time, the calculated dipole determines the angular dependence of the quadratic current and its rectified and second-harmonic components. These results provide a quantitative representation of magnetic-orientation-dependent Berry-curvature dipoles and show how carrier occupation influences both the response and its predictability.

</details>


### [222] [Differentiable powder X-ray diffraction enables analysis of evolving structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28564)
*Xiangyu Yin,Nina Andrejevic,Hemant Sharma,Ming Du,Aileen Luo,Michael Prince,Burak Guzelturk,Xiaoyi Zhang,Brian H. Toby,Mathew J. Cherukara*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出 diffPXRD——基于 PyTorch 的可微粉末 X 射线衍射（powder X-ray diffraction, PXRD）引擎，通过自动微分（automatic differentiation）实现无需重写导数代码的共享模型参数化精修，并使批量拟合速度提升逾七倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原位（in-situ）与操作态（operando）实验产生数百至数千张 PXRD 图谱，逐图精修会因弱信号导致结构轨迹噪声大；现有参数化精修程序在修改模型与高效拟合方面仍存在困难。

Method: diffPXRD 以 PyTorch 实现，利用自动微分计算导数，使共享模型可直接更换而无需编写新的导数代码，并支持批量（batched）拟合。

Result: 批量拟合比串行（serial）拟合快七倍以上；在操作态正极（cathode）序列中，仅保留 10% 光子计数（photon counts）时，共享晶格模型（shared lattice model）使晶格参数变异性降低 68.4%。共享模型还可描述 Li2S 的沉淀与溶解，并重构 Ca3Ti2O7 中的集体原子位移（collective atomic displacements）。

Conclusion: 可微编程框架为演化材料的多图谱 PXRD 分析提供了灵活且高效的共享模型精修途径，适用于弱信号条件下的结构轨迹提取及复杂相变过程刻画。

Abstract: Operando and in-situ experiments extend powder X-ray diffraction (PXRD) to materials that evolve during experiments, producing series of hundreds or thousands of patterns. Such series are commonly refined one pattern at a time, so weak signals give noisy structural trajectories. Parametric refinement instead fits a shared model to all patterns, but with most existing programs, changing the model or fitting it efficiently remains challenging. Here, we introduce diffPXRD, a differentiable PXRD engine in PyTorch. diffPXRD calculates derivatives by automatic differentiation, so a shared model can be changed without new derivative code. Batched fitting is also more than seven times faster than serial fitting. In an operando cathode series, a shared lattice model can reduce lattice-parameter variability by 68.4% when 10% of the photon counts are retained. We also show shared models can describe the precipitation and dissolution of Li2S and reconstruct collective atomic displacements in Ca3Ti2O7.

</details>


### [223] [Correcting topographic artifacts in three-dimensional ToF-SIMS: A practical guide from data-only reconstruction to correlative AFM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28593)
*Jacob Shusterman,Anton V. Ievlev*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文是一篇实用指南，按所需信息量将三维飞行时间二次离子质谱（3D ToF-SIMS）的形貌伪影校正分为三类，并在Pt/TaOx/Ta/Pt/Ti忆阻器件上以原位SIMS/AFM十阶段数据逐类评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准三维重构假设起始表面平坦且溅射速率单一，对具有表面形貌（topography）或分层溅射速率不同的样品会产生界面位置与深度标尺的畸变，需要系统化的校正方案指导。

Method: 按所需信息将校正方法分为三类：仅用SIMS数据、剖面前后原子力显微镜（AFM）形貌、以及包含中间阶段的AFM形貌；三者连同共用的数据准备步骤，应用于经原位（in situ）SIMS/AFM在十个阶段测量的模型器件。

Result: 仅数据校正在无需额外仪器下恢复相对形貌，但深度标尺依赖标称层厚且在最后一个标定界面以下无约束；前后AFM校正固定起止表面却假设中间均匀溅射，在器件邻近区域因材料变化而失效；多阶段AFM校正可跟踪这些变化并再现器件结构，代价是仪器时间与对部分溅射表面的重复成像。

Conclusion: 多阶段AFM校正在精度与代价间形成权衡，作者提供决策流程图（decision flowchart）与检查清单以帮助读者选择、验证并报告校正方案。

Abstract: Three-dimensional time-of-flight secondary ion mass spectrometry (3D ToF-SIMS) builds chemical volumes by stacking images acquired between sputter cycles. Standard reconstruction assumes a flat starting surface and a single sputter rate, so samples with topography, or with layers that sputter at different rates, yield distorted interface positions and depth scales. This article is a practical guide to correcting these artifacts. We group the available corrections by the information they need: the SIMS data alone, atomic force microscopy (AFM) topography before and after the profile, or AFM topography also at intermediate stages. All three are applied to a model Pt/TaOx/Ta/Pt/Ti crossbar memristor device measured with in situ SIMS/AFM at ten stages, together with the data preparation they share. Each correction method is evaluated using the known device geometry and the measured surface evolution. The data-only correction requires no additional instrumentation and restores relative topography, but its depth scale depends on nominal layer thicknesses and remains unconstrained below the last calibrated interface. AFM before and after the profile fixes the start and end surfaces but assumes uniform sputtering in between, which fails next to the device, where the surroundings change material during the profile. Multi-stage AFM correction follows these changes and reproduces the structure of the device, at the cost of instrument time and repeated imaging of a partially sputtered surface. A decision flowchart and a checklist help readers choose, validate and report a correction.

</details>


### [224] [GBClaw: An agentic AI framework for atomistic grain boundary research and mechanistic discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28629)
*Yazhuo Liu,Kunqing Ding,Xiaoguo Gong,Zifeng Wang,Yichen Yang,Yifeng Che,Wen Chen,Josh Kacher,Olivier Pierron,Shigenobu Ogata,Ting Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出智能体式AI框架GBClaw，用于研究一般晶界（general grain boundaries）的结构与变形行为，并在镍的14个非对称倾转晶界上验证其机制分析能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一般晶界占多晶材料实际界面的绝大多数，但因结构复杂与界面失配（interfacial mismatch）而研究不足；将智能体式AI（agentic AI）扩展到领域密集型机制研究仍具挑战。

Method: GBClaw将大语言模型（large language models, LLMs）集成到模块化架构中，包含编排器、专用智能体、领域工具、模拟接口与分层记忆（hierarchical memory），并通过动态重规划（dynamic replanning）与迭代工作流精炼连接系统模拟与针对性机制分析。

Result: 在镍的14个一般非对称倾转晶界中表征了界面缺陷结构并识别出多种变形模式；对比有无次级晶界错配位错（secondary GB misfit dislocations）的边界滑移，发现剪切强度存在显著差异，表明缺陷层级（defect hierarchies）对滑移阻力的关键作用；研究者引导的后续计算将异常高的剪切阻力归因于亚稳界面结构。

Conclusion: 智能体式AI与领域专业知识的结合可衔接高通量计算筛选（high-throughput computational screening）与对复杂材料界面的深层物理理解。

Abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence (AI) systems hold immense potential for automating complex workflows in materials discovery and design, but extending them to domain-intensive mechanistic investigations remains challenging. Here, we present GBClaw, an agentic AI framework for investigating the structure and deformation of general grain boundaries (GBs). Although general GBs constitute the vast majority of real-world interfaces in polycrystalline materials, they remain understudied because their structural complexity and interfacial mismatch complicate experimental and modeling research. GBClaw integrates large language models (LLMs) into a modular architecture comprising an orchestrator, specialized agents, domain-specific tools, simulation interfaces, and hierarchical memory. Through dynamic replanning and iterative workflow refinement, the framework effectively connects systematic simulations with targeted mechanistic analysis. Applied to 14 general asymmetric tilt GBs in nickel, GBClaw characterized interfacial defect structures and identified diverse deformation modes across these incommensurate boundaries. Comparing boundary sliding with and without secondary GB misfit dislocations revealed substantial differences in shear strength, underscoring the critical role of defect hierarchies in sliding resistance. Furthermore, researcher-guided follow-up calculations traced an anomalously high shear resistance to metastable boundary structures. This work demonstrates that combining agentic AI with domain expertise bridges high-throughput computational screening with deep physical understanding of complex material interfaces.

</details>

### [225] [The Electron-Recoil Response Tail and the LZ High-Energy Event Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27105)
*Triparno Bandyopadhyay,Rajarajeswari Ramasamy,Nishitha TP*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该论文指出，LZ实验已发布的电子反冲（electron-recoil）背景模拟模型对分布尾部缺乏实验约束，仅凭均值、方差、偏度及中心分位数一致的分布，其尾部概率可相差逾五个数量级；尾部与中心刻画吻合到0.14%即可使该贡献脱离可忽略范围，0.47%便与整个背景相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ合作组发布的模拟配置规定了确定的响应尾部，但公开的刻度（calibration）信息未给出物理尾部可偏离该模型的界限，而背景期望恰恰依赖这一尾部概率，因此需评估该不确定性对背景预期的影响。

Method: 从合作组公开的模拟配置重建连续电子反冲对LZ背景期望的贡献；构造在均值、方差、标准化偏度（standardised skewness）上完全一致、且事件条件10%、50%、90% N_e分位数吻合约1%的分布族，比较其相关尾部概率的差异，并考察尾部与中心刻画吻合程度（0.14%、0.47%）对应的贡献量级。

Result: 在中心矩与分位数高度一致（约1%）的分布之间，相关尾部概率差异超过五个数量级；尾部吻合至0.14%时分位数水平即足以将电子反冲贡献抬出可忽略范围，吻合至0.47%时其量级可与比较区域（comparison region）所引全部背景相当。

Conclusion: 现有公开信息不足以约束电子反冲尾部，背景期望存在被严重低估的风险；论文据此指出可判定该问题的具体测量，需以实验数据约束尾部行为。

Abstract: We reconstruct the continuum electron-recoil contribution to the LZ background expectation from the collaboration's released simulation configuration. The released model specifies a definite response tail, but the published calibration information supplies no bound on how far the physical tail may depart from it at the probabilities the expectation depends on. Distributions reproducing the executable model's mean, variance, and standardised skewness exactly, and its event-conditioned 10, 50, and 90\% \(N_e\) quantiles to about one per cent, differ in the relevant tail probability by more than five orders of magnitude. The ladder is short: a tail agreeing with that central characterisation to 0.14\% in the quantiles lifts the electron-recoil contribution out of the negligible range, and one agreeing to 0.47\% makes it comparable to the entire background quoted for the comparison region. We go on to identify the measurements that would settle it.

</details>


### [226] [Pretraining on boosted boson decays for polarization tagging of hadronic $W$ bosons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27346)
*Saptaparna Bhattacharya*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该文研究纵向极化（longitudinally polarized）弱玻色子的喷注标记问题，指出受喷注内衰变产物接受度限制，衰变角（decay angle）携带了喷注内几乎全部极化信息，但更多信息位于喷注之外，事件级标记（event-level tagger）优于通用喷注标记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向极化弱玻色子（weak bosons）是检验矢量玻色子散射（vector-boson scattering）幺正化（unitarization）的关键探针，但其在W+jet事件中占比仅0.3%，且其与横向玻色子的喷注在质量与双叉结构（two-prong structure）上几乎相同，仅靠衰变角区分，因此需明确极化信息究竟位于何处以及标记器（tagger）能恢复多少。

Method: 要求两个衰变夸克均落在喷注内会截断衰变角分布，形成由 m/(p_T R) 决定的边界，作者据此给出任何强子极化标记器的上界；并在10^7个无极化喷注（含 boosted 玻色子、顶夸克与QCD）上预训练点边变换器（point-edge transformer），以每类783个带标签喷注固定变换器块进行微调，并与从头训练、通用预训练、基于子结构观测量（substructure observables）的提升决策树（boosted decision tree）比较；同时用知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）训练小型学生网络。

Result: 在允许的接受度内，衰变角基本携带W+jet事件中喷注的全部极化信息；微调后的网络优于从头训练，但与通用混合喷注预训练及BDT表现相当；最佳标记器恢复了衰变角所能提供的大部分（但非全部）区分能力，而参数量少数百倍的学生网络经蒸馏可与教师网络持平。利用反冲喷注（recoiling jet）重建的产生角（production angle）可提升所有标记器；在双玻色子产生（diboson production）与矢量玻色子散射的矩阵元（matrix-element）层面，产生运动学信息多于两个玻色子的衰变角。

Conclusion: 极化信息大量存在于喷注之外；以牺牲喷注标记器的通用性为代价、改用针对过程训练的事件级标记器可获得显著增益，这一策略可被纵向矢量玻色子散射的测量所利用。

Abstract: Longitudinally polarized weak bosons probe the unitarization of vector-boson scattering. At large boosts, longitudinal and transverse $W$ bosons produce jets with the same mass and two-prong structure, distinguished only by the decay angle. Longitudinal bosons are rare: they occur in only 0.3% of $W$+jet events at these large boosts. We ask where the polarization information of a boosted $W$ boson lies and how much a tagger can recover. Requiring both decay quarks within the jet truncates the decay-angle distribution at an edge set by $m/(p_T R)$, which we show bounds any hadronic polarization tagger. Within this acceptance, the decay angle carries essentially all the polarization information of a jet in $W$+jet events. Polarized samples are costly to simulate for vector-boson scattering, so we pretrain a point-edge transformer on $10^7$ unpolarized jets of boosted bosons, top quarks and QCD and test how much it recovers from few labels. Fine-tuned on 783 labeled jets per class with its transformer blocks kept fixed, the network pretrained to classify these jets improves on training from scratch, but generic pretraining on a broad mix of jets and a boosted decision tree on substructure observables do as well. The best taggers recover most, but not all, of the separation the decay angle allows, and student networks with a few hundred times fewer parameters, trained by knowledge distillation, match their teacher. More information lies outside the jet. The production angle, reconstructed with the recoiling jet, improves every tagger. In diboson production and vector-boson scattering at matrix-element level, the production kinematics carry more information than the decay angles of both bosons. Trading the generality of a jet tagger for an event-level tagger trained on the process thus brings significant gains, which measurements of longitudinal vector-boson scattering could exploit.

</details>


### [227] [Charmed baryon decays at BESIII](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27760)
*Chengzhi He*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: BESIII 在 4.60–4.95 GeV 累积 6.4 fb$^{-1}$ 数据、拥有世界最大 $\Lambda_c^+\bar\Lambda_c^-$ 对样本，本文汇总了粲重子（charmed baryon）衰变的多项最新成果，涵盖极化、包容衰变、半轻子与新衰变道观测及分波分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粲重子（charmed baryon）衰变实验数据长期匮乏，借助世界最大 $\Lambda_c^+\bar\Lambda_c^-$ 对样本可在极化、衰变分支比与动力学结构等方面开展此前不可行的精确测量与首次观测。

Method: 基于 BESIII 在质心能量 4.60–4.95 GeV 采集的 6.4 fb$^{-1}$ $e^+e^-$ 对撞数据，采用（部分工作借助深度学习（deep-learning）技术）事例重建与选择方法，对多个 $\Lambda_c^+$ 衰变道进行首次观测、精确测量与分波分析（partial wave analysis）。

Result: 报告了 $e^+e^-\to\Lambda_c^+\bar\Lambda_c^-$ 中 $\Lambda_c^+$ 横向极化（transverse polarization）的首次观测、包容衰变（inclusive decay）$\Lambda_c^+\to\Lambda X$ 的精确测量、$\Lambda_c^+\to ne^+\nu_e$ 与 $\Lambda_c^+\to n\pi^+\eta$ 的首次观测、$\Lambda_c^+\to pK^-e^+\nu_e$ 的首次观测及 $\Lambda_c^+\to\Sigma^\pm\pi^\mp e^+\nu_e$ 的迹象，以及 $\Lambda_c^+\to\Lambda\pi^+\eta$ 的分波分析。

Conclusion: 该系列结果表明 BESIII 在粲重子衰变研究上具备独特优势，所获数据与结果可为理解粲重子极化机制、半轻子衰变及强子动力学结构提供新的实验依据。

Abstract: BESIII has accumulated 6.4~fb$^{-1}$ of $e^+e^-$ collision data at center-of-mass energies from 4.60 to 4.95~GeV, corresponding to the world's largest sample of $Λ_c^+\barΛ_c^-$ pairs. This proceeding summarizes recent BESIII results on charmed baryon decays, including the first observation of the transverse polarization of the $Λ_c^+$ in $e^+e^-\toΛ_c^+\barΛ_c^-$, a precision measurement of the inclusive decay $Λ_c^+\toΛX$, the first observations of $Λ_c^+\to ne^+ν_e$ and $Λ_c^+\to nπ^+η$ with deep-learning techniques, the first observation of $Λ_c^+\to pK^-e^+ν_e$ together with evidence for $Λ_c^+\toΣ^\pmπ^\mp e^+ν_e$, and partial wave analyses of $Λ_c^+\toΛπ^+η$.

</details>


### [228] [Open Science with the LHCb Ntupling Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28296)
*Christine A. Aidala,Michael Buchar,Chris Burr,Dillon S. Fitzgerald,Cameron Duncan McClymont,Adam Morris,Sebastian Neubert,Piet Nogga,Ryunosuke O'Neil,Eduardo Rodrigues,Daan Eduard Rosendal,Tibor Šimko*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: LHCb 合作组开发了 LHCb Ntupling Service，一种按需生成并发布定制化开放数据的网络服务，首次将 Run 2 数据纳入开放访问范围，累计可探索数据超过 4 PB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型强子对撞机（Large Hadron Collider, LHC）所采集数据价值极高且已催生多项发现，为最大化科学产出，亟需保存数据并向未来分析者开放访问。

Method: LHCb 合作组（LHCb collaboration）提出 LHCb Ntupling Service，即一种用于按需生成（on-demand production）与发布定制化 LHCb 开放数据的网络服务（web service）。

Result: 该服务同时开放 Run 1 数据，并首次开放 LHCb 实验采集的 Run 2 质子-质子碰撞（proton-proton collision）数据，可探索数据总量超过 4 PB。

Conclusion: 该工作代表开放科学（open science）的一种新范式，通过按需生成与发布开放数据，提升 LHC 数据的可复用性与长期科学价值。

Abstract: The data samples collected at the Large Hadron Collider at CERN are highly valuable, and the analysis of these data has led to numerous discoveries and the 2025 Breakthrough Prize in Fundamental Physics. Preserving and providing open access to these data for future analysts is imperative to maximize the scientific output of the collected data. The LHCb collaboration has developed a novel approach to this pursuit of open science, the LHCb Ntupling Service: a web service for on-demand production and publishing of custom LHCb open data, providing open access to both Run 1, and for the first time, Run 2 proton-proton collision data collected by the LHCb experiment, amounting to over 4 PB of data to explore.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [229] [Commutative Coarse-graining on the Sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26896)
*Chayut Teeraratkul,Hussein Aluie*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 球面上的空间卷积滤波（spatial convolution）普遍违背与微分算子交换这一基本一致性要求，其根源在于弯曲几何（curved geometry）本身；本文论证广义球面卷积（generalized spherical convolution）是唯一满足交换性的线性算子形式，并通过不稳定射流（unstable jet）模拟验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滤波是粗粒化（coarse-graining）、大涡模拟（Large Eddy Simulation）、多尺度动力学分析与数据处理的基础工具，但球面上常用的滤波不满足与微分算子交换（commutation with differential operators）的条件，从而难以正确导出粗粒化动力学并保持跨尺度物理一致性。

Method: 剖析问题根源，指出其源于弯曲几何而非度规项（metric terms）、坐标系或数值网格；论证对给定标量核（scalar kernel），广义球面卷积是与径向投影（radial projection）、散度（divergence）和旋度（curl）均交换的唯一线性算子，并说明谱截断（spectral truncation）、小波变换（wavelet transform）、扩散（diffusion）、格林函数反演（Green's-function inversion）及机器学习卷积（machine-learned convolution）等一致算子必取此形式；最后用不稳定射流模拟进行数值检验。

Result: 在不稳定射流模拟中，广义球面卷积的交换性达到数值舍入误差（numerical round-off）量级；而忽略曲率的常用滤波在无散度处产生显著虚假散度（spurious divergence），梯度误差（gradient errors）与梯度本身量级相当。

Conclusion: 球面滤波的一致性问题具有几何内在性，广义球面卷积为各类线性一致性算子提供了统一且唯一的框架；忽略曲率的常规滤波会引入不可忽略的物理失真，应在球面粗粒化与多尺度分析中予以替代。

Abstract: Filtering by spatial convolution underlies coarse-graining and Large Eddy Simulation, the analysis of multiscale dynamics, and data processing across the sciences. Yet, as commonly practiced on the sphere, it violates a basic consistency requirement: commutation with differential operators, which is necessary for deriving coarse-grained dynamics and preserving the physics across lengthscales. We discuss the root of the problem, which is intrinsic to the curved geometry rather than being a result of metric terms, coordinate system, or numerical grid. A recently proposed generalized spherical convolution is, for a given scalar kernel, the unique linear operator commuting with the radial projection, divergence, and curl. Any consistent linear operator, be it spectral truncation, wavelet transform, diffusion, Green's-function inversion, or a machine-learned convolution, necessarily takes this form. From a simulation of an unstable jet, we demonstrate commutation to within numerical round-off, whereas common filtering that ignores curvature produces significant spurious divergence where none exists and gradient errors comparable to the gradients themselves.

</details>


<div id='math.CV'></div>

# math.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [230] [Hypercomplex Method for Solving Nonlinear PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27042)
*Vitalii Shpakivskyi*

Main category: math.CV

TL;DR: 本文发展了超复方法（hypercomplex method）构造一类非线性偏微分方程（nonlinear PDEs）的解：引入结构系数依赖单演函数（monogenic function）自身分量的二维交换结合代数族，建立相应的单演函数理论，并借助速矢变换（hodograph transformation）线性化非线性柯西–黎曼系统（Cauchy–Riemann system），从而给出构造解的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是为一类二阶非线性偏微分方程提供系统化的解构造途径，通过推广超复与单演函数框架，使其能够处理系数依赖未知函数的非线性方程，而不仅限于常系数线性情形。

Method: 构造一族二维交换结合代数（commutative associative algebras），其结构系数（structural coefficients）依赖于单演函数的分量本身；据此建立单演函数理论并获得非线性柯西–黎曼条件的类比形式。进一步利用速矢变换（hodograph transformation）将非线性柯西–黎曼系统线性化，把原问题化为求解变系数二阶线性偏微分方程并施以逆速矢变换。

Result: 证明单演函数的第一分量满足一大类系数依赖未知函数的二阶非线性偏微分方程；并建立非线性柯西–黎曼系统解与相应线性方程解之间的局部对应（local correspondence），由此得到生成原非线性方程解的构造性算法。文中给出若干示例，获得二阶非线性偏微分方程的显式（explicit）与隐式（implicit）解。

Conclusion: 该超复框架结合速矢变换，为系数依赖未知函数的非线性偏微分方程提供了一条构造性求解路径，并通过局部解对应关系与具体示例验证了方法的可行性。

Abstract: A hypercomplex method for constructing solutions of a class of nonlinear partial differential equations is developed. A family of two-dimensional commutative associative algebras is introduced in which the structural coefficients depend on a component of the monogenic function itself. A corresponding theory of monogenic functions is developed, and nonlinear analogues of the Cauchy-Riemann conditions are obtained. It is shown that the first component of a monogenic function satisfies a broad class of second-order nonlinear partial differential equations whose coefficients depend on the unknown function.
  A hodograph transformation is used to linearize the nonlinear Cauchy-Riemann system. As a result, the problem of constructing solutions of the nonlinear equations is reduced to solving a second-order linear partial differential equation with variable coefficients, followed by the inverse hodograph transformation. A local correspondence between solutions of the nonlinear Cauchy-Riemann system and solutions of the associated linear equation is established, yielding a constructive algorithm for generating solutions of the original nonlinear PDEs.
  Several examples are presented in which explicit and implicit solutions of nonlinear second-order PDEs are obtained by this method.

</details>
